# Fast Matrix Multiplication meets the Subdmodular Width

快速矩阵乘法遇上子模宽度

MAHMOUD ABO-KHAMIS, RelationalAI, USA

马哈茂德·阿布-哈米斯，RelationalAI，美国

XIAO HU, University of Waterloo, Canada

胡晓，滑铁卢大学，加拿大

DAN SUCIU, University of Washington, USA

丹·苏丘，华盛顿大学，美国

One fundamental question in database theory is the following: Given a Boolean conjunctive query $Q$ ,what is the best complexity for computing the answer to $Q$ in terms of the input database size $N$ ? When restricted to the class of combinatorial algorithms,the best known complexity for any query $Q$ is captured by the submodular width of $Q\left\lbrack  {3,4,{17}}\right\rbrack$ . However,beyond combinatorial algorithms,certain queries are known to admit faster algorithms that often involve a clever combination of fast matrix multiplication and data partitioning. Nevertheless, there is no systematic way to derive and analyze the complexity of such algorithms for arbitrary queries $Q$ .

数据库理论中的一个基本问题是：给定布尔连接查询$Q$，根据输入数据库大小$N$计算$Q$答案的最佳复杂度是什么？当限于组合算法类时，任何查询$Q$的最佳已知复杂度由$Q\left\lbrack  {3,4,{17}}\right\rbrack$的子模宽度决定。然而，超越组合算法，某些查询已知可采用更快的算法，这些算法通常巧妙结合了快速矩阵乘法和数据分区。但目前尚无系统方法为任意查询$Q$推导和分析此类算法的复杂度。

In this work, we introduce a general framework that captures the best complexity for answering any Boolean conjunctive query $Q$ using matrix multiplication. Our framework unifies both combinatorial and non-combinatorial techniques under the umbrella of information theory. It generalizes the notion of submodular width to a new stronger notion called the $\omega$ -submodular width that naturally incorporates the power of fast matrix multiplication. We describe a matching algorithm that computes the answer to any query $Q$ in time corresponding to the $\omega$ -submodular width of $Q$ . We show that our framework recovers the best known complexities for Boolean queries that have been studied in the literature, to the best of our knowledge, and also discovers new algorithms for some classes of queries that improve upon the best known complexities.

本文提出一个通用框架，用于捕捉利用矩阵乘法回答任何布尔连接查询$Q$的最佳复杂度。该框架在信息论范畴下统一了组合与非组合技术，将子模宽度推广为更强的新概念——$\omega$-子模宽度，自然融合了快速矩阵乘法的能力。我们描述了一种匹配算法，其计算查询$Q$答案的时间复杂度对应$Q$的$\omega$-子模宽度。研究表明，该框架不仅重现了文献中布尔查询的最佳已知复杂度，还为某些查询类发现了优于现有最佳复杂度的新算法。

CCS Concepts: $\cdot$ Theory of computation $\rightarrow$ Database query processing and optimization (theory); $\cdot$ Mathematics of computing $\rightarrow$ Information theory; - Computing methodologies $\rightarrow$ Linear algebra algorithms.

CCS概念：$\cdot$计算理论$\rightarrow$数据库查询处理与优化（理论）；$\cdot$计算数学$\rightarrow$信息论；-计算方法$\rightarrow$线性代数算法

Additional Key Words and Phrases: join algorithms; fast matrix multiplication; information theory; Shannon inequalities

附加关键词：连接算法；快速矩阵乘法；信息论；香农不等式

## 1 INTRODUCTION

## 1 引言

We focus on the problem of answering a Boolean conjunctive query $Q$ . In particular,we have a set $\operatorname{vars}\left( Q\right)$ of variables (or attributes) and a set atoms(Q)of relations where each relation $R\left( \mathbf{X}\right)  \in  \operatorname{atoms}\left( Q\right)$ is over a variable set $\mathbf{X} \subseteq  \operatorname{vars}\left( Q\right)$ . In particular,each relation $R\left( \mathbf{X}\right)$ is a list of satisfying assignments to the variables $\mathbf{X}$ ,and the query $Q$ asks whether there exists an assignment to all variables $\operatorname{vars}\left( Q\right)$ that simultaneously satisfies all relations in atoms(Q):

我们聚焦于布尔连接查询$Q$的应答问题。具体而言，给定变量（或属性）集合$\operatorname{vars}\left( Q\right)$和关系集合atoms(Q)，其中每个关系$R\left( \mathbf{X}\right)  \in  \operatorname{atoms}\left( Q\right)$作用于变量集$\mathbf{X} \subseteq  \operatorname{vars}\left( Q\right)$。每个关系$R\left( \mathbf{X}\right)$是变量$\mathbf{X}$的满足赋值列表，查询$Q$询问是否存在对所有变量$\operatorname{vars}\left( Q\right)$的赋值能同时满足atoms(Q)中的所有关系：

$$
Q\left( \right)  \mathrel{\text{:-}} \mathop{\bigwedge }\limits_{{R\left( \mathbf{X}\right)  \in  \operatorname{atoms}\left( Q\right) }}R\left( \mathbf{X}\right)  \tag{1}
$$

We assume the query to be fixed, hence its size is a constant, and we measure the runtime in terms of the total size of the input relations,denoted by $N$ ,i.e.,we use data complexity. For brevity, throughout the paper, we refer to a Boolean conjunctive query as just "query".

假设查询固定，其大小为常数，我们以输入关系总大小$N$衡量运行时（即采用数据复杂度）。为简洁起见，本文中"查询"特指布尔连接查询。

Using only combinatorial algorithms,the best known complexity for any query $Q$ is given by the submodular width $\left\lbrack  {3,4,{17}}\right\rbrack$ . However,when fast matrix multiplication is allowed,some isolated queries admit faster algorithms, but there is no general framework to derive such algorithms for any query. In this paper, we introduce such a framework that naturally unifies both combinatorial and non-combinatorial techniques under the umbrella of information theory. In particular, we generalize the submodular width to incorporate matrix multiplication and develop a matching algorithm. We show that our general algorithm matches or improves upon the best known custom algorithms for queries that have been studied in the literature.

仅使用组合算法时，任何查询$Q$的最佳已知复杂度由子模宽度$\left\lbrack  {3,4,{17}}\right\rbrack$给出。但允许快速矩阵乘法时，部分孤立查询可获得更快算法，却缺乏为任意查询推导此类算法的通用框架。本文提出的框架在信息论下自然统一组合与非组合技术，将子模宽度推广以融合矩阵乘法，并开发了匹配算法。该通用算法匹配或改进了文献中已研究查询的最佳定制算法。

### 1.1 Background

### 1.1 背景

1.1.1 Combinatorial Join Algorithms. We start with some background on combinatorial algorithms for queries. When restricted to the class of combinatorial algorithms, there are only three basic techniques that are sufficient to recover the best-known complexity over this class of algorithms for any query $Q$ :

1.1.1 组合连接算法。首先介绍查询的组合算法背景。限于组合算法类时，仅需三种基本技术即可为任何查询$Q$恢复该类算法的最佳已知复杂度：

- For-loops: Worst-case optimal join (WCOJ) algorithms [18, 19], like GenericJoin [20] or LeapFrog-TrieJoin [22], can be viewed as a sequence of nested for-loops, each of which iterates over possible assignments of one variable. For example, consider the Boolean triangle query:

- 循环结构：最坏情况最优连接(WCOJ)算法[18,19]，如GenericJoin[20]或LeapFrog-TrieJoin[22]，可视为嵌套循环序列，每个循环迭代一个变量的可能赋值。例如布尔三角形查询：

$$
{Q}_{ \bigtriangleup  }\left( \right)  :  - R\left( {X,Y}\right)  \land  S\left( {Y,Z}\right)  \land  T\left( {X,Z}\right)  \tag{2}
$$

One possible WCOJ algorithm consists of a for-loop over the intersection of $X$ -values from $R$ and $T$ ,and for each such assignment,a for-loop over the intersection of $Y$ -values from $R$ and $S$ ,and for each such assignment,a for-loop over the intersection of $Z$ -values from $S$ and $T$ . This simple algorithm gives a runtime of $O\left( {N}^{3/2}\right)$ for this query and $O\left( {N}^{{\rho }^{ * }\left( Q\right) }\right)$ in general,where ${\rho }^{ * }\left( Q\right)$ is the fractional edge cover number of $Q$ ,which is also an upper bound on the join size $\left\lbrack  {7,8,{15}}\right\rbrack$ .

一种可能的WCOJ算法由以下结构组成：首先对$X$值在$R$和$T$的交集进行for循环遍历，对每个赋值结果，再对$Y$值在$R$和$S$的交集进行for循环遍历，最后对$Z$值在$S$和$T$的交集进行for循环遍历。该简单算法针对该查询的时间复杂度为$O\left( {N}^{3/2}\right)$，通用情况下为$O\left( {N}^{{\rho }^{ * }\left( Q\right) }\right)$，其中${\rho }^{ * }\left( Q\right)$是$Q$的分数边覆盖数，同时也是连接结果大小$\left\lbrack  {7,8,{15}}\right\rbrack$的上界。

- Tree Decompositions (TDs): Sometimes, two nested loops can be (conditionally) independent of one other. For example, consider the query:

- 树分解(TDs)：有时两个嵌套循环可以（有条件地）相互独立。例如考虑以下查询：

$$
{Q}_{\Delta \Delta }\left( \right)  \mathrel{\text{:-}} R\left( {X,Y}\right)  \land  S\left( {Y,Z}\right)  \land  T\left( {X,Z}\right)  \land  {S}^{\prime }\left( {Y,{Z}^{\prime }}\right)  \land  {T}^{\prime }\left( {X,{Z}^{\prime }}\right)  \tag{3}
$$

We could solve it in time $O\left( {N}^{2}\right)$ using 4 nested for-loops over $X,Y,Z$ ,and ${Z}^{\prime }$ in order. However, note that once we fix the values of $X$ and $Y$ ,the two inner loops over $Z$ and ${Z}^{\prime }$ become independent, hence can be unnested. One way to capture and utilize such conditional independence is using the framework of tree decompositions, which are a form of query plans. In this example, we could break down the query using a tree decomposition, or TD for short, consisting of two "bags" (i.e. two subqueries in the query plan) where one bag corresponds to a triangle query over $\{ X,Y,Z\}$ while the other bag is a triangle query over $\left\{  {X,Y,{Z}^{\prime }}\right\}$ ,thus leading to a runtime of $O\left( {N}^{3/2}\right)$ . Using TDs (alongside for-loops),we can answer any query $Q$ in time $O\left( {N}^{\operatorname{fhtw}\left( Q\right) }\right)$ where fhtw(Q)is the fractional hypertree width of $Q\left\lbrack  {2,{15},{16}}\right\rbrack$ .

我们可以通过4个嵌套for循环依次遍历$X,Y,Z$和${Z}^{\prime }$，以$O\left( {N}^{2}\right)$时间复杂度求解。但注意到当固定$X$和$Y$的值后，对$Z$和${Z}^{\prime }$的两个内层循环将相互独立，因此可以解嵌套。捕获并利用这种条件独立性的方法之一是采用树分解框架，这是查询计划的一种形式。本例中，我们可以使用由两个"包"（即查询计划中的两个子查询）组成的树分解（简称TD）来分解查询：其中一个包对应$\{ X,Y,Z\}$上的三角形查询，另一个包对应$\left\{  {X,Y,{Z}^{\prime }}\right\}$上的三角形查询，从而将时间复杂度降至$O\left( {N}^{3/2}\right)$。借助树分解（配合for循环），我们可以在$O\left( {N}^{\operatorname{fhtw}\left( Q\right) }\right)$时间内解答任何查询$Q$，其中fhtw(Q)表示$Q\left\lbrack  {2,{15},{16}}\right\rbrack$的分数超树宽度。

- Data Partitioning: For-loops and TDs alone are not sufficient to unleash the full power of combinatorial join algorithms for all queries. Consider the following 4-cycle query:

- 数据分区：仅靠for循环和树分解无法为所有查询释放组合连接算法的全部潜力。考虑以下4环查询：

$$
{Q}_{\square }\left( \right)  :  - R\left( {X,Y}\right)  \land  S\left( {Y,Z}\right)  \land  T\left( {Z,W}\right)  \land  U\left( {W,X}\right)  \tag{4}
$$

${Q}_{\square }$ admits two TDs: one with two bags $\{ X,Y,Z\}$ and $\{ Z,W,X\}$ ,while the other with two bags $\{ Y,Z,W\}$ and $\{ W,X,Y\}$ . However,using either TD alone,we cannot achieve a runtime better than $O\left( {N}^{2}\right)$ . On the other hand,if we partition the input relations carefully into multiple parts, and select a proper TD for each part,we could achieve a runtime of $O\left( {N}^{3/2}\right)$ [6]. Partitioning is done based on the "degrees" of relations where we think of a (binary) relation like $R\left( {X,Y}\right)$ as a bipartite graph, and compute degrees of vertices accordingly. Taking the partitioning approach to the extreme ${}^{1}$ ,the PANDA algorithm $\left\lbrack  {3,4}\right\rbrack$ can solve any query $Q$ in time $\widetilde{O}\left( {N}^{\operatorname{subw}\left( Q\right) }\right)$ where $\operatorname{subw}\left( Q\right)$ is the submodular width of $Q\left\lbrack  {17}\right\rbrack  {.}^{2}$

${Q}_{\square }$允许两种树分解：一种包含$\{ X,Y,Z\}$和$\{ Z,W,X\}$两个包，另一种包含$\{ Y,Z,W\}$和$\{ W,X,Y\}$两个包。但单独使用任一种树分解，都无法获得优于$O\left( {N}^{2}\right)$的时间复杂度。反之，若将输入关系精心划分为多个分区，并为每个分区选择合适的树分解，则可实现$O\left( {N}^{3/2}\right)$的时间复杂度[6]。分区基于关系的"度数"进行——我们将$R\left( {X,Y}\right)$这样的二元关系视为二分图，并相应计算顶点度数。将分区方法发挥到极致${}^{1}$时，PANDA算法$\left\lbrack  {3,4}\right\rbrack$可以在$\widetilde{O}\left( {N}^{\operatorname{subw}\left( Q\right) }\right)$时间内求解任何查询$Q$，其中$\operatorname{subw}\left( Q\right)$表示$Q\left\lbrack  {17}\right\rbrack  {.}^{2}$的子模宽度。

The submodular width is a single definition that combines the above three techniques, and the corresponding PANDA algorithm achieves (up to a polylogarithmic factor) the best-known complexity for any query $Q$ over combinatorial algorithms. The submodular width and PANDA have a deep connection to information theory. At a very high level, the submodular width of a given query $Q$ can be thought of as aiming to capture the best complexity of answering $Q$ using the following meta-algorithm: Think of (binary) input relations,like $R\left( {X,Y}\right)$ above,as bipartite graphs, and partition each of them into (a polylogarithmic number of) parts that are almost "uniform", i.e. where all vertices within the same part have roughly the same degree. Now each part of the data can be described by its combination of degrees, called "degree configuration". For each degree configuration, we pick the best TD, go over its bags, and solve the corresponding subqueries, using for-loops. ${}^{3}$ Instead of reasoning about degree configurations directly,we model them as edge-dominated polymatroids, or ED-polymatroids for short, which is an information-theoretic concept. In particular, given a degree configuration, the corresponding polymatroid is roughly the entropy of a certain probability distribution over the join of input relations having the given degree configuration. (Formal Definition will be given in Sec. 3.) Using polymatroids as a proxy to degree configurations allows us to transform a database problem into an information-theoretic problem. To sum up, the submodular width has the following skeleton (the formal definition will be given later):

次模宽度是一个统合上述三种技术的单一概念，其对应的PANDA算法在组合算法范畴内实现了（至多相差多对数因子）已知最优的查询$Q$复杂度。次模宽度与PANDA算法同信息论存在深刻关联。简言之，给定查询$Q$的次模宽度可视为通过以下元算法求解$Q$的最佳复杂度：将（二元）输入关系（如前文$R\left( {X,Y}\right)$）视作二分图，将其划分为若干"均匀"部分（多对数数量级），即同一部分内所有顶点度数大致相同。每个数据块可由其度数组合（称为"度数配置"）描述。针对每种度数配置，我们选取最优树分解（TD），遍历其各包并通过循环求解相应子查询。${}^{3}$我们采用信息论概念——边支配多拟阵（简称ED多拟阵）来建模度数配置。具体而言，给定度数配置对应的多拟阵，本质上就是具有该度数配置的输入关系连接上某个概率分布的熵。（形式化定义见第3节）以多拟阵作为度数配置的代理，可将数据库问题转化为信息论问题。综上所述，次模宽度的核心框架如下（形式化定义后述）：

1.1.2 Beyond Combinatorial Join Algorithms. For certain queries, there are known non-combinatorial algorithms with lower complexity than the best known combinatorial algorithms. In addition to the three techniques mentioned above, these non-combinatorial algorithms typically involve a fourth technique, which is matrix multiplication, or MM for short. For background, given two $n \times  n$ matrices,there are algebraic algorithms that can multiply them in time $o\left( {n}^{3}\right)$ . The matrix multiplication exponent $\omega$ is the smallest exponent $\alpha$ where this multiplication can be done in time $O\left( {n}^{\alpha  + o\left( 1\right) }\right)$ . It was first discovered by Strassen [21] that $\omega  < 3$ ,and to date,the best known upper bound for $\omega$ is 2.371552 [23]. For certain queries,incorporating MM can lead to faster algorithms by first partitioning the data based on degrees, and then for each part, we choose to either perform an MM or use a traditional combinatorial algorithm (consisting of a TD and for-loops). Which choice is better depends on the degree configuration of the part. For parts with low degrees, combinatorial algorithms are typically better, while parts with high degrees tend to benefit from MM. The complexities of such algorithms typically involve $\omega$ . For example,for the triangle query ${Q}_{\Delta }$ ,there is a non-combinatorial algorithm with complexity $O\left( {N}^{\frac{2\omega }{\omega  + 1}}\right) \left\lbrack  6\right\rbrack   \cdot  {}^{4}$ However,such non-combinatorial algorithms are only known for some isolated queries; see Table 1 for a summary of known results. There is no general framework for answering any query $Q$ using MM.

1.1.2 超越组合连接算法。对于特定查询，存在已知非组合算法的复杂度低于最优组合算法。除前述三种技术外，这些非组合算法通常涉及第四种技术——矩阵乘法（简称MM）。背景知识：给定两个$n \times  n$矩阵，代数算法可在$o\left( {n}^{3}\right)$时间内完成乘法运算。矩阵乘法指数$\omega$是指能在$O\left( {n}^{\alpha  + o\left( 1\right) }\right)$时间内完成乘法的最小指数$\alpha$。Strassen[21]首次发现$\omega  < 3$，目前$\omega$的最佳上界为2.371552[23]。对于特定查询，通过先基于度数划分数据，再针对各部分选择执行MM或传统组合算法（含TD和循环），可得到更快的算法。选择取决于该部分的度数配置——低度数部分通常适合组合算法，而高度数部分则倾向采用MM。此类算法的复杂度通常涉及$\omega$。例如三角形查询${Q}_{\Delta }$存在复杂度为$O\left( {N}^{\frac{2\omega }{\omega  + 1}}\right) \left\lbrack  6\right\rbrack   \cdot  {}^{4}$的非组合算法。但此类算法目前仅适用于特定孤立查询（见表1汇总），尚缺乏利用MM处理任意查询$Q$的通用框架。

### 1.2 Our Contributions

### 1.2 我们的贡献

In this paper, we make the following contributions: (Recall that a "query" refers to a Boolean conjunctive query.)

本文贡献如下（注："查询"均指布尔连接查询）：

- We introduce a generalization of the submodular width of a query $Q$ ,called the $\omega$ -submodular width of $Q$ ,and denoted by $\omega$ -subw(Q),that naturally incorporates the power of matrix multiplication. The $\omega$ -submodular width is always upper bounded by the submodular width,and becomes identical when $\omega  = 3$ .

- 我们提出查询$Q$次模宽度的广义形式——$\omega$-次模宽度（记作$\omega$-subw(Q)），其自然整合了矩阵乘法的能力。$\omega$-次模宽度始终不大于次模宽度，当$\omega  = 3$时两者等同。

Table 1. A summary of prior results and the corresponding results obtained by our framework. $\bar{\omega }\left( {a,b,c}\right)$ is the smallest exponent for multiplying two rectangular matrices of sizes ${n}^{a} \cdot  {n}^{b}$ and ${n}^{b} \cdot  {n}^{c}$ within $O\left( {n}^{\bar{\omega }\left( {a,b,c}\right) }\right)$ time. In contrast, ${\omega }^{\square }\left( {a,b,c}\right)$ is the smallest upper bound on $\bar{\omega }\left( {a,b,c}\right)$ that is obtained through square matrix multiplication. In particular, ${\omega }^{\square }\left( {a,b,c}\right) \overset{\text{ def }}{ = }\max \{ a + b + \left( {\omega  - 2}\right) c,a + \left( {\omega  - 2}\right) b + c,\left( {\omega  - 2}\right) a + b + c\}$ ; see Sec. 3. Obviously, $\bar{\omega }\left( {a,b,c}\right)  \leq  {\omega }^{\square }\left( {a,b,c}\right)$ . Moreover,this becomes an equality when $\omega  = 2$ or when $a =$ $b = c$ ; see Sec. 3. The symbol ${\bar{c}}_{k}$ is the best-known exponent for detecting cycles using rectangular matrix multiplication [10,Theorem 1.3],while ${c}_{k}^{\square }$ is the smallest upper bound on ${\bar{c}}_{k}$ that is obtained through square matrix multiplication; see Eq. (43) and (45). By definition, ${\bar{c}}_{k} \leq  {c}_{k}^{\square }$ ,and this becomes an equality when $\omega  = 2$ . Moreover,this is an equality when $k$ is odd as well as $k = 4$ or 6 ; see [10].

表1. 现有研究成果与本框架对应结果的对比摘要。$\bar{\omega }\left( {a,b,c}\right)$表示在$O\left( {n}^{\bar{\omega }\left( {a,b,c}\right) }\right)$时间内对尺寸为${n}^{a} \cdot  {n}^{b}$和${n}^{b} \cdot  {n}^{c}$的矩形矩阵进行乘法运算的最小指数。相比之下，${\omega }^{\square }\left( {a,b,c}\right)$是通过方阵乘法得到的$\bar{\omega }\left( {a,b,c}\right)$最小上界。特别地，${\omega }^{\square }\left( {a,b,c}\right) \overset{\text{ def }}{ = }\max \{ a + b + \left( {\omega  - 2}\right) c,a + \left( {\omega  - 2}\right) b + c,\left( {\omega  - 2}\right) a + b + c\}$；参见第3节。显然，$\bar{\omega }\left( {a,b,c}\right)  \leq  {\omega }^{\square }\left( {a,b,c}\right)$。此外，当$\omega  = 2$或$a =$$b = c$时该式取等；参见第3节。符号${\bar{c}}_{k}$表示利用矩形矩阵乘法检测循环的最优已知指数[10,定理1.3]，而${c}_{k}^{\square }$是通过方阵乘法得到的${\bar{c}}_{k}$最小上界；参见式(43)和(45)。根据定义，${\bar{c}}_{k} \leq  {c}_{k}^{\square }$，当$\omega  = 2$时该式取等。此外，当$k$为奇数且$k = 4$或6时亦成立；参见[10]。

<!-- Media -->

- We introduce a general framework to compute any query $Q$ in time $\widetilde{O}\left( {N}^{\omega \text{-subw}\left( Q\right) }\right)$ for any ratio- ${\text{nal}}^{5}\omega$ ,where $\widetilde{O}$ hides a polylogarithmic factor in $N$ . Our framework unifies known combinatorial and non-combinatorial techniques under the umbrella of information theory.

- 我们提出一个通用框架，可在$\widetilde{O}\left( {N}^{\omega \text{-subw}\left( Q\right) }\right)$时间内计算任意查询$Q$（其中${\text{nal}}^{5}\omega$为任意比率，$\widetilde{O}$表示$N$中的多对数因子）。该框架在信息论体系下统一了已知的组合与非组合技术。

- We show that for any query $Q$ ,our framework recovers the best known complexity for $Q$ over both combinatorial and non-combinatorial algorithms. See Table 1.

- 我们证明对于任意查询$Q$，本框架在组合算法与非组合算法中均能复现当前最优复杂度。详见表1。

- We show that there are classes of queries where our framework discovers new algorithms with strictly lower complexity than the best-known ones. See Table 1.

- 我们展示在某些查询类别中，本框架能发现严格优于现有最优算法的新算法。详见表1。

### 1.3 Paper Outline

### 1.3 论文结构

In Section 2, we present a high-level overview of our framework and illustrate it using the triangle query ${Q}_{\bigtriangleup }$ . In Section 3,we give formal definitions for background concepts. We formally define the $\omega$ -submodular width in Section 4,and show how to compute it for several classes of queries in Section 5. In Sections 6 and 7,we give an algorithm for computing the $\omega$ -submodular for any query $Q$ and an algorithm for computing the answer to any query $Q$ in $\omega$ -subdmodular width time,while deferring some technical details to the appendix. We conclude in Section 8.

第2节概述框架原理并以三角查询${Q}_{\bigtriangleup }$为例说明；第3节给出背景概念的形式化定义；第4节正式定义$\omega$-子模宽度；第5节展示多类查询的计算方法；第6-7节分别给出计算任意查询$Q$的$\omega$-子模宽度算法及在$\omega$-子模宽度时间内求解查询$Q$的算法（技术细节见附录）；第8节总结全文。

## 2 OVERVIEW

## 2 框架概览

We give here a simplified overview of our framework. We start with how to generalize the subd-modular width to incorporate MM, and to that end, we need to answer two basic questions: - Q1: How can we express the complexity of MM in terms of polymatroids?

本节简析框架原理。首先探讨如何推广子模宽度以整合矩阵乘法(MM)，这需要解决两个基本问题：- Q1：如何用多拟阵表述MM复杂度？

---

<!-- Footnote -->

${}^{5}$ When $\omega$ is not rational,we can take any rational upper bound.

${}^{5}$当$\omega$为无理数时，可取任意有理上界。

<!-- Footnote -->

---

- Q2: How can we develop a notion of query plans that naturally reconciles TDs with MM?

- Q2：如何构建能自然协调树分解(TDs)与MM的查询计划概念？

### 2.1 Q1: Translating MM complexity into polymatroids

### 2.1 Q1：将MM复杂度转化为多拟阵

Given a query $Q$ of the form (1),a polymatroid is a function $\mathbf{h} : {2}^{\operatorname{vars}\left( Q\right) } \rightarrow  {\mathbb{R}}_{ + }$ that satisfies Shannon inequalities. By that,we mean $\mathbf{h}$ is monotone (i.e. $h\left( X\right)  \leq  h\left( Y\right)$ for all $X \subseteq  Y \subseteq  \operatorname{vars}\left( Q\right)$ ), submodular (i.e. $h\left( X\right)  + h\left( Y\right)  \geq  h\left( {X \cup  Y}\right)  + h\left( {X \cap  Y}\right)$ for all $X,Y \subseteq  \operatorname{vars}\left( Q\right)$ ),and satisfies $h\left( \varnothing \right)  = 0$ . A polymatroid is edge-dominated if $h\left( X\right)  \leq  1$ for all $R\left( X\right)  \in  \operatorname{atoms}\left( Q\right)$ . The subdmodular width, given by Eq. (5), uses edge-dominated polymatroids as a proxy for the degree configurations of different part of the data.

给定形式(1)的查询$Q$时，多拟阵（polymatroid）是满足香农不等式（Shannon inequalities）的函数$\mathbf{h} : {2}^{\operatorname{vars}\left( Q\right) } \rightarrow  {\mathbb{R}}_{ + }$。具体而言，$\mathbf{h}$具有单调性（即对所有$X \subseteq  Y \subseteq  \operatorname{vars}\left( Q\right)$满足$h\left( X\right)  \leq  h\left( Y\right)$）、子模性（即对所有$X,Y \subseteq  \operatorname{vars}\left( Q\right)$满足$h\left( X\right)  + h\left( Y\right)  \geq  h\left( {X \cup  Y}\right)  + h\left( {X \cap  Y}\right)$）且满足$h\left( \varnothing \right)  = 0$。若对所有$R\left( X\right)  \in  \operatorname{atoms}\left( Q\right)$有$h\left( X\right)  \leq  1$，则称该多拟阵为边支配的。公式(5)给出的子模宽度（submodular width）使用边支配多拟阵作为数据不同部分的度配置（degree configurations）的代理。

Throughout the paper,we assume that $\omega$ is a fixed constant within the range $\left\lbrack  {2,3}\right\rbrack$ . Given two rectangular matrices of dimensions ${n}^{a} \times  {n}^{b}$ and ${n}^{b} \times  {n}^{c}$ ,we can multiply them by partitioning them into square blocks of dimensions ${n}^{d} \times  {n}^{d}$ where $d\overset{\text{ def }}{ = }\min \left( {a,b,c}\right)$ and then multiplying each pair of blocks using square matrix multiplication in time ${n}^{d \cdot  \omega }$ . This leads to an overall runtime of ${n}^{{\omega }^{\bar{\omega }}\left( {a,b,c}\right) }$ where ${\omega }^{\square }\left( {a,b,c}\right)$ is defined below and $\gamma \overset{\text{ def }}{ = }\omega  - 2$ : (Proof is in Sec. 3.)

本文始终假设$\omega$是$\left\lbrack  {2,3}\right\rbrack$范围内的固定常数。给定维度分别为${n}^{a} \times  {n}^{b}$和${n}^{b} \times  {n}^{c}$的两个矩形矩阵，可通过将其划分为${n}^{d} \times  {n}^{d}$的方形块（其中$d\overset{\text{ def }}{ = }\min \left( {a,b,c}\right)$）进行乘法运算，每对块采用${n}^{d \cdot  \omega }$时间的方阵乘法，最终得到${n}^{{\omega }^{\bar{\omega }}\left( {a,b,c}\right) }$的总运行时间（其中${\omega }^{\square }\left( {a,b,c}\right)$定义如下且$\gamma \overset{\text{ def }}{ = }\omega  - 2$）。（证明见第3节）

$$
{\omega }^{\square }\left( {a,b,c}\right) \overset{\text{ def }}{ = }\max \{ a + b + \gamma  \cdot  c,\;a + \gamma  \cdot  b + c,\;\gamma  \cdot  a + b + c\}  \tag{6}
$$

Now suppose we have two relations $R\left( {X,Y}\right)$ and $S\left( {Y,Z}\right)$ ,and we want to compute $P\left( {X,Z}\right)$ :- $R\left( {X,Y}\right)  \land  S\left( {Y,Z}\right)$ ,by viewing $R$ and $S$ as two matrices of dimensions ${n}^{a} \times  {n}^{b}$ and ${n}^{b} \times  {n}^{c}$ respectively and multiplying them. In the polymatroid world,we can think of $h\left( X\right) ,h\left( Y\right)$ ,and $h\left( Z\right)$ as representing $a,b$ ,and $c$ ,respectively. Motivated by this,we define the following new information measure:

现假设有两个关系$R\left( {X,Y}\right)$和$S\left( {Y,Z}\right)$，需计算$P\left( {X,Z}\right)$ :- $R\left( {X,Y}\right)  \land  S\left( {Y,Z}\right)$，方法是将$R$和$S$视作维度分别为${n}^{a} \times  {n}^{b}$和${n}^{b} \times  {n}^{c}$的矩阵进行乘法。在多拟阵范畴中，可将$h\left( X\right) ,h\left( Y\right)$和$h\left( Z\right)$分别理解为表示$a,b$和$c$的载体。基于此，我们定义以下新的信息度量：

$$
\operatorname{MM}\left( {X;Y;Z}\right) \overset{\text{ def }}{ = }\max \left( {h\left( X\right)  + h\left( Y\right)  + {\gamma h}\left( Z\right) ,h\left( X\right)  + {\gamma h}\left( Y\right)  + h\left( Z\right) ,{\gamma h}\left( X\right)  + h\left( Y\right)  + h\left( Z\right) }\right)  \tag{7}
$$

And now,we can use $\operatorname{MM}\left( {X;Y;Z}\right)$ to capture the complexity of the above $\mathrm{{MM}}$ ,on log-scale. We also extend the MM notation to allow treating multiple variables as a single dimension. For example, given the query ${Q}_{\bigtriangleup \bigtriangleup }$ from Eq. (3),we use $\operatorname{MM}\left( {X;Y;Z{Z}^{\prime }}\right)$ to refer to the cost of $\mathrm{{MM}}$ where we treat $Z$ and ${Z}^{\prime }$ as a single dimension. In particular,we view $R\left( {X,Y}\right)$ as one matrix and $S\left( {Y,Z}\right)  \land  {S}^{\prime }\left( {Y,{Z}^{\prime }}\right)$ as another matrix,and multiply them to get $P\left( {X,Z,{Z}^{\prime }}\right)$ .

现在，我们可以利用$\operatorname{MM}\left( {X;Y;Z}\right)$在对数尺度上捕捉上述$\mathrm{{MM}}$的复杂性。我们还扩展了MM表示法，允许将多个变量视为单一维度。例如，给定公式(3)中的查询${Q}_{\bigtriangleup \bigtriangleup }$，我们使用$\operatorname{MM}\left( {X;Y;Z{Z}^{\prime }}\right)$来表示将$Z$和${Z}^{\prime }$视为单一维度时$\mathrm{{MM}}$的成本。具体来说，我们将$R\left( {X,Y}\right)$视为一个矩阵，$S\left( {Y,Z}\right)  \land  {S}^{\prime }\left( {Y,{Z}^{\prime }}\right)$视为另一个矩阵，通过相乘得到$P\left( {X,Z,{Z}^{\prime }}\right)$。

### 2.2 Q2: Query plans for MM

### 2.2 Q2：面向MM的查询计划

Variable Elimination $\left\lbrack  {{11},{12},{25},{26}}\right\rbrack$ is a language for expressing query plans that is known to be equivalent to TDs [2] for combinatorial join algorithms. We show that variable elimination can be naturally extended to incorporate MM in its query plans. For background,given a query $Q$ , variable elimination refers to the process of picking an order $\sigma$ of the variables $\operatorname{vars}\left( Q\right)$ ,known as variable elimination order, or VEO for short, and then going through the variables in order and "eliminating" them one-by-one. Eliminating a variable $X$ from a query $Q$ means transforming $Q$ into an equivalent query ${Q}^{\prime }$ that doesn’t contain $X$ ,and this is done by removing all relations that contain $X$ and creating a new relation with all variables that co-occurred with $X$ . (Formal Definition will be given in Sec. 3.) For example,given the 4-cycle query ${Q}_{\square }$ from Eq. (4),we can eliminate $Y$ by computing a new relation $P\left( {X,Z}\right)  :  - R\left( {X,Y}\right)  \land  S\left( {Y,Z}\right)$ and now the remaining query becomes a triangle query:

变量消解$\left\lbrack  {{11},{12},{25},{26}}\right\rbrack$是一种用于表达查询计划的语言，已知其对于组合连接算法等价于TDs[2]。我们证明变量消解可以自然扩展，将MM纳入其查询计划中。作为背景知识，给定查询$Q$，变量消解指的是选择变量$\operatorname{vars}\left( Q\right)$的排序$\sigma$（称为变量消解顺序，简称VEO），然后按顺序逐个"消解"这些变量。从查询$Q$中消解变量$X$意味着将$Q$转换为不包含$X$的等价查询${Q}^{\prime }$，这通过移除所有包含$X$的关系，并创建一个包含所有与$X$共现变量的新关系来实现。（正式定义将在第3节给出。）例如，给定公式(4)中的4环查询${Q}_{\square }$，我们可以通过计算新关系$P\left( {X,Z}\right)  :  - R\left( {X,Y}\right)  \land  S\left( {Y,Z}\right)$来消解$Y$，此时剩余查询变为三角形查询：

$$
{Q}_{\square }^{\prime }\left( \right)  :  - P\left( {X,Z}\right)  \land  T\left( {Z,W}\right)  \land  U\left( {W,X}\right) 
$$

We use ${U}_{Y}^{\sigma }$ to refer to the set of variables involved in the subquery that eliminates $Y$ ,which is $\{ X,Y,Z\}$ in this example. Every VEO is equivalent to a TD,and vice versa [2]. In this example, any VEO that eliminates either $Y$ or $W$ first is equivalent to a TD with two bags $\{ X,Y,Z\}$ and $\{ Z,W,X\}$ ,whereas all remaining VEOs are equivalent to the other TD described before.

我们使用${U}_{Y}^{\sigma }$指代参与消解$Y$的子查询所涉及的变量集合，本例中即为$\{ X,Y,Z\}$。每个VEO都等价于一个TD，反之亦然[2]。本例中，任何优先消解$Y$或$W$的VEO都等价于具有两个包$\{ X,Y,Z\}$和$\{ Z,W,X\}$的TD，而其余VEO则等价于之前描述的另一TD。

In the presence of MM,VEOs become more expressive. For example,the triangle query ${Q}_{\Delta }$ has only one trivial TD with a single bag $\{ X,Y,Z\}$ . Now,suppose we have a VEO that eliminates $Y$ first. There are two different ways to eliminate $Y$ :

在MM存在的情况下，VEOs的表达能力更强。例如，三角形查询${Q}_{\Delta }$仅有一个包含单包$\{ X,Y,Z\}$的简单TD。现在假设我们有一个优先消解$Y$的VEO，存在两种不同的消解方式：

- Either compute the full join combinatorially using for-loops,and then project $Y$ away. This computation costs $h\left( {XYZ}\right)$ on log-scale.

- 要么使用循环组合计算完整连接，然后投影移除$Y$。该计算在对数尺度上耗费$h\left( {XYZ}\right)$。

- Or view $R\left( {X,Y}\right)$ and $S\left( {Y,Z}\right)$ as matrices and multiply them to get $P\left( {X,Z}\right)$ . This costs $\operatorname{MM}\left( {X;Y;Z}\right)$ .

- 或者将$R\left( {X,Y}\right)$和$S\left( {Y,Z}\right)$视为矩阵相乘得到$P\left( {X,Z}\right)$。此操作耗费$\operatorname{MM}\left( {X;Y;Z}\right)$。

Alternatively,we could have eliminated either $X$ or $Z$ first. In this simple example,there is only a single way to eliminate a variable,say $Y$ ,using MM,but in general,there could be several,and we can choose the best of them. For example,in the query ${Q}_{\bigtriangleup  \bigtriangleup  }$ from Eq. (3), $Y$ occurs in three relations, and we can arrange them into two matrices in different ways. One way is to join $S\left( {Y,Z}\right)$ and ${S}^{\prime }\left( {Y,{Z}^{\prime }}\right)$ into a single matrix ${S}^{\prime \prime }\left( {Y,Z{Z}^{\prime }}\right)$ and multiply it with the matrix $R\left( {X,Y}\right)$ leading to a cost of $\mathrm{{MM}}\left( {X;Y;Z{Z}^{\prime }}\right)$ . Alternatively,we could have obtained costs $\mathrm{{MM}}\left( {{XZ};Y;{Z}^{\prime }}\right)$ or $\mathrm{{MM}}\left( {X{Z}^{\prime };Y;Z}\right)$ , and we will see later that there are even more options! ${}^{6}$ Given a VEO $\sigma$ and a variable $Y$ ,we use ${\mathrm{{EMM}}}_{Y}^{\sigma }$ to denote the minimum cost of eliminating $Y$ using MM. In contrast, $h\left( {U}_{Y}^{\sigma }\right)$ is the cost of eliminating $Y$ using for-loops. For example,in ${Q}_{\Delta },{\mathrm{{EMM}}}_{Y}^{\sigma } = \mathrm{{MM}}\left( {X;Y;Z}\right)$ and $h\left( {U}_{Y}^{\sigma }\right)  = h\left( {XYZ}\right)$ , whereas in ${Q}_{\Delta \Delta },{\mathrm{{EMM}}}_{Y}^{\sigma } = \min \left( {\operatorname{MM}\left( {X;Y;Z{Z}^{\prime }}\right) ,\operatorname{MM}\left( {{XZ};Y;{Z}^{\prime }}\right) ,\operatorname{MM}\left( {X{Z}^{\prime };Y;Z}\right) ,\ldots }\right)$ and $h\left( {U}_{Y}^{\sigma }\right)  =$ $h\left( {{XYZ}{Z}^{\prime }}\right)$ .

或者，我们可以先消去$X$或$Z$。在这个简单示例中，仅存在单一方式通过矩阵乘法(MM)消去变量（例如$Y$），但通常可能存在多种消解路径，我们可以选择最优方案。例如在公式(3)的查询${Q}_{\bigtriangleup  \bigtriangleup  }$中，$Y$出现在三个关系中，我们可以将其排列成两种不同形式的矩阵：一种是将$S\left( {Y,Z}\right)$与${S}^{\prime }\left( {Y,{Z}^{\prime }}\right)$连接为单一矩阵${S}^{\prime \prime }\left( {Y,Z{Z}^{\prime }}\right)$后与矩阵$R\left( {X,Y}\right)$相乘，产生$\mathrm{{MM}}\left( {X;Y;Z{Z}^{\prime }}\right)$的计算代价；另一种方案可能产生$\mathrm{{MM}}\left( {{XZ};Y;{Z}^{\prime }}\right)$或$\mathrm{{MM}}\left( {X{Z}^{\prime };Y;Z}\right)$的代价，后文还将展示更多选择可能！${}^{6}$给定变量消解顺序(VEO)$\sigma$和变量$Y$，我们用${\mathrm{{EMM}}}_{Y}^{\sigma }$表示使用MM消解$Y$的最小代价。相比之下，$h\left( {U}_{Y}^{\sigma }\right)$表示使用循环消解的代价。例如在${Q}_{\Delta },{\mathrm{{EMM}}}_{Y}^{\sigma } = \mathrm{{MM}}\left( {X;Y;Z}\right)$和$h\left( {U}_{Y}^{\sigma }\right)  = h\left( {XYZ}\right)$中表现为...而在${Q}_{\Delta \Delta },{\mathrm{{EMM}}}_{Y}^{\sigma } = \min \left( {\operatorname{MM}\left( {X;Y;Z{Z}^{\prime }}\right) ,\operatorname{MM}\left( {{XZ};Y;{Z}^{\prime }}\right) ,\operatorname{MM}\left( {X{Z}^{\prime };Y;Z}\right) ,\ldots }\right)$和$h\left( {U}_{Y}^{\sigma }\right)  =$中则呈现$h\left( {{XYZ}{Z}^{\prime }}\right)$。

### 2.3 Defining the $\omega$ -submodular width

### 2.3 $\omega$-次模宽度的定义

Putting pieces together,we are now ready to define our notion of $\omega$ -subdmodular width of a query $Q$ . We take the maximum over all ED-polymatroids $\mathbf{h}$ ,and for each polymatroid,we take the minimum over all VEOs $\mathbf{\sigma }$ . For each $\mathbf{\sigma }$ ,we take the maximum elimination cost over all variables $X$ , where the elimination cost of $X$ is the minimum over all possible ways to eliminate $X$ using either for-loops or MM:

综合上述要素，我们现在可以定义查询$Q$的$\omega$-次模宽度概念：首先遍历所有ED-多拟阵$\mathbf{h}$取最大值，针对每个多拟阵再遍历所有变量消解顺序(VEO)$\mathbf{\sigma }$取最小值。对于每个$\mathbf{\sigma }$，计算所有变量$X$的最大消解代价——该消解代价是指通过循环或矩阵乘法消解$X$的所有可能方式中的最小代价：

(8)

<!-- Media -->

<!-- figureText: $\omega$ -subw(Q) max min max min( $h\left( {U}_{X}^{\sigma }\right)$ ${\mathrm{{EMM}}}_{X}^{\sigma }$ variable $X$ cost of eliminating cost of eliminating worst variable $X$ using for-loops $X$ using MM elimination cost ED-polymatroid $\mathbf{h}$ VEO $\sigma$ worst part best query plan of the data for this part -->

<img src="https://cdn.noedgeai.com/01962f4c-bb1e-7c29-a953-970d329368b1_5.jpg?x=145&y=1320&w=1273&h=171&r=0"/>

<!-- Media -->

To compare the above to the submodular width, we include below an alternative definition of the submodular width that is equivalent to Eq. (5). The equivalence follows from the equivalence of VEOs and TDs [2]:

为与次模宽度进行对比，我们在下方给出公式(5)的等价定义。这种等价性源于变量消解顺序(VEO)与树分解(TD)的等价关系[2]：

(9)

<!-- Media -->

<!-- figureText: ED-polymatroid $\mathbf{h}$ VEO $\sigma$ max $h\left( {U}_{X}^{\sigma }\right)$ variable $X$ cost of eliminating worst variable $X$ using for-loops elimination cost worst part best query plan of the data for this part -->

<img src="https://cdn.noedgeai.com/01962f4c-bb1e-7c29-a953-970d329368b1_5.jpg?x=302&y=1660&w=961&h=168&r=0"/>

<!-- Media -->

The only difference between Eq. (8) and Eq. (9) is the inclusion of ${\mathrm{{EMM}}}_{X}^{\sigma }$ in the former. This shows that $\omega$ -subw(Q)is always upper bounded by subw(Q). We show later that they become identical when $\omega  = 3$ .

公式(8)与公式(9)的唯一区别在于前者包含${\mathrm{{EMM}}}_{X}^{\sigma }$。这表明$\omega$-次模宽度始终不大于次模宽度。后文将证明当$\omega  = 3$时两者完全等同。

For example, ${Q}_{ \bigtriangleup  }$ has 6 different VEOs. For a fixed VEO,the cost of eliminating the first variable dominates the other two, thus we ignore the two. We have seen before that the cost of eliminating $Y$ is $\min \left( {h\left( {XYZ}\right) ,\operatorname{MM}\left( {X;Y;Z}\right) }\right)$ ,which also happens to be the cost of eliminating either $X$ or ${Z}^{7}$ Hence,the $\omega$ -subdmodular width becomes:

例如，${Q}_{ \bigtriangleup  }$存在6种不同的虚拟消除顺序(VEO)。对于固定VEO，消除首个变量的成本远高于后两个变量，因此忽略后两者。前文已证明消除$Y$的成本为$\min \left( {h\left( {XYZ}\right) ,\operatorname{MM}\left( {X;Y;Z}\right) }\right)$，该数值恰好也等于消除$X$或${Z}^{7}$的成本。因此，$\omega$-次模宽度可表示为：

$$
\omega  - \operatorname{subw}\left( {Q}_{\bigtriangleup }\right)  = \mathop{\max }\limits_{{\text{ED-polymatroid }h}}\min \left( {h\left( {XYZ}\right) ,\operatorname{MM}\left( {X;Y;Z}\right) }\right)  \tag{10}
$$

---

<!-- Footnote -->

${}^{6}$ In particular,later we will extend the notion of MM to allow for group-by variables,and we will also generalize the concept of VEOs to allow eliminating multiple variables at once.

${}^{6}$特别地，后续我们将扩展MM概念以支持分组变量，同时将VEO概念泛化为支持同时消除多个变量。

### 2.4 Computing the $\omega$ -submodular width

### 2.4 计算$\omega$-次模宽度

Now that we have defined the $\omega$ -submodular width,our next concern is how to compute it for a given query $Q$ . The $\omega$ -submodular width is a deeply nested expression of min and max. (Recall that ${\mathrm{{EMM}}}_{X}^{\sigma }$ is a minimum of potentially many terms of the form $\operatorname{MM}\left( {X;Y;Z}\right)$ ,each of which is a maximum of three terms in Eq. (7).) To compute $\omega$ -subw(Q),we first pull all max operators outside by distributing min over $\mathop{\max }\limits^{8}$ ,and then swap the order of the max operators so that the max over $\mathbf{h}$ is the inner most max. Applying this to Eq. (10),we get:

在定义了$\omega$-次模宽度后，接下来需要解决如何为给定查询$Q$计算该值。该宽度是min与max的深层嵌套表达式（需注意${\mathrm{{EMM}}}_{X}^{\sigma }$是形如$\operatorname{MM}\left( {X;Y;Z}\right)$的多个项的最小值，其中每个项又是公式(7)中三个项的最大值）。为计算$\omega$-次模宽度subw(Q)，我们首先通过min对$\mathop{\max }\limits^{8}$的分配律将所有max运算符外提，然后交换max运算符顺序使$\mathbf{h}$上的max成为最内层。将此应用于公式(10)可得：

$$
\omega \text{-subw}\left( {Q}_{\bigtriangleup }\right)  = \max \left( {\mathop{\max }\limits_{{\text{ED-polymatroid }h}}\min \left( {h\left( {XYZ}\right) ,h\left( X\right)  + h\left( Y\right)  + {\gamma h}\left( Z\right) }\right) ,}\right. 
$$

$$
\mathop{\max }\limits_{{E\text{ D-polymatroid }\mathbf{h}}}\min \left( {h\left( {XYZ}\right) ,h\left( X\right)  + {\gamma h}\left( Y\right)  + h\left( Z\right) }\right) ,
$$

$$
\mathop{\max }\limits_{{\text{ED-polymatroid }\mathbf{h}}}\min \left( {h\left( {XYZ}\right) ,{\gamma h}\left( X\right)  + h\left( Y\right)  + h\left( Z\right) )}\right)  \tag{11}
$$

Assume that $\gamma \overset{\text{ def }}{ = }\omega  - 2$ is fixed,and consider the first term inside the outermost max above. We can turn this term into a linear program (LP) by introducing a new variable $t$ and replacing the min operator with a max of $t$ subject to some upper bounds on $t$ :

假设$\gamma \overset{\text{ def }}{ = }\omega  - 2$固定，考察最外层max内的首项。通过引入新变量$t$并将min运算符替换为受$t$上界约束的max，可将此项转化为线性规划(LP)：

$$
\mathop{\max }\limits_{\substack{{t \in  \mathbb{R}} \\  {\text{ ED-polymatroid }h} }}\{ t\; \mid  \;t \leq  h\left( {XYZ}\right) ,\;t \leq  h\left( X\right)  + h\left( Y\right)  + {\gamma h}\left( Z\right) \}  \tag{12}
$$

Let opt denote the optimal objective value of the above LP. We will show that opt $= \frac{2\omega }{\omega  + 1}$ . Since the other two LPs are similar,it follows that $\omega$ -subw $\left( {Q}_{\bigtriangleup }\right)  = \frac{2\omega }{\omega  + 1}$ .

设opt表示上述LP的最优目标值。我们将证明opt$= \frac{2\omega }{\omega  + 1}$。由于另两个LP情况类似，可推得$\omega$-次模宽度subw$\left( {Q}_{\bigtriangleup }\right)  = \frac{2\omega }{\omega  + 1}$。

First,we show that opt $\geq  \frac{2\omega }{\omega  + 1}$ . To that end,consider the following polymatroid (where $h\left( \varnothing \right)  = 0$ ):

首先证明opt$\geq  \frac{2\omega }{\omega  + 1}$。为此考察以下多拟阵（其中$h\left( \varnothing \right)  = 0$）：

$$
h\left( X\right)  = h\left( Y\right)  = h\left( Z\right)  = \frac{2}{\omega  + 1},\;h\left( {XY}\right)  = h\left( {YZ}\right)  = h\left( {XZ}\right)  = 1,\;h\left( {XYZ}\right)  = \frac{2\omega }{\omega  + 1}.
$$

It can be verified that this is a valid polymatroid (for any $\omega  \in  \left\lbrack  {2,3}\right\rbrack$ ),it is edge-dominated,and forms a feasible (primal) solution to the LP (alongside $\left. {t = \frac{2\omega }{\omega  + 1}}\right)$ ,thus proving that opt $\geq  \frac{2\omega }{\omega  + 1}$ . In the next section,we will show a feasible dual solution that proves opt $\leq  \frac{2\omega }{\omega  + 1}$ .

经验证这是有效的多拟阵（对任意$\omega  \in  \left\lbrack  {2,3}\right\rbrack$），其具有边支配性，并构成LP的可行（原始）解（连同$\left. {t = \frac{2\omega }{\omega  + 1}}\right)$），由此证明opt$\geq  \frac{2\omega }{\omega  + 1}$。下节将给出证明opt$\leq  \frac{2\omega }{\omega  + 1}$的可行对偶解。

### 2.5 Computing query answers in $\omega$ -subdmodular width time

### 2.5 在$\omega$-次模宽度时间内计算查询答案

We now give a simplified overview of our algorithm for computing the answer to a query $Q$ in time $\widetilde{O}\left( {N}^{\omega  - \operatorname{subw}\left( Q\right) }\right)$ for any rational $\omega {.}^{9}$ We will use the triangle query ${Q}_{ \bigtriangleup  }$ as an example. It should be noted however that this simple example is not sufficient to reveal the major technical challenges of designing the general algorithm. Many of these challenges are unique to matrix multiplication, and are not encountered in the original PANDA algorithm [3, 4].

现简要说明在$\widetilde{O}\left( {N}^{\omega  - \operatorname{subw}\left( Q\right) }\right)$时间内计算查询$Q$答案的算法，该算法适用于任何有理数$\omega {.}^{9}$。以三角查询${Q}_{ \bigtriangleup  }$为例，但需注意此简单案例不足以揭示通用算法设计的主要技术挑战。这些挑战多与矩阵乘法相关，在原始PANDA算法[3,4]中未曾出现。

---

<!-- Footnote -->

${}^{7}$ Note that $\operatorname{MM}\left( {X;Y;Z}\right)$ is symmetric.

${}^{7}$注意$\operatorname{MM}\left( {X;Y;Z}\right)$具有对称性。

${}^{8}$ Note that $\min \left( {a,\max \left( {b,c}\right) }\right)  = \max \left( {\min \left( {a,b}\right) ,\min \left( {a,c}\right) }\right)$

${}^{8}$ 注意 $\min \left( {a,\max \left( {b,c}\right) }\right)  = \max \left( {\min \left( {a,b}\right) ,\min \left( {a,c}\right) }\right)$

${}^{9}$ If $\omega$ is not rational,then any rational upper bound works.

${}^{9}$ 若$\omega$不是有理数，则任意有理数上界均可适用。

<!-- Footnote -->

---

A Shannon inequality is an inequality that holds over all polymatroids $\mathbf{h}$ . The following Shannon inequality corresponds to a feasible dual solution to the LP from Eq. (12):

香农不等式(Shannon inequality)是指对所有多拟阵$\mathbf{h}$均成立的不等式。下列香农不等式对应于方程(12)中线性规划的一个可行对偶解：

<!-- Media -->

$$
\omega \underset{\begin{matrix} V \\  \text{ ( for loop cost ) } \end{matrix}}{\underbrace{h\left( {XYZ}\right) }} + \underset{\begin{matrix} V \\  \text{ ( for loop cost ) } \end{matrix}}{\underbrace{h\left( X\right)  + h\left( Y\right)  + {\gamma h}\left( Z\right) }} \leq  2\underset{\begin{matrix} V \\  \text{ ( ( R ( X,Y )) } \end{matrix}}{\underbrace{h\left( {XY}\right) }} + \left( {\omega  - 1}\right) \underset{\begin{matrix} V \\  \text{ ( S ( Y,Z )) } \end{matrix}}{\underbrace{h\left( {YZ}\right) }} + \left( {\omega  - 1}\right) \underset{\begin{matrix} V \\  \text{ ( T ( X,Z )) } \end{matrix}}{\underbrace{h\left( {XZ}\right) }} \tag{13}
$$

<!-- Media -->

In particular, the above is a Shannon inequality because it is a sum of the following submodularities:

特别地，上述不等式属于香农不等式，因为它是下列子模性之和：

$$
h\left( {XYZ}\right)  + h\left( X\right)  \leq  h\left( {XY}\right)  + h\left( {XZ}\right) 
$$

$$
h\left( {XYZ}\right)  + h\left( Y\right)  \leq  h\left( {XY}\right)  + h\left( {YZ}\right) 
$$

$$
{\gamma h}\left( {XYZ}\right)  + {\gamma h}\left( Z\right)  \leq  {\gamma h}\left( {XZ}\right)  + {\gamma h}\left( {YZ}\right) 
$$

Since $\mathbf{h}$ is edge-dominated,each term on the RHS of Eq. (13) is upper bounded by 1,hence the RHS is $\leq  {2\omega }$ . On the other hand,by Eq. (12),the LHS is at least $\left( {\omega  + 1}\right) t$ . Therefore,Inequality (13) implies $t \leq  \frac{2\omega }{\omega  + 1}$ ,hence opt $\leq  \frac{2\omega }{\omega  + 1}$ . Each term on the RHS of Eq. (13) corresponds to one input relation, whereas each group of terms on the LHS corresponds to the cost of solving a subquery in the plan. In particular,the group $h\left( {XYZ}\right)$ corresponds to the cost of solving the query using for-loops,whereas the group $h\left( X\right)  + h\left( Y\right)  + {\gamma h}\left( Z\right)$ corresponds to one of three terms that capture the cost of solving the query using MM.

由于$\mathbf{h}$是边支配的，方程(13)右侧各项的上界均为1，因此右侧为$\leq  {2\omega }$。另一方面，根据方程(12)，左侧至少为$\left( {\omega  + 1}\right) t$。故不等式(13)蕴含$t \leq  \frac{2\omega }{\omega  + 1}$，从而最优解$\leq  \frac{2\omega }{\omega  + 1}$。方程(13)右侧各项对应一个输入关系，而左侧每组项对应计划中解决子查询的成本。具体而言，组$h\left( {XYZ}\right)$对应使用for循环求解查询的成本，而组$h\left( X\right)  + h\left( Y\right)  + {\gamma h}\left( Z\right)$对应使用矩阵乘法(MM)求解查询时三项成本之一。

First, the algorithm constructs a proof sequence of the Shannon inequality (13), which is a step-by-step proof of the inequality that transforms the RHS into the LHS. Figure 1 (left) shows the proof sequence for Eq. (13). Then, the algorithm translates each proof step into a corresponding database operation. In particular, initially each term on the RHS of Eq. (13) corresponds to an input relation. Each time we apply a proof step replacing some terms on the RHS with some other terms, we simultaneously apply a database operation replacing the corresponding relations with some new relations. Figure 1 (right) shows the corresponding database operations, which together make the algorithm for answering ${Q}_{\Delta }$ . In this example,there are only two types of proof steps:

首先，算法构建香农不等式(13)的证明序列，这是将右侧逐步转化为左侧的证明过程。图1(左)展示了方程(13)的证明序列。接着，算法将每个证明步骤转换为对应的数据库操作。具体而言，初始时方程(13)右侧各项对应输入关系。每当应用证明步骤替换右侧某些项时，同步执行数据库操作将对应关系替换为新关系。图1(右)展示了相应数据库操作，这些操作共同构成回答${Q}_{\Delta }$的算法。本例中仅存在两类证明步骤：

- Decomposition Step of the form $h\left( {XY}\right)  \rightarrow  h\left( X\right)  + h\left( {Y \mid  X}\right)$ . Let $R\left( {X,Y}\right)$ be the relation corresponding to $h\left( {XY}\right)$ . The corresponding database operation is to partition $R\left( {X,Y}\right)$ into two parts based on the degree of $X$ ,i.e. the number of matching $Y$ -values for a given $X$ . In particular, $X$ -values with degree $> \Delta \overset{\text{ def }}{ = }{N}^{\frac{\omega  - 1}{\omega  + 1}}$ go into in the "heavy" part ${R}_{h}\left( X\right)$ ,whereas the remaining $X$ -values (along with their matching $Y$ -values) go into the "light" part ${R}_{\ell }\left( {X,Y}\right)$ . Note that $\left| {R}_{h}\right|$ cannot exceed $N/\Delta  = {N}^{\frac{2}{\omega  + 1}}$ .

- 形式为$h\left( {XY}\right)  \rightarrow  h\left( X\right)  + h\left( {Y \mid  X}\right)$的分解步骤。令$R\left( {X,Y}\right)$表示对应$h\left( {XY}\right)$的关系。相应数据库操作是根据$X$的度数（即给定$X$值匹配的$Y$值数量）将$R\left( {X,Y}\right)$划分为两部分。具体而言，度数超过$> \Delta \overset{\text{ def }}{ = }{N}^{\frac{\omega  - 1}{\omega  + 1}}$的$X$值归入"重"部${R}_{h}\left( X\right)$，其余$X$值（及其匹配的$Y$值）归入"轻"部${R}_{\ell }\left( {X,Y}\right)$。注意$\left| {R}_{h}\right|$不得超过$N/\Delta  = {N}^{\frac{2}{\omega  + 1}}$。

- Submodularity Step ${}^{10}$ of the form $h\left( {XZ}\right)  + h\left( {Y \mid  X}\right)  \rightarrow  h\left( {XYZ}\right)$ . The corresponding database operation is to join the two corresponding relations, $T\left( {X,Z}\right)  \bowtie  {R}_{\ell }\left( {X,Y}\right)$ . Since ${R}_{\ell }$ is the light part,this join takes time $N \cdot  {N}^{\frac{\omega  - 1}{\omega  + 1}} = {N}^{\frac{2\omega }{\omega  + 1}}$ ,as desired. The same goes for the other submodularity steps.

- 次模性步骤 ${}^{10}$ 的形式为 $h\left( {XZ}\right)  + h\left( {Y \mid  X}\right)  \rightarrow  h\left( {XYZ}\right)$。对应的数据库操作是连接两个相关关系 $T\left( {X,Z}\right)  \bowtie  {R}_{\ell }\left( {X,Y}\right)$。由于 ${R}_{\ell }$ 是轻量部分，该连接操作耗时 $N \cdot  {N}^{\frac{\omega  - 1}{\omega  + 1}} = {N}^{\frac{2\omega }{\omega  + 1}}$，符合预期。其他次模性步骤同理。

The three submodularity steps compute three relations ${Q}_{\ell ,1},{Q}_{\ell ,2},{Q}_{\ell ,3}$ covering triangles(X,Y,Z) where either $X,Y$ ,or $Z$ is light. We still need to account for triangles where all three are heavy. For those,we use ${R}_{h}\left( X\right) ,{S}_{h}\left( Y\right)$ ,and ${T}_{h}\left( Z\right)$ to from two matrices ${M}_{1}\left( {X,Y}\right)$ and ${M}_{2}\left( {Y,Z}\right)$ ,and then multiply them to get $M\left( {X,Z}\right)$ . Since $\left| {R}_{h}\right| ,\left| {S}_{h}\right| ,\left| {T}_{h}\right|  \leq  {N}^{\frac{2}{\omega  + 1}}$ ,this multiplication takes time ${N}^{\frac{2\omega }{\omega  + 1}}$ ,as desired. Finally,we join $M\left( {X,Z}\right)$ with $T\left( {X,Z}\right)$ to get ${Q}_{h}\left( {X,Z}\right)$ . There exists a triangle if and only if either one of ${Q}_{\ell ,1},{Q}_{\ell ,2},{Q}_{\ell ,3}$ or ${Q}_{h}$ is non-empty. ${}^{11}$

三次次模性步骤计算出三个关系 ${Q}_{\ell ,1},{Q}_{\ell ,2},{Q}_{\ell ,3}$，覆盖了满足 $X,Y$ 或 $Z$ 为轻量节点的三角形(X,Y,Z)。仍需处理三个节点均为重量的三角形。对此，我们使用 ${R}_{h}\left( X\right) ,{S}_{h}\left( Y\right)$ 和 ${T}_{h}\left( Z\right)$ 构建两个矩阵 ${M}_{1}\left( {X,Y}\right)$ 与 ${M}_{2}\left( {Y,Z}\right)$，通过矩阵乘法得到 $M\left( {X,Z}\right)$。由于 $\left| {R}_{h}\right| ,\left| {S}_{h}\right| ,\left| {T}_{h}\right|  \leq  {N}^{\frac{2}{\omega  + 1}}$，该乘法运算耗时 ${N}^{\frac{2\omega }{\omega  + 1}}$，符合预期。最后将 $M\left( {X,Z}\right)$ 与 $T\left( {X,Z}\right)$ 连接得到 ${Q}_{h}\left( {X,Z}\right)$。当且仅当 ${Q}_{\ell ,1},{Q}_{\ell ,2},{Q}_{\ell ,3}$ 或 ${Q}_{h}$ 非空时，三角形存在。${}^{11}$

---

<!-- Footnote -->

${}^{10}$ Note that $h\left( {XZ}\right)  + h\left( {Y \mid  X}\right)  \geq  h\left( {XYZ}\right)$ is just another form of the submodularity $h\left( {XZ}\right)  + h\left( {XY}\right)  \geq  h\left( X\right)  + h\left( {XYZ}\right)$ , hence the name.

${}^{10}$ 注意 $h\left( {XZ}\right)  + h\left( {Y \mid  X}\right)  \geq  h\left( {XYZ}\right)$ 本质上是次模性 $h\left( {XZ}\right)  + h\left( {XY}\right)  \geq  h\left( X\right)  + h\left( {XYZ}\right)$ 的另一种表现形式，故得此名。

${}^{11}$ Recall that inequality (13) comes from the optimal dual solution of the LP in Eq. (12),which comes from the first term (out of three) inside the outer max in Eq. (11). There are two other Shannon inequalities that come from the other two terms. In
