## 3 PRELIMINARIES

## 3 预备知识

In this section, we present the formal definitions and notations for various background concepts that were introduced informally in the introduction.

本节将正式定义引言中非正式介绍的各种背景概念及其表示法。

Hypergraphs. A hypergraph $\mathcal{H}$ is a pair $\mathcal{H} = \left( {\mathcal{V},\mathcal{E}}\right)$ ,where $\mathcal{V}$ is a set of vertices,and $\mathcal{E} \subseteq  {2}^{\mathcal{V}}$ is a set of hyperedges. Each hyperedge $Z \in  \mathcal{E}$ is a subset of $\mathcal{V}$ . We typically use $k$ to denote the number of vertices in $\mathcal{V}$ . Given a hypergraph $\mathcal{H} = \left( {\mathcal{V},\mathcal{E}}\right)$ and a vertex $X \in  \mathcal{V}$ ,we define:

超图。超图$\mathcal{H}$是一个二元组$\mathcal{H} = \left( {\mathcal{V},\mathcal{E}}\right)$，其中$\mathcal{V}$是顶点集合，$\mathcal{E} \subseteq  {2}^{\mathcal{V}}$是超边集合。每条超边$Z \in  \mathcal{E}$都是$\mathcal{V}$的子集。通常用$k$表示$\mathcal{V}$中的顶点数量。给定超图$\mathcal{H} = \left( {\mathcal{V},\mathcal{E}}\right)$和顶点$X \in  \mathcal{V}$，我们定义：

- ${\partial }_{\mathcal{H}}\left( X\right)$ is the set of hyperedges that contain $X$ ,i.e. ${\partial }_{\mathcal{H}}\left( X\right) \overset{\text{ def }}{ = }\{ Z \in  \mathcal{E} \mid  X \in  Z\}$ .

- ${\partial }_{\mathcal{H}}\left( X\right)$是包含$X$的超边集合，即${\partial }_{\mathcal{H}}\left( X\right) \overset{\text{ def }}{ = }\{ Z \in  \mathcal{E} \mid  X \in  Z\}$。

- ${U}_{\mathcal{H}}\left( X\right)$ is the union of all hyperedges that contain $X$ ,i.e. ${U}_{\mathcal{H}}\left( X\right) \overset{\text{ def }}{ = }\mathop{\bigcup }\limits_{{Z \in  {\partial }_{\mathcal{H}}\left( X\right) }}Z$ .

- ${U}_{\mathcal{H}}\left( X\right)$是包含$X$的所有超边的并集，即${U}_{\mathcal{H}}\left( X\right) \overset{\text{ def }}{ = }\mathop{\bigcup }\limits_{{Z \in  {\partial }_{\mathcal{H}}\left( X\right) }}Z$。

- ${N}_{\mathcal{H}}\left( X\right)$ is the set of neighbors of $X$ (excluding $X$ ),i.e. ${N}_{\mathcal{H}}\left( X\right) \overset{\text{ def }}{ = }{U}_{\mathcal{H}}\left( X\right)  \smallsetminus  \{ X\}$ .

- ${N}_{\mathcal{H}}\left( X\right)$是$X$的邻域集合（不含$X$本身），即${N}_{\mathcal{H}}\left( X\right) \overset{\text{ def }}{ = }{U}_{\mathcal{H}}\left( X\right)  \smallsetminus  \{ X\}$。

When $\mathcal{H}$ is clear from the context,we drop the subscript and simply write $\partial \left( X\right) ,U\left( X\right)$ ,and $N\left( X\right)$ . Given a query $Q$ of the form (1),the hypergraph of $Q$ is a hypergraph $\mathcal{H} = \left( {\mathcal{V},\mathcal{E}}\right)$ where $\mathcal{V}\overset{\text{ def }}{ = }\operatorname{vars}\left( Q\right)$ and $\mathcal{E}\overset{\text{ def }}{ = }\{ Z \mid  R\left( Z\right)  \in  \operatorname{atoms}\left( Q\right) \}$ . We often use a query $Q$ and its hypergraph $\mathcal{H}$ interchangeably, e.g. in the contexts of tree decompositions, submodular width, etc.

当上下文明确时，我们省略下标简记为$\partial \left( X\right) ,U\left( X\right)$和$N\left( X\right)$。对于形式(1)的查询$Q$，其超图是满足$\mathcal{V}\overset{\text{ def }}{ = }\operatorname{vars}\left( Q\right)$且$\mathcal{E}\overset{\text{ def }}{ = }\{ Z \mid  R\left( Z\right)  \in  \operatorname{atoms}\left( Q\right) \}$的超图$\mathcal{H} = \left( {\mathcal{V},\mathcal{E}}\right)$。在树分解、次模宽度等场景中，我们常将查询$Q$与其超图$\mathcal{H}$互换使用。

Tree Decompositions. Given a hypergraph $\mathcal{H} = \left( {\mathcal{V},\mathcal{E}}\right)$ (or a query $Q$ whose hypergraph is $\mathcal{H}$ ),a tree decomposition,or ${TD}$ for short,is a pair $\left( {T,\chi }\right)$ ,where $T$ is a tree,and $\chi  :$ nodes $\left( T\right)  \rightarrow  {2}^{\mathcal{V}}$ is a map from the nodes of $T$ to subsets of $\mathcal{V}$ ,that satisfies the following properties:

树分解。给定超图$\mathcal{H} = \left( {\mathcal{V},\mathcal{E}}\right)$（或其超图为$\mathcal{H}$的查询$Q$），树分解（简称${TD}$）是二元组$\left( {T,\chi }\right)$，其中$T$是树结构，$\chi  :$节点$\left( T\right)  \rightarrow  {2}^{\mathcal{V}}$是从$T$节点到$\mathcal{V}$子集的映射，需满足以下性质：

- For every hyperedge $Z \in  \mathcal{E}$ ,there is a node $t \in  \operatorname{nodes}\left( T\right)$ such that $Z \subseteq  \chi \left( t\right)$ .

- 对每条超边$Z \in  \mathcal{E}$，存在节点$t \in  \operatorname{nodes}\left( T\right)$使得$Z \subseteq  \chi \left( t\right)$。

- For every vertex $X \in  \mathcal{V}$ ,the set $\{ t \in  \operatorname{nodes}\left( T\right)  \mid  X \in  \chi \left( t\right) \}$ forms a connected sub-tree of $T$ . Each set $\chi \left( t\right)$ is called a bag of the tree decomposition. We use $\mathcal{T}\left( \mathcal{H}\right)$ to denote the set of all tree decompositions of $\mathcal{H}$ . A tree decomposition is called trivial if it consists of a single bag containing all vertices. A tree decomposition $\left( {T,\chi }\right)$ is called redundant if it contains two different bags ${t}_{1} \neq$ ${t}_{2} \in$ nodes(T)where $\chi \left( {t}_{1}\right)  \subseteq  \chi \left( {t}_{2}\right)$ . It is well-known that every redundant tree decomposition can be converted into a non-redundant one by removing bags that are contained in other bags.

- 对于每个顶点$X \in  \mathcal{V}$，集合$\{ t \in  \operatorname{nodes}\left( T\right)  \mid  X \in  \chi \left( t\right) \}$构成$T$的连通子树。每个集合$\chi \left( t\right)$称为树分解的袋(bag)。我们用$\mathcal{T}\left( \mathcal{H}\right)$表示$\mathcal{H}$的所有树分解的集合。若树分解仅包含一个包含所有顶点的袋，则称其为平凡树分解。当树分解$\left( {T,\chi }\right)$包含两个不同袋${t}_{1} \neq$和${t}_{2} \in$（其中$\chi \left( {t}_{1}\right)  \subseteq  \chi \left( {t}_{2}\right)$）时，称为冗余树分解。众所周知，通过移除被其他袋包含的冗余袋，可将任何冗余树分解转换为非冗余形式。

Variable Elimination. Let $\mathcal{H} = \left( {\mathcal{V},\mathcal{E}}\right)$ be a hypergraph, $k\overset{\text{ def }}{ = }\left| \mathcal{V}\right|$ ,and $\pi \left( \mathcal{V}\right)$ denote the set of all permutations of $\mathcal{V}$ . Given a fixed permutation $\sigma  = \left( {{X}_{1},\ldots ,{X}_{k}}\right)  \in  \pi \left( \mathcal{V}\right)$ ,we define a sequence of

变量消元法(Variable Elimination)。设$\mathcal{H} = \left( {\mathcal{V},\mathcal{E}}\right)$为超图(hypergraph)，$k\overset{\text{ def }}{ = }\left| \mathcal{V}\right|$，且$\pi \left( \mathcal{V}\right)$表示$\mathcal{V}$的所有排列组合的集合。给定固定排列$\sigma  = \left( {{X}_{1},\ldots ,{X}_{k}}\right)  \in  \pi \left( \mathcal{V}\right)$，我们定义如下序列：

---

<!-- Footnote -->

this simple example, we are lucky enough since they lead to the same algorithm described above. In general, there is a fairly complicated combinatorial argument to reason about many Shannon inequalities at once. See Sections E. 5 and E.6.

在这个简单例子中，我们足够幸运因为它们最终都导向上述相同算法。一般情况下，需要相当复杂的组合论证来同时处理多个香农不等式(Shannon inequalities)。参见附录E.5和E.6节。

<!-- Footnote -->

---

hypergraphs ${\mathcal{H}}_{1}^{\sigma },\ldots ,{\mathcal{H}}_{k}^{\sigma }$ ,called an elimination hypergraph sequence,as follows: ${\mathcal{H}}_{1}^{\sigma }\overset{\text{ def }}{ = }\mathcal{H}$ ,and for $i = 1,\ldots ,k - 1$ ,the hypergraph ${\mathcal{H}}_{i + 1}^{\sigma } = \left( {{\mathcal{V}}_{i + 1}^{\sigma },{\mathcal{E}}_{i + 1}^{\sigma }}\right)$ is defined recursively in terms of the previous hypergraph ${\mathcal{H}}_{i}^{\sigma } = \left( {{\mathcal{V}}_{i}^{\sigma },{\mathcal{E}}_{i}^{\sigma }}\right)$ using:

超图序列${\mathcal{H}}_{1}^{\sigma },\ldots ,{\mathcal{H}}_{k}^{\sigma }$（称为消元超图序列）定义如下：${\mathcal{H}}_{1}^{\sigma }\overset{\text{ def }}{ = }\mathcal{H}$，且对于$i = 1,\ldots ,k - 1$，超图${\mathcal{H}}_{i + 1}^{\sigma } = \left( {{\mathcal{V}}_{i + 1}^{\sigma },{\mathcal{E}}_{i + 1}^{\sigma }}\right)$根据前一个超图${\mathcal{H}}_{i}^{\sigma } = \left( {{\mathcal{V}}_{i}^{\sigma },{\mathcal{E}}_{i}^{\sigma }}\right)$递归定义为：

$$
{\mathcal{V}}_{i + 1}^{\sigma }\overset{\text{ def }}{ = }{\mathcal{V}}_{i}^{\sigma } \smallsetminus  \left\{  {X}_{i}\right\}  ,\;{\mathcal{E}}_{i + 1}^{\sigma }\overset{\text{ def }}{ = }{\mathcal{E}}_{i}^{\sigma } \smallsetminus  {\partial }_{{\mathcal{H}}_{i}^{\sigma }}\left( {X}_{i}\right)  \cup  \left\{  {{N}_{{\mathcal{H}}_{i}^{\sigma }}\left( {X}_{i}\right) }\right\}  .
$$

In words, ${\mathcal{H}}_{i + 1}^{\sigma }$ results from ${\mathcal{H}}_{i}^{\sigma }$ by removing the vertex ${X}_{i}$ and replacing all hyperedges that contain ${X}_{i}$ with a single hyperedge which is their union minus ${X}_{i}$ . We refer to the permutation $\sigma$ as a variable elimination order,or VEO for short. For convenience,we define ${\partial }_{i}^{\sigma }\overset{\text{ def }}{ = }{\partial }_{{\mathcal{H}}_{i}^{\sigma }}\left( {X}_{i}\right)$ and also define ${U}_{i}^{\sigma }$ ,and ${N}_{i}^{\sigma }$ analogously. Given a number $k$ ,we use $\left\lbrack  k\right\rbrack$ to denote the set $\{ 1,\ldots ,k\}$ .

简言之，${\mathcal{H}}_{i + 1}^{\sigma }$是通过从${\mathcal{H}}_{i}^{\sigma }$中移除顶点${X}_{i}$，并将所有包含${X}_{i}$的超边替换为它们的并集减去${X}_{i}$的单一超边而得到。我们称排列$\sigma$为变量消元序(Variable Elimination Order)，简称VEO。为方便起见，定义${\partial }_{i}^{\sigma }\overset{\text{ def }}{ = }{\partial }_{{\mathcal{H}}_{i}^{\sigma }}\left( {X}_{i}\right)$，同理定义${U}_{i}^{\sigma }$和${N}_{i}^{\sigma }$。给定数值$k$，用$\left\lbrack  k\right\rbrack$表示集合$\{ 1,\ldots ,k\}$。

Proposition 3.1 (Equivalence of TDs and VEOs [2]). Given $\mathcal{H} = \left( {\mathcal{V},\mathcal{E}}\right)$ where $k = \left| \mathcal{V}\right|$ :

命题3.1（树分解与变量消元序的等价性[2]）。给定$\mathcal{H} = \left( {\mathcal{V},\mathcal{E}}\right)$，其中$k = \left| \mathcal{V}\right|$：

(1) For every $\operatorname{TD}\left( {T,\chi }\right)  \in  \mathcal{T}\left( \mathcal{H}\right)$ ,there exists a $\operatorname{VEO}\sigma  \in  \pi \left( \mathcal{V}\right)$ that satisfies: for every $i \in  \left\lbrack  k\right\rbrack$ , there exists a node $t \in  \operatorname{nodes}\left( T\right)$ such that ${U}_{i}^{\sigma } \subseteq  \chi \left( t\right)$ .

(1) 对于每一个$\operatorname{TD}\left( {T,\chi }\right)  \in  \mathcal{T}\left( \mathcal{H}\right)$，存在一个$\operatorname{VEO}\sigma  \in  \pi \left( \mathcal{V}\right)$满足：对于每一个$i \in  \left\lbrack  k\right\rbrack$，存在节点$t \in  \operatorname{nodes}\left( T\right)$使得${U}_{i}^{\sigma } \subseteq  \chi \left( t\right)$。

(2) For every VEO $\sigma  \in  \pi \left( \mathcal{V}\right)$ ,there exists a $\operatorname{TD}\left( {T,\chi }\right)  \in  \mathcal{T}\left( \mathcal{H}\right)$ that satisfies: For every node $t \in  \operatorname{nodes}\left( T\right)$ ,there exists $i \in  \left\lbrack  k\right\rbrack$ such that $\chi \left( t\right)  \subseteq  {U}_{i}^{\sigma }$ .

(2) 对于每个VEO（变量消除顺序）$\sigma  \in  \pi \left( \mathcal{V}\right)$，存在一个$\operatorname{TD}\left( {T,\chi }\right)  \in  \mathcal{T}\left( \mathcal{H}\right)$满足：对于每个节点$t \in  \operatorname{nodes}\left( T\right)$，存在$i \in  \left\lbrack  k\right\rbrack$使得$\chi \left( t\right)  \subseteq  {U}_{i}^{\sigma }$。

The Submodular Width. Given a set $\mathcal{V}$ ,a function $\mathbf{h} : {2}^{\mathcal{V}} \rightarrow  {\mathbb{R}}_{ + }$ is called a polymatroid if it satisfies the following properties:

子模宽度。给定集合$\mathcal{V}$，若函数$\mathbf{h} : {2}^{\mathcal{V}} \rightarrow  {\mathbb{R}}_{ + }$满足以下性质，则称其为多拟阵：

$$
h\left( X\right)  + h\left( Y\right)  \geq  h\left( {X \cup  Y}\right)  + h\left( {X \cap  Y}\right) \;\forall X,Y \subseteq  \mathcal{V}\;\text{(submodularity)} \tag{14}
$$

$$
h\left( \mathbf{X}\right)  \leq  h\left( \mathbf{Y}\right) \;\forall \mathbf{X} \subset  \mathbf{Y} \subseteq  \mathcal{V}\;\text{ (monotonicity) } \tag{15}
$$

$$
h\left( \varnothing \right)  = 0\;\text{(strictness)} \tag{16}
$$

The above properties are also known as Shannon inequalities. We use ${\Gamma }_{\mathcal{V}}$ to denote the set of all polymatroids over $\mathcal{V}$ . When $\mathcal{V}$ is clear from the context,we drop $\mathcal{V}$ and simply write $\Gamma$ . Given a polymatroid $\mathbf{h}$ and sets $X,Y,Z \subseteq  \mathcal{V}$ ,we use ${XY}$ as a shorthand for $X \cup  Y$ ,and we define:

上述性质亦称为香农不等式。我们用${\Gamma }_{\mathcal{V}}$表示$\mathcal{V}$上所有多拟阵的集合。当$\mathcal{V}$在上下文中明确时，省略$\mathcal{V}$简记为$\Gamma$。给定多拟阵$\mathbf{h}$及集合$X,Y,Z \subseteq  \mathcal{V}$，用${XY}$作为$X \cup  Y$的简写，并定义：

$$
h\left( {Y \mid  X}\right) \overset{\text{ def }}{ = }h\left( {XY}\right)  - h\left( X\right)  \tag{17}
$$

$$
h\left( {Y;Z \mid  X}\right) \overset{\text{ def }}{ = }h\left( {XY}\right)  + h\left( {XZ}\right)  - h\left( X\right)  - h\left( {XYZ}\right)  \tag{18}
$$

Using the above notation,we can rewrite Eq. (15) as $h\left( {\mathbf{Y} \mid  \mathbf{X}}\right)  \geq  0$ ,and Eq. (14) as $h\left( {\mathbf{X};\mathbf{Y} \mid  \mathbf{X} \cap  \mathbf{Y}}\right)  \geq  0$ . Given a hypergraph $\mathcal{H} = \left( {\mathcal{V},\mathcal{E}}\right)$ ,a function $\mathbf{h} : {2}^{\mathcal{V}} \rightarrow  {\mathbb{R}}_{ + }$ is called edge-dominated if it satisfies $h\left( \mathbf{X}\right)  \leq  1$ for all $\mathbf{X} \in  \mathcal{E}$ . We use ${\mathrm{{ED}}}_{\mathcal{H}}$ to denote the set of all edge-dominated functions over $\mathcal{H}$ . When $\mathcal{H}$ is clear from the context,we drop $\mathcal{H}$ and simply write ED.

使用上述符号，可将式(15)重写为$h\left( {\mathbf{Y} \mid  \mathbf{X}}\right)  \geq  0$，式(14)重写为$h\left( {\mathbf{X};\mathbf{Y} \mid  \mathbf{X} \cap  \mathbf{Y}}\right)  \geq  0$。给定超图$\mathcal{H} = \left( {\mathcal{V},\mathcal{E}}\right)$，若函数$\mathbf{h} : {2}^{\mathcal{V}} \rightarrow  {\mathbb{R}}_{ + }$对所有$\mathbf{X} \in  \mathcal{E}$满足$h\left( \mathbf{X}\right)  \leq  1$，则称其为边支配函数。用${\mathrm{{ED}}}_{\mathcal{H}}$表示$\mathcal{H}$上所有边支配函数的集合。当$\mathcal{H}$在上下文中明确时，省略$\mathcal{H}$简记为ED。

Given a hypergraph $\mathcal{H} = \left( {\mathcal{V},\mathcal{E}}\right)$ ,the submodular width [17] of $\mathcal{H}$ is defined as follows:

给定超图$\mathcal{H} = \left( {\mathcal{V},\mathcal{E}}\right)$，其子模宽度[17]定义如下：

$$
\operatorname{subw}\left( \mathcal{H}\right) \overset{\text{ def }}{ = }\mathop{\max }\limits_{{h \in  \Gamma  \cap  \mathrm{{ED}}}}\mathop{\min }\limits_{{\left( {T,\chi }\right)  \in  \mathcal{T}\left( \mathcal{H}\right) }}\mathop{\max }\limits_{{t \in  \operatorname{nodes}\left( T\right) }}h\left( {\chi \left( t\right) }\right)  \tag{19}
$$

Based on Proposition 3.1 along with the fact that a polymatroid $\mathbf{h} : {2}^{\mathcal{V}} \rightarrow  {\mathbb{R}}_{ + }$ is monotone (Eq. (15)), we can equivalently define the submodular width using VEOs as follows:

基于命题3.1及多拟阵$\mathbf{h} : {2}^{\mathcal{V}} \rightarrow  {\mathbb{R}}_{ + }$的单调性（式(15)），可等价地使用VEOs（变量消除顺序）定义子模宽度如下：

$$
\operatorname{subw}\left( \mathcal{H}\right) \overset{\text{ def }}{ = }\mathop{\max }\limits_{{h \in  \Gamma  \cap  \mathrm{{ED}}}}\mathop{\min }\limits_{{\sigma  \in  \pi \left( \mathcal{V}\right) }}\mathop{\max }\limits_{{i \in  \left\lbrack  k\right\rbrack  }}h\left( {U}_{i}^{\sigma }\right)  \tag{20}
$$

Fast Matrix Multiplication. We show here how to multiply two rectangular matrices $A$ and $B$ of dimensions ${n}^{a} \times  {n}^{b}$ and ${n}^{b} \times  {n}^{c}$ in the runtime ${n}^{{\omega }^{ \circ  }\left( {a,b,c}\right) }$ ,as defined by Eq. (6). Let $d = \min \left( {a,b,c}\right)$ . We partition $A$ into $\frac{{n}^{a}}{{n}^{d}} \times  \frac{{n}^{b}}{{n}^{d}}$ blocks and $B$ into $\frac{{n}^{b}}{{n}^{d}} \times  \frac{{n}^{c}}{{n}^{d}}$ blocks. Therefore,we have to perform ${n}^{a + b + c - {3d}}$ block multiplications,each of which takes time $O\left( {n}^{d \cdot  \omega }\right)$ . The overall complexity,on $\left( {\log n}\right)$ -scale,is $a + b + c - \left( {3 - \omega }\right) \min \left( {a,b,c}\right)$ ,which gives Eq. (6). When $\omega  = 2$ ,this algorithm for rectangular matrix multiplication is already optimal because the complexity from Eq. (6) becomes linear in the sizes of the input and output matrices. However,when $\omega  > 2$ ,there could be faster algorithms that are not based on square matrix multiplication; see e.g. [14]. We define $\bar{\omega }\left( {a,b,c}\right)$ as the smallest exponent for multiplying two rectangular matrices of sizes ${n}^{a} \times  {n}^{b}$ and ${n}^{b} \times  {n}^{c}$ within $O\left( {n}^{\bar{\omega }\left( {a,b,c}\right) }\right)$ time. Based on the above discussion,we have $\bar{\omega }\left( {a,b,c}\right)  \leq  {\omega }^{\square }\left( {a,b,c}\right)$ and this becomes an equality when $\omega  = 2$ or when $a = b = c$ .

快速矩阵乘法。本文展示如何将维度分别为${n}^{a} \times  {n}^{b}$和${n}^{b} \times  {n}^{c}$的两个矩形矩阵$A$与$B$在${n}^{{\omega }^{ \circ  }\left( {a,b,c}\right) }$运行时内相乘，如公式(6)所定义。设$d = \min \left( {a,b,c}\right)$。我们将$A$划分为$\frac{{n}^{a}}{{n}^{d}} \times  \frac{{n}^{b}}{{n}^{d}}$个区块，$B$划分为$\frac{{n}^{b}}{{n}^{d}} \times  \frac{{n}^{c}}{{n}^{d}}$个区块。因此需要执行${n}^{a + b + c - {3d}}$次区块乘法运算，每次耗时$O\left( {n}^{d \cdot  \omega }\right)$。在$\left( {\log n}\right)$尺度上的总体复杂度为$a + b + c - \left( {3 - \omega }\right) \min \left( {a,b,c}\right)$，由此得到公式(6)。当$\omega  = 2$时，该矩形矩阵乘法算法已达最优，因为公式(6)的复杂度相对于输入输出矩阵的尺寸呈线性。但当$\omega  > 2$时，可能存在不基于方阵乘法的更快速算法，例如参见文献[14]。我们定义$\bar{\omega }\left( {a,b,c}\right)$为在$O\left( {n}^{\bar{\omega }\left( {a,b,c}\right) }\right)$时间内完成尺寸${n}^{a} \times  {n}^{b}$与${n}^{b} \times  {n}^{c}$的矩形矩阵相乘的最小指数。基于上述讨论，当$\omega  = 2$或$a = b = c$时，$\bar{\omega }\left( {a,b,c}\right)  \leq  {\omega }^{\square }\left( {a,b,c}\right)$成立等式。

## 4 THE $\omega$ -SUBMODULAR WIDTH: FORMAL DEFINITION

## 4 $\omega$-子模宽度的形式化定义

While Eq. (8) gives a high-level sketch of our definition of the $\omega$ -submodular width,we aim here to provide the full formal definition. To that end, we start with some auxiliary definitions.

虽然公式(8)给出了$\omega$-子模宽度定义的高层概要，但本文旨在提供完整的形式化定义。为此，我们首先给出若干辅助定义。

### 4.1 Generalizing variable elimination orders

### 4.1 变量消除顺序的泛化

In the traditional submodular width (Eq. (9)), it was sufficient to consider variable elimination orders that eliminate only one variable at a time. However once we allow using MM to eliminate variables, there could be situations where eliminating multiple variables at once using a single MM might be cheaper than eliminating the same set of variables one at a time using a sequence of MMs. Motivated by this observation, our first task is to generalize the notion of a VEO and an elimination hypergraph sequence to allow eliminating multiple variables at once.

传统子模宽度（公式(9)）中仅需考虑每次消除单个变量的消除顺序。但当我们允许使用MM（矩阵乘法）消除变量时，可能出现这种情况：通过单次MM同时消除多个变量，比使用一系列MM逐个消除相同变量集更高效。基于此观察，我们的首要任务是将VEO（变量消除顺序）和消除超图序列的概念泛化，以支持同时消除多个变量。

First,we lift the definitions of ${\partial }_{\mathcal{H}}\left( X\right) ,{U}_{\mathcal{H}}\left( X\right)$ ,and ${N}_{\mathcal{H}}\left( X\right)$ from a single variable $X$ to a set of variables $X$ . Given a hypergraph $\mathcal{H} = \left( {\mathcal{V},\mathcal{E}}\right)$ and a non-empty set of variables $X \subseteq  \mathcal{V}$ ,we define ${\partial }_{\mathcal{H}}\left( \mathbf{X}\right)$ as the set of hyperedges in $\mathcal{H}$ that overlap with $\mathbf{X}$ :

首先，我们将${\partial }_{\mathcal{H}}\left( X\right) ,{U}_{\mathcal{H}}\left( X\right)$和${N}_{\mathcal{H}}\left( X\right)$的定义从单一变量$X$推广至变量集$X$。给定超图$\mathcal{H} = \left( {\mathcal{V},\mathcal{E}}\right)$和非空变量集$X \subseteq  \mathcal{V}$，定义${\partial }_{\mathcal{H}}\left( \mathbf{X}\right)$为$\mathcal{H}$中与$\mathbf{X}$相交的超边集合：

$$
{\partial }_{\mathcal{H}}\left( \mathbf{X}\right) \overset{\text{ def }}{ = }\{ Z \in  \mathcal{E} \mid  \mathbf{X} \cap  Z \neq  \varnothing \} ,\;{U}_{\mathcal{H}}\left( \mathbf{X}\right) \overset{\text{ def }}{ = }\mathop{\bigcup }\limits_{{Z \in  {\partial }_{\mathcal{H}}\left( \mathbf{X}\right) }}Z,\;{N}_{\mathcal{H}}\left( \mathbf{X}\right) \overset{\text{ def }}{ = }{U}_{\mathcal{H}}\left( \mathbf{X}\right)  \smallsetminus  \mathbf{X}.
$$

Definition 4.1 (Generalized Variable Elimination Order (GVEO)). Given a hypergraph $\mathcal{H} = \left( {\mathcal{V},\mathcal{E}}\right)$ , let $\bar{\pi }\left( \mathcal{V}\right)$ denote the set of all ordered partitions of $\mathcal{V}$ . Namely,each element $\bar{\sigma } \in  \bar{\pi }\left( \mathcal{V}\right)$ is a tuple $\left( {{X}_{1},{X}_{2},\ldots ,{X}_{\left| \bar{\sigma }\right| }}\right)$ of non-empty and pairwise disjoint sets ${X}_{1},\ldots ,{X}_{\left| \bar{\sigma }\right| }$ whose union is $\mathcal{V}$ . We refer to each $\bar{\sigma } \in  \bar{\pi }\left( \mathcal{V}\right)$ as a generalized variable elimination order,or GVEO for short. Given $\bar{\sigma } \in  \bar{\pi }\left( \mathcal{V}\right)$ , we define a generalized elimination hypergraph sequence ${\mathcal{H}}_{1}^{\bar{\sigma }},\ldots ,{\mathcal{H}}_{\left| \bar{\sigma }\right| }^{\bar{\sigma }}$ as follows: ${\mathcal{H}}_{1}^{\bar{\sigma }}\overset{\text{ def }}{ = }\mathcal{H}$ ,and for $i = 1,\ldots ,\left| \overline{\mathbf{\sigma }}\right|  - 1$ :

定义4.1（广义变量消元序(GVEO)）。给定超图$\mathcal{H} = \left( {\mathcal{V},\mathcal{E}}\right)$，令$\bar{\pi }\left( \mathcal{V}\right)$表示$\mathcal{V}$的所有有序划分集合。即每个元素$\bar{\sigma } \in  \bar{\pi }\left( \mathcal{V}\right)$是由非空且两两互斥的集合${X}_{1},\ldots ,{X}_{\left| \bar{\sigma }\right| }$构成的元组$\left( {{X}_{1},{X}_{2},\ldots ,{X}_{\left| \bar{\sigma }\right| }}\right)$，其并集为$\mathcal{V}$。我们将每个$\bar{\sigma } \in  \bar{\pi }\left( \mathcal{V}\right)$称为广义变量消元序（简称GVEO）。给定$\bar{\sigma } \in  \bar{\pi }\left( \mathcal{V}\right)$，定义广义消元超图序列${\mathcal{H}}_{1}^{\bar{\sigma }},\ldots ,{\mathcal{H}}_{\left| \bar{\sigma }\right| }^{\bar{\sigma }}$如下：${\mathcal{H}}_{1}^{\bar{\sigma }}\overset{\text{ def }}{ = }\mathcal{H}$，且对于$i = 1,\ldots ,\left| \overline{\mathbf{\sigma }}\right|  - 1$：

$$
{\mathcal{V}}_{i + 1}^{\bar{\sigma }}\overset{\text{ def }}{ = }{\mathcal{V}}_{i}^{\bar{\sigma }} \smallsetminus  {\mathbf{X}}_{i},\;{\mathcal{E}}_{i + 1}^{\bar{\sigma }}\overset{\text{ def }}{ = }{\mathcal{E}}_{i}^{\bar{\sigma }} \smallsetminus  {\partial }_{{\mathcal{H}}_{i}^{\bar{\sigma }}}\left( {\mathbf{X}}_{i}\right)  \cup  \left\{  {{N}_{{\mathcal{H}}_{i}^{\bar{\sigma }}}\left( {\mathbf{X}}_{i}\right) }\right\}  .
$$

We define ${\partial }_{i}^{\bar{\sigma }}\overset{\text{ def }}{ = }{\partial }_{{\mathcal{H}}_{i}^{\bar{\sigma }}}\left( {X}_{i}\right)$ ,just like before,and the same goes for ${U}_{i}^{\bar{\sigma }}$ and ${N}_{i}^{\bar{\sigma }}$ .

我们像之前那样定义${\partial }_{i}^{\bar{\sigma }}\overset{\text{ def }}{ = }{\partial }_{{\mathcal{H}}_{i}^{\bar{\sigma }}}\left( {X}_{i}\right)$，${U}_{i}^{\bar{\sigma }}$和${N}_{i}^{\bar{\sigma }}$的定义亦同。

### 4.2 Expressing MM runtime using polymatroids

### 4.2 使用多拟阵表达矩阵乘法运行时间

We now give the formal definition of the MM expression that generalizes the special case $\operatorname{MM}\left( {X;Y;Z}\right)$ that was given earlier in Eq. (6). Recall that $\omega$ is a constant in the range $\left\lbrack  {2,3}\right\rbrack$ and $\gamma \overset{\text{ def }}{ = }\omega  - 2$ .

现在给出矩阵乘法表达式(MM)的形式化定义，该定义推广了先前在公式(6)中给出的特例$\operatorname{MM}\left( {X;Y;Z}\right)$。注意$\omega$是介于$\left\lbrack  {2,3}\right\rbrack$与$\gamma \overset{\text{ def }}{ = }\omega  - 2$之间的常数。

Definition 4.2 (Matrix multiplication expression,MM). Let $\mathbf{h} : {2}^{\mathcal{V}} \rightarrow  {\mathbb{R}}_{ + }$ be a polymatroid. Given four pairwise disjoint subsets, $X,Y,Z,G \subseteq  \mathcal{V}$ ,we define the matrix multiplication expression ${MM}\left( {X;Y;Z \mid  G}\right)$ as follows:

定义4.2（矩阵乘法表达式，MM）。设$\mathbf{h} : {2}^{\mathcal{V}} \rightarrow  {\mathbb{R}}_{ + }$为多拟阵。给定四个两两互斥的子集$X,Y,Z,G \subseteq  \mathcal{V}$，定义矩阵乘法表达式${MM}\left( {X;Y;Z \mid  G}\right)$如下：

$$
\operatorname{MM}\left( {X;Y;Z \mid  G}\right) \overset{\text{ def }}{ = }\max \left( {h\left( {X \mid  G}\right)  + h\left( {Y \mid  G}\right)  + {\gamma h}\left( {Z \mid  G}\right)  + h\left( G\right) ,}\right. 
$$

$$
h\left( {X \mid  G}\right)  + {\gamma h}\left( {Y \mid  G}\right)  + h\left( {Z \mid  G}\right)  + h\left( G\right) ,
$$

$$
{\gamma h}\left( {X \mid  G}\right)  + h\left( {Y \mid  G}\right)  + h\left( {Z \mid  G}\right)  + h\left( G\right) ) \tag{21}
$$

When $G$ is empty,we write $\operatorname{MM}\left( {X;Y;Z}\right)$ as a shorthand for $\operatorname{MM}\left( {X;Y;Z \mid  \varnothing }\right)$ .

当$G$为空集时，我们用$\operatorname{MM}\left( {X;Y;Z}\right)$作为$\operatorname{MM}\left( {X;Y;Z \mid  \varnothing }\right)$的简写形式。

The following proposition intuitively says that the MM runtime is at least linear in the sizes of the two input matrices and the output matrix.

下述命题直观表明，矩阵乘法运行时间至少与两个输入矩阵及输出矩阵的大小呈线性关系。

Proposition 4.3. $\max \left( {h\left( {XYG}\right) ,h\left( {YZG}\right) ,h\left( {XZG}\right) }\right)  \leq  \operatorname{MM}\left( {X;Y;Z \mid  G}\right)$

命题4.3。$\max \left( {h\left( {XYG}\right) ,h\left( {YZG}\right) ,h\left( {XZG}\right) }\right)  \leq  \operatorname{MM}\left( {X;Y;Z \mid  G}\right)$

Proof. $h\left( {XYG}\right)  \leq  h\left( {X \mid  G}\right)  + h\left( {Y \mid  G}\right)  + h\left( G\right)  \leq  h\left( {X \mid  G}\right)  + h\left( {Y \mid  G}\right)  + {\gamma h}\left( {Z \mid  G}\right)  + h\left( G\right)$ .

证明。$h\left( {XYG}\right)  \leq  h\left( {X \mid  G}\right)  + h\left( {Y \mid  G}\right)  + h\left( G\right)  \leq  h\left( {X \mid  G}\right)  + h\left( {Y \mid  G}\right)  + {\gamma h}\left( {Z \mid  G}\right)  + h\left( G\right)$

Proposition 4.4. If $\omega  = 3$ ,then $h\left( {XYZG}\right)  \leq  \operatorname{MM}\left( {X;Y;Z \mid  G}\right)$ .

命题4.4 若$\omega  = 3$，则$h\left( {XYZG}\right)  \leq  \operatorname{MM}\left( {X;Y;Z \mid  G}\right)$。

Proof. When $\omega  = 3,h\left( {XYZG}\right)  \leq  h\left( {X \mid  G}\right)  + h\left( {Y \mid  G}\right)  + h\left( {Z \mid  G}\right)  + h\left( G\right)  = \operatorname{MM}\left( {X;Y;Z \mid  G}\right)$ .

证明 当$\omega  = 3,h\left( {XYZG}\right)  \leq  h\left( {X \mid  G}\right)  + h\left( {Y \mid  G}\right)  + h\left( {Z \mid  G}\right)  + h\left( G\right)  = \operatorname{MM}\left( {X;Y;Z \mid  G}\right)$时。

### 4.3 Variable elimination via matrix multiplication

### 4.3 基于矩阵乘法的变量消元

Here we present the formal definition of the quantity EMM introduced earlier in Section 2.2, which captures the cost of eliminating a variable (or set of variables) using matrix multiplication. Given a hypergraph $\mathcal{H} = \left( {\mathcal{V},\mathcal{E}}\right)$ and a set of vertices $X \subseteq  \mathcal{V}$ ,we take the neighboring hyperedges ${\partial }_{\mathcal{H}}\left( X\right)$ of $\mathbf{X}$ and assign them to two (potentially overlapping) sets of hyperedges $\mathcal{A}$ and $\mathcal{B}$ ,each of which will form a matrix in a matrix multiplication expression. Finally, we take the minimum expression over all such assignments.

本节正式定义第2.2节引入的EMM量，该量表示通过矩阵乘法消除变量（或变量集）的代价。给定超图$\mathcal{H} = \left( {\mathcal{V},\mathcal{E}}\right)$和顶点集$X \subseteq  \mathcal{V}$，我们取其邻接超边${\partial }_{\mathcal{H}}\left( X\right)$并将它们分配到两个（可能重叠的）超边集$\mathcal{A}$和$\mathcal{B}$，每个集合将构成矩阵乘法表达式中的一个矩阵。最终我们对所有此类分配取最小表达式。

Definition 4.5 (Variable elimination expression via matrix multiplication,EMM). Let $\mathcal{H} = \left( {\mathcal{V},\mathcal{E}}\right)$ be a hypergraph and $\mathbf{h} : {2}^{\mathcal{V}} \rightarrow  {\mathbb{R}}_{ + }$ be a polymatroid. Given a non-empty set of vertices $\mathbf{X} \in  \mathcal{V}$ ,we define the variable elimination expression via matrix multiplication,denoted by ${\operatorname{EMM}}_{\mathcal{H}}\left( \mathbf{X}\right)$ ,as:

定义4.5（基于矩阵乘法的变量消元表达式EMM）。设$\mathcal{H} = \left( {\mathcal{V},\mathcal{E}}\right)$为超图，$\mathbf{h} : {2}^{\mathcal{V}} \rightarrow  {\mathbb{R}}_{ + }$为多拟阵。给定非空顶点集$\mathbf{X} \in  \mathcal{V}$，我们定义基于矩阵乘法的变量消元表达式，记作${\operatorname{EMM}}_{\mathcal{H}}\left( \mathbf{X}\right)$，其形式为：

$$
{\operatorname{EMM}}_{\mathcal{H}}\left( \mathbf{X}\right) \overset{\text{ def }}{ = }\min \left\{  {\operatorname{MM}\left( {\left( {\mathbf{A} \smallsetminus  \mathbf{B}}\right)  \smallsetminus  \mathbf{G}\;;\;\left( {\mathbf{B} \smallsetminus  \mathbf{A}}\right)  \smallsetminus  \mathbf{G}\;;\;\mathbf{X} \mid  \mathbf{G}}\right) }\right. 
$$

$$
\exists \mathcal{A},\mathcal{B} \subseteq  {\partial }_{\mathcal{H}}\left( \mathbf{X}\right) ,\;\mathcal{A} \cup  \mathcal{B} = {\partial }_{\mathcal{H}}\left( \mathbf{X}\right) ,\;\mathbf{A} =  \cup  \mathcal{A},\;\mathbf{B} =  \cup  \mathcal{B},
$$

$$
\left. {X \subseteq  A \cap  B,\;\left( {A \cap  B}\right)  \smallsetminus  X\; \subseteq  \;G\; \subseteq  \;\left( {A \cup  B}\right)  \smallsetminus  X}\right\}   \tag{22}
$$

We can simplify the above expression further by excluding trivial combinations where either one of the two sets $\left( {\mathbf{A} \smallsetminus  \mathbf{B}}\right)  \smallsetminus  \mathbf{G}$ ,or $\left( {\mathbf{B} \smallsetminus  \mathbf{A}}\right)  \smallsetminus  \mathbf{G}$ is empty.

通过排除两个集合$\left( {\mathbf{A} \smallsetminus  \mathbf{B}}\right)  \smallsetminus  \mathbf{G}$或$\left( {\mathbf{B} \smallsetminus  \mathbf{A}}\right)  \smallsetminus  \mathbf{G}$为空集的平凡组合，可进一步简化上述表达式。

Example 4.6. Consider the following hypergraph representing a 4-clique:

示例4.6 考虑表示4-团结构的超图：

$$
\mathcal{H} = \left( {\{ X,Y,Z,W\} ,\;\{ \{ X,Y\} ,\{ X,Z\} ,\{ X,W\} ,\{ Y,Z\} ,\{ Y,W\} ,\{ Z,W\} \} }\right)  \tag{23}
$$

Here we have ${\partial }_{\mathcal{H}}\left( X\right)  = \{ \{ X,Y\} ,\{ X,Z\} ,\{ X,W\} \}$ ,and there are 6 different and non-trivial ways to assign them to $\mathcal{A}$ and $\mathcal{B}$ ,resulting in 6 different MM expressions:

此处${\partial }_{\mathcal{H}}\left( X\right)  = \{ \{ X,Y\} ,\{ X,Z\} ,\{ X,W\} \}$，共有6种不同的非平凡分配方式将其分配到$\mathcal{A}$和$\mathcal{B}$，产生6种不同的MM表达式：

$$
{\operatorname{EMM}}_{\mathcal{H}}\left( X\right)  = \min (\operatorname{MM}\left( {{YZ};W;X}\right) ,\;\operatorname{MM}\left( {{YW};Z;X}\right) ,\;\operatorname{MM}\left( {{ZW};Y;X}\right) ,
$$

$$
\operatorname{MM}\left( {Y;Z;X \mid  W}\right) ,\;\operatorname{MM}\left( {Y;W;X \mid  Z}\right) ,\;\operatorname{MM}\left( {Z;W;X \mid  Y}\right) ) \tag{24}
$$

The first three expressions above correspond to cases where $\mathcal{A}$ and $\mathcal{B}$ are disjoint,while the last three correspond to cases where $\mathcal{A}$ and $\mathcal{B}$ overlap. For example,the fourth expression $\operatorname{MM}\left( {Y;Z;X \mid  W}\right)$ results from taking $\mathcal{A} = \{ \{ X,Y\} ,\{ X,W\} \} ,\mathcal{B} = \{ \{ X,Z\} ,\{ X,W\} \}$ ,and $G = \{ W\}$ .

前三个表达式对应$\mathcal{A}$与$\mathcal{B}$不相交的情形，后三个则对应二者重叠的情形。例如第四个表达式$\operatorname{MM}\left( {Y;Z;X \mid  W}\right)$的生成条件是取$\mathcal{A} = \{ \{ X,Y\} ,\{ X,W\} \} ,\mathcal{B} = \{ \{ X,Z\} ,\{ X,W\} \}$及$G = \{ W\}$。

Let $\bar{\sigma } = \left( {{X}_{1},\ldots ,{X}_{\left| \bar{\sigma }\right| }}\right)$ be a generalized variable elimination order and ${\mathcal{H}}_{1}^{\bar{\sigma }},\ldots ,{\mathcal{H}}_{\left| \bar{\sigma }\right| }^{\bar{\sigma }}$ be the corresponding generalized hypergraph sequence (Definition 4.1). We use ${\mathrm{{EMM}}}_{i}^{\bar{\sigma }}$ to denote ${\mathrm{{EMM}}}_{{\mathcal{H}}_{i}^{\bar{\sigma }}}\left( {X}_{i}\right)$ .

设$\bar{\sigma } = \left( {{X}_{1},\ldots ,{X}_{\left| \bar{\sigma }\right| }}\right)$为广义变量消元序，${\mathcal{H}}_{1}^{\bar{\sigma }},\ldots ,{\mathcal{H}}_{\left| \bar{\sigma }\right| }^{\bar{\sigma }}$为对应的广义超图序列（定义4.1）。我们使用${\mathrm{{EMM}}}_{i}^{\bar{\sigma }}$表示${\mathrm{{EMM}}}_{{\mathcal{H}}_{i}^{\bar{\sigma }}}\left( {X}_{i}\right)$。

### 4.4 Putting pieces together

### 4.4 整合模块

We now have all the components in place to formally define the $\omega$ -submodular width.

现在我们已经具备所有要素，可以正式定义$\omega$-子模宽度。

Definition 4.7 ( $\omega$ -submodular width). Given a hypergraph $\mathcal{H} = \left( {\mathcal{V},\mathcal{E}}\right)$ ,the $\omega$ -submodular width of $\mathcal{H}$ ,denoted by $\omega$ -subw(H),is defined as follows:

定义4.7（$\omega$-子模宽度）。给定超图$\mathcal{H} = \left( {\mathcal{V},\mathcal{E}}\right)$，其$\omega$-子模宽度记作$\omega$-subw(H)，定义如下：

---

$$
\omega \text{-subw}\left( \mathcal{H}\right) \overset{\text{ def }}{ = }\mathop{\max }\limits_{{h \in  \Gamma  \cap  \mathrm{{ED}}}}\mathop{\min }\limits_{{\bar{\sigma } \in  \bar{\pi }\left( \mathcal{V}\right) }}\mathop{\max }\limits_{{i \in  \left\lbrack  \left| \bar{\sigma }\right| \right\rbrack  }}\min \left( {h\left( {U}_{i}^{\bar{\sigma }}\right) ,{\mathrm{{EMM}}}_{i}^{\bar{\sigma }}}\right) 
$$

---

(25)

To compare the $\omega$ -submodular width with the traditional submodular width,we include below a definition of the submodular width that is equivalent to Eq. (20), except that it uses GVEOs:

为比较$\omega$-子模宽度与传统子模宽度，我们在下方给出基于广义变量消元序的等价定义，该定义与公式(20)等效：

Proposition 4.8. The following is an equivalent definition of the submodular width of $\mathcal{H}$ :

命题4.8。以下是超图$\mathcal{H}$子模宽度的等价定义：

$$
\operatorname{subw}\left( \mathcal{H}\right) \overset{\text{ def }}{ = }\mathop{\max }\limits_{{h \in  \Gamma  \cap  \mathrm{{ED}}}}\mathop{\min }\limits_{{\bar{\sigma } \in  \bar{\pi }\left( \mathcal{V}\right) }}\mathop{\max }\limits_{{i \in  \left\lbrack  \left| \bar{\sigma }\right| \right\rbrack  }}h\left( {U}_{i}^{\bar{\sigma }}\right)  \tag{26}
$$

Proposition 4.9. For any hypergraph $\mathcal{H},\omega$ -subw $\left( \mathcal{H}\right)  \leq  \operatorname{subw}\left( \mathcal{H}\right)$ .

命题4.9。对于任意超图$\mathcal{H},\omega$-subw$\left( \mathcal{H}\right)  \leq  \operatorname{subw}\left( \mathcal{H}\right)$。

Proposition 4.10. If $\omega  = 3$ ,then for any hypergraph $\mathcal{H},\omega$ -subw $\left( \mathcal{H}\right)  = \operatorname{subw}\left( \mathcal{H}\right)$ .

命题4.10。若$\omega  = 3$，则对任意超图$\mathcal{H},\omega$-subw$\left( \mathcal{H}\right)  = \operatorname{subw}\left( \mathcal{H}\right)$。

Proposition 4.9 holds by comparing Eq. (25) to (26). Proposition 4.10 holds because Proposition 4.4 implies that when $\omega  = 3,h\left( {U}_{i}^{\bar{\sigma }}\right)  \leq  {\mathrm{{EMM}}}_{i}^{\bar{\sigma }}$ .

通过对比公式(25)与(26)可证命题4.9成立。命题4.10成立是因为命题4.4表明当$\omega  = 3,h\left( {U}_{i}^{\bar{\sigma }}\right)  \leq  {\mathrm{{EMM}}}_{i}^{\bar{\sigma }}$时成立。

For the purpose of computing the $\omega$ -submodular width,we propose an equivalent form of Eq. (25) that is easier to compute.

为计算$\omega$-子模宽度，我们提出公式(25)的等效形式以便于计算。

Proposition 4.11. The $\omega$ -submodular width of a hypergraph can be equivalently defined as follows:

命题4.11。超图的$\omega$-子模宽度可等价定义如下：

---

$$
\omega \text{-subw}\left( \mathcal{H}\right) \overset{\text{ def }}{ = }\mathop{\max }\limits_{{h \in  \Gamma  \cap  \mathrm{{ED}}}}\mathop{\min }\limits_{{\bar{\sigma } \in  \bar{\pi }\left( \mathcal{V}\right) }}\mathop{\max }\limits_{\substack{{i \in  \left\lbrack  \left| \bar{\sigma }\right| \right\rbrack  } \\  {\forall j < i,{U}_{i}^{\bar{\sigma }} \nsubseteq  {U}_{j}^{\bar{\sigma }}} }}\min \left( {h\left( {U}_{i}^{\bar{\sigma }}\right) ,{\mathrm{{EMM}}}_{i}^{\bar{\sigma }}}\right) 
$$

---

(27)

The only difference between Eq. (27) and Eq. (25) is that $\mathop{\max }\limits_{{i \in  \left\lbrack  \left| \bar{\sigma }\right| \right\rbrack  }}$ is more restricted in Eq. (27). In particular,we only need to consider $i$ where ${U}_{i}^{\bar{\sigma }}$ is not contained in any previous ${U}_{j}^{\bar{\sigma }}$ . For example, given the hypergraph of the triangle query ${Q}_{ \bigtriangleup  }$ from Eq. (2),Eq. (27) allows us to only consider the first variable elimination step,thus simplifying the $\omega$ -submodular width to become Eq. (10).

公式(27)与(25)的唯一区别在于$\mathop{\max }\limits_{{i \in  \left\lbrack  \left| \bar{\sigma }\right| \right\rbrack  }}$在(27)中受到更严格限制。具体而言，我们只需考虑$i$未被包含于之前任何${U}_{j}^{\bar{\sigma }}$的情况。例如对于公式(2)中的三角形查询超图${Q}_{ \bigtriangleup  }$，公式(27)允许我们仅需考虑首步变量消元，从而将$\omega$-子模宽度简化为公式(10)。

Example 4.12. Let's take the 4-clique hypergraph from Eq. (23). By Proposition 4.11, for any $\bar{\sigma } \in  \bar{\pi }\left( \mathcal{V}\right)$ ,we only need to consider ${U}_{1}^{\bar{\sigma }}$ . Hence,the $\omega$ -submodular width becomes:

示例4.12。以公式(23)的4-团超图为例。根据命题4.11，对任意$\bar{\sigma } \in  \bar{\pi }\left( \mathcal{V}\right)$只需考虑${U}_{1}^{\bar{\sigma }}$。因此$\omega$-子模宽度变为：

$$
\omega \text{-subw}\left( \mathcal{H}\right)  = \mathop{\max }\limits_{{h \in  \Gamma  \cap  \mathrm{{ED}}}}\min (h\left( {XYZW}\right) ,\;\operatorname{MM}\left( {{XY};Z;W}\right) ,\;\operatorname{MM}\left( {{XZ};Y;W}\right) \text{,}
$$

$$
\operatorname{MM}\left( {{XW};Y;Z}\right) ,\;\operatorname{MM}\left( {{YZ};X;W}\right) ,\;\operatorname{MM}\left( {{YW};X;Z}\right) ,
$$

$$
\operatorname{MM}\left( {{ZW};X;Y}\right) ,\;\operatorname{MM}\left( {Y;Z;W \mid  X}\right) ,\;\operatorname{MM}\left( {X;Z;W \mid  Y}\right) ,
$$

$$
\operatorname{MM}\left( {X;Y;W \mid  Z}\right) ,\;\operatorname{MM}\left( {X;Y;Z \mid  W}\right) ) \tag{28}
$$

## 5 THE $\omega$ -SUBMODULAR WIDTH FOR EXAMPLE QUERIES.

## 5 典型查询的$\omega$-子模宽度

We show in Table 2 the $\omega$ -submodular width for several classes of queries and compare it with the submodular width for each of them. Proofs can be found in Appendix C. They use a variety of techniques to obtain both upper and lower bounds on the $\omega$ -submodular width. Below are the hypergraphs for these query classes:

表2展示了几类查询的$\omega$-子模宽度，并与各自的子模宽度进行对比。证明详见附录C，其中运用多种技术获得$\omega$-子模宽度的上下界。以下是这些查询类的超图表示：

$k$ -Clique: $\mathcal{H} = \left( {\left\{  {{X}_{1},{X}_{2},\cdots ,{X}_{k}}\right\}  ,\left\{  {\left\{  {{X}_{i},{X}_{j}}\right\}   : i,j \in  \left\lbrack  k\right\rbrack  ,i \neq  j}\right\}  }\right)$

$k$ -团（Clique）: $\mathcal{H} = \left( {\left\{  {{X}_{1},{X}_{2},\cdots ,{X}_{k}}\right\}  ,\left\{  {\left\{  {{X}_{i},{X}_{j}}\right\}   : i,j \in  \left\lbrack  k\right\rbrack  ,i \neq  j}\right\}  }\right)$

(29)

$k$ -Cycle: $\mathcal{H} = \left( {\left\{  {{X}_{1},{X}_{2},\cdots ,{X}_{k}}\right\}  ,\left\{  {\left\{  {{X}_{i},{X}_{i + 1}}\right\}   : i \in  \left\lbrack  {k - 1}\right\rbrack  }\right\}   \cup  \left\{  \left\{  {{X}_{k},{X}_{1}}\right\}  \right\}  }\right)$

$k$ -环（Cycle）: $\mathcal{H} = \left( {\left\{  {{X}_{1},{X}_{2},\cdots ,{X}_{k}}\right\}  ,\left\{  {\left\{  {{X}_{i},{X}_{i + 1}}\right\}   : i \in  \left\lbrack  {k - 1}\right\rbrack  }\right\}   \cup  \left\{  \left\{  {{X}_{k},{X}_{1}}\right\}  \right\}  }\right)$

(30)

$k$ -Pyramid: $\mathcal{H} = \left( {\left\{  {Y,{X}_{1},{X}_{2},\cdots ,{X}_{k}}\right\}  ,\left\{  {\left\{  {Y,{X}_{1},}\right\}  ,\left\{  {Y,{X}_{2}}\right\}  ,\cdots ,\left\{  {Y,{X}_{k}}\right\}  ,\left\{  {{X}_{1},{X}_{2},\cdots ,{X}_{k}}\right\}  }\right\}  }\right)$

$k$ -金字塔（Pyramid）: $\mathcal{H} = \left( {\left\{  {Y,{X}_{1},{X}_{2},\cdots ,{X}_{k}}\right\}  ,\left\{  {\left\{  {Y,{X}_{1},}\right\}  ,\left\{  {Y,{X}_{2}}\right\}  ,\cdots ,\left\{  {Y,{X}_{k}}\right\}  ,\left\{  {{X}_{1},{X}_{2},\cdots ,{X}_{k}}\right\}  }\right\}  }\right)$

(31)

## 6 ALGORITHM FOR COMPUTING THE $\omega$ -SUBMODULAR WIDTH

## 6 $\omega$-子模宽度计算算法

We present in this section a mechanical algorithm for computing the value of the $\omega$ -submodular width for any given hypergraph. This algorithm is a crucial component in our algorithm for computing the answer to a query $Q$ in $\omega$ -submodular width time,as we will see in Section 7. Using Eq. (27) alone,it is not immediately clear how to compute the $\omega$ -submodular width of a hypergraph $\mathcal{H}$ because the outer $\mathop{\max }\limits_{{h \in  \Gamma  \cap  \mathrm{{ED}}}}$ ranges over an infinite set. Nevertheless,every other max and min in Eq. (27) ranges over a finite set whose cardinality only depends on $\mathcal{H}$ . Note that by Eq. (22), ${\mathrm{{EMM}}}_{i}^{\bar{\sigma }}$ is a minimum of a collection of terms,each of which has the from $\mathrm{{MM}}\left( {X;Y;Z \mid  G}\right)$ . Note also that each term $\operatorname{MM}\left( {X;Y;Z \mid  G}\right)$ is by itself a maximum of 3 terms,as described by Eq. (21).

本节提出一种机械算法，用于计算任意给定超图的$\omega$-子模宽度值。该算法是我们第7章将要介绍的、在$\omega$-子模宽度时间内求解查询$Q$答案的关键组件。仅凭公式(27)，如何计算超图$\mathcal{H}$的$\omega$-子模宽度并不直观，因为外层$\mathop{\max }\limits_{{h \in  \Gamma  \cap  \mathrm{{ED}}}}$遍历的是无限集合。然而公式(27)中其他所有max和min运算都作用于有限集合，其基数仅取决于$\mathcal{H}$。注意根据公式(22)，${\mathrm{{EMM}}}_{i}^{\bar{\sigma }}$是若干项的最小值，每项均具有$\mathrm{{MM}}\left( {X;Y;Z \mid  G}\right)$的形式。另需注意如公式(21)所述，每项$\operatorname{MM}\left( {X;Y;Z \mid  G}\right)$本身又是3个项的最大值。

In order to compute the $\omega$ -submodular width,the first step is to distribute every min over max in Eq. (27), thus pulling all max operators to the top level. To formally describe this distribution, we need some notation. Given an expression $e$ which is either a minimum or a maximum of a collection of terms,we use args(e)to denote the collection of terms over which the minimum or maximum is taken. Let $k$ be the number of vertices in $\mathcal{H}$ . Let $f : \bar{\pi }\left( \mathcal{V}\right)  \rightarrow  \left\lbrack  k\right\rbrack$ be a function that maps every generalized variable elimination order $\overline{\mathbf{\sigma }} \in  \bar{\pi }\left( \mathcal{V}\right)$ to a number $i$ between 1 and $\left| \overline{\mathbf{\sigma }}\right|$ that satisfies ${U}_{i}^{\overline{\mathbf{\sigma }}} \nsubseteq  {U}_{j}^{\overline{\mathbf{\sigma }}}$ for all $j \in  \left\lbrack  {i - 1}\right\rbrack$ . (Note that $\left| \overline{\mathbf{\sigma }}\right|  \leq  k$ for every $\overline{\mathbf{\sigma }}$ .) Let $\mathcal{F}$ be the set of all such functions $f$ . For a fixed function $f \in  \mathcal{F}$ ,let ${g}_{f}$ be a function that maps every pair $\left( {\bar{\sigma },\operatorname{MM}\left( {X;Y;Z \mid  G}\right) }\right)$ where $\bar{\sigma } \in  \bar{\pi }\left( \mathcal{V}\right)$ and $\operatorname{MM}\left( {X;Y;Z \mid  G}\right)$ is a term in $\operatorname{args}\left( {\operatorname{EMM}}_{f\left( \bar{\sigma }\right) }^{\bar{\sigma }}\right)$ to one of the three terms in $\operatorname{args}\left( {\operatorname{MM}\left( {X;Y;Z \mid  G}\right) }\right)$ from Eq. (21). Let ${\mathcal{G}}_{f}$ be the set of all such functions ${g}_{f}$ for a fixed $f$ . Then,by distributing the min over the max in Eq. (27),we get:

为计算$\omega$-子模宽度，第一步需将式(27)中所有min运算符分配到max运算符之上，从而将所有max运算符提升至顶层。为形式化描述该分配过程，需引入若干符号：给定表达式$e$（其为若干项的最小值或最大值），用args(e)表示参与该最小/最大运算的项集合。设$k$为$\mathcal{H}$中的顶点数量，$f : \bar{\pi }\left( \mathcal{V}\right)  \rightarrow  \left\lbrack  k\right\rbrack$为将每个广义变量消序$\overline{\mathbf{\sigma }} \in  \bar{\pi }\left( \mathcal{V}\right)$映射到1至$\left| \overline{\mathbf{\sigma }}\right|$间数值$i$的函数，且满足对所有$j \in  \left\lbrack  {i - 1}\right\rbrack$有${U}_{i}^{\overline{\mathbf{\sigma }}} \nsubseteq  {U}_{j}^{\overline{\mathbf{\sigma }}}$（注意对每个$\overline{\mathbf{\sigma }}$有$\left| \overline{\mathbf{\sigma }}\right|  \leq  k$）。令$\mathcal{F}$表示所有此类函数$f$的集合。对于固定函数$f \in  \mathcal{F}$，设${g}_{f}$为将每个二元组$\left( {\bar{\sigma },\operatorname{MM}\left( {X;Y;Z \mid  G}\right) }\right)$（其中$\bar{\sigma } \in  \bar{\pi }\left( \mathcal{V}\right)$且$\operatorname{MM}\left( {X;Y;Z \mid  G}\right)$为$\operatorname{args}\left( {\operatorname{EMM}}_{f\left( \bar{\sigma }\right) }^{\bar{\sigma }}\right)$中的项）映射到式(21)中$\operatorname{args}\left( {\operatorname{MM}\left( {X;Y;Z \mid  G}\right) }\right)$三个项之一的函数。令${\mathcal{G}}_{f}$表示固定$f$时所有此类函数${g}_{f}$的集合。通过分配式(27)中min于max之上，可得：

<!-- Media -->

---

$$
\begin{array}{l} \omega \text{-subw}\left( \mathcal{H}\right) \\  \overset{\text{ def }}{ = }\mathop{\max }\limits_{{h \in  \Gamma  \cap  \mathrm{{ED}}}}\;\mathop{\min }\limits_{{\bar{\sigma } \in  \bar{\pi }\left( \mathcal{V}\right) }}\;\mathop{\max }\limits_{{i \in  \left\lbrack  \left| \bar{\sigma }\right| \right\rbrack  }}\;\min \left( {h\left( {U}_{i}^{\bar{\sigma }}\right) ,{\mathrm{{EMM}}}_{i}^{\bar{\sigma }}}\right) \\  \end{array}
$$

$$
 = \mathop{\max }\limits_{{h \in  \Gamma  \cap  \mathrm{{ED}}}}\mathop{\max }\limits_{{f \in  \mathcal{F}}}\mathop{\min }\limits_{{\bar{\sigma } \in  \bar{\pi }\left( \mathcal{V}\right) }}\min \left( {h\left( {U}_{f\left( \bar{\sigma }\right) }^{\bar{\sigma }}\right) ,{\mathrm{{EMM}}}_{f\left( \bar{\sigma }\right) }^{\bar{\sigma }}}\right) 
$$

$$
 = \mathop{\max }\limits_{{h \in  \Gamma  \cap  \mathrm{{ED}}}}\mathop{\max }\limits_{{f \in  \mathcal{F}}}\mathop{\min }\limits_{{\bar{\sigma } \in  \bar{\pi }\left( \mathcal{V}\right) }}\;\min \left( {h\left( {U}_{f\left( \bar{\sigma }\right) }^{\bar{\sigma }}\right) ,\mathop{\min }\limits_{{{MM}\left( {X;Y;Z \mid  G}\right)  \in  \operatorname{args}\left( {\mathrm{{EMM}}}_{f\left( \bar{\sigma }\right) }^{\bar{\sigma }}\right) }}{MM}\left( {X;Y;Z \mid  G}\right) }\right) 
$$

$$
 = \mathop{\max }\limits_{{h \in  \Gamma  \cap  \mathrm{{ED}}}}\mathop{\max }\limits_{{f \in  \mathcal{F}}}\mathop{\min }\limits_{{\bar{\sigma } \in  \bar{\pi }\left( \mathcal{V}\right) }}\min \left( {h\left( {U}_{f\left( \bar{\sigma }\right) }^{\bar{\sigma }}\right) ,\mathop{\min }\limits_{{{MM}\left( {X;Y;Z \mid  G}\right)  \in  \operatorname{args}\left( {{EM}{M}_{f\left( \bar{\sigma }\right) }^{\bar{\sigma }}}\right) }}\;\mathop{\max }\limits_{{e \in  \operatorname{args}\left( {{MM}\left( {X;Y;Z \mid  G}\right) }\right) }}e}\right) 
$$

$$
 = \mathop{\max }\limits_{{h \in  \Gamma  \cap  \mathrm{{ED}}}}\mathop{\max }\limits_{{f \in  \mathcal{F}}}\mathop{\max }\limits_{{{g}_{f} \in  {\bar{g}}_{f}}}\mathop{\min }\limits_{{\bar{\sigma } \in  \bar{\pi }\left( \mathcal{V}\right) }}\min \left( {h\left( {U}_{f\left( \bar{\sigma }\right) }^{\bar{\sigma }}\right) ,\mathop{\min }\limits_{{\mathrm{{MM}}\left( {X;Y;Z \mid  G}\right)  \in  \operatorname{args}\left( {\mathrm{{EMM}}\frac{\bar{\sigma }}{f\left( \bar{\sigma }\right) }}\right) }}{g}_{f}\left( {\bar{\sigma },\mathrm{{MM}}\left( {X;Y;Z \mid  G}\right) }\right) }\right) 
$$

$$
 = \mathop{\max }\limits_{{f \in  \mathcal{F}}}\;\mathop{\max }\limits_{{{g}_{f} \in  {\mathcal{G}}_{f}}}
$$

$$
\omega \text{-subw}\left( \mathcal{H}\right)  = \mathop{\max }\limits_{{i \in  \left\lbrack  I\right\rbrack  }}

## 7 ALGORITHM FOR ANSWERING QUERIES IN ω-SUBMODULAR WIDTH TIME

## 7 ω-子模宽度时间内的查询应答算法

We show below our main result about evaluating a Boolean conjunctive query of the form (1) in $\omega$ -submodular width time. The theorem assumes that $\omega$ is a rational number,but can still be applied to the case where $\omega$ is irrational by taking any rational upper bound on $\omega$ . The runtime is measured in terms of $N$ ,which is the size of the input database instance,i.e., $N\overset{\text{ def }}{ = }\mathop{\sum }\limits_{{R\left( Z\right)  \in  \operatorname{atoms}\left( Q\right) }}\left| R\right|$ . The $\widetilde{O}$ -notation hides a polylogarithmic factor in $N$ .

下文展示我们在ω-子模宽度时间内评估布尔连接查询(1)形式的主要结果。该定理假设$\omega$为有理数，但通过取$\omega$的任一有理上界，仍可推广至无理数情形。时间复杂度以输入数据库实例大小$N$（即$N\overset{\text{ def }}{ = }\mathop{\sum }\limits_{{R\left( Z\right)  \in  \operatorname{atoms}\left( Q\right) }}\left| R\right|$）度量。$\widetilde{O}$记号表示隐藏$N$的多对数因子。

THEOREM 7.1. Assuming that $\omega$ is a rational number,given a Boolean conjunctive query $Q$ and a corresponding input database instance $D$ ,there is an algorithm that computes the answer to $Q$ in time $\widetilde{O}\left( {N}^{\omega  - \operatorname{subw}\left( Q\right) }\right)$ ,where $N$ is the size of $D$ .

定理7.1. 假设$\omega$为有理数，给定布尔连接查询$Q$及对应输入数据库实例$D$，存在算法可在$\widetilde{O}\left( {N}^{\omega  - \operatorname{subw}\left( Q\right) }\right)$时间内计算出$Q$的应答，其中$N$为$D$的规模。

The proof is quite involved and requires developing a series of technical tools. It also relies on the algorithm for computing the $\omega$ -submodular width of a hypergraph,which was given in Section 6. The full proof can be found in Appendix E.

该证明过程相当复杂，需要开发一系列技术工具，并依赖于第6节给出的计算超图$\omega$-子模宽度的算法。完整证明详见附录E。

## 8 CONCLUSION

## 8 结论

We proposed a general framework for evaluating Boolean conjunctive queries that naturally subsumes both combinatorial and non-combinatorial techniques under the umbrella of information theory. Our framework generalizes the notion of submodular width by incorporating matrix multiplication, and provides a matching algorithm for evaluating queries in the corresponding time complexity. Using this framework, we show how to recover the best known complexity for various classes of queries as well as improve the complexity for some of them.

我们提出了一个评估布尔连接查询的通用框架，自然地将组合与非组合技术统一在信息论体系下。该框架通过引入矩阵乘法推广了子模宽度的概念，并提供了在相应时间复杂度内评估查询的匹配算法。借助此框架，我们不仅重现了多类查询的最优已知复杂度，还改进了部分查询的复杂度。

Our framework can be straightforwardly extended from Boolean conjunctive queries to count queries and more generally to aggregate queries over any semiring, albeit without free variables. In particular, in the combinatorial world, the transition from Boolean to aggregate queries is done by adapting the submodular width to become the Sharp-submodular width [1]. The latter is a variant of the submodular width that is obtained by relaxing the notion of polymatroids. In the presence of matrix multiplication, a similar transition from Boolean to aggregate queries is possible by adapting the $\omega$ -submodular width to become the Sharp- $\omega$ -submodular width.

我们的框架可直接从布尔连接查询扩展到计数查询，更广义地说，可推广至任意半环上的聚合查询（尽管不含自由变量）。在组合领域，通过将子模宽度调整为Sharp-子模宽度[1]，实现了从布尔查询到聚合查询的转换。后者是通过放宽多拟阵概念获得的子模宽度变体。在矩阵乘法存在时，类似地可通过调整$\omega$-子模宽度为Sharp-$\omega$-子模宽度来实现这种转换。

### A.2 Tree Decompositions

### A.2 树分解

Example A.2 (Tree Decompositions of a Hypergraph). Consider the following hypergraph $\mathcal{H} =$ (V,E)that represents a 4-cycle:

示例A.2（超图的树分解）。考虑表示4-环的下列超图$\mathcal{H} =$(V,E)：

$$
\mathcal{V} = \{ A,B,C,D\} ,\;\mathcal{E} = \{ \{ A,B\} ,\{ B,C\} ,\{ C,D\} ,\{ D,A\} \}  \tag{34}
$$

This hypergraph has the following two (non-trivial and non-redundant) tree decompositions:

该超图具有以下两个（非平凡且非冗余的）树分解：

- A tree decomposition $\left( {{T}_{1},{\chi }_{1}}\right)$ with two nodes ${t}_{11}$ and ${t}_{12}$ corresponding to two bags, ${\chi }_{1}\left( {t}_{11}\right)  =$ $\{ A,B,C\}$ and ${\chi }_{1}\left( {t}_{12}\right)  = \{ C,D,A\}$ .

- 含两个节点${t}_{11}$和${t}_{12}$的树分解$\left( {{T}_{1},{\chi }_{1}}\right)$，对应两个包${\chi }_{1}\left( {t}_{11}\right)  =$$\{ A,B,C\}$与${\chi }_{1}\left( {t}_{12}\right)  = \{ C,D,A\}$

- A tree decomposition $\left( {{T}_{2},{\chi }_{2}}\right)$ with two nodes ${t}_{21}$ and ${t}_{22}$ corresponding to two bags, ${\chi }_{2}\left( {t}_{21}\right)  =$ $\{ B,C,D\}$ and ${\chi }_{2}\left( {t}_{22}\right)  = \{ D,A,B\}$ .

- 含两个节点${t}_{21}$和${t}_{22}$的树分解$\left( {{T}_{2},{\chi }_{2}}\right)$，对应两个包${\chi }_{2}\left( {t}_{21}\right)  =$$\{ B,C,D\}$与${\chi }_{2}\left( {t}_{22}\right)  = \{ D,A,B\}$

### A.3 Variable Elimination

### A.3 变量消除

Example A.3 (Elimination Hypergraph Sequence). Consider the 4-cycle hypergraph $\mathcal{H}$ from Example A. 2 and the variable elimination order ${\mathbf{\sigma }}_{1} = \left( {B,C,D,A}\right)$ . This elimination order results in the following sequence of hypergraphs:

示例A.3（消除超图序列）。考虑示例A.2中的4-循环超图$\mathcal{H}$及变量消除顺序${\mathbf{\sigma }}_{1} = \left( {B,C,D,A}\right)$。该消除顺序会产生以下超图序列：

$$
{\mathcal{H}}_{1}^{{\sigma }_{1}} = \left( {\{ A,B,C,D\} ,\{ \{ A,B\} ,\{ B,C\} ,\{ C,D\} ,\{ D,A\} \} }\right) 
$$

$$
{\mathcal{H}}_{2}^{{\sigma }_{1}} = \left( {\{ A,C,D\} ,\{ \{ A,C\} ,\{ C,D\} ,\{ D,A\} \} }\right) 
$$

$$
{\mathcal{H}}_{3}^{{\sigma }_{1}} = \left( {\{ A,D\} ,\{ \{ D,A\} \} }\right) 
$$

$$
{\mathcal{H}}_{4}^{{\sigma }_{1}} = \left( {\{ A\} ,\{ \{ A\} \} }\right) 
$$

In contrast,the order ${\mathbf{\sigma }}_{2} = \left( {A,B,C,D}\right)$ results in the following sequence:

相比之下，顺序${\mathbf{\sigma }}_{2} = \left( {A,B,C,D}\right)$会产生以下序列：

$$
{\mathcal{H}}_{1}^{{\sigma }_{2}} = \left( {\{ A,B,C,D\} ,\{ \{ A,B\} ,\{ B,C\} ,\{ C,D\} ,\{ D,A\} \} }\right) 
$$

$$
{\mathcal{H}}_{2}^{{\sigma }_{2}} = \left( {\{ B,C,D\} ,\{ \{ D,B\} ,\{ B,C\} ,\{ C,D\} \} }\right) 
$$

$$
{\mathcal{H}}_{3}^{{\sigma }_{2}} = \left( {\{ C,D\} ,\{ \{ C,D\} \} }\right) 
$$

$$
{\mathcal{H}}_{4}^{{\sigma }_{2}} = \left( {\{ D\} ,\{ \{ D\} \} }\right) 
$$

Example A. 4 (Equivalence of Tree Decompositions and Variable Elimination Orders). Continuing with Example A.3, consider the 4-cycle hypergraph in Eq. (34). The variable elimination order ${\mathbf{\sigma }}_{1} = \left( {B,C,D,A}\right)$ results in:

示例A.4（树分解与变量消除顺序的等价性）。延续示例A.3，考虑公式(34)中的4-循环超图。变量消除顺序${\mathbf{\sigma }}_{1} = \left( {B,C,D,A}\right)$将产生：

$$
{U}_{1}^{{\sigma }_{1}} = \{ A,B,C\} ,\;{U}_{2}^{{\sigma }_{1}} = \{ C,D,A\} ,\;{U}_{3}^{{\sigma }_{1}} = \{ D,A\} ,\;{U}_{4}^{{\sigma }_{1}} = \{ A\} .
$$

Note that ${\sigma }_{1}$ "subsumes" the tree decomposition $\left( {{T}_{1},{\chi }_{1}}\right)$ from Example A.2,in the sense that the pair $\left( {\left( {{T}_{1},{\chi }_{1}}\right) ,{\mathbf{\sigma }}_{1}}\right)$ satisfies part 1 of Proposition 3.1. Also, $\left( {{T}_{1},{\chi }_{1}}\right)$ "subsumes" ${\mathbf{\sigma }}_{1}$ in the sense that the pair $\left( {\left( {{T}_{1},{\chi }_{1}}\right) ,{\mathbf{\sigma }}_{1}}\right)$ satisfies part 2 of Proposition 3.1.

注意${\sigma }_{1}$“包含”了示例A.2中的树分解$\left( {{T}_{1},{\chi }_{1}}\right)$，即这对$\left( {\left( {{T}_{1},{\chi }_{1}}\right) ,{\mathbf{\sigma }}_{1}}\right)$满足命题3.1的第一部分。同时$\left( {{T}_{1},{\chi }_{1}}\right)$也“包含”${\mathbf{\sigma }}_{1}$，因为$\left( {\left( {{T}_{1},{\chi }_{1}}\right) ,{\mathbf{\sigma }}_{1}}\right)$满足命题3.1的第二部分。

In contrast,the variable order ${\mathbf{\sigma }}_{2} = \left( {A,B,C,D}\right)$ results in:

相比之下，变量顺序${\mathbf{\sigma }}_{2} = \left( {A,B,C,D}\right)$会产生：

$$
{U}_{1}^{{\sigma }_{2}} = \{ D,A,B\} ,\;{U}_{2}^{{\sigma }_{2}} = \{ B,C,D\} ,\;{U}_{3}^{{\sigma }_{2}} = \{ C,D\} ,\;{U}_{4}^{{\sigma }_{2}} = \{ D\} .
$$

In particular, ${\mathbf{\sigma }}_{2}$ above subsumes and is subsumed by the tree decomposition $\left( {{T}_{2},{\chi }_{2}}\right)$ .

特别地，上述${\mathbf{\sigma }}_{2}$既包含又被树分解$\left( {{T}_{2},{\chi }_{2}}\right)$所包含。

### A.4 The Submodular Width

### A.4 次模宽度

We summarize here how to compute the submodular width of a given hypergraph $\mathcal{H} = \left( {\mathcal{V},\mathcal{E}}\right)$ ,as defined in Eq. (19). To that end,we need to apply a couple of tricks. Let $\mathcal{T}\left( \mathcal{H}\right)  = \left\{  {\left( {{T}_{1},{\chi }_{1}}\right) ,\left( {{T}_{2},{\chi }_{2}}\right) ,\ldots }\right.$ , $\left. \left( {{T}_{K},{\chi }_{K}}\right) \right\}$ be the list of all tree decompositions of $\mathcal{H}$ . Let $\mathbf{t} = \left( {{t}_{1},{t}_{2},\ldots ,{t}_{K}}\right)  \in  \operatorname{nodes}\left( {T}_{1}\right)  \times  \ldots  \times$ nodes $\left( {T}_{K}\right)$ denote a tuple of nodes,one from each tree decomposition. (Note that ${t}_{1},\ldots ,{t}_{K}$ are not nodes from the same tree decomposition.) By distributing ${}^{12}$ the min over the max in Eq. (19),we can equivalently write (where $\left\lbrack  K\right\rbrack$ denotes $\{ 1,\ldots ,K\}$ ):

此处总结如何计算给定超图$\mathcal{H} = \left( {\mathcal{V},\mathcal{E}}\right)$的次模宽度（如公式(19)定义）。为此需要运用几个技巧。设$\mathcal{T}\left( \mathcal{H}\right)  = \left\{  {\left( {{T}_{1},{\chi }_{1}}\right) ,\left( {{T}_{2},{\chi }_{2}}\right) ,\ldots }\right.$、$\left. \left( {{T}_{K},{\chi }_{K}}\right) \right\}$为$\mathcal{H}$所有树分解的列表，令$\mathbf{t} = \left( {{t}_{1},{t}_{2},\ldots ,{t}_{K}}\right)  \in  \operatorname{nodes}\left( {T}_{1}\right)  \times  \ldots  \times$节点$\left( {T}_{K}\right)$表示来自各树分解的节点元组（注意${t}_{1},\ldots ,{t}_{K}$并非来自同一树分解）。通过将公式(19)中的min与max运算符交换位置，可等价表示为（其中$\left\lbrack  K\right\rbrack$代表$\{ 1,\ldots ,K\}$）：

(35) (36)

<!-- Media -->

Eq. (36) follows by swapping the two max operators in Eq. (35). Now for a fixed tuple $\left( {{t}_{1},\ldots ,{t}_{K}}\right)$ , consider the inner optimization problem:

公式(36)由交换公式(35)的两个max运算符得到。对于固定元组$\left( {{t}_{1},\ldots ,{t}_{K}}\right)$，考虑内部优化问题：

$$
\mathop{\max }\limits_{{\mathbf{h} \in  \Gamma  \cap  \mathrm{{ED}}}}\mathop{\min }\limits_{{i \in  \left\lbrack  K\right\rbrack  }}h\left( {{\chi }_{i}\left( {t}_{i}\right) }\right)  \tag{37}
$$

Although the constraints $\mathbf{h} \in  \Gamma  \cap  \mathrm{{ED}}$ are linear,Eq. (37) is still not a linear program (LP) because the objective is a minimum of linear functions. However, we can convert (37) into an LP by introducing a new variable $w$ that is upper bounded by each term in the minimum:

尽管约束条件$\mathbf{h} \in  \Gamma  \cap  \mathrm{{ED}}$是线性的，但公式(37)仍非线性规划(LP)，因其目标函数是线性函数的最小值。不过可通过引入新变量$w$（该变量被最小值中的各项上界约束）将其转化为LP：

$$
\mathop{\max }\limits_{{w,h \in  \Gamma  \cap  \mathrm{{ED}}}}\left\{  {w \mid  w \leq  h\left( {{\chi }_{1}\left( {t}_{1}\right) }\right) ,\ldots ,w \leq  h\left( {{\chi }_{K}\left( {t}_{K}\right) }\right) }\right\}   \tag{38}
$$

As a result, computing the submodular width can be reduced to taking the maximum solution of a finite number of LPs,one for each tuple $\left( {{t}_{1},\ldots ,{t}_{K}}\right)  \in  \operatorname{nodes}\left( {T}_{1}\right)  \times  \ldots  \times  \operatorname{nodes}\left( {T}_{K}\right)$ .

因此，计算子模宽度可转化为求解有限数量线性规划的最大解，每个元组$\left( {{t}_{1},\ldots ,{t}_{K}}\right)  \in  \operatorname{nodes}\left( {T}_{1}\right)  \times  \ldots  \times  \operatorname{nodes}\left( {T}_{K}\right)$对应一个线性规划。

Example A.5. Consider the 4-cycle hypergraph from Example A.2. The submodular width of this hypergraph can be computed by considering the two tree decompositions $\left( {{T}_{1},{\chi }_{1}}\right)$ and $\left( {{T}_{2},{\chi }_{2}}\right)$ from Example A.2. Applying Eq. (19), we get:

示例A.5. 考虑示例A.2中的4-循环超图。通过分析示例A.2中的两个树分解$\left( {{T}_{1},{\chi }_{1}}\right)$和$\left( {{T}_{2},{\chi }_{2}}\right)$，并应用公式(19)，可计算出该超图的子模宽度：

$$
\operatorname{subw}\left( \mathcal{H}\right)  = \mathop{\max }\limits_{{h \in  \Gamma  \cap  \mathrm{{ED}}}}\min \left( {\max \left( {h\left( {ABC}\right) ,h\left( {CDA}\right) }\right) ,\max \left( {h\left( {BCD}\right) ,h\left( {DAB}\right) }\right) }\right) 
$$

By distributing the min over the inner max and then swapping the two max operators, we get:

通过将最小值分配到内部最大值上，再交换两个最大值运算符，可得：

$$
\operatorname{subw}\left( \mathcal{H}\right)  = \max \left( {\mathop{\max }\limits_{{h \in  \Gamma  \cap  \mathrm{{ED}}}}\min \left( {h\left( {ABC}\right) ,h\left( {BCD}\right) }\right) ,\;\mathop{\max }\limits_{{h \in  \Gamma  \cap  \mathrm{{ED}}}}\min \left( {h\left( {ABC}\right) ,h\left( {DAB}\right) }\right) ,}\right. 
$$

$$
\mathop{\max }\limits_{{h \in  \Gamma  \cap  \mathrm{{ED}}}}\min \left( {h\left( {CDA}\right) ,h\left( {BCD}\right) }\right) ,\;\left. {\mathop{\max }\limits_{{h \in  \Gamma  \cap  \mathrm{{ED}}}}\min \left( {h\left( {CDA}\right) ,h\left( {DAB}\right) }\right) }\right) 
$$

Let’s take the first term inside the max as an example $\mathop{\max }\limits_{{h \in  \Gamma  \cap  \mathrm{{ED}}}}\min \left( {h\left( {ABC}\right) ,h\left( {BCD}\right) }\right)$ . This term is equivalent to the following LP:

以max运算符内的第一项$\mathop{\max }\limits_{{h \in  \Gamma  \cap  \mathrm{{ED}}}}\min \left( {h\left( {ABC}\right) ,h\left( {BCD}\right) }\right)$为例，该项等价于以下线性规划：

$$
\mathop{\max }\limits_{{w,h \in  \Gamma  \cap  \mathrm{{ED}}}}\{ w \mid  w \leq  h\left( {ABC}\right) ,w \leq  h\left( {BCD}\right) \} 
$$

The above LP has an optimal objective value of $3/2$ . The other three terms inside the max are similar. Therefore, the submodular width of the 4-cycle hypergraph is 3/2.

该线性规划的最优目标值为$3/2$。max运算符内其余三项同理。因此，4-循环超图的子模宽度为3/2。

---

This appendix presents missing proofs from Section 4.

本附录提供第4章节缺失的证明。

Proposition 4.8. The following is an equivalent definition of the submodular width of $\mathcal{H}$ :

命题4.8. 以下是$\mathcal{H}$子模宽度的等价定义：

$$
\operatorname{subw}\left( \mathcal{H}\right) \overset{\text{ def }}{ = }\mathop{\max }\limits_{{h \in  \Gamma  \cap  \mathrm{{ED}}}}\mathop{\min }\limits_{{\bar{\sigma } \in  \bar{\pi }\left( \mathcal{V}\right) }}\mathop{\max }\limits_{{i \in  \left\lbrack  \left| \bar{\sigma }\right| \right\rbrack  }}h\left( {U}_{i}^{\bar{\sigma }}\right) 
$$

Proof. The above definition is obviously upper bounded by Eq. (20) since $\bar{\pi }\left( \mathcal{V}\right)$ is a superset of $\pi \left( \mathcal{V}\right)$ . In order to show the opposite direction,consider an arbitrary $\bar{\sigma } = \left( {{X}_{1},\ldots ,{X}_{\left| \bar{\sigma }\right| }}\right)  \in$ $\bar{\pi }\left( \mathcal{V}\right)$ . Consider the non-negative quantity $\varphi \left( \left| \overline{\mathbf{\sigma }}\right| \right) \overset{\text{ def }}{ = }\mathop{\sum }\limits_{{i \in  \left\lbrack  \left| \overline{\mathbf{\sigma }}\right| \right\rbrack  }}\left( {\left| {X}_{i}\right|  - 1}\right)$ . As long as $\overline{\mathbf{\sigma }}$ contains some ${X}_{i}$ whose size it at least 2,this quantity will be positive,and we will construct another generalized variable elimination order $\overline{{\mathbf{\sigma }}^{\prime }}$ that reduces this quantity and where every ${U}_{j}^{\overline{{\mathbf{\sigma }}^{\prime }}}$ is a subset of some ${U}_{i}^{\bar{\sigma }}$ . WLOG assume that $\left| {X}_{1}\right|  > 1$ . Let $Y$ be an arbitrary element of ${X}_{1}$ ,and define $\overline{{\sigma }^{\prime }} = \left( {\{ Y\} ,\mathbf{X}\smallsetminus \{ Y\} ,{\mathbf{X}}_{2},\ldots ,{\mathbf{X}}_{\left| \bar{\sigma }\right| }}\right)$ . Clearly,both ${U}_{1}^{\overline{{\sigma }^{\prime }}}$ and ${U}_{2}^{\overline{{\sigma }^{\prime }}}$ are subsets of ${U}_{1}^{\bar{\sigma }}$ . Moreover, ${\mathcal{H}}_{3}^{\overline{{\sigma }^{\prime }}}$ is identical to ${\mathcal{H}}_{2}^{\bar{\sigma }}$ ,hence by induction,every ${U}_{j}^{\overline{{\sigma }^{\prime }}}$ is a subset of some ${U}_{i}^{\bar{\sigma }}$ .

证明。上述定义显然以方程(20)为上界，因为$\bar{\pi }\left( \mathcal{V}\right)$是$\pi \left( \mathcal{V}\right)$的超集。为证相反方向，考虑任意$\bar{\sigma } = \left( {{X}_{1},\ldots ,{X}_{\left| \bar{\sigma }\right| }}\right)  \in$$\bar{\pi }\left( \mathcal{V}\right)$。考察非负量$\varphi \left( \left| \overline{\mathbf{\sigma }}\right| \right) \overset{\text{ def }}{ = }\mathop{\sum }\limits_{{i \in  \left\lbrack  \left| \overline{\mathbf{\sigma }}\right| \right\rbrack  }}\left( {\left| {X}_{i}\right|  - 1}\right)$。只要$\overline{\mathbf{\sigma }}$包含至少大小为2的${X}_{i}$，该量即为正，此时我们将构造另一个广义变量消元序$\overline{{\mathbf{\sigma }}^{\prime }}$以减小该量，且其中每个${U}_{j}^{\overline{{\mathbf{\sigma }}^{\prime }}}$都是某个${U}_{i}^{\bar{\sigma }}$的子集。不失一般性假设$\left| {X}_{1}\right|  > 1$。令$Y$为${X}_{1}$的任意元素，定义$\overline{{\sigma }^{\prime }} = \left( {\{ Y\} ,\mathbf{X}\smallsetminus \{ Y\} ,{\mathbf{X}}_{2},\ldots ,{\mathbf{X}}_{\left| \bar{\sigma }\right| }}\right)$。显然，${U}_{1}^{\overline{{\sigma }^{\prime }}}$与${U}_{2}^{\overline{{\sigma }^{\prime }}}$均为${U}_{1}^{\bar{\sigma }}$的子集。此外，${\mathcal{H}}_{3}^{\overline{{\sigma }^{\prime }}}$与${\mathcal{H}}_{2}^{\bar{\sigma }}$相同，故由归纳法可知每个${U}_{j}^{\overline{{\sigma }^{\prime }}}$都是某个${U}_{i}^{\bar{\sigma }}$的子集。

Proposition 4.11. The $\omega$ -submodular width of a hypergraph can be equivalently defined as follows:

命题4.11。超图的$\omega$-次模宽度可等价定义如下：

$$
\omega \text{-subw}\left( \mathcal{H}\right) \overset{\text{ def }}{ = }\mathop{\max }\limits_{{h \in  \Gamma  \cap  \mathrm{{ED}}}}\mathop{\min }\limits_{{\bar{\sigma } \in  \bar{\pi }\left( \mathcal{V}\right) }}\mathop{\max }\limits_{\substack{{i \in  \left\lbrack  \left| \bar{\sigma }\right| \right\rbrack  } \\  {\forall j < i,{U}_{i}^{\bar{\sigma }} \nsubseteq  {U}_{j}^{\bar{\sigma }}} }}\min \left( {h\left( {U}_{i}^{\bar{\sigma }}\right) ,\mathrm{{EM}}{M}_{i}^{\bar{\sigma }}}\right) 
$$

Proof. Consider an arbitrary polymatroid $\mathbf{h} \in  \Gamma  \cap  \mathrm{{ED}}$ and a generalized elimination order $\bar{\sigma } \in  \bar{\pi }\left( \mathcal{V}\right)$ . It suffices to show that for any pair(j,i)where $j < i$ and ${U}_{i}^{\bar{\sigma }} \subseteq  {U}_{j}^{\bar{\sigma }}$ ,we must have $h\left( {U}_{i}^{\bar{\sigma }}\right)  \leq  \min \left\{  {h\left( {U}_{j}^{\bar{\sigma }}\right) ,{\mathrm{{EMM}}}_{j}^{\bar{\sigma }}}\right\}$ . In this case,we further notice that ${X}_{j} \cap  {U}_{i}^{\bar{\sigma }} = \varnothing$ since $j < i$ hence the variables ${X}_{j}$ must have been eliminated before ${U}_{i}^{\bar{\sigma }}$ was created. This implies that ${U}_{i}^{\bar{\sigma }} \subseteq  {U}_{j}^{\bar{\sigma }} \smallsetminus  {X}_{j}$ . By the monotonicity of $\mathbf{h}\left( {\mathrm{{Eq}}\left( {15}\right) }\right) ,h\left( {U}_{i}^{\bar{\sigma }}\right)  \leq  h\left( {{U}_{j}^{\bar{\sigma }} \smallsetminus  {X}_{j}}\right)  \leq  h\left( {U}_{j}^{\bar{\sigma }}\right)$ . Moreover,by Proposition 4.3, we have $h\left( {{U}_{j}^{\bar{\sigma }} \smallsetminus  {X}_{j}}\right)  \leq  {\mathrm{{EMM}}}_{j}^{\bar{\sigma }}$ .

证明。考虑任意多拟阵$\mathbf{h} \in  \Gamma  \cap  \mathrm{{ED}}$及广义消元序$\bar{\sigma } \in  \bar{\pi }\left( \mathcal{V}\right)$。只需证明对于满足$j < i$与${U}_{i}^{\bar{\sigma }} \subseteq  {U}_{j}^{\bar{\sigma }}$的任意对(j,i)，必有$h\left( {U}_{i}^{\bar{\sigma }}\right)  \leq  \min \left\{  {h\left( {U}_{j}^{\bar{\sigma }}\right) ,{\mathrm{{EMM}}}_{j}^{\bar{\sigma }}}\right\}$。此时我们进一步注意到，由于$j < i$，变量${X}_{j}$必定在${U}_{i}^{\bar{\sigma }}$生成前已被消去，这意味着${U}_{i}^{\bar{\sigma }} \subseteq  {U}_{j}^{\bar{\sigma }} \smallsetminus  {X}_{j}$。根据$\mathbf{h}\left( {\mathrm{{Eq}}\left( {15}\right) }\right) ,h\left( {U}_{i}^{\bar{\sigma }}\right)  \leq  h\left( {{U}_{j}^{\bar{\sigma }} \smallsetminus  {X}_{j}}\right)  \leq  h\left( {U}_{j}^{\bar{\sigma }}\right)$的单调性，加之命题4.3可得$h\left( {{U}_{j}^{\bar{\sigma }} \smallsetminus  {X}_{j}}\right)  \leq  {\mathrm{{EMM}}}_{j}^{\bar{\sigma }}$。

## C MISSING DETAILS FROM SECTION 5

## C 第五章缺失细节

We show here how to compute the $\omega$ -submodular width for the classes of queries that are given by Table 2 in Section 5. We start with some preliminaries:

此处展示如何计算表2所示查询类的$\omega$-子模宽度。首先给出预备知识：

Definition C.1 (Fractional Edge Covering Number). For a hypergraph $\mathcal{H} = \left( {\mathcal{V},\mathcal{E}}\right)$ ,a fractional edge covering is a function $W : \mathcal{E} \rightarrow  \left\lbrack  {0,1}\right\rbrack$ such that $\mathop{\sum }\limits_{{e \in  \mathcal{E} : X \in  e}}W\left( e\right)  \geq  1$ holds for each vertex $X \in  \mathcal{V}$ . The fractional edge covering number of $\mathcal{H}$ ,denoted as ${\rho }^{ * }\left( \mathcal{H}\right)$ ,is defined as the minimum weight assigned to hyperedges over all possible fractional edge covering,i.e., ${\rho }^{ * }\left( \mathcal{H}\right)  = \mathop{\min }\limits_{W}\mathop{\sum }\limits_{{e \in  \mathcal{E}}}W\left( e\right)$ .

定义C.1（分数边覆盖数）。对于超图$\mathcal{H} = \left( {\mathcal{V},\mathcal{E}}\right)$，分数边覆盖是满足对每个顶点$X \in  \mathcal{V}$有$\mathop{\sum }\limits_{{e \in  \mathcal{E} : X \in  e}}W\left( e\right)  \geq  1$的函数$W : \mathcal{E} \rightarrow  \left\lbrack  {0,1}\right\rbrack$。超图$\mathcal{H}$的分数边覆盖数记作${\rho }^{ * }\left( \mathcal{H}\right)$，定义为所有可能分数边覆盖中超边被赋予的最小权重，即${\rho }^{ * }\left( \mathcal{H}\right)  = \mathop{\min }\limits_{W}\mathop{\sum }\limits_{{e \in  \mathcal{E}}}W\left( e\right)$。

Proposition C.2. In any hypergraph $\mathcal{H} = \left( {\mathcal{V},\mathcal{E}}\right)$ ,for any polymatroid $\mathbf{h} \in  \Gamma  \cap  \mathrm{{ED}},h\left( \mathcal{V}\right)  \leq$ ${\rho }^{ * }\left( \mathcal{H}\right)$ .

命题C.2。在任意超图$\mathcal{H} = \left( {\mathcal{V},\mathcal{E}}\right)$中，对任意多拟阵$\mathbf{h} \in  \Gamma  \cap  \mathrm{{ED}},h\left( \mathcal{V}\right)  \leq$有${\rho }^{ * }\left( \mathcal{H}\right)$。

### C.1 Clique Hypergraphs

### C.1 团超图

Proposition C.3. In a $k$ -clique hypergraph $\mathcal{H} = \left( {\left\{  {{X}_{1},{X}_{2},\cdots ,{X}_{k}}\right\}  ,\left\{  {\left\{  {{X}_{i},{X}_{j}}\right\}   : i,j \in  \left\lbrack  k\right\rbrack  ,i \neq  j}\right\}  }\right)$ , for any generalized elimination ordering $\bar{\sigma } \in  \pi \left( \mathcal{V}\right)$ and any $i \in  \left\lbrack  \bar{\sigma }\right\rbrack  ,{U}_{i}^{\bar{\sigma }} \subseteq  \mathcal{V} = {U}_{1}^{\bar{\sigma }}$ .

命题C.3. 在$k$-团超图$\mathcal{H} = \left( {\left\{  {{X}_{1},{X}_{2},\cdots ,{X}_{k}}\right\}  ,\left\{  {\left\{  {{X}_{i},{X}_{j}}\right\}   : i,j \in  \left\lbrack  k\right\rbrack  ,i \neq  j}\right\}  }\right)$中，对于任意广义消元序$\bar{\sigma } \in  \pi \left( \mathcal{V}\right)$及任意$i \in  \left\lbrack  \bar{\sigma }\right\rbrack  ,{U}_{i}^{\bar{\sigma }} \subseteq  \mathcal{V} = {U}_{1}^{\bar{\sigma }}$。

From Proposition C. 3 and Definition 4.7,we can simplify $\omega$ -subw for clique hypergraphs $\mathcal{H}$ as below:

根据命题C.3与定义4.7，可将团超图$\mathcal{H}$的$\omega$-子权重简化为：

$$
\omega \text{-subw}\left( \mathcal{H}\right)  = \mathop{\max }\limits_{{h \in  \Gamma  \cap  \mathrm{{ED}}}}\min \left( {h\left( \mathcal{V}\right) ,\mathop{\min }\limits_{{\bar{\sigma } \in  \bar{\pi }\left( \mathcal{V}\right) }}{\mathrm{{EMM}}}_{1}^{\bar{\sigma }}}\right)  \tag{39}
$$

Proposition C.4. In a clique hypergraph $\mathcal{H} = \left( {\mathcal{V},\mathcal{E}}\right)$ ,with a polymatroid $\mathbf{h} \in  \Gamma  \cap  \mathrm{{ED}}$ such that $h\left( {\mathbf{A} \mid  \mathbf{B}}\right)  = h\left( \mathbf{A}\right)$ for arbitrary $\mathbf{A},\mathbf{B} \subseteq  \mathcal{V}$ with $\mathbf{A} \cap  \mathbf{B} = \varnothing$ ,for any generalized elimination ordering $\bar{\sigma } \in  \pi \left( \mathcal{V}\right) ,$

命题C.4. 在团超图$\mathcal{H} = \left( {\mathcal{V},\mathcal{E}}\right)$中，若存在多拟阵$\mathbf{h} \in  \Gamma  \cap  \mathrm{{ED}}$使得对任意满足$\mathbf{A} \cap  \mathbf{B} = \varnothing$的$\mathbf{A},\mathbf{B} \subseteq  \mathcal{V}$有$h\left( {\mathbf{A} \mid  \mathbf{B}}\right)  = h\left( \mathbf{A}\right)$，则对于任意广义消元序$\bar{\sigma } \in  \pi \left( \mathcal{V}\right) ,$

$$
{\mathrm{{EMM}}}_{1}^{\bar{\sigma }} = \mathop{\min }\limits_{\left( \mathbf{A},\mathbf{B},\mathbf{C}\right) }\mathop{\min }\limits_{{\text{is a partition of }\mathcal{V}}}\mathrm{{MM}}\left( {\mathbf{A},\mathbf{B},\mathbf{C}}\right) 
$$

Proof. The direction ${\mathrm{{EMM}}}_{1}^{\bar{\sigma }} \geq  \mathop{\min }\limits_{{\left( {A,B,C}\right) \text{ is a partition of }\mathcal{V}}}\mathrm{{MM}}\left( {A,B,C;\varnothing }\right)$ follows (4.5). Below,we focus on:

证明. 方向${\mathrm{{EMM}}}_{1}^{\bar{\sigma }} \geq  \mathop{\min }\limits_{{\left( {A,B,C}\right) \text{ is a partition of }\mathcal{V}}}\mathrm{{MM}}\left( {A,B,C;\varnothing }\right)$由(4.5)式可得。以下我们重点论证：

$$
{\mathrm{{EMM}}}_{1}^{\bar{\sigma }} = \mathop{\min }\limits_{{\left( {\mathbf{A},\mathbf{B},\mathbf{C},\mathbf{D}}\right) \text{ is a partition of }\mathcal{V}}}\operatorname{MM}\left( {\mathbf{A},\mathbf{B},\mathbf{C} \mid  \mathbf{D}}\right) 
$$

$$
 = \mathop{\min }\limits_{{\left( {\mathbf{A},\mathbf{B},\mathbf{C},\mathbf{D}}\right) \text{ is a partition of }\mathcal{V}}}\min \left\{  \begin{array}{l} {\gamma h}\left( {\mathbf{A} \mid  \mathbf{D}}\right)  + h\left( {\mathbf{B} \mid  \mathbf{D}}\right)  + h\left( {\mathbf{C} \mid  \mathbf{D}}\right)  + h\left( \mathbf{D}\right) \\  h\left( {\mathbf{A} \mid  \mathbf{D}}\right)  + {\gamma h}\left( {\mathbf{B} \mid  \mathbf{D}}\right)  + h\left( {\mathbf{C} \mid  \mathbf{D}}\right)  + h\left( \mathbf{D}\right) \\  h\left( {\mathbf{A} \mid  \mathbf{D}}\right)  + h\left( {\mathbf{B} \mid  \mathbf{D}}\right)  + {\gamma h}\left( {\mathbf{C} \mid  \mathbf{D}}\right)  + h\left( \mathbf{D}\right)  \end{array}\right. 
$$

$$
 = \mathop{\min }\limits_{{\left( {\mathbf{A},\mathbf{B},\mathbf{C},\mathbf{D}}\right) \text{ is a partition of }\mathcal{V}}}\min \left\{  \begin{array}{l} {\gamma h}\left( \mathbf{A}\right)  + h\left( \mathbf{B}\right)  + h\left( \mathbf{C}\right)  + h\left( \mathbf{D}\right) \\  h\left( \mathbf{A}\right)  + {\gamma h}\left( \mathbf{B}\right)  + h\left( \mathbf{C}\right)  + h\left( \mathbf{D}\right) \\  h\left( \mathbf{A}\right)  + h\left( \mathbf{B}\right)  + {\gamma h}\left( \mathbf{C}\right)  + h\left( \mathbf{D}\right)  \end{array}\right. 
$$

$$
 \leq  \mathop{\min }\limits_{{\left( {\mathbf{A},\mathbf{B},\mathbf{C},\mathbf{D}}\right) \text{ is a partition of }\mathcal{V}}}\min \left\{  \begin{array}{l} {\gamma h}\left( \mathbf{A}\right)  + h\left( \mathbf{B}\right)  + h\left( \mathbf{{CD}}\right) \\  h\left( \mathbf{A}\right)  + {\gamma h}\left( \mathbf{B}\right)  + h\left( \mathbf{{CD}}\right) \\  h\left( \mathbf{A}\right)  + h\left( \mathbf{B}\right)  + {\gamma h}\left( \mathbf{{CD}}\right)  \end{array}\right. 
$$

$$
 = \mathop{\min }\limits_{\left( \mathbf{A},\mathbf{B},\mathbf{C}\right) }\operatorname{MM}\left( {\mathbf{A},\mathbf{B},\mathbf{C}}\right) 
$$

Together, we have completed the proof.

综上，我们完成了证明。

LEMMA C.5. For the 3-clique hypergraph as defined in (2), $\omega$ -subw $\left( \mathcal{H}\right)  = \frac{2\omega }{\omega  + 1}$ .

引理C.5. 对于(2)式定义的3-团超图，$\omega$-子权重$\left( \mathcal{H}\right)  = \frac{2\omega }{\omega  + 1}$。

Proof. Direction $\omega$ -subw $\left( \mathcal{H}\right)  \leq  \frac{2\omega }{\omega  + 1}$ . We partition all polymatroids $h \in  \Gamma  \cap  \mathrm{{ED}}$ into the following two cases:

证明. 方向$\omega$-子权重$\left( \mathcal{H}\right)  \leq  \frac{2\omega }{\omega  + 1}$。我们将所有多拟阵$h \in  \Gamma  \cap  \mathrm{{ED}}$划分为以下两种情况：

- Case 1: there exists a pair of vertices with their conditional entropy is smaller than $\frac{\omega  - 1}{\omega  + 1}$ ,say $h\left( {Y \mid  X}\right)  \leq  \frac{\omega  - 1}{\omega  + 1}$ . In this case,we have $h\left( {XYZ}\right)  = h\left( {ZX}\right)  + h\left( {Y \mid  {ZX}}\right)  \leq  h\left( {ZX}\right)  + h\left( {Y \mid  X}\right)  \leq  1 + \frac{\omega  - 1}{\omega  + 1} =$ $\frac{2\omega }{\omega  + 1}$ ,where $h\left( {Y \mid  {ZX}}\right)  \leq  h\left( {Y \mid  X}\right)$ follows the sub-modularity of $h$ and $h\left( {ZX}\right)  \leq  1$ follows the edge-domination of $h$ .

- 情况1：存在顶点对的条件熵小于$\frac{\omega  - 1}{\omega  + 1}$，例如$h\left( {Y \mid  X}\right)  \leq  \frac{\omega  - 1}{\omega  + 1}$。此时有$h\left( {XYZ}\right)  = h\left( {ZX}\right)  + h\left( {Y \mid  {ZX}}\right)  \leq  h\left( {ZX}\right)  + h\left( {Y \mid  X}\right)  \leq  1 + \frac{\omega  - 1}{\omega  + 1} =$$\frac{2\omega }{\omega  + 1}$，其中$h\left( {Y \mid  {ZX}}\right)  \leq  h\left( {Y \mid  X}\right)$源于$h$的次模性，$h\left( {ZX}\right)  \leq  1$源于$h$的边支配性。

- Case 2: None of the above,i.e.,for each pair of vertices,their conditional entropy is larger than $\frac{\omega  - 1}{\omega  + 1}$ . As $h\left( {Y \mid  X}\right)  > \frac{\omega  - 1}{\omega  + 1}$ and $h\left( {XY}\right)  \leq  1$ ,we have $h\left( X\right)  = h\left( {XY}\right)  - h\left( {Y \mid  X}\right)  \leq  1 - \frac{\omega  - 1}{\omega  + 1} = \frac{2}{\omega  + 1}$ . Similarly, we have $h\left( Y\right) ,h\left( Z\right)  \leq  \frac{2}{\omega  + 1}$ . Consider a generalized elimination ordering $\overline{\mathbf{\sigma }} = \left( {\{ X\} ,\{ Y\} ,\{ Z\} }\right)$ . We have ${\mathrm{{EMM}}}_{1}^{\bar{\sigma }} \leq  \mathrm{{MM}}\left( {Y;Z;X}\right)  = \frac{2\omega }{\omega  + 1}$ by choosing $\mathcal{A} = \{ \{ X,Y\} \}$ and $\mathcal{B} = \{ \{ X,Z\} \}$ .

- 情况2：以上皆非，即对于每对顶点，它们的条件熵大于$\frac{\omega  - 1}{\omega  + 1}$。由于$h\left( {Y \mid  X}\right)  > \frac{\omega  - 1}{\omega  + 1}$和$h\left( {XY}\right)  \leq  1$，我们得到$h\left( X\right)  = h\left( {XY}\right)  - h\left( {Y \mid  X}\right)  \leq  1 - \frac{\omega  - 1}{\omega  + 1} = \frac{2}{\omega  + 1}$。同理可得$h\left( Y\right) ,h\left( Z\right)  \leq  \frac{2}{\omega  + 1}$。考虑一个广义消元序$\overline{\mathbf{\sigma }} = \left( {\{ X\} ,\{ Y\} ,\{ Z\} }\right)$，通过选择$\mathcal{A} = \{ \{ X,Y\} \}$和$\mathcal{B} = \{ \{ X,Z\} \}$，我们得出${\mathrm{{EMM}}}_{1}^{\bar{\sigma }} \leq  \mathrm{{MM}}\left( {Y;Z;X}\right)  = \frac{2\omega }{\omega  + 1}$。

Direction $\omega$ -subw $\left( \mathcal{H}\right)  \geq  \frac{2\omega }{\omega  + 1}$ . We identify a polymatroid $\mathbf{h}$ below. Let $a,b,c,d$ be independently

方向$\omega$-子模$\left( \mathcal{H}\right)  \geq  \frac{2\omega }{\omega  + 1}$。我们在下方识别出一个多拟阵$\mathbf{h}$。设$a,b,c,d$为独立

random variables with $h\left( a\right)  = h\left( b\right)  = h\left( c\right)  = \frac{\omega  - 1}{\omega  + 1}$ and $h\left( d\right)  = \frac{3 - \omega }{\omega  + 1}$ . Let $X = \left( {ad}\right) ,Y = \left( {bd}\right) ,Z = \left( {cd}\right)$ .

随机变量，具有$h\left( a\right)  = h\left( b\right)  = h\left( c\right)  = \frac{\omega  - 1}{\omega  + 1}$和$h\left( d\right)  = \frac{3 - \omega }{\omega  + 1}$。令$X = \left( {ad}\right) ,Y = \left( {bd}\right) ,Z = \left( {cd}\right)$。

- $h\left( X\right)  = h\left( Y\right)  = h\left( Z\right)  = \frac{2}{\omega  + 1}$ ;

- $h\left( {XY}\right)  = h\left( {XZ}\right)  = h\left( {YZ}\right)  = 1$ ;

- $h\left( {XYZ}\right)  = \frac{2\omega }{\omega  + 1}$ .

Consider a generalized elimination ordering $\overline{\mathbf{\sigma }} = \left( {\{ X\} ,\{ Y\} ,\{ Z\} }\right) ,{\operatorname{EMM}}_{1}^{\overline{\mathbf{\sigma }}} = \operatorname{MM}\left( {Y,Z,X}\right)  = \frac{2\omega }{\omega  + 1}$ . Hence, $\min \left( {h\left( \mathcal{V}\right) ,\mathop{\min }\limits_{{\bar{\sigma } \in  \bar{\pi }\left( \mathcal{V}\right) }}{\mathrm{{EMM}}}_{1}^{\bar{\sigma }}}\right)  = \frac{2\omega }{\omega  + 1}$ holds for $\mathbf{h}$ . From (39),we conclude $\omega$ -subw $\left( \mathcal{H}\right)  \geq  \frac{2\omega }{\omega  + 1}$ . The other generalized elimination orderings are the same.

考虑一个广义消元序$\overline{\mathbf{\sigma }} = \left( {\{ X\} ,\{ Y\} ,\{ Z\} }\right) ,{\operatorname{EMM}}_{1}^{\overline{\mathbf{\sigma }}} = \operatorname{MM}\left( {Y,Z,X}\right)  = \frac{2\omega }{\omega  + 1}$。因此，对于$\mathbf{h}$，$\min \left( {h\left( \mathcal{V}\right) ,\mathop{\min }\limits_{{\bar{\sigma } \in  \bar{\pi }\left( \mathcal{V}\right) }}{\mathrm{{EMM}}}_{1}^{\bar{\sigma }}}\right)  = \frac{2\omega }{\omega  + 1}$成立。由(39)式可推出$\omega$-子模$\left( \mathcal{H}\right)  \geq  \frac{2\omega }{\omega  + 1}$。其他广义消元序情况相同。
