LEMMA C.6. For the 4-clique hypergraph as defined in (23), $\omega  - \operatorname{subw}\left( \mathcal{H}\right)  = \frac{\omega  + 1}{2}$ .

引理C.6. 对于式(23)定义的4-团超图，$\omega  - \operatorname{subw}\left( \mathcal{H}\right)  = \frac{\omega  + 1}{2}$。

Proof. Direction $\omega$ -subw $\left( \mathcal{H}\right)  \leq  \frac{\omega  + 1}{2}$ . We partition all polymatroids $\mathbf{h} \in  \Gamma  \cap  \mathrm{{ED}}$ into the following two cases:

证明。方向$\omega$-子模$\left( \mathcal{H}\right)  \leq  \frac{\omega  + 1}{2}$。我们将所有多拟阵$\mathbf{h} \in  \Gamma  \cap  \mathrm{{ED}}$划分为以下两种情况：

Case 1: there exists a vertex such that its all conditional entropies are smaller than $\frac{1}{2}$ ,say, $W$ is such a vertex with $h\left( {X \mid  W}\right) ,h\left( {Y \mid  W}\right) ,h\left( {Z \mid  W}\right)  \leq  \frac{1}{2}$ . Consider a generalized elimination ordering $\overline{\mathbf{\sigma }} = \left( {\{ X\} ,\{ Y\} ,\{ Z\} ,\{ W\} }\right)$ . By choosing $\mathcal{A} = \{ \{ X,W\} ,\{ Y,W\} \}$ and $\mathcal{B} = \{ \{ X,W\} ,\{ Z,W\} \}$ ,

情形1：存在一个顶点，其所有条件熵均小于$\frac{1}{2}$，例如$W$就是这样一个顶点，满足$h\left( {X \mid  W}\right) ,h\left( {Y \mid  W}\right) ,h\left( {Z \mid  W}\right)  \leq  \frac{1}{2}$。考虑广义消元序$\overline{\mathbf{\sigma }} = \left( {\{ X\} ,\{ Y\} ,\{ Z\} ,\{ W\} }\right)$。通过选择$\mathcal{A} = \{ \{ X,W\} ,\{ Y,W\} \}$和$\mathcal{B} = \{ \{ X,W\} ,\{ Z,W\} \}$，

$$
{\operatorname{EMM}}_{1}^{\bar{\sigma }} \leq  \operatorname{MM}\left( {Y;Z;X \mid  W}\right)  = \max \left\{  \begin{array}{ll} {\gamma h}\left( {Y \mid  W}\right)  + h\left( {X \mid  W}\right)  + h\left( {Z \mid  W}\right)  + h\left( W\right) & \\  h\left( {Y \mid  W}\right)  + {\gamma h}\left( {Z \mid  W}\right)  + h\left( {X \mid  W}\right)  + h\left( W\right) &  \leq  \frac{\gamma }{2} + \frac{1}{2} + 1 = \frac{\omega  + 1}{2}. \\  h\left( {Y \mid  W}\right)  + h\left( {Z \mid  W}\right)  + {\gamma h}\left( {X \mid  W}\right)  + h\left( W\right) &  \end{array}\right. 
$$

Case 2: None of the above,i.e.,each vertex has at least one conditional entropy larger than $\frac{1}{2}$ . Wlog, suppose $h\left( {Y \mid  X}\right)  > \frac{1}{2}$ . As $h\left( {XY}\right)  \leq  1$ ,we have $h\left( X\right)  = h\left( {XY}\right)  - h\left( {Y \mid  X}\right)  < 1 - \frac{1}{2} = \frac{1}{2}$ . Similarly,we have $h\left( Y\right) ,h\left( Z\right) ,h\left( W\right)  < \frac{1}{2}$ . Consider a generalized elimination ordering $\overline{\mathbf{\sigma }} = \left( {\{ X\} ,\{ Y\} ,\{ Z\} ,\{ W\} }\right)$ . We have ${\mathrm{{EMM}}}_{1}^{\bar{\sigma }} \leq  \mathrm{{MM}}\left( {Y;Z;X \mid  W}\right)  = \frac{\omega  + 1}{2}$ .

情形2：不满足上述条件，即每个顶点至少有一个条件熵大于$\frac{1}{2}$。不失一般性，假设$h\left( {Y \mid  X}\right)  > \frac{1}{2}$。由于$h\left( {XY}\right)  \leq  1$，我们得到$h\left( X\right)  = h\left( {XY}\right)  - h\left( {Y \mid  X}\right)  < 1 - \frac{1}{2} = \frac{1}{2}$。同理可得$h\left( Y\right) ,h\left( Z\right) ,h\left( W\right)  < \frac{1}{2}$。考虑广义消元序$\overline{\mathbf{\sigma }} = \left( {\{ X\} ,\{ Y\} ,\{ Z\} ,\{ W\} }\right)$，我们有${\mathrm{{EMM}}}_{1}^{\bar{\sigma }} \leq  \mathrm{{MM}}\left( {Y;Z;X \mid  W}\right)  = \frac{\omega  + 1}{2}$。

Direction $\omega$ -subw $\left( \mathcal{H}\right)  \geq  \frac{\omega  + 1}{2}$ . We identify a polymatroid $\mathbf{h} \in  \Gamma  \cap  \mathrm{{ED}}$ as follows: let $X,Y,Z,W$ be independently random variables with $h\left( X\right)  = h\left( Y\right)  = h\left( Z\right)  = h\left( W\right)  = \frac{1}{2}$ . Then,we have $h\left( \mathcal{V}\right)  = 2$ . From Proposition C.4,for an arbitrary generalized elimination ordering $\bar{\sigma } \in  \pi \left( \mathcal{V}\right) ,{\operatorname{EMM}}_{1}^{\bar{\sigma }} = \frac{\omega  + 1}{2}$ . From (39),we conclude $\omega$ -subw $\left( \mathcal{H}\right)  \geq  \frac{\omega  + 1}{2}$ .

方向$\omega$-子模权重$\left( \mathcal{H}\right)  \geq  \frac{\omega  + 1}{2}$。我们按如下方式定义多拟阵$\mathbf{h} \in  \Gamma  \cap  \mathrm{{ED}}$：令$X,Y,Z,W$为独立随机变量，满足$h\left( X\right)  = h\left( Y\right)  = h\left( Z\right)  = h\left( W\right)  = \frac{1}{2}$。于是得到$h\left( \mathcal{V}\right)  = 2$。根据命题C.4，对于任意广义消元序$\bar{\sigma } \in  \pi \left( \mathcal{V}\right) ,{\operatorname{EMM}}_{1}^{\bar{\sigma }} = \frac{\omega  + 1}{2}$。由式(39)可证$\omega$-子模权重$\left( \mathcal{H}\right)  \geq  \frac{\omega  + 1}{2}$。

LEMMA C.8. For a $k$ -clique hypergraph $\mathcal{H} = \left( {\mathcal{V},\mathcal{E}}\right)$ for $k \geq  6$ ,where $\mathcal{V} = \left\{  {{X}_{1},{X}_{2},\cdots ,{X}_{k}}\right\}$ and $\mathcal{E} = \{ \{ {X}_{i},{X}_{j}\}  : i,j \in  \left\lbrack  k\right\rbrack  ,i \neq  j\} ,\omega$ -subw $\left( \mathcal{H}\right)  = \frac{1}{2} \cdot  \lceil \frac{k}{3}\rceil  + \frac{1}{2} \cdot  \lceil \frac{k - 1}{3}\rceil  + \frac{1}{2} \cdot  \lfloor \frac{k}{3}\rfloor  \cdot  \left( {\omega  - 2}\right) .$

引理 C.8. 对于$k$-团超图$\mathcal{H} = \left( {\mathcal{V},\mathcal{E}}\right)$，其中$\mathcal{V} = \left\{  {{X}_{1},{X}_{2},\cdots ,{X}_{k}}\right\}$且$\mathcal{E} = \{ \{ {X}_{i},{X}_{j}\}  : i,j \in  \left\lbrack  k\right\rbrack  ,i \neq  j\} ,\omega$-子权重$\left( \mathcal{H}\right)  = \frac{1}{2} \cdot  \lceil \frac{k}{3}\rceil  + \frac{1}{2} \cdot  \lceil \frac{k - 1}{3}\rceil  + \frac{1}{2} \cdot  \lfloor \frac{k}{3}\rfloor  \cdot  \left( {\omega  - 2}\right) .$

Proof. Direction $\omega  - \operatorname{sub}w\left( \mathcal{H}\right)  \leq  \frac{1}{2} \cdot  \left\lceil  \frac{k}{3}\right\rceil   + \frac{1}{2} \cdot  \left\lceil  \frac{k - 1}{3}\right\rceil   + \frac{1}{2} \cdot  \left\lfloor  \frac{k}{3}\right\rfloor   \cdot  \left( {\omega  - 2}\right)$ . Let $i = \left\lfloor  \frac{k}{3}\right\rfloor$ and $j = \left\lceil  \frac{k - 1}{3}\right\rceil$ . Let $\mathbf{X} = \left\{  {{X}_{1},\cdots ,{X}_{i}}\right\}  ,Y = \left\{  {{X}_{i + 1},\cdots ,{X}_{i + j}}\right\}$ and $Z = \left\{  {{X}_{i + j + 1},\cdots ,{X}_{k}}\right\}$ . we consider a generalized elimination ordering $\overline{\mathbf{\sigma }} = \left( {\mathbf{X},\mathbf{Y},\mathbf{Z}}\right)$ . Implied by Proposition C. $2,h\left( \mathbf{X}\right)  \leq  \frac{1}{2} \cdot  \left\lfloor  \frac{k}{3}\right\rfloor  ,h\left( \mathbf{Y}\right)  \leq  \frac{1}{2} \cdot  \left\lceil  \frac{k - 1}{3}\right\rceil$ , and $h\left( Z\right)  \leq  \frac{1}{2} \cdot  \left\lceil  \frac{k}{3}\right\rceil$ .

证明. 方向$\omega  - \operatorname{sub}w\left( \mathcal{H}\right)  \leq  \frac{1}{2} \cdot  \left\lceil  \frac{k}{3}\right\rceil   + \frac{1}{2} \cdot  \left\lceil  \frac{k - 1}{3}\right\rceil   + \frac{1}{2} \cdot  \left\lfloor  \frac{k}{3}\right\rfloor   \cdot  \left( {\omega  - 2}\right)$. 设$i = \left\lfloor  \frac{k}{3}\right\rfloor$与$j = \left\lceil  \frac{k - 1}{3}\right\rceil$. 令$\mathbf{X} = \left\{  {{X}_{1},\cdots ,{X}_{i}}\right\}  ,Y = \left\{  {{X}_{i + 1},\cdots ,{X}_{i + j}}\right\}$且$Z = \left\{  {{X}_{i + j + 1},\cdots ,{X}_{k}}\right\}$. 我们考虑一个广义消元序$\overline{\mathbf{\sigma }} = \left( {\mathbf{X},\mathbf{Y},\mathbf{Z}}\right)$. 由命题C.$2,h\left( \mathbf{X}\right)  \leq  \frac{1}{2} \cdot  \left\lfloor  \frac{k}{3}\right\rfloor  ,h\left( \mathbf{Y}\right)  \leq  \frac{1}{2} \cdot  \left\lceil  \frac{k - 1}{3}\right\rceil$隐含可得，且$h\left( Z\right)  \leq  \frac{1}{2} \cdot  \left\lceil  \frac{k}{3}\right\rceil$.

$$
{\mathrm{{EMM}}}_{1}^{\bar{\sigma }} \leq  \operatorname{MM}\left( {Y;Z;X}\right)  = h\left( Y\right)  + h\left( Z\right)  + {\gamma h}\left( X\right)  = \frac{1}{2} \cdot  \left\lceil  \frac{k}{3}\right\rceil   + \frac{1}{2} \cdot  \left\lceil  \frac{k - 1}{3}\right\rceil   + \frac{1}{2} \cdot  \left\lfloor  \frac{k}{3}\right\rfloor   \cdot  \left( {\omega  - 2}\right) 
$$

Direction $\omega$ -subw $\left( \mathcal{H}\right)  \geq  \frac{1}{2} \cdot  \left\lceil  \frac{k}{3}\right\rceil   + \frac{1}{2} \cdot  \left\lceil  \frac{k - 1}{3}\right\rceil   + \frac{1}{2} \cdot  \left\lfloor  \frac{k}{3}\right\rfloor   \cdot  \left( {\omega  - 2}\right)$ . We identify a polymatroid $\mathbf{h}$ as follows: let ${X}_{1},{X}_{2},\cdots ,{X}_{k}$ be independently random variables with $h\left( {X}_{i}\right)  = \frac{1}{2}$ for $i \in  \left\lbrack  k\right\rbrack$ . Then,we have $h\left( \mathcal{V}\right)  = \frac{k}{2}$ . From Proposition C. 4,for an arbitrary generalized elimination ordering $\overline{\mathbf{\sigma }} \in  \pi \left( \mathcal{V}\right)$ , ${\mathrm{{EMM}}}_{1}^{\bar{\sigma }} = \frac{1}{2} \cdot  \left\lceil  \frac{k}{3}\right\rceil   + \frac{1}{2} \cdot  \left\lceil  \frac{k - 1}{3}\right\rceil   + \frac{1}{2} \cdot  \left\lfloor  \frac{k}{3}\right\rfloor   \cdot  \left( {\omega  - 2}\right)$ . From (39),we conclude $\omega$ -subw $\left( \mathcal{H}\right)  \geq  \frac{\omega }{2} + 1$ .

方向$\omega$ -子权重$\left( \mathcal{H}\right)  \geq  \frac{1}{2} \cdot  \left\lceil  \frac{k}{3}\right\rceil   + \frac{1}{2} \cdot  \left\lceil  \frac{k - 1}{3}\right\rceil   + \frac{1}{2} \cdot  \left\lfloor  \frac{k}{3}\right\rfloor   \cdot  \left( {\omega  - 2}\right)$。我们按如下方式定义多拟阵$\mathbf{h}$：设${X}_{1},{X}_{2},\cdots ,{X}_{k}$为独立随机变量，其中$h\left( {X}_{i}\right)  = \frac{1}{2}$对于$i \in  \left\lbrack  k\right\rbrack$成立。由此可得$h\left( \mathcal{V}\right)  = \frac{k}{2}$。根据命题C.4，对于任意广义消元序$\overline{\mathbf{\sigma }} \in  \pi \left( \mathcal{V}\right)$，${\mathrm{{EMM}}}_{1}^{\bar{\sigma }} = \frac{1}{2} \cdot  \left\lceil  \frac{k}{3}\right\rceil   + \frac{1}{2} \cdot  \left\lceil  \frac{k - 1}{3}\right\rceil   + \frac{1}{2} \cdot  \left\lfloor  \frac{k}{3}\right\rfloor   \cdot  \left( {\omega  - 2}\right)$成立。由式(39)可推出$\omega$ -子权重$\left( \mathcal{H}\right)  \geq  \frac{\omega }{2} + 1$。

LEMMA C.9. For the following hypergraph representing a $4 -$ cycle $\mathcal{H},\omega  - \operatorname{subw}\left( \mathcal{H}\right)  = 2 - \frac{3}{2 \cdot  \min \left\{  {\omega ,\frac{5}{2}}\right\}   + 1}$ .

\mathcal{H} = \left( {\{ X,Y,Z,W\} ,\;\{ \{ X,Y\} ,\{ Y,Z\} ,\{ Z,W\} ,\{ W,X\} \} }\right)  \tag{41}

Proof. Direction $\omega$ -subw $\left( \mathcal{H}\right)  \leq  2 - \frac{3}{2 \cdot  \min \left\{  {\omega ,\frac{5}{2}}\right\}   + 1}$ . For simplicity,for vertex $X$ ,we denote $h\left( {Y \mid  X}\right)$ , $h\left( {W \mid  X}\right)$ as the neighboring conditional entropy of $X$ . Similar denotations apply to $Y,Z,W$ . We partition all polymatroids $\mathbf{h} \in  \Gamma  \cap  \mathrm{{ED}}$ into the following four cases:

证明。方向$\omega$ -子权重$\left( \mathcal{H}\right)  \leq  2 - \frac{3}{2 \cdot  \min \left\{  {\omega ,\frac{5}{2}}\right\}   + 1}$。为简化表述，对顶点$X$，记$h\left( {Y \mid  X}\right)$和$h\left( {W \mid  X}\right)$为其邻接条件熵。类似记号适用于$Y,Z,W$。我们将所有多拟阵$\mathbf{h} \in  \Gamma  \cap  \mathrm{{ED}}$划分为以下四种情形：

- Case 1: each vertex has some neighboring conditional entropy larger than $\Delta$ . Wlog,assume vertex $X$ has $h\left( {Y \mid  X}\right)  > \Delta$ . As $h\left( {XY}\right)  \leq  1$ ,we have $h\left( X\right)  = h\left( {XY}\right)  - h\left( {Y \mid  X}\right)  \leq  1 - \Delta$ . Similarly,we can show $h\left( Y\right) ,h\left( Z\right) ,h\left( W\right)  < 1 - \Delta$ . Consider a generalized elimination ordering $\bar{\sigma } = \left( {\{ X\} ,\{ Z\} ,\{ Y\} ,\{ W\} }\right)$ . By choosing $\mathcal{A} = \{ \{ X,Y\} \}$ and $\mathcal{B} = \{ \{ X,W\} \}$ ,we have ${\mathrm{{EMM}}}_{1}^{\bar{\sigma }} \leq  \mathrm{{MM}}\left( {Y;W;X}\right)  \leq  \omega \left( {1 - \Delta }\right)$ . Meanwhile, $h\left( {U}_{1}^{\bar{\sigma }}\right)  = h\left( {XYW}\right)  \leq  h\left( {XY}\right)  + h\left( W\right)  \leq  2 - \Delta$ . By choosing $\mathcal{A} = \{ \{ Z,Y\} \}$ and $\mathcal{B} = \{ \{ Z,W\} \}$ ,we have ${\operatorname{EMM}}_{2}^{\bar{\sigma }} \leq  \mathrm{{MM}}\left( {Y;W;Z}\right)  \leq  \omega (1 -$ $\Delta )$ . Meanwhile, $h\left( {U}_{2}^{\bar{\sigma }}\right)  = h\left( {YZW}\right)  \leq  h\left( {YZ}\right)  + h\left( W\right)  \leq  2 - \Delta$ . As ${U}_{3}^{\bar{\sigma }},{U}_{4}^{\bar{\sigma }} \subseteq  {U}_{1}^{\bar{\sigma }}$ ,we have $\mathop{\max }\limits_{{i \in  \left\lbrack  \left| \bar{\sigma }\right| \right\rbrack  }}\min \left( {h\left( {U}_{i}^{\bar{\sigma }}\right) ,{\mathrm{{EMM}}}_{i}^{\bar{\sigma }}}\right)  \leq  \min \{ \omega \left( {1 - \Delta }\right) ,2 - \Delta \} .$

- 情形1：每个顶点都存在邻域条件熵大于$\Delta$的情况。不失一般性，假设顶点$X$具有$h\left( {Y \mid  X}\right)  > \Delta$。由于$h\left( {XY}\right)  \leq  1$，我们得到$h\left( X\right)  = h\left( {XY}\right)  - h\left( {Y \mid  X}\right)  \leq  1 - \Delta$。同理可证$h\left( Y\right) ,h\left( Z\right) ,h\left( W\right)  < 1 - \Delta$。考虑广义消元序$\bar{\sigma } = \left( {\{ X\} ,\{ Z\} ,\{ Y\} ,\{ W\} }\right)$，通过选取$\mathcal{A} = \{ \{ X,Y\} \}$和$\mathcal{B} = \{ \{ X,W\} \}$，可得${\mathrm{{EMM}}}_{1}^{\bar{\sigma }} \leq  \mathrm{{MM}}\left( {Y;W;X}\right)  \leq  \omega \left( {1 - \Delta }\right)$。同时满足$h\left( {U}_{1}^{\bar{\sigma }}\right)  = h\left( {XYW}\right)  \leq  h\left( {XY}\right)  + h\left( W\right)  \leq  2 - \Delta$。再选择$\mathcal{A} = \{ \{ Z,Y\} \}$与$\mathcal{B} = \{ \{ Z,W\} \}$时，有${\operatorname{EMM}}_{2}^{\bar{\sigma }} \leq  \mathrm{{MM}}\left( {Y;W;Z}\right)  \leq  \omega (1 -$$\Delta )$。此外$h\left( {U}_{2}^{\bar{\sigma }}\right)  = h\left( {YZW}\right)  \leq  h\left( {YZ}\right)  + h\left( W\right)  \leq  2 - \Delta$。鉴于${U}_{3}^{\bar{\sigma }},{U}_{4}^{\bar{\sigma }} \subseteq  {U}_{1}^{\bar{\sigma }}$，最终推导出$\mathop{\max }\limits_{{i \in  \left\lbrack  \left| \bar{\sigma }\right| \right\rbrack  }}\min \left( {h\left( {U}_{i}^{\bar{\sigma }}\right) ,{\mathrm{{EMM}}}_{i}^{\bar{\sigma }}}\right)  \leq  \min \{ \omega \left( {1 - \Delta }\right) ,2 - \Delta \} .$

- Case 2: one vertex has all neighboring conditional entropies smaller than $\Delta$ ,and the other three vertices have some neighboring conditional entropies larger than $\Delta$ . Wlog,assume $Z$ is the vertex with $h\left( {Y \mid  Z}\right) ,h\left( {W \mid  Z}\right)  \leq  \Delta$ . Similar to Case 1,we can have $h\left( X\right) ,h\left( Y\right) ,h\left( W\right)  < 1 - \Delta$ . Consider a generalized elimination ordering $\overline{\mathbf{\sigma }} = \left( {\{ X\} ,\{ Z\} ,\{ Y\} ,\{ W\} }\right)$ . By choosing $\mathcal{A} = \{ \{ X,Y\} \}$ and $\mathcal{B} = \{ \{ X,W\} \}$ ,we have ${\mathrm{{EMM}}}_{1}^{\bar{\sigma }} \leq  \mathrm{{MM}}\left( {Y;W;X}\right)  \leq  \omega \left( {1 - \Delta }\right)$ . Meanwhile, $h\left( {U}_{1}^{\bar{\sigma }}\right)  = h\left( {XYW}\right)  \leq$

- 情形2：某一顶点的所有邻接条件熵均小于$\Delta$，而其余三个顶点存在邻接条件熵大于$\Delta$的情况。不失一般性，设$Z$为满足$h\left( {Y \mid  Z}\right) ,h\left( {W \mid  Z}\right)  \leq  \Delta$的顶点。类比情形1，可得$h\left( X\right) ,h\left( Y\right) ,h\left( W\right)  < 1 - \Delta$。考虑广义消元序$\overline{\mathbf{\sigma }} = \left( {\{ X\} ,\{ Z\} ,\{ Y\} ,\{ W\} }\right)$，通过选取$\mathcal{A} = \{ \{ X,Y\} \}$和$\mathcal{B} = \{ \{ X,W\} \}$，可证${\mathrm{{EMM}}}_{1}^{\bar{\sigma }} \leq  \mathrm{{MM}}\left( {Y;W;X}\right)  \leq  \omega \left( {1 - \Delta }\right)$。同时有$h\left( {U}_{1}^{\bar{\sigma }}\right)  = h\left( {XYW}\right)  \leq$

$h\left( {XY}\right)  + h\left( W\right)  \leq  2 - \Delta$ . We have $h\left( {U}_{2}^{\bar{\sigma }}\right)  = h\left( {YZW}\right)  \leq  h\left( {Y \mid  {ZW}}\right)  + h\left( {ZW}\right)  \leq  h\left( {Y \mid  Z}\right)  + h\left( {ZW}\right)  \leq$ $1 + \Delta$ . As ${U}_{3}^{\bar{\sigma }},{U}_{4}^{\bar{\sigma }} \subseteq  {U}_{1}^{\bar{\sigma }}$ ,we have:

$h\left( {XY}\right)  + h\left( W\right)  \leq  2 - \Delta$。由$h\left( {U}_{2}^{\bar{\sigma }}\right)  = h\left( {YZW}\right)  \leq  h\left( {Y \mid  {ZW}}\right)  + h\left( {ZW}\right)  \leq  h\left( {Y \mid  Z}\right)  + h\left( {ZW}\right)  \leq$$1 + \Delta$可得。因${U}_{3}^{\bar{\sigma }},{U}_{4}^{\bar{\sigma }} \subseteq  {U}_{1}^{\bar{\sigma }}$，故有：

$$
\mathop{\max }\limits_{{i \in  \left\lbrack  \left| \bar{\sigma }\right| \right\rbrack  }}\min \left( {h\left( {U}_{i}^{\bar{\sigma }}\right) ,{\mathrm{{EMM}}}_{i}^{\bar{\sigma }}}\right)  \leq  \max \{ \min \{ \omega \left( {1 - \Delta }\right) ,2 - \Delta \} ,1 + \Delta \} .
$$

- Case 3: two non-tangent vertices have all their neighboring conditional entropies smaller than $\Delta$ . Suppose $X,Z$ are two non-tangent vertices with all their neighboring conditional entropies smaller than $\Delta$ . Consider a generalized elimination ordering $\overline{\mathbf{\sigma }} = \left( {\{ X\} ,\{ Z\} ,\{ Y\} ,\{ W\} }\right)$ . We have $h\left( {U}_{1}^{\bar{\sigma }}\right)  = h\left( {XYW}\right)  \leq  h\left( {W \mid  {XY}}\right)  + h\left( {XY}\right)  \leq  h\left( {W \mid  X}\right)  + h\left( {XY}\right)  \leq  1 + \Delta$ . Similarly, $h\left( {U}_{2}^{\bar{\sigma }}\right)  = h\left( {ZYW}\right)  \leq  h\left( {W \mid  {ZY}}\right)  + h\left( {ZY}\right)  \leq  h\left( {W \mid  Z}\right)  + h\left( {ZY}\right)  \leq  1 + \Delta$ . As ${U}_{3}^{\bar{\sigma }},{U}_{4}^{\bar{\sigma }} \subseteq  {U}_{1}^{\bar{\sigma }}$ ,we have $\mathop{\max }\limits_{{i \in  \left\lbrack  \left| \bar{\sigma }\right| \right\rbrack  }}\min \left( {h\left( {U}_{i}^{\bar{\sigma }}\right) ,{\mathrm{{EMM}}}_{i}^{\bar{\sigma }}}\right)  \leq  1 + \Delta .$

- 情形3：两个非切点顶点的所有邻接条件熵均小于$\Delta$。假设$X,Z$为两个所有邻接条件熵均小于$\Delta$的非切点顶点。考虑广义消元序$\overline{\mathbf{\sigma }} = \left( {\{ X\} ,\{ Z\} ,\{ Y\} ,\{ W\} }\right)$，可得$h\left( {U}_{1}^{\bar{\sigma }}\right)  = h\left( {XYW}\right)  \leq  h\left( {W \mid  {XY}}\right)  + h\left( {XY}\right)  \leq  h\left( {W \mid  X}\right)  + h\left( {XY}\right)  \leq  1 + \Delta$。同理有$h\left( {U}_{2}^{\bar{\sigma }}\right)  = h\left( {ZYW}\right)  \leq  h\left( {W \mid  {ZY}}\right)  + h\left( {ZY}\right)  \leq  h\left( {W \mid  Z}\right)  + h\left( {ZY}\right)  \leq  1 + \Delta$。由于${U}_{3}^{\bar{\sigma }},{U}_{4}^{\bar{\sigma }} \subseteq  {U}_{1}^{\bar{\sigma }}$，故得$\mathop{\max }\limits_{{i \in  \left\lbrack  \left| \bar{\sigma }\right| \right\rbrack  }}\min \left( {h\left( {U}_{i}^{\bar{\sigma }}\right) ,{\mathrm{{EMM}}}_{i}^{\bar{\sigma }}}\right)  \leq  1 + \Delta .$

- Case 4: two tangent vertices have all their neighboring conditional entropies smaller than $\Delta$ ,and the other two have some neighboring conditional entropy larger than $\Delta$ . Wlog,assume $Z,W$ are two tangent vertices with all their neighboring conditional entropies smaller than $\Delta$ . Similar as above,we have $h\left( X\right) ,h\left( Y\right)  \leq  1 - \Delta$ . We have two different observations:

- 情形4：两个相切顶点的所有邻接条件熵均小于$\Delta$，而另外两个顶点存在邻接条件熵大于$\Delta$的情况。不失一般性，假设$Z,W$为所有邻接条件熵均小于$\Delta$的两个相切顶点。同理可得$h\left( X\right) ,h\left( Y\right)  \leq  1 - \Delta$。我们得到两种不同的观测结果：

- Consider a generalized elimination ordering $\overline{\mathbf{\sigma }} = \left( {\{ Y\} ,\{ W\} ,\{ X\} ,\{ Z\} }\right)$ . We have $h\left( {U}_{1}^{\overline{\mathbf{\sigma }}}\right)  =$ $h\left( {XYZ}\right)  \leq  h\left( X\right)  + h\left( {YZ}\right)  \leq  2 - \Delta$ and $h\left( {U}_{2}^{\bar{\sigma }}\right)  = h\left( {XZW}\right)  \leq  h\left( X\right)  + h\left( {ZW}\right)  \leq  2 - \Delta$ . As ${U}_{3}^{\bar{\sigma }},{U}_{4}^{\bar{\sigma }} \subseteq  {U}_{1}^{\bar{\sigma }}$ ,we have $\mathop{\max }\limits_{{i \in  \left\lbrack  \left| \bar{\sigma }\right| \right\rbrack  }}\min \left( {h\left( {U}_{i}^{\bar{\sigma }}\right) ,{\mathrm{{EMM}}}_{i}^{\bar{\sigma }}}\right)  \leq  2 - \Delta$ .

- 考虑广义消元序$\overline{\mathbf{\sigma }} = \left( {\{ Y\} ,\{ W\} ,\{ X\} ,\{ Z\} }\right)$。根据$h\left( {U}_{1}^{\overline{\mathbf{\sigma }}}\right)  =$$h\left( {XYZ}\right)  \leq  h\left( X\right)  + h\left( {YZ}\right)  \leq  2 - \Delta$和$h\left( {U}_{2}^{\bar{\sigma }}\right)  = h\left( {XZW}\right)  \leq  h\left( X\right)  + h\left( {ZW}\right)  \leq  2 - \Delta$，当${U}_{3}^{\bar{\sigma }},{U}_{4}^{\bar{\sigma }} \subseteq  {U}_{1}^{\bar{\sigma }}$时，可得$\mathop{\max }\limits_{{i \in  \left\lbrack  \left| \bar{\sigma }\right| \right\rbrack  }}\min \left( {h\left( {U}_{i}^{\bar{\sigma }}\right) ,{\mathrm{{EMM}}}_{i}^{\bar{\sigma }}}\right)  \leq  2 - \Delta$。

* 若$h\left( {Y \mid  Z}\right)  \leq  \frac{\Delta }{2}$且$h\left( {X \mid  W}\right)  \leq  \frac{\Delta }{2}$，则有$h\left( {XYZW}\right)  \leq  h\left( {X \mid  {YZW}}\right)  + h\left( {Y \mid  {ZW}}\right)  + h\left( {ZW}\right)  \leq$$h\left( {X \mid  W}\right)  + h\left( {Y \mid  Z}\right)  + h\left( {ZW}\right)  \leq  1 + \Delta .$

* Otherwise,either $h\left( {Y \mid  Z}\right)  > \frac{\Delta }{2}$ or $h\left( {X \mid  W}\right)  > \frac{\Delta }{2}$ . Wlog,assume $h\left( {Y \mid  Z}\right)  > \frac{\Delta }{2}$ . Similar as above,we have $h\left( Z\right)  \geq  1 - \frac{\Delta }{2}$ . Consider a generalized elimination ordering $\overline{\mathbf{\sigma }} =$ $\left( {\{ Y\} ,\{ W\} ,\{ Z\} ,\{ X\} }\right)$ . As ${U}_{3}^{\bar{\sigma }},{U}_{4}^{\bar{\sigma }} \subseteq  {U}_{1}^{\bar{\sigma }}$ ,we have:

* 否则，要么$h\left( {Y \mid  Z}\right)  > \frac{\Delta }{2}$，要么$h\left( {X \mid  W}\right)  > \frac{\Delta }{2}$。不失一般性，假设$h\left( {Y \mid  Z}\right)  > \frac{\Delta }{2}$。与上文类似，我们得到$h\left( Z\right)  \geq  1 - \frac{\Delta }{2}$。考虑一个广义消元序$\overline{\mathbf{\sigma }} =$$\left( {\{ Y\} ,\{ W\} ,\{ Z\} ,\{ X\} }\right)$。通过选择$\mathcal{A} = \{ \{ X,Y\} \}$和$\mathcal{B} = \left\{  {\{ Y,Z\} }\right\}$，可得${\operatorname{EMM}}_{1}^{\bar{\sigma }} \leq$$\operatorname{MM}\left( {X;Z;Y \mid  \varnothing }\right)  = \left( {\omega  - 1}\right) \left( {1 - \Delta }\right)  + \left( {1 - \frac{\Delta }{2}}\right)$且$h\left( {U}_{1}^{\bar{\sigma }}\right)  = h\left( {XYZ}\right)  \leq  h\left( X\right)  + h\left( {YZ}\right)  \leq  2 - \Delta .$此外，$h\left( {U}_{2}^{\bar{\sigma }}\right)  = h\left( {ZWX}\right)  \leq  h\left( {Z \mid  {WX}}\right)  + h\left( {WX}\right)  \leq  h\left( {Z \mid  W}\right)  + h\left( {WX}\right)  \leq  1 + \Delta$。由于${U}_{3}^{\bar{\sigma }},{U}_{4}^{\bar{\sigma }} \subseteq  {U}_{1}^{\bar{\sigma }}$，我们有：

$$
\mathop{\max }\limits_{{i \in  \left\lbrack  \left| \bar{\sigma }\right| \right\rbrack  }}\min \left( {h\left( {U}_{i}^{\bar{\sigma }}\right) ,{\mathrm{{EMM}}}_{i}^{\bar{\sigma }}}\right)  \leq  \max \left\{  {1 + \Delta ,\min \left\{  {\left( {\omega  - 1}\right) \left( {1 - \Delta }\right)  + \left( {1 - \frac{\Delta }{2}}\right) ,2 - \Delta }\right\}  }\right\}  .
$$

Putting all cases together, we obtain the upper bound:

综合所有情况，得到上界：

$$
\max \left\{  {1 + \Delta ,\min \{ \left( {\omega  - 1}\right) \left( {1 - \Delta }\right)  + 1 - \frac{\Delta }{2},2 - \Delta \} }\right\}  
$$

If $\frac{5}{2} \leq  \omega  \leq  3$ ,we set $\Delta  = \frac{1}{2}$ to obtain the upper bound as $\frac{3}{2}$ . If $2 \leq  \omega  \leq  \frac{5}{2}$ ,we set $\Delta  = \frac{2\left( {\omega  - 1}\right) }{{2\omega } + 1}$ to obtain the upper bound as $\frac{{4\omega } - 1}{{2\omega } + 1}$ . Putting two cases together,we obtain the desired upper bound.

若$\frac{5}{2} \leq  \omega  \leq  3$，设$\Delta  = \frac{1}{2}$以获得上界$\frac{3}{2}$；若$2 \leq  \omega  \leq  \frac{5}{2}$，设$\Delta  = \frac{2\left( {\omega  - 1}\right) }{{2\omega } + 1}$以获得上界$\frac{{4\omega } - 1}{{2\omega } + 1}$。合并两种情况，即得所需上界。

The case with $\overline{\mathbf{\sigma }}\left\lbrack  1\right\rbrack   = \{ Y\}$ is the same.

- $\bar{\sigma }\left\lbrack  1\right\rbrack   = \{ X\} .h\left( {U}_{1}^{\bar{\sigma }}\right)  = h\left( {XYW}\right)  = \frac{{4\omega } - 1}{{2\omega } + 1}$ 和 ${\mathrm{{EMM}}}_{1}^{\bar{\sigma }} = \mathrm{{MM}}\left( {Y,W,X}\right)  = \frac{\omega  + 2}{{2\omega } + 1} + \frac{3\left( {\omega  - 1}\right) }{{2\omega } + 1} = \frac{{4\omega } - 1}{{2\omega } + 1}$ 。含 $\overline{\mathbf{\sigma }}\left\lbrack  1\right\rbrack   = \{ Y\}$ 的情况同理。

- $\overline{\sigma }\left\lbrack  1\right\rbrack   = \{ W\} .h\left( {U}_{1}^{\overline{\sigma }}\right)  = h\left( {XZW}\right)  = \frac{3\omega }{{2\omega } + 1}$ and ${\mathrm{{EMM}}}_{1}^{\overline{\sigma }} = \mathrm{{MM}}\left( {X,Z,W}\right)  = \frac{2\left( {\omega  + 2}\right) }{{2\omega } + 1} + \frac{3\left( {\omega  - 2}\right) }{{2\omega } + 1} = \frac{{5\omega } - 2}{{2\omega } + 1} >$ $\frac{{4\omega } - 1}{{2\omega } + 1}$ . The case with $\overline{\mathbf{\sigma }}\left\lbrack  1\right\rbrack   = \{ Z\}$ is the same. - $\left| {\bar{\sigma }\left\lbrack  1\right\rbrack  }\right|  = 2.h\left( {U}_{1}^{\bar{\sigma }}\right)  = h\left( \mathcal{V}\right)  = \frac{{4\omega } - 1}{{2\omega } + 1}$ and ${\mathrm{{EMM}}}_{1}^{\bar{\sigma }} \geq  \mathrm{{MM}}\left( {Y,W,X}\right)  = \frac{{4\omega } - 1}{{2\omega } + 1}$ .

- $\overline{\sigma }\left\lbrack  1\right\rbrack   = \{ W\} .h\left( {U}_{1}^{\overline{\sigma }}\right)  = h\left( {XZW}\right)  = \frac{3\omega }{{2\omega } + 1}$ 和 ${\mathrm{{EMM}}}_{1}^{\overline{\sigma }} = \mathrm{{MM}}\left( {X,Z,W}\right)  = \frac{2\left( {\omega  + 2}\right) }{{2\omega } + 1} + \frac{3\left( {\omega  - 2}\right) }{{2\omega } + 1} = \frac{{5\omega } - 2}{{2\omega } + 1} >$ $\frac{{4\omega } - 1}{{2\omega } + 1}$ 。含 $\overline{\mathbf{\sigma }}\left\lbrack  1\right\rbrack   = \{ Z\}$ 的情况同理。- $\left| {\bar{\sigma }\left\lbrack  1\right\rbrack  }\right|  = 2.h\left( {U}_{1}^{\bar{\sigma }}\right)  = h\left( \mathcal{V}\right)  = \frac{{4\omega } - 1}{{2\omega } + 1}$ 和 ${\mathrm{{EMM}}}_{1}^{\bar{\sigma }} \geq  \mathrm{{MM}}\left( {Y,W,X}\right)  = \frac{{4\omega } - 1}{{2\omega } + 1}$ 。

[10] defines an exponent ${\bar{c}}_{k}$ for detecting a $k$ -cycle in a graph,and the definition is based on rectangular matrix multiplication. We define below an upper bound, ${c}_{k}^{ \sqcup  }$ ,on ${\bar{c}}_{k}$ that uses only square matrix multiplication and show that $\omega$ -subw $\left( \mathcal{H}\right)  \leq  {c}_{k}^{\square }$ for any $k$ -cycle graph $\mathcal{H}$ . In particular, ${\bar{c}}_{k} \leq  {c}_{k}^{\square }$ and this becomes an equality when $\omega  = 2$ . Moreover,[10] shows that ${\bar{c}}_{k} = {c}_{k}^{\square }$ when $k$ is odd as well as $k = 4$ or 6 . More details can be found in [10].

[10]定义了一个指数 ${\bar{c}}_{k}$ 用于检测图中的 $k$ -环，该定义基于矩形矩阵乘法。下文我们将定义一个仅使用方阵乘法的上界 ${c}_{k}^{ \sqcup  }$ ，并证明对于任何 $k$ -环图 $\mathcal{H}$ 都有 $\omega$ -子波 $\left( \mathcal{H}\right)  \leq  {c}_{k}^{\square }$ 。特别地，${\bar{c}}_{k} \leq  {c}_{k}^{\square }$ 且当 $\omega  = 2$ 时取等号。此外，[10]证明了当 $k$ 为奇数及 $k = 4$ 或6时 ${\bar{c}}_{k} = {c}_{k}^{\square }$ 。更多细节详见[10]。

First,we recall the definition of ${\bar{c}}_{k}$ from [10]. Recall from Table 1 that $\bar{\omega }\left( {a,b,c}\right)$ is the smallest exponent for multiplying two rectangular matrices of sizes ${n}^{a} \cdot  {n}^{b}$ and ${n}^{b} \cdot  {n}^{c}$ within $O\left( {n}^{\bar{\omega }\left( {a,b,c}\right) }\right)$ time,whereas ${\omega }^{\square }\left( {a,b,c}\right)$ is the smallest upper bound on $\bar{\omega }\left( {a,b,c}\right)$ that is obtained through square matrix multiplication,i.e. ${\omega }^{\square }\left( {a,b,c}\right) \overset{\text{ def }}{ = }\max \{ a + b + \left( {\omega  - 2}\right) c,a + \left( {\omega  - 2}\right) b + c,\left( {\omega  - 2}\right) a + b + c\}$ . In particular, $\bar{\omega }\left( {a,b,c}\right)  \leq  {\omega }^{\square }\left( {a,b,c}\right)$ ,and this becomes an equality when $\omega  = 2$ or when $a = b = c$ . Given a graph of size $N$ ,let $\mathbf{D} = {\left\{  0,{\log }_{N}2,2{\log }_{N}2,\cdots ,1\right\}  }^{2k}$ . For each vector $\overrightarrow{d},\left\lbrack  {10}\right\rbrack$ defines a function ${\bar{P}}_{i,j}^{\overrightarrow{d}}$ (for $i < j$ ) in a recursive way as follows:

首先，我们回顾文献[10]中关于${\bar{c}}_{k}$的定义。由表1可知，$\bar{\omega }\left( {a,b,c}\right)$表示在$O\left( {n}^{\bar{\omega }\left( {a,b,c}\right) }\right)$时间内对尺寸为${n}^{a} \cdot  {n}^{b}$与${n}^{b} \cdot  {n}^{c}$的矩形矩阵进行乘法运算的最小指数，而${\omega }^{\square }\left( {a,b,c}\right)$则是通过方阵乘法（即${\omega }^{\square }\left( {a,b,c}\right) \overset{\text{ def }}{ = }\max \{ a + b + \left( {\omega  - 2}\right) c,a + \left( {\omega  - 2}\right) b + c,\left( {\omega  - 2}\right) a + b + c\}$）得到的$\bar{\omega }\left( {a,b,c}\right)$最小上界。特别地，$\bar{\omega }\left( {a,b,c}\right)  \leq  {\omega }^{\square }\left( {a,b,c}\right)$成立，当$\omega  = 2$或$a = b = c$时该式取等号。给定规模为$N$的图，设$\mathbf{D} = {\left\{  0,{\log }_{N}2,2{\log }_{N}2,\cdots ,1\right\}  }^{2k}$。对于每个向量$\overrightarrow{d},\left\lbrack  {10}\right\rbrack$，递归地定义函数${\bar{P}}_{i,j}^{\overrightarrow{d}}$（适用于$i < j$）如下：

$$
{\bar{P}}_{i,j}^{\overrightarrow{d}} = \min \left\{  {{\bar{P}}_{i,j - 1}^{\overrightarrow{d}} + {d}_{j - 1}^{ + },{\bar{P}}_{i + 1,j}^{\overrightarrow{d}} + {d}_{i + 1}^{ - },\mathop{\min }\limits_{{i < r < j}}\left\{  {{\bar{P}}_{i,r}^{\overrightarrow{d}},{\bar{P}}_{r,j}^{\overrightarrow{d}},\bar{\omega }\left( {1 - {d}_{i},1 - {d}_{r},1 - {d}_{j}}\right) }\right\}  }\right\}   \tag{42}
$$

with ${\bar{P}}_{i,i + 1}^{\overrightarrow{d}} = 1$ . Finally,[10] defines ${\bar{c}}_{k}$ as follow:

其中${\bar{P}}_{i,i + 1}^{\overrightarrow{d}} = 1$。最终，文献[10]将${\bar{c}}_{k}$定义如下：

$$
{\bar{c}}_{k} = \mathop{\max }\limits_{{\overrightarrow{d} = \left( {{d}_{1}^{ - },{d}_{1}^{ + },{d}_{2}^{ - },{d}_{2}^{ + },\cdots ,{d}_{k}^{ - },{d}_{k}^{ + }}\right)  \in  \mathrm{D}}}\min \left\{  {\mathop{\min }\limits_{{i \in  \left\lbrack  k\right\rbrack  }}2 - {d}_{i},\mathop{\min }\limits_{{i,j \in  \left\lbrack  k\right\rbrack   : i < j}}\max \left\{  {{\bar{P}}_{i,j}^{\overrightarrow{d}},{\bar{P}}_{j,i}^{\overrightarrow{d}}}\right\}  }\right\}   \tag{43}
$$

In contrast,by replacing $\bar{\omega }\left( {1 - {d}_{i},1 - {d}_{r},1 - {d}_{j}}\right)$ with ${\omega }^{\square }\left( {1 - {d}_{i},1 - {d}_{r},1 - {d}_{j}}\right)$ in Eq. (42),we obtain our variants ${P}_{i,j}^{\overrightarrow{d}}$ and ${c}_{k}^{\square }$ defined below (with ${P}_{i,i + 1}^{\overrightarrow{d}} = 1$ ):

相比之下，将式(42)中的$\bar{\omega }\left( {1 - {d}_{i},1 - {d}_{r},1 - {d}_{j}}\right)$替换为${\omega }^{\square }\left( {1 - {d}_{i},1 - {d}_{r},1 - {d}_{j}}\right)$后，我们得到如下定义的变体${P}_{i,j}^{\overrightarrow{d}}$与${c}_{k}^{\square }$（其中${P}_{i,i + 1}^{\overrightarrow{d}} = 1$）：

$$
{P}_{i,j}^{\overrightarrow{d}} = \min \left\{  {{P}_{i,j - 1}^{\overrightarrow{d}} + {d}_{j - 1}^{ + },{P}_{i + 1,j}^{\overrightarrow{d}} + {d}_{i + 1}^{ - },\mathop{\min }\limits_{{i < r < j}}\left\{  {{P}_{i,r}^{\overrightarrow{d}},{P}_{r,j}^{\overrightarrow{d}},{\omega }^{\square }\left( {1 - {d}_{i},1 - {d}_{r},1 - {d}_{j}}\right) }\right\}  }\right\}   \tag{44}
$$

$$
{c}_{k}^{\square } = \mathop{\max }\limits_{{\overrightarrow{d} = \left( {{d}_{1}^{ - },{d}_{1}^{ + },{d}_{2}^{ - },{d}_{2}^{ + },\cdots ,{d}_{k}^{ - },{d}_{k}^{ + }}\right)  \in  \mathrm{D}}}\min \left\{  {\mathop{\min }\limits_{{i \in  \left\lbrack  k\right\rbrack  }}2 - {d}_{i},\mathop{\min }\limits_{{i,j \in  \left\lbrack  k\right\rbrack   : i < j}}\max \left\{  {{P}_{i,j}^{\overrightarrow{d}},{P}_{j,i}^{\overrightarrow{d}}}\right\}  }\right\}   \tag{45}
$$

LEMMA C.10. For the following hypergraph representing a $k$ -cycle:

引理C.10. 对于表示$k$循环的以下超图：

$$
\mathcal{H} = \left( {\left\{  {{X}_{1},{X}_{2},\cdots ,{X}_{k}}\right\}  ,\;\left\{  {\left\{  {{X}_{1},{X}_{2}}\right\}  ,\left\{  {{X}_{2},{X}_{3}}\right\}  ,\cdots ,\left\{  {{X}_{k - 1},{X}_{k}}\right\}  ,\left\{  {{X}_{k},{X}_{1}}\right\}  }\right\}  }\right)  \tag{46}
$$

$\omega  - \operatorname{subw}\left( \mathcal{H}\right)  \leq  {c}_{k}^{\square }$ as defined in Eq. (45).

如式(45)所定义的$\omega  - \operatorname{subw}\left( \mathcal{H}\right)  \leq  {c}_{k}^{\square }$。

Proof of Lemma C.10. We partition all polymatroids $\mathbf{h}$ into the following ${\left( 1 + \log N\right) }^{\left| \mathcal{V}\right| }$ cases. For simplicity,let ${X}_{1} = {X}_{k + 1}$ . Each polymatroid $\mathbf{h} \in  \Gamma  \cap  \mathrm{{ED}}$ is associated with a $k$ -tuple vector $\overrightarrow{d} = \left( {{d}_{1}^{ - },{d}_{1}^{ + },{d}_{2}^{ - },{d}_{2}^{ + },\cdots ,{d}_{k}^{ - },{d}_{k}^{ + }}\right)  \in  \mathbf{D}$ such that ${d}_{i}^{ - } \leq  h\left( {{X}_{i - 1} \mid  {X}_{i}}\right)  < {d}_{i}^{ - } + {\log }_{N}2$ and ${d}_{i}^{ + } \leq  h\left( {{X}_{i + 1} \mid  {X}_{i}}\right)  <$ ${d}_{i}^{ + } + {\log }_{N}2$ . We have the following two observations:

引理C.10的证明。我们将所有多拟阵$\mathbf{h}$划分为以下${\left( 1 + \log N\right) }^{\left| \mathcal{V}\right| }$种情形。为简化表述，令${X}_{1} = {X}_{k + 1}$。每个多拟阵$\mathbf{h} \in  \Gamma  \cap  \mathrm{{ED}}$都关联着一个$k$维元组向量$\overrightarrow{d} = \left( {{d}_{1}^{ - },{d}_{1}^{ + },{d}_{2}^{ - },{d}_{2}^{ + },\cdots ,{d}_{k}^{ - },{d}_{k}^{ + }}\right)  \in  \mathbf{D}$，使得${d}_{i}^{ - } \leq  h\left( {{X}_{i - 1} \mid  {X}_{i}}\right)  < {d}_{i}^{ - } + {\log }_{N}2$且${d}_{i}^{ + } \leq  h\left( {{X}_{i + 1} \mid  {X}_{i}}\right)  <$${d}_{i}^{ + } + {\log }_{N}2$。我们得到以下两个观察结果：

- For each $i \in  \left\lbrack  k\right\rbrack$ ,consider a generalized elimination ordering $\overline{\mathbf{\sigma }} = \left( {\left\{  {X}_{i - 1}\right\}  ,\cdots ,\left\{  {X}_{1}\right\}  ,\left\{  {X}_{k}\right\}  ,\cdots ,\left\{  {X}_{i}\right\}  }\right)$ . As $h\left( {{X}_{i - 1} \mid  {X}_{i}}\right)  \geq  {d}_{i}^{ - }$ and $h\left( {{X}_{i - 1}{X}_{i}}\right)  \leq  1$ ,we have $h\left( {X}_{i}\right)  = h\left( {{X}_{i - 1}{X}_{i}}\right)  - h\left( {{X}_{i - 1} \mid  {X}_{i}}\right)  < 1 - {d}_{i}^{ - }$ . Similarly,we have $h\left( {X}_{i}\right)  < 1 - {d}_{i}^{ + }$ . For simplicity,we define ${d}_{i} = \max \left\{  {{d}_{i}^{ - },{d}_{i}^{ + }}\right\}$ . For each $i \in  \left\lbrack  k\right\rbrack$ ,we have $h\left( {{X}_{i}{X}_{j}{X}_{j + 1}}\right)  \leq  h\left( {X}_{i}\right)  + h\left( {{X}_{j}{X}_{j + 1}}\right)  < \min \left\{  {1 - {d}_{i}^{ - },1 - {d}_{i}^{ + }}\right\}   + 1 = 2 - {d}_{i}$ . So, $h\left( {U}_{j}^{\bar{\sigma }}\right)  \leq  2 - {d}_{i}$ for any $j \in  \left\lbrack  \left| \bar{\sigma }\right| \right\rbrack$ ,i.e., $\mathop{\max }\limits_{{j \in  \left\lbrack  \left| \bar{\sigma }\right| \right\rbrack  }}h\left( {U}_{j}^{\bar{\sigma }}\right)  \leq  2 - {d}_{i}$ .

- 对于每个$i \in  \left\lbrack  k\right\rbrack$，考虑广义消元序$\overline{\mathbf{\sigma }} = \left( {\left\{  {X}_{i - 1}\right\}  ,\cdots ,\left\{  {X}_{1}\right\}  ,\left\{  {X}_{k}\right\}  ,\cdots ,\left\{  {X}_{i}\right\}  }\right)$。由于$h\left( {{X}_{i - 1} \mid  {X}_{i}}\right)  \geq  {d}_{i}^{ - }$和$h\left( {{X}_{i - 1}{X}_{i}}\right)  \leq  1$，可得$h\left( {X}_{i}\right)  = h\left( {{X}_{i - 1}{X}_{i}}\right)  - h\left( {{X}_{i - 1} \mid  {X}_{i}}\right)  < 1 - {d}_{i}^{ - }$。同理可得$h\left( {X}_{i}\right)  < 1 - {d}_{i}^{ + }$。为简化表述，我们定义${d}_{i} = \max \left\{  {{d}_{i}^{ - },{d}_{i}^{ + }}\right\}$。对于每个$i \in  \left\lbrack  k\right\rbrack$，都有$h\left( {{X}_{i}{X}_{j}{X}_{j + 1}}\right)  \leq  h\left( {X}_{i}\right)  + h\left( {{X}_{j}{X}_{j + 1}}\right)  < \min \left\{  {1 - {d}_{i}^{ - },1 - {d}_{i}^{ + }}\right\}   + 1 = 2 - {d}_{i}$。因此，对于任意$j \in  \left\lbrack  \left| \bar{\sigma }\right| \right\rbrack$都有$h\left( {U}_{j}^{\bar{\sigma }}\right)  \leq  2 - {d}_{i}$，即$\mathop{\max }\limits_{{j \in  \left\lbrack  \left| \bar{\sigma }\right| \right\rbrack  }}h\left( {U}_{j}^{\bar{\sigma }}\right)  \leq  2 - {d}_{i}$。

- For each pair of distinct values $i,j \in  \left\lbrack  k\right\rbrack$ (whog assume $i < j$ ),we define ${\overline{\mathbf{\sigma }}}_{i,j}^{j}$ as an ordering of $\left\{  {{X}_{i + 1},{X}_{i + 2},\cdots ,{X}_{j - 1}}\right\}$ as follows:

- 对于每对不同的值$i,j \in  \left\lbrack  k\right\rbrack$（假设$i < j$），我们将${\overline{\mathbf{\sigma }}}_{i,j}^{j}$定义为$\left\{  {{X}_{i + 1},{X}_{i + 2},\cdots ,{X}_{j - 1}}\right\}$的如下排序：

$$
{\overline{\mathbf{\sigma }}}_{i,j}^{\overrightarrow{d}} = \begin{cases} \left( {{\overline{\mathbf{\sigma }}}_{i,j - 1}^{\overrightarrow{d}},{X}_{j - 1}}\right) & \text{ if }{P}_{i,j}^{\overrightarrow{d}} = {P}_{i,j - 1}^{\overrightarrow{d}} + {d}_{j - 1}^{ + }, \\  \left( {{\overline{\mathbf{\sigma }}}_{i + 1,j}^{\overrightarrow{d}},{X}_{i + 1}}\right) & \text{ if }{P}_{i,j}^{\overrightarrow{d}} = {P}_{i + 1,j}^{\overrightarrow{d}} + {d}_{i + 1}^{ - }, \\  \left( {{\overline{\mathbf{\sigma }}}_{i,r}^{\overrightarrow{d}},{\overline{\mathbf{\sigma }}}_{r,j}^{\overrightarrow{d}},{X}_{r}}\right) & \text{ if }{P}_{i,j}^{\overrightarrow{d}} = \min \left\{  {{P}_{i,r}^{\overrightarrow{d}},{P}^{\overrightarrow{d}}{f}_{r,j},{\omega }^{ \boxdot  }\left( {1 - {d}_{i},1 - {d}_{r},1 - {d}_{j}}\right) }\right\}  , \end{cases}
$$

and ${\overline{\mathbf{\sigma }}}_{i,i + 1}^{\overrightarrow{d}} = \varnothing$ . At last,we construct a generalized elimination order ${\overline{\mathbf{\sigma }}}^{\overrightarrow{d}} = \left( {{\overline{\mathbf{\sigma }}}_{i,j}^{\overrightarrow{d}},{\overline{\mathbf{\sigma }}}_{j,i}^{\overrightarrow{d}},{X}_{i},{X}_{j}}\right)$ . It can be proved that $\mathop{\max }\limits_{{\ell  \in  \left\lbrack  {\bar{\sigma }}_{i,j}^{\bar{d}}\right\rbrack  }}\min \left\{  {h\left( {U}_{\ell }^{{\bar{\sigma }}_{i,j}^{\bar{d}}}\right) ,{\operatorname{EMM}}_{\ell }^{{\bar{\sigma }}_{i,j}^{\bar{d}}}}\right\}   \leq  {P}_{i,j}^{\bar{d}}$ . In the base case when $j = i + 1$ , $h\left( \varnothing \right)  = 0 \leq  1$ trivially holds. In general,we distinguish three cases:

以及${\overline{\mathbf{\sigma }}}_{i,i + 1}^{\overrightarrow{d}} = \varnothing$。最终，我们构建了一个广义消除序${\overline{\mathbf{\sigma }}}^{\overrightarrow{d}} = \left( {{\overline{\mathbf{\sigma }}}_{i,j}^{\overrightarrow{d}},{\overline{\mathbf{\sigma }}}_{j,i}^{\overrightarrow{d}},{X}_{i},{X}_{j}}\right)$。可以证明$\mathop{\max }\limits_{{\ell  \in  \left\lbrack  {\bar{\sigma }}_{i,j}^{\bar{d}}\right\rbrack  }}\min \left\{  {h\left( {U}_{\ell }^{{\bar{\sigma }}_{i,j}^{\bar{d}}}\right) ,{\operatorname{EMM}}_{\ell }^{{\bar{\sigma }}_{i,j}^{\bar{d}}}}\right\}   \leq  {P}_{i,j}^{\bar{d}}$。在基例中当$j = i + 1$时，$h\left( \varnothing \right)  = 0 \leq  1$显然成立。一般情况下，我们区分三种情形：

$\left. {{d}_{i},1 - {d}_{r},1 - {d}_{j}}\right)$ ,since $h\left( {X}_{i}\right)  \leq  1 - {d}_{i},h\left( {X}_{r}\right)  \leq  1 - {d}_{r}$ and $h\left( {X}_{j}\right)  \leq  1 - {d}_{j}$ . Together,we have:

$\left. {{d}_{i},1 - {d}_{r},1 - {d}_{j}}\right)$ ，由于 $h\left( {X}_{i}\right)  \leq  1 - {d}_{i},h\left( {X}_{r}\right)  \leq  1 - {d}_{r}$ 和 $h\left( {X}_{j}\right)  \leq  1 - {d}_{j}$ 。综上可得：

$$
\mathop{\max }\limits_{{\ell  \in  \left\lbrack  \left| {\overrightarrow{\sigma }}_{i,j}^{\overrightarrow{d}}\right| \right\rbrack  }}\min \left\{  {h\left( {U}_{\ell }^{{\overrightarrow{\sigma }}_{i,j}^{\overrightarrow{d}}}\right) ,{\operatorname{EMM}}_{\ell }^{{\overrightarrow{\sigma }}_{i,j}^{\overrightarrow{d}}}}\right\}   \leq  \min \left\{  {{P}_{i,r}^{\overrightarrow{d}},{P}_{r,j}^{\overrightarrow{d}},{\omega }^{\square }\left( {1 - {d}_{i},1 - {d}_{r},1 - {d}_{j}}\right) }\right\}  
$$

We define a generalized elimination ordering ${\overline{\mathbf{\sigma }}}^{\overrightarrow{d}} = \left( {{\overline{\mathbf{\sigma }}}_{i,j}^{\overrightarrow{d}},{\overline{\mathbf{\sigma }}}_{j,i}^{\overrightarrow{d}},{X}_{i},{X}_{j}}\right)$ . Hence,

我们定义一个广义消元序 ${\overline{\mathbf{\sigma }}}^{\overrightarrow{d}} = \left( {{\overline{\mathbf{\sigma }}}_{i,j}^{\overrightarrow{d}},{\overline{\mathbf{\sigma }}}_{j,i}^{\overrightarrow{d}},{X}_{i},{X}_{j}}\right)$ 。因此，

$$
\mathop{\max }\limits_{{\ell  \in  \left\lbrack  \left| {\bar{\sigma }}^{\overrightarrow{d}}\right| \right\rbrack  }}\min \left\{  {h\left( {U}_{\ell }^{{\bar{\sigma }}^{\overrightarrow{d}}}\right) ,{\mathrm{{EMM}}}_{\ell }^{{\bar{\sigma }}^{\overrightarrow{d}}}}\right\}   \leq  \max \left\{  {{P}_{i,j}^{\overrightarrow{d}},{P}_{j,i}^{\overrightarrow{d}}}\right\}  .
$$

Combining these two cases and applying these arguments to all possible $\overrightarrow{d}$ ,we have

综合这两种情况并将论证应用于所有可能的 $\overrightarrow{d}$ ，可得

$$
\omega \text{-subw}\left( \mathcal{H}\right)  \leq  \mathop{\min }\limits_{{\overrightarrow{d} = \left( {{d}_{1}^{ - },{d}_{1}^{ + },{d}_{2}^{ - },{d}_{2}^{ + },\cdots ,{d}_{k}^{ - },{d}_{k}^{ + }}\right) }}\min \left\{  {\mathop{\min }\limits_{{i \in  \left\lbrack  k\right\rbrack  }}2 - {d}_{i},\mathop{\min }\limits_{{i,j \in  \left\lbrack  k\right\rbrack   : i < j}}\max \left\{  {{P}_{i,j}^{\overrightarrow{d}},{P}_{j,i}^{\overrightarrow{d}}}\right\}  }\right\}   = {c}_{k}^{\square }\text{.}
$$

### C.3 Clustered Hypergraphs

### C.3 聚类超图

Definition C. 11 (Clustered Hypergraph). A hypergraph $\mathcal{H} = \left( {\mathcal{V},\mathcal{E}}\right)$ is clustered if for any pair of vertices ${X}_{i},{X}_{j} \in  \mathcal{V}$ ,there exists some hypergraph $e \in  \mathcal{E}$ such that ${X}_{i},{X}_{j} \in  e$ .

定义 C.11（聚类超图）。若超图 $\mathcal{H} = \left( {\mathcal{V},\mathcal{E}}\right)$ 满足对于任意顶点对 ${X}_{i},{X}_{j} \in  \mathcal{V}$ 都存在某个超图 $e \in  \mathcal{E}$ 使得 ${X}_{i},{X}_{j} \in  e$ ，则称其为聚类超图。

LEMMA C.12. A clustered hypergraph $\mathcal{H} = \left( {\mathcal{V},\mathcal{E}}\right)$ satisfies the following properties:

引理 C.12。聚类超图 $\mathcal{H} = \left( {\mathcal{V},\mathcal{E}}\right)$ 具有以下性质：

- $\operatorname{subw}\left( \mathcal{H}\right)  = {\rho }^{ * }\left( \mathcal{H}\right)$ .

Let $\mathbf{A} = \left\{  {X}_{2}\right\}$ and $\mathbf{B} = \left\{  {X}_{3}\right\}$ . Note that $h\left( \mathbf{A}\right)  \leq  \left( {1 - \Delta }\right)$ and $h\left( \mathbf{B}\right)  \leq  \left( {1 - \Delta }\right)$ . We assume that $\Delta$ is a parameter such that $\Delta  \geq  \left( {1 - \Delta }\right)$ holds,i.e., $\Delta  \geq  \frac{1}{2}$ . Consider a generalized elimination ordering $\overline{\mathbf{\sigma }} = \left( {\{ Y\} ,\mathbf{A},\mathbf{B},\left\{  {X}_{1}\right\}  }\right)$ . We have

情形2：对每个$i \in  \left\lbrack  3\right\rbrack$都有$h\left( {{X}_{i} \mid  Y}\right)  \leq  \frac{\Delta }{2}$。此时，$h\left( \mathcal{V}\right)  \leq  h\left( {{X}_{1} \mid  Y}\right)  + h\left( {{X}_{2} \mid  Y}\right)  + h\left( {{X}_{3}Y}\right)  \leq  \Delta  + 1$。情形3：上述情形均不满足，即对每个$i \in  \left\lbrack  3\right\rbrack$都有$h\left( {Y \mid  {X}_{i}}\right)  > \Delta$。由于$h\left( {{X}_{i}Y}\right)  \leq  1$且$h\left( {Y \mid  {X}_{i}}\right)  > \Delta$，可得$h\left( {X}_{i}\right)  = h\left( {{X}_{i}Y}\right)  - h\left( {Y \mid  {X}_{i}}\right)  \leq  1 - \Delta$。又因$h\left( {{X}_{i}Y}\right)  \leq  1$与$h\left( {{X}_{i} \mid  Y}\right)  > \frac{\Delta }{k - 1}$，故有$h\left( Y\right)  =$$h\left( {{X}_{i}Y}\right)  - h\left( {{X}_{i} \mid  Y}\right)  \leq  1 - \frac{\Delta }{2}$。设$\mathbf{A} = \left\{  {X}_{2}\right\}$且$\mathbf{B} = \left\{  {X}_{3}\right\}$。注意到$h\left( \mathbf{A}\right)  \leq  \left( {1 - \Delta }\right)$且$h\left( \mathbf{B}\right)  \leq  \left( {1 - \Delta }\right)$。我们假设$\Delta$是满足$\Delta  \geq  \left( {1 - \Delta }\right)$的参数，即$\Delta  \geq  \frac{1}{2}$。考虑广义消元序$\overline{\mathbf{\sigma }} = \left( {\{ Y\} ,\mathbf{A},\mathbf{B},\left\{  {X}_{1}\right\}  }\right)$，可得

$$
{\mathrm{{EMM}}}_{1}^{\bar{\sigma }} \leq  \operatorname{MM}\left( {\mathbf{A};\mathbf{B};Y \mid  {X}_{1}}\right)  \leq  {\gamma h}\left( \mathbf{A}\right)  + h\left( \mathbf{B}\right)  + h\left( {{X}_{1}Y}\right)  \leq  \gamma \left( {1 - \Delta }\right)  + \left( {1 - \Delta }\right)  + 1

LEMMA C.14. For the following hypergraph representing a k-pyramid:

引理 C.14。对于表示k-金字塔的以下超图：

$$
\mathcal{H} = \left( {\left\{  {Y,{X}_{1},{X}_{2},\cdots ,{X}_{k}}\right\}  ,\left\{  {\left\{  {Y,{X}_{1}}\right\}  ,\left\{  {Y,{X}_{2}}\right\}  ,\cdots ,\left\{  {Y,{X}_{k}}\right\}  ,\left\{  {{X}_{1},{X}_{2},\cdots ,{X}_{k}}\right\}  }\right\}  }\right)  \tag{48}

for $k \geq  3,\omega  - \operatorname{sub}w\left( \mathcal{H}\right)  \leq  2 - \frac{2}{\omega  \cdot  \left( {k - 1}\right)  - k + 3}$ .

Proof of Lemma C.14. We partition all polymatroids $\mathbf{h}$ into the following two cases:

引理C.14的证明。我们将所有多拟阵$\mathbf{h}$划分为以下两种情况：

- Case 1: there exists some $i \in  \left\lbrack  k\right\rbrack$ such that $h\left( {Y \mid  {X}_{i}}\right)  \leq  \Delta$ . In this case, $h\left( \mathcal{V}\right)  \leq  h\left( {Y \mid  {X}_{1}{X}_{2}\cdots {X}_{k}}\right)  +$ $h\left( {{X}_{1}{X}_{2}\cdots {X}_{k}}\right)  \leq  h\left( {Y \mid  {X}_{i}}\right)  + h\left( {{X}_{1}{X}_{2}\cdots {X}_{k}}\right)  \leq  \Delta  + 1;$

- 情况1：存在某个$i \in  \left\lbrack  k\right\rbrack$使得$h\left( {Y \mid  {X}_{i}}\right)  \leq  \Delta$。此时，$h\left( \mathcal{V}\right)  \leq  h\left( {Y \mid  {X}_{1}{X}_{2}\cdots {X}_{k}}\right)  +$$h\left( {{X}_{1}{X}_{2}\cdots {X}_{k}}\right)  \leq  h\left( {Y \mid  {X}_{i}}\right)  + h\left( {{X}_{1}{X}_{2}\cdots {X}_{k}}\right)  \leq  \Delta  + 1;$

- Case 2: $h\left( {{X}_{i} \mid  Y}\right)  \leq  \frac{\Delta }{k - 1}$ for each $i \in  \left\lbrack  k\right\rbrack$ . In this case, $h\left( \mathcal{V}\right)  \leq  \mathop{\sum }\limits_{{i \in  \left\lbrack  {k - 1}\right\rbrack  }}h\left( {{X}_{1} \mid  Y}\right)  + h\left( {{X}_{k}Y}\right)  \leq  \Delta  + 1$ .

- 情况2：对每个$i \in  \left\lbrack  k\right\rbrack$都有$h\left( {{X}_{i} \mid  Y}\right)  \leq  \frac{\Delta }{k - 1}$。此时，$h\left( \mathcal{V}\right)  \leq  \mathop{\sum }\limits_{{i \in  \left\lbrack  {k - 1}\right\rbrack  }}h\left( {{X}_{1} \mid  Y}\right)  + h\left( {{X}_{k}Y}\right)  \leq  \Delta  + 1$。

- Case 3: none of the cases above,i.e., $h\left( {Y \mid  {X}_{i}}\right)  > \Delta$ for each $i \in  \left\lbrack  k\right\rbrack$ . As $h\left( {{X}_{i}Y}\right)  \leq  1$ and $h\left( {Y \mid  {X}_{i}}\right)  > \Delta$ , we have $h\left( {X}_{i}\right)  = h\left( {{X}_{i}Y}\right)  - h\left( {Y \mid  {X}_{i}}\right)  \leq  1 - \Delta$ . As $h\left( {{X}_{i}Y}\right)  \leq  1$ and $h\left( {{X}_{i} \mid  Y}\right)  > \frac{\Delta }{k - 1}$ ,we have $h\left( Y\right)  = h\left( {{X}_{i}Y}\right)  - h\left( {{X}_{i} \mid  Y}\right)  \leq  1 - \frac{\Delta }{k - 1}.$

- 情况3：以上情况均不满足，即对每个$i \in  \left\lbrack  k\right\rbrack$都有$h\left( {Y \mid  {X}_{i}}\right)  > \Delta$。由于$h\left( {{X}_{i}Y}\right)  \leq  1$且$h\left( {Y \mid  {X}_{i}}\right)  > \Delta$，可得$h\left( {X}_{i}\right)  = h\left( {{X}_{i}Y}\right)  - h\left( {Y \mid  {X}_{i}}\right)  \leq  1 - \Delta$。又因$h\left( {{X}_{i}Y}\right)  \leq  1$与$h\left( {{X}_{i} \mid  Y}\right)  > \frac{\Delta }{k - 1}$，故有$h\left( Y\right)  = h\left( {{X}_{i}Y}\right)  - h\left( {{X}_{i} \mid  Y}\right)  \leq  1 - \frac{\Delta }{k - 1}.$

We further distinguish two more cases depending on whether $k$ is even or odd.

我们根据$k$的奇偶性进一步区分两种子情况。

- When $k$ is odd. Let $\mathbf{A} = \left\{  {{X}_{2},{X}_{3},\cdots ,{X}_{\frac{k + 1}{2}}}\right\}$ and $\mathbf{B} = \left\{  {{X}_{\frac{k + 1}{2} + 1},{X}_{\frac{k + 1}{2} + 2},\cdots ,{X}_{k}}\right\}$ . Note that $h\left( \mathbf{A}\right)  \leq  \left( {1 - \Delta }\right)  \cdot  \frac{k - 1}{2}$ and $h\left( \mathbf{B}\right)  \leq  \left( {1 - \Delta }\right)  \cdot  \frac{k - 1}{2}$ . In this case,we assume that $\Delta  > \left( {1 - \Delta }\right)  \cdot  \frac{k - 1}{2}$ . Consider a generalized elimination ordering $\overline{\mathbf{\sigma }} = \left( {\{ Y\} ,\mathbf{A},\mathbf{B},\left\{  {X}_{1}\right\}  }\right)$ . We have

- 当$k$为奇数时。设$\mathbf{A} = \left\{  {{X}_{2},{X}_{3},\cdots ,{X}_{\frac{k + 1}{2}}}\right\}$且$\mathbf{B} = \left\{  {{X}_{\frac{k + 1}{2} + 1},{X}_{\frac{k + 1}{2} + 2},\cdots ,{X}_{k}}\right\}$。注意$h\left( \mathbf{A}\right)  \leq  \left( {1 - \Delta }\right)  \cdot  \frac{k - 1}{2}$且$h\left( \mathbf{B}\right)  \leq  \left( {1 - \Delta }\right)  \cdot  \frac{k - 1}{2}$。在此情况下，我们假设$\Delta  > \left( {1 - \Delta }\right)  \cdot  \frac{k - 1}{2}$。考虑广义消元序$\overline{\mathbf{\sigma }} = \left( {\{ Y\} ,\mathbf{A},\mathbf{B},\left\{  {X}_{1}\right\}  }\right)$，可得

$$
{\mathrm{{EMM}}}_{1}^{\bar{\sigma }} \leq  \mathrm{{MM}}\left( {\mathbf{A};\mathbf{B};Y \mid  {X}_{1}}\right)  \leq  {\gamma h}\left( \mathbf{A}\right)  + h\left( \mathbf{B}\right)  + h\left( {{X}_{1}Y}\right)  \leq  \left( {\gamma  + 1}\right) \left( {1 - \Delta }\right)  \cdot  \frac{k - 1}{2} + 1
$$

Combining all the cases, we obtain the desired upper bound:

综合所有情形，我们得到所需的上界：

$$
1 + \max \left\{  {\Delta ,\left( {\gamma  + 1}\right) \left( {1 - \Delta }\right)  \cdot  \frac{k - 1}{2}}\right\}   = 2 - \frac{2}{\omega  \cdot  \left( {k - 1}\right)  - k + 3}.
$$

- When $k$ is even. We partition all polymatroids $\mathbf{h}$ into the following two cases: Let $\mathbf{A} =$ $\left\{  {{X}_{1},{X}_{2},{X}_{3},\cdots ,{X}_{\frac{k}{2}}}\right\}$ and $\mathbf{B} = \left\{  {{X}_{\frac{k}{2} + 1},{X}_{\frac{k}{2} + 1},\cdots ,{X}_{k}}\right\}$ . Note that $h\left( \mathbf{A}\right)  \leq  \left( {1 - \Delta }\right)  \cdot  \frac{k}{2}$ and $h\left( \mathbf{B}\right)  \leq$ $\left( {1 - \Delta }\right)  \cdot  \frac{k}{2}$ . We assume that $\Delta$ is a parameter such that $1 - \frac{\Delta }{k - 1} > \left( {1 - \Delta }\right)  \cdot  \frac{k}{2}$ . Consider a generalized elimination ordering $\overline{\mathbf{\sigma }} = \left( {\{ Y\} ,\mathbf{A},\mathbf{B}}\right)$ . We have

- 当$k$为偶数时。我们将所有多拟阵$\mathbf{h}$划分为以下两种情况：设$\mathbf{A} =$$\left\{  {{X}_{1},{X}_{2},{X}_{3},\cdots ,{X}_{\frac{k}{2}}}\right\}$且$\mathbf{B} = \left\{  {{X}_{\frac{k}{2} + 1},{X}_{\frac{k}{2} + 1},\cdots ,{X}_{k}}\right\}$。注意$h\left( \mathbf{A}\right)  \leq  \left( {1 - \Delta }\right)  \cdot  \frac{k}{2}$且$h\left( \mathbf{B}\right)  \leq$$\left( {1 - \Delta }\right)  \cdot  \frac{k}{2}$。假设$\Delta$是满足$1 - \frac{\Delta }{k - 1} > \left( {1 - \Delta }\right)  \cdot  \frac{k}{2}$的参数。考虑广义消元序$\overline{\mathbf{\sigma }} = \left( {\{ Y\} ,\mathbf{A},\mathbf{B}}\right)$，可得

$$
{\mathrm{{EMM}}}_{1}^{\bar{\sigma }} \leq  \mathrm{{MM}}\left( {\mathbf{A};\mathbf{B};Y}\right)  \leq  {\gamma h}\left( \mathbf{A}\right)  + h\left( \mathbf{B}\right)  + h\left( Y\right)  \leq  \gamma \left( {1 - \Delta }\right)  \cdot  \frac{k}{2} + \left( {1 - \Delta }\right)  \cdot  \frac{k}{2} + 1 - \frac{\Delta }{k - 1}
$$

Combining all the cases, we obtain the desired upper bound:

综合所有情形，我们得到所需的上界：

$$
1 + \max \left\{  {\Delta ,\gamma \left( {1 - \Delta }\right)  \cdot  \frac{k}{2} + \left( {1 - \Delta }\right)  \cdot  \frac{k}{2} - \frac{\Delta }{k - 1}}\right\}   \geq  2 - \frac{2}{\omega  \cdot  \left( {k - 1}\right)  - k + 3}
$$

LEMMA C.15. For the following hypergraph

引理 C.15. 对于下述超图

$$
\mathcal{H} = \left( {\{ X,Y,Z,W,L\} ,\;\{ \{ X,Y,W\} ,\{ X,Y,L\} ,\{ X,Z\} ,\{ Y,Z\} ,\{ Z,W,L\} \} }\right) 
$$

$\omega  - \operatorname{subw}\left( \mathcal{H}\right)  \leq  2 - \frac{1}{2\left( {\omega  - 2}\right)  + 3}$ .

Remark. For the hypergraph $\mathcal{H}$ in Lemma C. 15,subw $\left( \mathcal{H}\right)  = \frac{9}{5}$ . If $\omega  = 3,\omega$ -subw $\left( \mathcal{H}\right)  \leq  2 - \frac{1}{{2\omega } - 1} =$ $\operatorname{subw}\left( \mathcal{H}\right)  = \frac{9}{5}$ . If $\omega  < 3,\omega$ -subw $\left( \mathcal{H}\right)  < \operatorname{subw}\left( \mathcal{H}\right)$ .

备注. 对于引理C.15中的超图$\mathcal{H}$，子宽$\left( \mathcal{H}\right)  = \frac{9}{5}$。若$\omega  = 3,\omega$-子宽$\left( \mathcal{H}\right)  \leq  2 - \frac{1}{{2\omega } - 1} =$$\operatorname{subw}\left( \mathcal{H}\right)  = \frac{9}{5}$。若$\omega  < 3,\omega$-子宽$\left( \mathcal{H}\right)  < \operatorname{subw}\left( \mathcal{H}\right)$。

Proof. Let $\Delta  = \frac{2\left( {\omega  - 1}\right) }{{2\omega } - 1}$ . We partition all polymatroids $\mathbf{h}$ into the following cases:

证明. 设$\Delta  = \frac{2\left( {\omega  - 1}\right) }{{2\omega } - 1}$。我们将所有多拟阵$\mathbf{h}$划分为以下情形：

- Case 1: $h\left( {{XY} \mid  W}\right)  \leq  \Delta$ or $h\left( {{ZL} \mid  W}\right)  \leq  \Delta$ . whog,suppose $h\left( {{XY} \mid  W}\right)  \leq  \Delta$ . We have $h\left( {XYZWL}\right)  \leq$ $h\left( {{XY} \mid  {ZWL}}\right)  + h\left( {ZWL}\right)  \leq  h\left( {{XY} \mid  W}\right)  + h\left( {ZWL}\right)  \leq  1 + \Delta .$

- 情况1：$h\left( {{XY} \mid  W}\right)  \leq  \Delta$ 或 $h\left( {{ZL} \mid  W}\right)  \leq  \Delta$。假设 $h\left( {{XY} \mid  W}\right)  \leq  \Delta$，我们得到 $h\left( {XYZWL}\right)  \leq$ $h\left( {{XY} \mid  {ZWL}}\right)  + h\left( {ZWL}\right)  \leq  h\left( {{XY} \mid  W}\right)  + h\left( {ZWL}\right)  \leq  1 + \Delta .$

- Case 2: $h\left( {{XY} \mid  L}\right)  \leq  \Delta$ or $h\left( {{ZW} \mid  L}\right)  \leq  \Delta$ . This case is similar to Case 1.

- 情况2：$h\left( {{XY} \mid  L}\right)  \leq  \Delta$ 或 $h\left( {{ZW} \mid  L}\right)  \leq  \Delta$。此情况与情况1类似。

- Case 3: $h\left( {X \mid  Z}\right)  \leq  \frac{\Delta }{2}$ and $h\left( {Y \mid  Z}\right)  \leq  \frac{\Delta }{2}$ . We have $h\left( {XYZWL}\right)  \leq  h\left( {X \mid  {ZWL}}\right)  + h\left( {Y \mid  {ZWL}}\right)  +$ $h\left( {ZWL}\right)  \leq  h\left( {X|Z}\right)  + h\left( {Y|Z}\right)  + h\left( {ZWL}\right)  \leq  1 + \Delta .$

- 情况3：$h\left( {X \mid  Z}\right)  \leq  \frac{\Delta }{2}$ 且 $h\left( {Y \mid  Z}\right)  \leq  \frac{\Delta }{2}$。我们得到 $h\left( {XYZWL}\right)  \leq  h\left( {X \mid  {ZWL}}\right)  + h\left( {Y \mid  {ZWL}}\right)  +$ $h\left( {ZWL}\right)  \leq  h\left( {X|Z}\right)  + h\left( {Y|Z}\right)  + h\left( {ZWL}\right)  \leq  1 + \Delta .$

- Case 4: either $h\left( {L \mid  {XY}}\right)  \leq  \frac{{2\Delta } - 1}{3}$ or $h\left( {W \mid  {XY}}\right)  \leq  \frac{{2\Delta } - 1}{3}$ ,and either $h\left( {Z \mid  X}\right)  \leq  \frac{1 + \Delta }{3}$ or $h\left( {Z \mid  Y}\right)  \leq$ $\frac{1 + \Delta }{3}$ . Wlog,suppose $h\left( {L \mid  {XY}}\right)  \leq  \frac{{2\Delta } - 1}{3}$ and $h\left( {Z \mid  X}\right)  \leq  \frac{1 + \Delta }{3}$ . In this case,we have $h\left( {XYZWL}\right)  \leq$ $h\left( {L \mid  {XYW}}\right)  + h\left( {Z \mid  {XYW}}\right)  + h\left( {XYW}\right)  \leq  1 + \Delta .$

- 情况4：要么 $h\left( {L \mid  {XY}}\right)  \leq  \frac{{2\Delta } - 1}{3}$ 要么 $h\left( {W \mid  {XY}}\right)  \leq  \frac{{2\Delta } - 1}{3}$，且要么 $h\left( {Z \mid  X}\right)  \leq  \frac{1 + \Delta }{3}$ 要么 $h\left( {Z \mid  Y}\right)  \leq$ $\frac{1 + \Delta }{3}$。不妨假设 $h\left( {L \mid  {XY}}\right)  \leq  \frac{{2\Delta } - 1}{3}$ 且 $h\left( {Z \mid  X}\right)  \leq  \frac{1 + \Delta }{3}$。在此情况下，我们得到 $h\left( {XYZWL}\right)  \leq$ $h\left( {L \mid  {XYW}}\right)  + h\left( {Z \mid  {XYW}}\right)  + h\left( {XYW}\right)  \leq  1 + \Delta .$

- Case 5: none of the cases above. As $h\left( {{XY} \mid  W}\right)  > \Delta$ and $h\left( {XYW}\right)  \leq  1$ ,we have $h\left( W\right)  = h\left( {XYW}\right)  -$ $h\left( {{XY} \mid  W}\right)  < 1 - \Delta$ . As $h\left( {{XY} \mid  L}\right)  > \Delta$ and $h\left( {XYL}\right)  \leq  1$ ,we have $h\left( L\right)  < 1 - \Delta$ . As $h\left( {X \mid  Z}\right)  > \frac{\Delta }{2}$ and $h\left( {XZ}\right)  \leq  1$ ,we have $h\left( Z\right)  < 1 - \frac{\Delta }{2}$ . If either $h\left( {L \mid  {XY}}\right)  \leq  \frac{{2\Delta } - 1}{3}$ or $h\left( {W \mid  {XY}}\right)  \leq  \frac{{2\Delta } - 1}{3}$ ,but $h\left( {Z \mid  X}\right)  \leq  \frac{1 + \Delta }{3}$ and $h\left( {Z \mid  Y}\right)  \leq  \frac{1 + \Delta }{3}$ ,we have $h\left( X\right)  \leq  1 - \frac{1 + \Delta }{3} = \frac{2 - \Delta }{3}$ and $h\left( Y\right)  \leq  1 - \frac{1 + \Delta }{3} = \frac{2 - \Delta }{3}$ . Hence, $h\left( {XY}\right)  \leq  \frac{2\left( {2 - \Delta }\right) }{3}$ . If both $h\left( {L \mid  {XY}}\right)  > \frac{{2\Delta } - 1}{3}$ and $h\left( {W \mid  {XY}}\right)  > \frac{{2\Delta } - 1}{3}$ ,we have $h\left( {XY}\right)  \leq$ $1 - \frac{{2\Delta } - 1}{3} = \frac{2\left( {2 - \Delta }\right) }{3}$ . Consider a generalized elimination ordering $\overline{\mathbf{\sigma }} = \left( {\{ {XY}\} ,\{ Z\} ,\{ W\} ,\{ L\} }\right)$ .

- 情况5：不满足上述任何条件。由于$h\left( {{XY} \mid  W}\right)  > \Delta$与$h\left( {XYW}\right)  \leq  1$，可得$h\left( W\right)  = h\left( {XYW}\right)  -$$h\left( {{XY} \mid  W}\right)  < 1 - \Delta$。鉴于$h\left( {{XY} \mid  L}\right)  > \Delta$和$h\left( {XYL}\right)  \leq  1$，我们得出$h\left( L\right)  < 1 - \Delta$。当$h\left( {X \mid  Z}\right)  > \frac{\Delta }{2}$且$h\left( {XZ}\right)  \leq  1$时，存在$h\left( Z\right)  < 1 - \frac{\Delta }{2}$。若$h\left( {L \mid  {XY}}\right)  \leq  \frac{{2\Delta } - 1}{3}$或$h\left( {W \mid  {XY}}\right)  \leq  \frac{{2\Delta } - 1}{3}$成立，但$h\left( {Z \mid  X}\right)  \leq  \frac{1 + \Delta }{3}$与$h\left( {Z \mid  Y}\right)  \leq  \frac{1 + \Delta }{3}$同时满足，则得到$h\left( X\right)  \leq  1 - \frac{1 + \Delta }{3} = \frac{2 - \Delta }{3}$和$h\left( Y\right)  \leq  1 - \frac{1 + \Delta }{3} = \frac{2 - \Delta }{3}$。因此，$h\left( {XY}\right)  \leq  \frac{2\left( {2 - \Delta }\right) }{3}$。当$h\left( {L \mid  {XY}}\right)  > \frac{{2\Delta } - 1}{3}$与$h\left( {W \mid  {XY}}\right)  > \frac{{2\Delta } - 1}{3}$均成立时，可得$h\left( {XY}\right)  \leq$$1 - \frac{{2\Delta } - 1}{3} = \frac{2\left( {2 - \Delta }\right) }{3}$。考虑广义消元序$\overline{\mathbf{\sigma }} = \left( {\{ {XY}\} ,\{ Z\} ,\{ W\} ,\{ L\} }\right)$。

$$
{\mathrm{{EMM}}}_{1}^{\bar{\sigma }} \leq  \mathrm{{MM}}\left( {Z;W;{XY} \mid  L}\right)  \leq  \max \left\{  {\begin{array}{l} {\gamma h}\left( Z\right)  + h\left( W\right)  + h\left( {XYL}\right) \\  h\left( Z\right)  + {\gamma h}\left( W\right)  + h\left( {XYL}\right) \\  h\left( Z\right)  + h\left( W\right)  + {\gamma h}\left( {XYL}\right)  \end{array} \leq  \left( {\omega  - 2}\right)  \cdot  \left( {1 - \Delta }\right)  + 1 - \frac{\Delta }{2} + 1}\right. 
$$

Putting everything together, we obtain the desired upper bound

综合所有因素，我们得到期望的上界

$$
\max \{ 1 + \Delta ,\left( {\omega  - 2}\right)  \cdot  \left( {1 - \Delta }\right)  + 2 - \frac{\Delta }{2}\}  = 2 - \frac{1}{2\left( {\omega  - 2}\right)  + 3}.
$$

## D 第6节缺失细节

In Eq. (33), $\mathcal{I}$ is the number of max terms at the top level. For every $i \in  \left\lbrack  \mathcal{I}\right\rbrack$ ,the $i$ -th term is a maximum over $\mathbf{h} \in  \Gamma  \cap  \mathrm{{ED}}$ of a minimum of ${L}_{i} + {J}_{i}$ terms that are divided into two sets:

在式(33)中，$\mathcal{I}$表示顶层最大项的数量。对于每个$i \in  \left\lbrack  \mathcal{I}\right\rbrack$，第$i$项是对$\mathbf{h} \in  \Gamma  \cap  \mathrm{{ED}}$个被分为两组的最小项取最大值：

- ${L}_{i}$ terms of the form $h\left( U\right)$ corresponding to $h\left( {U}_{i}^{\bar{\sigma }}\right)$ from Eq. (27).

- ${L}_{i}$个形如$h\left( U\right)$的项，对应式(27)中的$h\left( {U}_{i}^{\bar{\sigma }}\right)$。

- ${J}_{i}$ terms of the form $h\left( {X \mid  G}\right)  + h\left( {Y \mid  G}\right)  + {\gamma h}\left( {Z \mid  G}\right)  + h\left( G\right)$ corresponding to terms in Eq. (21).

- ${J}_{i}$个形如$h\left( {X \mid  G}\right)  + h\left( {Y \mid  G}\right)  + {\gamma h}\left( {Z \mid  G}\right)  + h\left( G\right)$的项，对应式(21)中的项。

Suppose that $\omega$ (and by extension $\gamma$ ) is a fixed constant. For a fixed $i \in  \left\lbrack  I\right\rbrack$ ,the inner expression in Eq. (33) is equivalent to an LP,as we now show. The condition $\mathbf{h} \in  \Gamma  \cap  \mathrm{{ED}}$ is a finite collection of linear constraints. The objective function is a minimum of ${L}_{i} + {J}_{i}$ linear functions of $\mathbf{h}$ . To convert it to a linear objective function,we introduce a fresh variable $t$ that is lower bounded by each of these ${L}_{i} + {J}_{i}$ linear functions,and we set the objective to maximize $t$ as follows:

假设$\omega$（进而$\gamma$）为固定常数。对于给定的$i \in  \left\lbrack  I\right\rbrack$，公式(33)中的内部表达式等价于线性规划问题，现证明如下：条件$\mathbf{h} \in  \Gamma  \cap  \mathrm{{ED}}$构成有限线性约束集合，目标函数是$\mathbf{h}$的${L}_{i} + {J}_{i}$个线性函数的最小值。为转化为线性目标函数，引入新变量$t$使其下界受这${L}_{i} + {J}_{i}$个线性函数约束，并将目标设定为最大化$t$，具体如下：

$$
\mathop{\max }\limits_{{t,h \in  \Gamma  \cap  \mathrm{{ED}}}}\left\{  {t\;\mid \;\forall \ell  \in  \left\lbrack  {L}_{i}\right\rbrack  ,\;t \leq  h\left( {U}_{i\ell }\right) ,}\right. 
$$

$$
\left. {\forall j \in  \left\lbrack  {J}_{i}\right\rbrack  ,\;t \leq  h\left( {{\mathbf{X}}_{ij} \mid  {\mathbf{G}}_{ij}}\right)  + h\left( {{\mathbf{Y}}_{ij} \mid  {\mathbf{G}}_{ij}}\right)  + {\gamma h}\left( {{\mathbf{Z}}_{ij} \mid  {\mathbf{G}}_{ij}}\right)  + h\left( {\mathbf{G}}_{ij}\right) }\right\}   \tag{49}
$$

Hence,computing the $\omega$ -submodular width of a hypergraph $\mathcal{H}$ reduces to solving $\mathcal{I}$ linear programs of the form in Eq. (49),and taking the maximum of their optimal values. ${}^{13}$ Section 2.4 exemplifies the above using the hypergraph of the triangle query ${Q}_{\Delta }$ (Eq. (2)).

因此，计算超图$\mathcal{H}$的$\omega$-子模宽度可转化为求解公式(49)形式的$\mathcal{I}$个线性规划问题，并取其最优解的最大值。${}^{13}$第2.4节通过三角形查询${Q}_{\Delta }$的超图（公式(2)）具体说明了上述过程。

Example D.1. Consider the 4-clique hypergraph from Eq. (23). The $\omega$ -submodular width of this hypergraph is given by Eq. (28). Inside the min, we have 10 different terms MM, each of which is a maximum of 3 terms. By distributing the min over the max,we get $\mathcal{I} = {3}^{10} = {59049}$ terms. Our mechanical algorithm for computing the $\omega$ -submodular width of this hypergraph consists of exhaustively solving an LP for each one of these $\mathcal{I}$ terms and takeing their maximum optimal objective value,which turns out to be $\frac{\omega  + 1}{2}$ in this example. There are smarter but non-algorithmic ways to reach the same conclusion, as shown in Lemma C.6.

示例D.1 考虑公式(23)中的4-团超图。该超图的$\omega$-子模宽度由公式(28)给出。min运算符内包含10个不同的MM项，每项均为3个项的最大值。通过将min分配到max上，得到$\mathcal{I} = {3}^{10} = {59049}$个项。本案例中计算该超图$\omega$-子模宽度的机械化算法需穷举求解这$\mathcal{I}$个项各自的线性规划问题，并取其最大最优目标值（本例中结果为$\frac{\omega  + 1}{2}$）。如引理C.6所示，存在更智能但非算法化的方法可得出相同结论。

## E 第7章节补充材料

In this appendix, we prove Theorem 7.1 by showing our algorithm for evaluating a Boolean conjunctive query in $\omega$ -submodular width time. To that end,we need to develop a series of technical tools, which we explain in the following subsections.

本附录通过展示$\omega$-子模宽度时间内布尔连接查询求值算法，完成定理7.1的证明。为此需要建立系列技术工具，详见后续小节。

### E.1 Upper bound on the matrix multiplication expression

### E.1 矩阵乘法表达式的上界

We start with introducing an upper bound on the matrix multiplication expression from Definition 4.2.

我们首先给出定义4.2中矩阵乘法表达式的上界。

Definition E. $1\left( {\omega \text{-dominant triple}\left( {\alpha ,\beta ,\zeta }\right) }\right)$ . A triple of numbers $\left( {\alpha ,\beta ,\zeta }\right)$ is called $\omega$ -dominant if it satisfies the following conditions:

定义E.$1\left( {\omega \text{-dominant triple}\left( {\alpha ,\beta ,\zeta }\right) }\right)$ 若数值三元组$\left( {\alpha ,\beta ,\zeta }\right)$满足下列条件，则称其为$\omega$-支配的：

$$
\alpha ,\beta  \geq  1, \tag{50}
$$

$$
\zeta  \geq  0 \tag{51}
$$

$$
\alpha  + \beta  + \zeta  \geq  \omega  \tag{52}
$$

Proposition E. 2 (Upper Bound on MM $\left( {X;Y;Z \mid  G}\right)$ ). Let the triples $\left( {{\alpha }_{1},{\beta }_{1},{\zeta }_{1}}\right) ,\left( {{\alpha }_{2},{\beta }_{2},{\zeta }_{2}}\right)$ , and $\left( {{\alpha }_{3},{\beta }_{3},{\zeta }_{3}}\right)$ be $\omega$ -dominant. For any polymatroid $\mathbf{h} : {2}^{\mathcal{V}} \rightarrow  {\mathbb{R}}_{ + }$ and pairwise-disjoint subsets $X,Y,Z,G \subseteq  \mathcal{V}$ ,the following inequality holds:

命题E.2（MM$\left( {X;Y;Z \mid  G}\right)$上界） 设三元组$\left( {{\alpha }_{1},{\beta }_{1},{\zeta }_{1}}\right) ,\left( {{\alpha }_{2},{\beta }_{2},{\zeta }_{2}}\right)$和$\left( {{\alpha }_{3},{\beta }_{3},{\zeta }_{3}}\right)$均为$\omega$-支配的。对于任意多拟阵$\mathbf{h} : {2}^{\mathcal{V}} \rightarrow  {\mathbb{R}}_{ + }$及两两不相交子集$X,Y,Z,G \subseteq  \mathcal{V}$，下列不等式成立：

$$
\operatorname{MM}\left( {\mathbf{X};\mathbf{Y};\mathbf{Z} \mid  \mathbf{G}}\right)  \leq  \max \left( {{\alpha }_{1}h\left( {\mathbf{X} \mid  \mathbf{G}}\right)  + {\beta }_{1}h\left( {\mathbf{Y} \mid  \mathbf{G}}\right)  + {\zeta }_{1}h\left( {\mathbf{Z} \mid  \mathbf{G}}\right)  + h\left( \mathbf{G}\right) ,}\right. 
$$

$$
{\alpha }_{2}h\left( {\mathbf{X} \mid  \mathbf{G}}\right)  + {\zeta }_{2}h\left( {\mathbf{Y} \mid  \mathbf{G}}\right)  + {\beta }_{2}h\left( {\mathbf{Z} \mid  \mathbf{G}}\right)  + h\left( \mathbf{G}\right) ,
$$

$$
{\zeta }_{3}h\left( {\mathbf{X} \mid  \mathbf{G}}\right)  + {\alpha }_{3}h\left( {\mathbf{Y} \mid  \mathbf{G}}\right)  + {\beta }_{3}h\left( {\mathbf{Z} \mid  \mathbf{G}}\right)  + h\left( \mathbf{G}\right) ) \tag{53}
$$

---

<!-- Footnote -->

${}^{13}$ If $\omega$ is not fixed,then Eq. (49) is not an LP because the coefficient $\gamma$ depends on $\omega$ . Hence,we end up having constraints that are quadratic in terms of $\omega$ and $\mathbf{h}$ .

${}^{13}$ 若$\omega$非固定，则公式(49)不构成线性规划问题，因为系数$\gamma$依赖于$\omega$。这将导致关于$\omega$和$\mathbf{h}$的二次约束条件。

<!-- Footnote -->

---

Proof. WLOG suppose that $h\left( {Z \mid  G}\right)$ is the minimum among the three terms $h\left( {X \mid  G}\right) ,h\left( {Y \mid  G}\right)$ and $h\left( {Z \mid  G}\right)$ . Then, $\operatorname{MM}\left( {X;Y;Z \mid  G}\right)$ becomes identical to $h\left( {X \mid  G}\right)  + h\left( {Y \mid  G}\right)  + {\gamma h}\left( {Z \mid  G}\right)  + h\left( G\right)$ ,and the following inequality holds:

证明。不失一般性，假设$h\left( {Z \mid  G}\right)$是三项$h\left( {X \mid  G}\right) ,h\left( {Y \mid  G}\right)$与$h\left( {Z \mid  G}\right)$中的最小值，则$\operatorname{MM}\left( {X;Y;Z \mid  G}\right)$等同于$h\left( {X \mid  G}\right)  + h\left( {Y \mid  G}\right)  + {\gamma h}\left( {Z \mid  G}\right)  + h\left( G\right)$，且下列不等式成立：

$$
h\left( {X \mid  G}\right)  + h\left( {Y \mid  G}\right)  + {\gamma h}\left( {Z \mid  G}\right)  + h\left( G\right)  \leq  {\alpha }_{1}h\left( {X \mid  G}\right)  + {\beta }_{1}h\left( {Y \mid  G}\right)  + {\zeta }_{1}h\left( {Z \mid  G}\right)  + h\left( G\right) 
$$

### E.2 Class of Shannon Inequalities

### E.2 香农不等式类

We now introduce the class of Shannon inequalities at the center of our algorithms.

本节介绍算法核心的香农不等式类。

Definition E.3 ( $\omega$ -Shannon Inequality). A Shannon inequality is called an $\omega$ -Shannon inequality if it has the following form:

定义 E.3（$\omega$-香农不等式）。若某香农不等式具有如下形式，则称其为$\omega$-香农不等式：

$$
\mathop{\sum }\limits_{{\ell  \in  \left\lbrack  L\right\rbrack  }}{\lambda }_{\ell }h\left( {U}_{\ell }\right)  + \mathop{\sum }\limits_{{j \in  \left\lbrack  J\right\rbrack  }}\left( {{\alpha }_{j}h\left( {{X}_{j} \mid  {G}_{j}}\right)  + {\beta }_{j}h\left( {{Y}_{j} \mid  {G}_{j}}\right)  + {\zeta }_{j}h\left( {{Z}_{j} \mid  {G}_{j}}\right)  + {\kappa }_{j}h\left( {G}_{j}\right) }\right)  \leq  \mathop{\sum }\limits_{{i \in  \left\lbrack  I\right\rbrack  }}{w}_{i}h\left( {{Y}_{i} \mid  {X}_{i}}\right) 
$$

(54)

where

其中

- all coefficients ${\lambda }_{\ell },{\alpha }_{j},{\beta }_{j},{\zeta }_{j}$ and ${w}_{i}$ are non-negative,

- 所有系数${\lambda }_{\ell },{\alpha }_{j},{\beta }_{j},{\zeta }_{j}$和${w}_{i}$均为非负数

- all coefficients ${\kappa }_{j}$ are positive,

- 所有系数${\kappa }_{j}$均为正数

- and all triples $\left( {\frac{{\alpha }_{j}}{{\kappa }_{j}},\frac{{\beta }_{j}}{{\kappa }_{j}},\frac{{\zeta }_{j}}{{\kappa }_{j}}}\right)$ are $\omega$ -dominant.

- 且所有三元组$\left( {\frac{{\alpha }_{j}}{{\kappa }_{j}},\frac{{\beta }_{j}}{{\kappa }_{j}},\frac{{\zeta }_{j}}{{\kappa }_{j}}}\right)$均满足$\omega$-支配性

Following [4], the following proposition follows immediately from Farkas' Lemma. Recall the notation for $h\left( {Y;Z \mid  X}\right)$ from Eq. (18).

根据文献[4]，由法卡斯引理可直接推出下述命题。回顾式(18)中关于$h\left( {Y;Z \mid  X}\right)$的记号。

Proposition E.4. Given an $\omega$ -Shannon inequality of the form (54),there must exist non-negative vectors $\mathbf{m}\overset{\text{ def }}{ = }{\left( {m}_{p}\right) }_{p \in  \left\lbrack  P\right\rbrack  }$ and $s\overset{\text{ def }}{ = }{\left( {s}_{q}\right) }_{q \in  \left\lbrack  Q\right\rbrack  }$ such that the following equality is an identity over symbolic variables $h\left( X\right)$ for $X \subseteq  \mathcal{V}$ where $h\left( \varnothing \right)  = 0$ :

命题 E.4。给定形如(54)的$\omega$-香农不等式，必存在非负向量$\mathbf{m}\overset{\text{ def }}{ = }{\left( {m}_{p}\right) }_{p \in  \left\lbrack  P\right\rbrack  }$和$s\overset{\text{ def }}{ = }{\left( {s}_{q}\right) }_{q \in  \left\lbrack  Q\right\rbrack  }$使得下式对$X \subseteq  \mathcal{V}$的符号变量$h\left( X\right)$（其中$h\left( \varnothing \right)  = 0$）构成恒等式：

$$
\mathop{\sum }\limits_{{\ell  \in  \left\lbrack  L\right\rbrack  }}{\lambda }_{\ell }h\left( {\mathbf{U}}_{\ell }\right)  + \mathop{\sum }\limits_{{j \in  \left\lbrack  J\right\rbrack  }}\left( {{\alpha }_{j}h\left( {{\mathbf{X}}_{j} \mid  {\mathbf{G}}_{j}}\right)  + {\beta }_{j}h\left( {{\mathbf{Y}}_{j} \mid  {\mathbf{G}}_{j}}\right)  + {\zeta }_{j}h\left( {{\mathbf{Z}}_{j} \mid  {\mathbf{G}}_{j}}\right)  + {\kappa }_{j}h\left( {\mathbf{G}}_{j}\right) }\right)  = \mathop{\sum }\limits_{{i \in  \left\lbrack  I\right\rbrack  }}{\mathbf{w}}_{i}h\left( {{\mathbf{Y}}_{i} \mid  {\mathbf{X}}_{i}}\right) 
$$

$$
 - \mathop{\sum }\limits_{{p \in  \left\lbrack  P\right\rbrack  }}{m}_{p}h\left( {{Y}_{p} \mid  {X}_{p}}\right)  - \mathop{\sum }\limits_{{q \in  \left\lbrack  Q\right\rbrack  }}{s}_{q}h\left( {{Y}_{q};{Z}_{q} \mid  {X}_{q}}\right) 
$$

(55)

We use the standard notation $\parallel \lambda {\parallel }_{1}$ to denote $\mathop{\sum }\limits_{{\ell  \in  \left\lbrack  L\right\rbrack  }}\left| {\lambda }_{\ell }\right|$ .

采用标准记号$\parallel \lambda {\parallel }_{1}$表示$\mathop{\sum }\limits_{{\ell  \in  \left\lbrack  L\right\rbrack  }}\left| {\lambda }_{\ell }\right|$。

Proposition E.5. Given any $\omega$ -Shannon inequality of the form (54),the following inequality holds:

命题 E.5。给定任意形如(54)的$\omega$-香农不等式，下列不等式成立：

$$
\parallel \lambda {\parallel }_{1} + \parallel \kappa {\parallel }_{1} \leq  \mathop{\sum }\limits_{{i \in  \left\lbrack  I\right\rbrack   \mid  {X}_{i} = \varnothing }}{w}_{i} \tag{56}
$$

Proof. Every $\omega$ -Shannon inequality (54) must hold for the following polymatroid:

证明。每个$\omega$-香农不等式(54)对下述多拟阵必然成立：

$$
h\left( \mathbf{W}\right)  = \left\{  \begin{array}{ll} 0 & \text{ if }\mathbf{W} = \varnothing \\  1 & \text{ otherwise } \end{array}\right. 
$$

When evaluated over the above polymatroid,the RHS of identity (55) is at most $\mathop{\sum }\limits_{{i \in  \left\lbrack  I\right\rbrack   \mid  {X}_{i} = \varnothing }}{w}_{i}$ , while the LHS is at least $\parallel \lambda {\parallel }_{1} + \parallel \kappa {\parallel }_{1}$ . Note that for every $j \in  \left\lbrack  J\right\rbrack$ ,we must have ${\alpha }_{j} \geq  {\kappa }_{j}$ because the triple $\left( {\frac{{\alpha }_{j}}{{\kappa }_{j}},\frac{{\beta }_{j}}{{\kappa }_{j}},\frac{{\zeta }_{j}}{{\kappa }_{j}}}\right)$ is $\omega$ -dominant. Hence,when ${\mathbf{G}}_{j} = \varnothing$ ,the term ${\alpha }_{j}h\left( {{\mathbf{X}}_{j} \mid  {\mathbf{G}}_{j}}\right)$ contributes at least ${\kappa }_{j}$ to the LHS of (55).

在上述多拟阵上求值时，恒等式(55)的右式至多为$\mathop{\sum }\limits_{{i \in  \left\lbrack  I\right\rbrack   \mid  {X}_{i} = \varnothing }}{w}_{i}$，而左式至少为$\parallel \lambda {\parallel }_{1} + \parallel \kappa {\parallel }_{1}$。注意对每个$j \in  \left\lbrack  J\right\rbrack$，因三元组$\left( {\frac{{\alpha }_{j}}{{\kappa }_{j}},\frac{{\beta }_{j}}{{\kappa }_{j}},\frac{{\zeta }_{j}}{{\kappa }_{j}}}\right)$满足$\omega$-支配性，必有${\alpha }_{j} \geq  {\kappa }_{j}$。故当${\mathbf{G}}_{j} = \varnothing$时，项${\alpha }_{j}h\left( {{\mathbf{X}}_{j} \mid  {\mathbf{G}}_{j}}\right)$对(55)左式的贡献至少为${\kappa }_{j}$。

Definition E. 6 (An integral $\omega$ -Shannon inequality). An $\omega$ -Shannon inequality of the form (54) is called integral if

定义 E.6（积分$\omega$-香农不等式）。若形如(54)式的$\omega$-香农不等式满足以下条件，则称其为积分不等式：

- all coefficients ${\lambda }_{\ell },{\alpha }_{j},{\beta }_{j},{\zeta }_{j},{\kappa }_{j}$ and ${w}_{i}$ are integers,

- 所有系数 ${\lambda }_{\ell },{\alpha }_{j},{\beta }_{j},{\zeta }_{j},{\kappa }_{j}$ 和 ${w}_{i}$ 均为整数，

- and there exist non-negative integers ${\left( {m}_{p}\right) }_{p \in  \left\lbrack  P\right\rbrack  }$ and ${\left( {s}_{q}\right) }_{q \in  \left\lbrack  Q\right\rbrack  }$ such that the identity in Eq. (55) holds.

- 且存在非负整数 ${\left( {m}_{p}\right) }_{p \in  \left\lbrack  P\right\rbrack  }$ 和 ${\left( {s}_{q}\right) }_{q \in  \left\lbrack  Q\right\rbrack  }$ 使得方程(55)中的恒等式成立。

### E.3 Generalizing the Reset Lemma

### E.3 重置引理的泛化

We present here a highly non-trivial generalization of the Reset Lemma from [3, 4]. In particular, given an integral $\omega$ -Shannon inequality (54),this lemma allows us to sacrifice any unconditional term $h\left( {{Y}_{i} \mid  \varnothing }\right)$ on the RHS while losing at most one unit from the quantity $\parallel \lambda {\parallel }_{1} + \parallel \kappa {\parallel }_{1}$ on the LHS. Unlike the original Reset Lemma [3,4],this lemma has to support proper conditionals $h\left( {{\mathbf{X}}_{j} \mid  {\mathbf{G}}_{j}}\right)$ on the LHS of the $\omega$ -Shannon inequality. Moreover,this lemma has to maintain the $\omega$ -dominance property of the triples $\left( {\frac{{\alpha }_{j}}{{\kappa }_{j}},\frac{{\beta }_{j}}{{\kappa }_{j}},\frac{{\zeta }_{j}}{{\kappa }_{j}}}\right)$ ,which will be crucial for the join algorithm introduced later.

本文提出对文献[3,4]中重置引理的重要泛化。具体而言，给定一个积分型$\omega$-香农不等式(54)，该引理允许我们牺牲右侧任何无条件项$h\left( {{Y}_{i} \mid  \varnothing }\right)$，同时最多从左侧量$\parallel \lambda {\parallel }_{1} + \parallel \kappa {\parallel }_{1}$中损失一个单位。与原始重置引理[3,4]不同，本引理必须支持$\omega$-香农不等式左侧的适当条件项$h\left( {{\mathbf{X}}_{j} \mid  {\mathbf{G}}_{j}}\right)$。此外，该引理还需保持三元组$\left( {\frac{{\alpha }_{j}}{{\kappa }_{j}},\frac{{\beta }_{j}}{{\kappa }_{j}},\frac{{\zeta }_{j}}{{\kappa }_{j}}}\right)$的$\omega$-支配性质，这对后文提出的连接算法至关重要。

LEMMA E.7 (GENERALIZED RESET LEMMA). Given an integral $\omega$ -Shannon inequality of the form (54), suppose that for some ${i}_{0} \in  \left\lbrack  I\right\rbrack$ ,we have ${\mathbf{X}}_{{i}_{0}} = \varnothing$ and ${w}_{{i}_{0}} > 0$ . Then,there exist non-negative integers ${\lambda }_{\ell }^{\prime },{\alpha }_{j}^{\prime },{\beta }_{j}^{\prime },{\zeta }_{j}^{\prime },{\kappa }_{j}^{\prime },{w}_{i}^{\prime }$ for which the following is also an integral $\omega$ -Shannon inequality:

引理 E.7（广义重置引理）. 给定形如(54)的积分型$\omega$-香农不等式，若存在${i}_{0} \in  \left\lbrack  I\right\rbrack$使得${\mathbf{X}}_{{i}_{0}} = \varnothing$且${w}_{{i}_{0}} > 0$，则存在非负整数${\lambda }_{\ell }^{\prime },{\alpha }_{j}^{\prime },{\beta }_{j}^{\prime },{\zeta }_{j}^{\prime },{\kappa }_{j}^{\prime },{w}_{i}^{\prime }$使得下式仍为积分型$\omega$-香农不等式：

$$
\mathop{\sum }\limits_{{\ell  \in  \left\lbrack  L\right\rbrack  }}{\lambda }_{\ell }^{\prime }h\left( {U}_{\ell }\right)  + \mathop{\sum }\limits_{{j \in  \left\lbrack  J\right\rbrack  }}\left( {{\alpha }_{j}^{\prime }h\left( {{X}_{j} \mid  {G}_{j}}\right)  + {\beta }_{j}^{\prime }h\left( {{Y}_{j} \mid  {G}_{j}}\right)  + {\zeta }_{j}^{\prime }h\left( {{Z}_{j} \mid  {G}_{j}}\right)  + {\kappa }_{j}^{\prime }h\left( {G}_{j}\right) }\right)  \leq  \mathop{\sum }\limits_{{i \in  \left\lbrack  I\right\rbrack  }}{w}_{i}^{\prime }h\left( {{Y}_{i} \mid  {X}_{i}}\right) 
$$

and the coefficients satisfy the following conditions:

且系数满足下列条件：

$$
{w}_{{i}_{0}}^{\prime } \leq  {w}_{{i}_{0}} - 1
$$

$$
{w}_{i}^{\prime } \leq  {w}_{i}\;\forall i \in  \left\lbrack  I\right\rbrack   \smallsetminus  \left\{  {i}_{0}\right\}  ,
$$

$$
\parallel {\mathbf{\lambda }}^{\prime }{\parallel }_{1} + \parallel {\mathbf{\kappa }}^{\prime }{\parallel }_{1} \geq  \parallel \mathbf{\lambda }{\parallel }_{1} + \parallel \mathbf{\kappa }{\parallel }_{1} - 1 \tag{57}
$$

Proof. Let $W\overset{\text{ def }}{ = }{Y}_{{i}_{0}}$ . We recognize the following cases:

证明. 设$W\overset{\text{ def }}{ = }{Y}_{{i}_{0}}$，考虑以下情形：

- If $W = {U}_{\ell }$ for some $\ell  \in  \left\lbrack  L\right\rbrack$ where ${\lambda }_{\ell } > 0$ ,we set ${\lambda }_{\ell }^{\prime }\overset{\text{ def }}{ = }{\lambda }_{\ell } - 1$ and ${w}_{{i}_{0}}^{\prime }\overset{\text{ def }}{ = }{w}_{{i}_{0}} - 1$ . All other coefficients ${\lambda }_{\ell }^{\prime },{\alpha }_{j}^{\prime },{\beta }_{j}^{\prime },{\zeta }_{j}^{\prime },{\kappa }_{j}^{\prime },{w}_{i}^{\prime }$ remain the same as the original ${\lambda }_{\ell },{\alpha }_{j},{\beta }_{j},{\zeta }_{j},{\kappa }_{j},{w}_{i}$ . The new inequality remains a valid integral $\omega$ -Shannon inequality since we dropped $h\left( {W \mid  \varnothing }\right)$ from both sides.

- 若对满足${\lambda }_{\ell } > 0$的某个$\ell  \in  \left\lbrack  L\right\rbrack$有$W = {U}_{\ell }$，则令${\lambda }_{\ell }^{\prime }\overset{\text{ def }}{ = }{\lambda }_{\ell } - 1$且${w}_{{i}_{0}}^{\prime }\overset{\text{ def }}{ = }{w}_{{i}_{0}} - 1$。其余系数${\lambda }_{\ell }^{\prime },{\alpha }_{j}^{\prime },{\beta }_{j}^{\prime },{\zeta }_{j}^{\prime },{\kappa }_{j}^{\prime },{w}_{i}^{\prime }$保持与原${\lambda }_{\ell },{\alpha }_{j},{\beta }_{j},{\zeta }_{j},{\kappa }_{j},{w}_{i}$相同。由于等式两边同时消去了$h\left( {W \mid  \varnothing }\right)$，新不等式仍是有效的积分型$\omega$-香农不等式。

- If $\mathbf{W} = {\mathbf{G}}_{j}{\mathbf{X}}_{j}$ for some $j \in  \left\lbrack  J\right\rbrack$ where ${\alpha }_{j},{\kappa }_{j} > 0$ ,then we set ${\alpha }_{j}^{\prime }\overset{\text{ def }}{ = }{\alpha }_{j} - 1,{\kappa }_{j}^{\prime }\overset{\text{ def }}{ = }{\kappa }_{j} - 1$ ,and ${w}_{{i}_{0}}^{\prime }\overset{\text{ def }}{ = }{w}_{{i}_{0}} - 1$ . The inequality still holds since we dropped $h\left( {W \mid  \varnothing }\right)$ from both sides. If ${\kappa }_{j}^{\prime } = 0$ , then we drop $j$ from $\left\lbrack  J\right\rbrack$ . Otherwise,the triple $\left( {\frac{{\alpha }_{j}^{\prime }}{{\kappa }_{j}^{\prime }},\frac{{\beta }_{j}}{{\kappa }_{j}^{\prime }},\frac{{\zeta }_{j}}{{\kappa }_{j}^{\prime }}}\right)$ is still $\omega$ -dominant (Definition E.1). In particular, inequality (50) continues to hold because:

- 若对某个$\mathbf{W} = {\mathbf{G}}_{j}{\mathbf{X}}_{j}$存在$j \in  \left\lbrack  J\right\rbrack$使得${\alpha }_{j},{\kappa }_{j} > 0$，则令${\alpha }_{j}^{\prime }\overset{\text{ def }}{ = }{\alpha }_{j} - 1,{\kappa }_{j}^{\prime }\overset{\text{ def }}{ = }{\kappa }_{j} - 1$且${w}_{{i}_{0}}^{\prime }\overset{\text{ def }}{ = }{w}_{{i}_{0}} - 1$。不等式依然成立，因我们移除了两侧的$h\left( {W \mid  \varnothing }\right)$。若${\kappa }_{j}^{\prime } = 0$，则从$\left\lbrack  J\right\rbrack$中移除$j$。否则，三元组$\left( {\frac{{\alpha }_{j}^{\prime }}{{\kappa }_{j}^{\prime }},\frac{{\beta }_{j}}{{\kappa }_{j}^{\prime }},\frac{{\zeta }_{j}}{{\kappa }_{j}^{\prime }}}\right)$仍保持$\omega$支配性（定义E.1）。特别地，不等式(50)继续成立的原因如下：

$$
\frac{{\alpha }_{j}^{\prime }}{{\kappa }_{j}^{\prime }} = \frac{{\alpha }_{j} - 1}{{\kappa }_{j} - 1} \geq  \frac{{\kappa }_{j} - 1}{{\kappa }_{j} - 1} = 1.
$$

Moreover, inequality (52) continues to hold because of the following:

此外，由于以下原因，不等式(52)仍然成立：

$$
\frac{{\alpha }_{j}^{\prime } + {\beta }_{j} + {\zeta }_{j}}{{\kappa }_{j}^{\prime }} = \frac{{\alpha }_{j} + {\beta }_{j} + {\zeta }_{j} - 1}{{\kappa }_{j} - 1}
$$

$$
 \geq  \frac{{\kappa }_{j}\omega  - 1}{{\kappa }_{j} - 1}
$$

(Inductively by Eq. (52))

（根据等式(52)归纳）

$$
 = \omega  + \frac{\omega  - 1}{\underset{ \geq  0}{\underbrace{{\kappa }_{j} - 1}}} \tag{58}
$$

- If $W = {G}_{j}{Y}_{j}$ for some $j \in  \left\lbrack  J\right\rbrack$ where ${\beta }_{j},{\kappa }_{j} > 0$ ,then this is symmetric to the previous case. Namely,we set ${\beta }_{j}^{\prime }\overset{\text{ def }}{ = }{\beta }_{j} - 1,{\kappa }_{j}^{\prime }\overset{\text{ def }}{ = }{\kappa }_{j} - 1$ ,and ${w}_{{i}_{0}}^{\prime }\overset{\text{ def }}{ = }{w}_{{i}_{0}} - 1$ .

- 若对某个$W = {G}_{j}{Y}_{j}$存在$j \in  \left\lbrack  J\right\rbrack$使得${\beta }_{j},{\kappa }_{j} > 0$，则此情形与前一情形对称。即，我们设${\beta }_{j}^{\prime }\overset{\text{ def }}{ = }{\beta }_{j} - 1,{\kappa }_{j}^{\prime }\overset{\text{ def }}{ = }{\kappa }_{j} - 1$且${w}_{{i}_{0}}^{\prime }\overset{\text{ def }}{ = }{w}_{{i}_{0}} - 1$。

- If $W = {G}_{j}{Z}_{j}$ for some $j \in  \left\lbrack  J\right\rbrack$ where ${\zeta }_{j},{\kappa }_{j} > 0$ ,then we set ${\zeta }_{j}^{\prime }\overset{\text{ def }}{ = }{\zeta }_{j} - 1,{\kappa }_{j}^{\prime }\overset{\text{ def }}{ = }{\kappa }_{j} - 1$ ,and ${w}_{{i}_{0}}^{\prime }\overset{\text{ def }}{ = }{w}_{{i}_{0}} - 1$ . If ${\kappa }_{j}^{\prime } = 0$ ,then we drop $j$ from $\left\lbrack  J\right\rbrack$ . Otherwise,the triple $\left( {\frac{{\alpha }_{j}}{{\kappa }_{j}^{\prime }},\frac{{\beta }_{j}}{{\kappa }_{j}^{\prime }},\frac{{\zeta }_{j}^{\prime }}{{\kappa }_{j}^{\prime }}}\right)$ is still $\omega$ -dominant. In particular,similar to above,inequality (52) continues to hold as follows:
