- 若对某个$W = {G}_{j}{Z}_{j}$存在$j \in  \left\lbrack  J\right\rbrack$使得${\zeta }_{j},{\kappa }_{j} > 0$，则令${\zeta }_{j}^{\prime }\overset{\text{ def }}{ = }{\zeta }_{j} - 1,{\kappa }_{j}^{\prime }\overset{\text{ def }}{ = }{\kappa }_{j} - 1$且${w}_{{i}_{0}}^{\prime }\overset{\text{ def }}{ = }{w}_{{i}_{0}} - 1$。若${\kappa }_{j}^{\prime } = 0$，则从$\left\lbrack  J\right\rbrack$中移除$j$。否则，三元组$\left( {\frac{{\alpha }_{j}}{{\kappa }_{j}^{\prime }},\frac{{\beta }_{j}}{{\kappa }_{j}^{\prime }},\frac{{\zeta }_{j}^{\prime }}{{\kappa }_{j}^{\prime }}}\right)$仍具有$\omega$支配性。特别地，类似于上述情况，不等式(52)继续成立如下：

$$
\frac{{\alpha }_{j} + {\beta }_{j} + {\zeta }_{j}^{\prime }}{{\kappa }_{j}^{\prime }} = \frac{{\alpha }_{j} + {\beta }_{j} + {\zeta }_{j} - 1}{{\kappa }_{j} - 1} \geq  \omega 
$$

The last inequality follows from Eq. (58).

末不等式由等式(58)得出。

- If $W = {G}_{j}$ for some $j \in  \left\lbrack  J\right\rbrack$ and ${\kappa }_{j} > \max \left( {{\alpha }_{j},{\beta }_{j},{\zeta }_{j}}\right)$ ,then we set ${\kappa }_{j}^{\prime }\overset{\text{ def }}{ = }{\kappa }_{j} - 1$ and ${w}_{{i}_{0}}^{\prime }\overset{\text{ def }}{ = }{w}_{{i}_{0}} - 1$ . The triple $\left( {\frac{{\alpha }_{j}}{{\kappa }_{j}^{\prime }},\frac{{\beta }_{j}}{{\kappa }_{j}^{\prime }},\frac{{\zeta }_{j}}{{\kappa }_{j}^{\prime }}}\right)$ is still $\omega$ -dominant. On the other hand,if $W = {G}_{j}$ and ${\kappa }_{j} \leq  \max \left( {{\alpha }_{j},{\beta }_{j},{\zeta }_{j}}\right)$ , then ${\kappa }_{j}h\left( {\mathbf{G}}_{j}\right)$ already cancels out with one of the three terms ${\alpha }_{j}h\left( {{X}_{j} \mid  {\mathbf{G}}_{j}}\right) ,{\beta }_{j}h\left( {{Y}_{j} \mid  {\mathbf{G}}_{j}}\right) ,{\zeta }_{j}h\left( {{Z}_{j} \mid  {\mathbf{G}}_{j}}\right)$ on the LHS. Hence, this case falls under the next case.

- 若$W = {G}_{j}$对某些$j \in  \left\lbrack  J\right\rbrack$与${\kappa }_{j} > \max \left( {{\alpha }_{j},{\beta }_{j},{\zeta }_{j}}\right)$成立，则我们设${\kappa }_{j}^{\prime }\overset{\text{ def }}{ = }{\kappa }_{j} - 1$和${w}_{{i}_{0}}^{\prime }\overset{\text{ def }}{ = }{w}_{{i}_{0}} - 1$。三元组$\left( {\frac{{\alpha }_{j}}{{\kappa }_{j}^{\prime }},\frac{{\beta }_{j}}{{\kappa }_{j}^{\prime }},\frac{{\zeta }_{j}}{{\kappa }_{j}^{\prime }}}\right)$仍保持$\omega$主导性。另一方面，若$W = {G}_{j}$且${\kappa }_{j} \leq  \max \left( {{\alpha }_{j},{\beta }_{j},{\zeta }_{j}}\right)$，则${\kappa }_{j}h\left( {\mathbf{G}}_{j}\right)$已与左侧三个项${\alpha }_{j}h\left( {{X}_{j} \mid  {\mathbf{G}}_{j}}\right) ,{\beta }_{j}h\left( {{Y}_{j} \mid  {\mathbf{G}}_{j}}\right) ,{\zeta }_{j}h\left( {{Z}_{j} \mid  {\mathbf{G}}_{j}}\right)$之一相抵消。因此，此情形归入下一种情况。

- In all other cases, $h\left( {W \mid  \varnothing }\right)$ must cancel out with some term on the RHS of the identity (55),just like in the original Reset Lemma $\left\lbrack  {3,4}\right\rbrack$ . Here we recognize the same three cases from the original Reset Lemma, which we repeat below for self-containment:

- 其余所有情况下，$h\left( {W \mid  \varnothing }\right)$必与恒等式(55)右侧某项相抵消，如同原始重置引理$\left\lbrack  {3,4}\right\rbrack$所述。此处我们识别出与原始重置引理相同的三种情形，为保持完整性复述如下：

- Case 1: $h\left( W\right)$ cancels out with some other term $h\left( {{Y}_{i} \mid  {X}_{i}}\right)$ for some $i \in  \left\lbrack  I\right\rbrack   \smallsetminus  \left\{  {i}_{0}\right\}$ where ${X}_{i} = W$ . In this case, we apply the following rewrite to the RHS of the identity (55):

- 情形1：当${X}_{i} = W$时，$h\left( W\right)$与某个$i \in  \left\lbrack  I\right\rbrack   \smallsetminus  \left\{  {i}_{0}\right\}$对应的其他项$h\left( {{Y}_{i} \mid  {X}_{i}}\right)$相抵消。此时我们对恒等式(55)右侧应用如下重写：

$$
h\left( \mathbf{W}\right)  + h\left( {{Y}_{i} \mid  \mathbf{W}}\right)  = h\left( {{Y}_{i}\mathbf{W}}\right)  \tag{59}
$$

And now inductively,our target is to cancel out the newly added term $h\left( {{Y}_{i}W}\right)$ .

现在通过归纳法，我们的目标是抵消新添加的项$h\left( {{Y}_{i}W}\right)$。

- Case 2: $h\left( W\right)$ cancels out with some term $- h\left( {{Y}_{p} \mid  {X}_{p}}\right)$ from some $p \in  \left\lbrack  P\right\rbrack$ where $W = {X}_{p}{Y}_{p}$ . In this case, we apply the following rewrite to the RHS of the identity (55):

- 情形2：当$W = {X}_{p}{Y}_{p}$时，$h\left( W\right)$与来自某个$p \in  \left\lbrack  P\right\rbrack$的项$- h\left( {{Y}_{p} \mid  {X}_{p}}\right)$相抵消。此时我们对恒等式(55)右侧应用如下重写：

$$
h\left( \mathbf{W}\right)  - h\left( {{Y}_{p} \mid  {\mathbf{X}}_{p}}\right)  = h\left( \mathbf{W}\right)  - h\left( {{\mathbf{X}}_{p}{Y}_{p}}\right)  + h\left( {\mathbf{X}}_{p}\right)  = h\left( {\mathbf{X}}_{p}\right)  \tag{60}
$$

And now,we proceed inductively to eliminate the new term $h\left( {X}_{p}\right)$ .

现在，我们通过归纳法继续消除新项$h\left( {X}_{p}\right)$。

- Case 3: $h\left( W\right)$ cancels out with some term $- h\left( {{Y}_{q};{Z}_{q} \mid  {X}_{q}}\right)$ for some $q \in  \left\lbrack  Q\right\rbrack$ where $W = {X}_{q}{Y}_{q}$ . In this case, we apply the rewrite:

- 情形3：当$W = {X}_{q}{Y}_{q}$时，$h\left( W\right)$与某个$q \in  \left\lbrack  Q\right\rbrack$对应的项$- h\left( {{Y}_{q};{Z}_{q} \mid  {X}_{q}}\right)$相抵消。此时我们应用重写：

$$
h\left( \mathbf{W}\right)  - h\left( {{Y}_{q};{Z}_{q} \mid  {\mathbf{X}}_{q}}\right)  = h\left( \mathbf{W}\right)  - h\left( {{\mathbf{X}}_{q}{Y}_{q}}\right)  - h\left( {{\mathbf{X}}_{q}{Z}_{q}}\right)  + h\left( {\mathbf{X}}_{q}\right)  + h\left( {{\mathbf{X}}_{q}{Y}_{q}{Z}_{q}}\right) 
$$

$$
 = h\left( {{\mathbf{X}}_{q}{\mathbf{Y}}_{q}{\mathbf{Z}}_{q}}\right)  - h\left( {{\mathbf{Z}}_{q} \mid  {\mathbf{X}}_{q}}\right)  \tag{61}
$$

And now we inductively eliminate $h\left( {{X}_{q}{Y}_{q}{Z}_{q}}\right)$ .

现在我们通过归纳法消除$h\left( {{X}_{q}{Y}_{q}{Z}_{q}}\right)$。

By applying each one of the above three cases, the following integral quantity always decreases by at least one, thus proving that this process must terminate:

应用上述三种情形中的任意一种时，下列积分量总是至少减少一，由此证明该过程必然终止：

$$
\parallel \mathbf{w}{\parallel }_{1} + \parallel \mathbf{m}{\parallel }_{1} + 2\parallel \mathbf{s}{\parallel }_{1} \tag{62}
$$

In particular,Case 1 decreases $\parallel \mathbf{w}{\parallel }_{1}$ by 1,Case 2 decreases $\parallel \mathbf{m}{\parallel }_{1}$ by 1,and Case 3 increases $\parallel \mathbf{m}{\parallel }_{1}$ by 1 and decreases $\parallel \mathbf{s}{\parallel }_{1}$ by 1 .

具体而言，情况1使$\parallel \mathbf{w}{\parallel }_{1}$减少1，情况2使$\parallel \mathbf{m}{\parallel }_{1}$减少1，情况3使$\parallel \mathbf{m}{\parallel }_{1}$增加1且使$\parallel \mathbf{s}{\parallel }_{1}$减少1。

### E.4 Generalizing the Proof Sequence Construction

### E.4 证明序列构造的泛化

The following is a generalization of the proof sequence construction from [3,4]. In particular, the original proof sequence construction cannot be used directly because an $\omega$ -Shannon inequality (54) might contain proper conditionals $h\left( {{X}_{j} \mid  {G}_{j}}\right)$ on the LHS. The other challenge here is maintaining the $\omega$ -dominance property of the triples $\left( {\frac{{\alpha }_{j}}{{\kappa }_{j}},\frac{{\beta }_{j}}{{\kappa }_{j}},\frac{{\zeta }_{j}}{{\kappa }_{j}}}\right)$ ,which is needed later to get a corresponding join algorithm that uses matrix multiplication.

下文是对文献[3,4]中证明序列构造的泛化。原始证明序列构造无法直接使用，因为$\omega$-香农不等式(54)的左侧可能包含适当条件项$h\left( {{X}_{j} \mid  {G}_{j}}\right)$。另一挑战在于维持三元组$\left( {\frac{{\alpha }_{j}}{{\kappa }_{j}},\frac{{\beta }_{j}}{{\kappa }_{j}},\frac{{\zeta }_{j}}{{\kappa }_{j}}}\right)$的$\omega$-支配性质，该性质后续用于推导基于矩阵乘法的连接算法。

Theorem E. 8 (Generalized Proof Sequence Construction). Given an integral $\omega$ -Shannon inequality of the form (54), there exists a finite sequence of steps that transforms the RHS of the inequality into the LHS. Each step in the sequence replaces some terms on the RHS with smaller terms, thus the full sequence proves that the LHS is smaller than the RHS. Moreover, each step has either one of the following forms:

定理E.8（广义证明序列构造）。给定形如(54)的整型$\omega$-香农不等式，存在有限步骤序列可将不等式右侧转化为左侧。序列中每一步都将右侧某些项替换为更小项，从而完整序列证明左侧小于右侧。每个步骤具有以下形式之一：

- Decomposition Step: $h\left( {XY}\right)  \rightarrow  h\left( X\right)  + h\left( {Y \mid  X}\right)$ .

- 分解步骤：$h\left( {XY}\right)  \rightarrow  h\left( X\right)  + h\left( {Y \mid  X}\right)$。

- Composition Step: $h\left( X\right)  + h\left( {Y \mid  X}\right)  \rightarrow  h\left( {XY}\right)$ .

- 组合步骤：$h\left( X\right)  + h\left( {Y \mid  X}\right)  \rightarrow  h\left( {XY}\right)$。

- Monotonicity Step: $h\left( {XY}\right)  \rightarrow  h\left( X\right)$ .

- 单调性步骤：$h\left( {XY}\right)  \rightarrow  h\left( X\right)$。

- Submodularity Step: $h\left( {Y \mid  X}\right)  \rightarrow  h\left( {Y \mid  {XZ}}\right)$ .

- 次模性步骤：$h\left( {Y \mid  X}\right)  \rightarrow  h\left( {Y \mid  {XZ}}\right)$。

Note that by Shannon inequalities, each one of the above steps replaces one or two terms with smaller terms. In particular,

需注意根据香农不等式，上述每一步都将一或两项替换为更小项。具体而言：

$$
h\left( {XY}\right)  = h\left( X\right)  + h\left( {Y \mid  X}\right) 
$$

(by Eq. (17))

（由公式(17)可得）

$$
h\left( {XY}\right)  \geq  h\left( X\right) 
$$

$$
\text{(by Eq. (15))}
$$

$$
h\left( {Y \mid  X}\right)  \geq  h\left( {Y \mid  {XZ}}\right) 
$$

(by Eq. (14))

（由公式(14)可得）

For the purpose of proving the above theorem,we will represent an $\omega$ -Shannon inequality slightly differently from Eq. (54). In particular, some terms on the LHS of Eq. (54) might already occur on the RHS, therefore we could decompose the RHS into two parts: One part which is a subset of terms on the LHS, and another part containing the remaining RHS terms. Formally, we can represent an $\omega$ -Shannon inequality as follows:

为证明该定理，我们将采用与公式(54)略有不同的$\omega$-香农不等式表示法。由于公式(54)左侧某些项可能已存在于右侧，可将右侧分解为两部分：与左侧项重合的子集，以及包含其余右侧项的补充部分。形式化表示如下：

$$
\mathop{\sum }\limits_{{\ell  \in  \left\lbrack  L\right\rbrack  }}{\lambda }_{\ell }h\left( {\mathbf{U}}_{\ell }\right)  + \mathop{\sum }\limits_{{j \in  \left\lbrack  J\right\rbrack  }}\left( {{\alpha }_{j}h\left( {{\mathbf{X}}_{j} \mid  {\mathbf{G}}_{j}}\right)  + {\beta }_{j}h\left( {{\mathbf{Y}}_{j} \mid  {\mathbf{G}}_{j}}\right)  + {\zeta }_{j}h\left( {{\mathbf{Z}}_{j} \mid  {\mathbf{G}}_{j}}\right)  + {\kappa }_{j}h\left( {\mathbf{G}}_{j}\right) }\right)  \leq  
$$

$$
\mathop{\sum }\limits_{{j \in  \left\lbrack  J\right\rbrack  }}\left( {{\widehat{\alpha }}_{j}h\left( {{\mathbf{X}}_{j} \mid  {\mathbf{G}}_{j}}\right)  + {\widehat{\beta }}_{j}h\left( {{\mathbf{Y}}_{j} \mid  {\mathbf{G}}_{j}}\right)  + {\widehat{\zeta }}_{j}h\left( {{\mathbf{Z}}_{j} \mid  {\mathbf{G}}_{j}}\right)  + {\widehat{\kappa }}_{j}h\left( {\mathbf{G}}_{j}\right) }\right)  + \mathop{\sum }\limits_{{i \in  \left\lbrack  I\right\rbrack  }}{\widehat{w}}_{i}h\left( {{\mathbf{Y}}_{i} \mid  {\mathbf{X}}_{i}}\right)  \tag{63}
$$

where in addition to the conditions from Definition E.3, we also require that

除定义E.3的条件外，还需满足：

- For all $j \in  \left\lbrack  J\right\rbrack$ ,we have $0 \leq  {\widehat{\alpha }}_{j} \leq  {\alpha }_{j},0 \leq  {\widehat{\beta }}_{j} \leq  {\beta }_{j},0 \leq  {\widehat{\zeta }}_{j} \leq  {\zeta }_{j}$ ,and $0 \leq  {\widehat{\kappa }}_{j} \leq  {\kappa }_{j}$ .

- 对所有$j \in  \left\lbrack  J\right\rbrack$，满足$0 \leq  {\widehat{\alpha }}_{j} \leq  {\alpha }_{j},0 \leq  {\widehat{\beta }}_{j} \leq  {\beta }_{j},0 \leq  {\widehat{\zeta }}_{j} \leq  {\zeta }_{j}$且$0 \leq  {\widehat{\kappa }}_{j} \leq  {\kappa }_{j}$。

- For all $i \in  \left\lbrack  I\right\rbrack$ ,we have $0 \leq  {\widehat{w}}_{i} \leq  {w}_{i}$ .

- 对所有$i \in  \left\lbrack  I\right\rbrack$，满足$0 \leq  {\widehat{w}}_{i} \leq  {w}_{i}$。

- Furthermore,for each $j \in  \left\lbrack  J\right\rbrack$ ,we can assume that ${\widehat{\alpha }}_{j} + {\widehat{\beta }}_{j} + {\widehat{\zeta }}_{j} + {\widehat{\kappa }}_{j} < {\alpha }_{j} + {\beta }_{j} + {\zeta }_{j} + {\kappa }_{j}$ . Otherwise, we could drop the term $j$ from both sides.

- 此外，对每个$j \in  \left\lbrack  J\right\rbrack$，可假定${\widehat{\alpha }}_{j} + {\widehat{\beta }}_{j} + {\widehat{\zeta }}_{j} + {\widehat{\kappa }}_{j} < {\alpha }_{j} + {\beta }_{j} + {\zeta }_{j} + {\kappa }_{j}$。否则可直接从等式两边移除$j$项。

- Finally,we assume,for all $j \in  \left\lbrack  J\right\rbrack$ ,

- 最后，我们假设，对于所有$j \in  \left\lbrack  J\right\rbrack$，

$$
{\kappa }_{j} - {\widehat{\kappa }}_{j} \geq  \max \left( {{\alpha }_{j} - {\widehat{\alpha }}_{j},{\beta }_{j} - {\widehat{\beta }}_{j},{\zeta }_{j} - {\widehat{\zeta }}_{j}}\right)  \tag{64}
$$

By Proposition E.4,for every inequality of the form (63),there exist non-negative vectors $\mathbf{m}\overset{\text{ def }}{ = }$ ${\left( {m}_{p}\right) }_{p \in  \left\lbrack  P\right\rbrack  }$ and $s\overset{\text{ def }}{ = }{\left( {s}_{q}\right) }_{q \in  \left\lbrack  Q\right\rbrack  }$ such that the following identity holds over symbolic variables $h\left( X\right)$ for $X \subseteq  \mathcal{V}$ where $h\left( \varnothing \right)  = 0$ :

根据命题E.4，对于形式如(63)的每个不等式，存在非负向量$\mathbf{m}\overset{\text{ def }}{ = }$${\left( {m}_{p}\right) }_{p \in  \left\lbrack  P\right\rbrack  }$和$s\overset{\text{ def }}{ = }{\left( {s}_{q}\right) }_{q \in  \left\lbrack  Q\right\rbrack  }$，使得以下恒等式在符号变量$h\left( X\right)$上对$X \subseteq  \mathcal{V}$成立，其中$h\left( \varnothing \right)  = 0$：

$$
\mathop{\sum }\limits_{{\ell  \in  \left\lbrack  L\right\rbrack  }}{\lambda }_{\ell }h\left( {U}_{\ell }\right)  + \mathop{\sum }\limits_{{j \in  \left\lbrack  J\right\rbrack  }}\left( {{\alpha }_{j}h\left( {{X}_{j} \mid  {G}_{j}}\right)  + {\beta }_{j}h\left( {{Y}_{j} \mid  {G}_{j}}\right)  + {\zeta }_{j}h\left( {{Z}_{j} \mid  {G}_{j}}\right)  + {\kappa }_{j}h\left( {G}_{j}\right) }\right)  = 
$$

$$
\mathop{\sum }\limits_{{j \in  \left\lbrack  J\right\rbrack  }}\left( {{\widehat{\alpha }}_{j}h\left( {{\mathbf{X}}_{j} \mid  {\mathbf{G}}_{j}}\right)  + {\widehat{\beta }}_{j}h\left( {{\mathbf{Y}}_{j} \mid  {\mathbf{G}}_{j}}\right)  + {\widehat{\zeta }}_{j}h\left( {{\mathbf{Z}}_{j} \mid  {\mathbf{G}}_{j}}\right)  + {\widehat{\kappa }}_{j}h\left( {\mathbf{G}}_{j}\right) }\right)  + \mathop{\sum }\limits_{{i \in  \left\lbrack  I\right\rbrack  }}{\widehat{w}}_{i}h\left( {{\mathbf{Y}}_{i} \mid  {\mathbf{X}}_{i}}\right) 
$$

$$
 - \mathop{\sum }\limits_{{p \in  \left\lbrack  P\right\rbrack  }}{m}_{p}h\left( {{Y}_{p} \mid  {\mathbf{X}}_{p}}\right)  - \mathop{\sum }\limits_{{q \in  \left\lbrack  Q\right\rbrack  }}{s}_{q}h\left( {{Y}_{q};{Z}_{q} \mid  {\mathbf{X}}_{q}}\right)  \tag{65}
$$

Proof of THEOREM E.8. An inequality of the form (54) can be converted to the from (63) by initializing ${\widehat{w}}_{i} = {w}_{i}$ for all $i \in  \left\lbrack  I\right\rbrack$ and ${\widehat{\alpha }}_{j} = {\widehat{\beta }}_{j} = {\widehat{\zeta }}_{j} = {\widehat{\kappa }}_{j} = 0$ for all $j \in  \left\lbrack  J\right\rbrack$ . Consider an inequality of the form (63) and the corresponding identity (65). If $\parallel \mathbf{\lambda }{\parallel }_{1} + \parallel \mathbf{\kappa } - \widehat{\mathbf{\kappa }}{\parallel }_{1} = 0$ ,then by Condition (64), we have ${\widehat{\alpha }}_{j} = {\alpha }_{j},{\widehat{\beta }}_{j} = {\beta }_{j},{\widehat{\zeta }}_{j} = {\zeta }_{j}$ and ${\widehat{\kappa }}_{j} = {\kappa }_{j}$ for all $j$ and we are done. Now assume that $\parallel \lambda {\parallel }_{1} + \parallel \kappa  - \widehat{\kappa }{\parallel }_{1} > 0$ . By Proposition E.5,we have

定理E.8的证明。形式如(54)的不等式可通过初始化${\widehat{w}}_{i} = {w}_{i}$（对所有$i \in  \left\lbrack  I\right\rbrack$）和${\widehat{\alpha }}_{j} = {\widehat{\beta }}_{j} = {\widehat{\zeta }}_{j} = {\widehat{\kappa }}_{j} = 0$（对所有$j \in  \left\lbrack  J\right\rbrack$）转换为形式(63)。考虑形式(63)的不等式及对应恒等式(65)。若$\parallel \mathbf{\lambda }{\parallel }_{1} + \parallel \mathbf{\kappa } - \widehat{\mathbf{\kappa }}{\parallel }_{1} = 0$，则根据条件(64)，对所有$j$有${\widehat{\alpha }}_{j} = {\alpha }_{j},{\widehat{\beta }}_{j} = {\beta }_{j},{\widehat{\zeta }}_{j} = {\zeta }_{j}$和${\widehat{\kappa }}_{j} = {\kappa }_{j}$，证毕。现假设$\parallel \lambda {\parallel }_{1} + \parallel \kappa  - \widehat{\kappa }{\parallel }_{1} > 0$。根据命题E.5，我们有

$$
\mathop{\sum }\limits_{{i \in  \left\lbrack  I\right\rbrack   \mid  {X}_{i} = \varnothing }}{\widehat{w}}_{i} \geq  \parallel \lambda {\parallel }_{1} + \parallel \mathbf{\kappa } - \widehat{\mathbf{\kappa }}{\parallel }_{1} > 0.
$$

Hence,there exists some ${i}_{0} \in  \left\lbrack  I\right\rbrack$ such that ${\mathbf{X}}_{{i}_{0}} = \varnothing$ and ${\widehat{w}}_{{i}_{0}} > 0$ . Define $\mathbf{W}\overset{\text{ def }}{ = }{Y}_{{i}_{0}}$ . We recognize the following cases:

因此，存在某个${i}_{0} \in  \left\lbrack  I\right\rbrack$使得${\mathbf{X}}_{{i}_{0}} = \varnothing$且${\widehat{w}}_{{i}_{0}} > 0$。定义$\mathbf{W}\overset{\text{ def }}{ = }{Y}_{{i}_{0}}$。我们识别以下情形：

- If there exists $\ell  \in  \left\lbrack  L\right\rbrack$ where ${\mathbf{U}}_{\ell } = \mathbf{W}$ and ${\lambda }_{\ell } > 0$ ,then we reduce both ${\lambda }_{\ell }$ and ${\widehat{w}}_{{i}_{0}}$ by one thus canceling out the term $h\left( \mathbf{W}\right)$ from both sides of the identity (65).

- 若存在$\ell  \in  \left\lbrack  L\right\rbrack$其中${\mathbf{U}}_{\ell } = \mathbf{W}$且${\lambda }_{\ell } > 0$，则将${\lambda }_{\ell }$和${\widehat{w}}_{{i}_{0}}$各减一，从而消去恒等式(65)两侧的$h\left( \mathbf{W}\right)$项。

- If there exists $j \in  \left\lbrack  J\right\rbrack$ where ${\mathbf{G}}_{j}{\mathbf{X}}_{j} = \mathbf{W}$ and ${\alpha }_{j} > {\widehat{\alpha }}_{j}$ ,then we apply the following decomposition step to the term $h\left( {{Y}_{{i}_{0}} \mid  {X}_{{i}_{0}}}\right)  = h\left( {W \mid  \varnothing }\right)$ on the RHS of (65):

- 若存在$j \in  \left\lbrack  J\right\rbrack$其中${\mathbf{G}}_{j}{\mathbf{X}}_{j} = \mathbf{W}$且${\alpha }_{j} > {\widehat{\alpha }}_{j}$，则对(65)式右侧的$h\left( {{Y}_{{i}_{0}} \mid  {X}_{{i}_{0}}}\right)  = h\left( {W \mid  \varnothing }\right)$项应用如下分解步骤：

$$
h\left( \mathbf{W}\right)  \rightarrow  h\left( {\mathbf{G}}_{j}\right)  + h\left( {{\mathbf{X}}_{j} \mid  {\mathbf{G}}_{j}}\right) 
$$

and now we have $h\left( {{X}_{j} \mid  {G}_{j}}\right)$ on both sides of the identity (65). We increase ${\widehat{\alpha }}_{j}$ by one thus pairing the two terms $h\left( {{\mathbf{X}}_{j} \mid  {\mathbf{G}}_{j}}\right)$ with one another. Inequality (64) continues to hold.

此时恒等式(65)两侧均含$h\left( {{X}_{j} \mid  {G}_{j}}\right)$。我们将${\widehat{\alpha }}_{j}$增加一，从而使两个$h\left( {{\mathbf{X}}_{j} \mid  {\mathbf{G}}_{j}}\right)$项相互配对。不等式(64)仍成立。

- If there exists $j \in  \left\lbrack  J\right\rbrack$ where either ${G}_{j}{Y}_{j} = W$ and ${\beta }_{j} > {\widehat{\beta }}_{j}$ ,or ${G}_{j}{Z}_{j} = W$ and ${\zeta }_{j} > {\widehat{\zeta }}_{j}$ ,then this is similar to the previous case.

- 若存在$j \in  \left\lbrack  J\right\rbrack$使得${G}_{j}{Y}_{j} = W$与${\beta }_{j} > {\widehat{\beta }}_{j}$，或${G}_{j}{Z}_{j} = W$与${\zeta }_{j} > {\widehat{\zeta }}_{j}$成立，则此情形类似于前例。

- If there exists $j \in  \left\lbrack  J\right\rbrack$ where ${\mathbf{G}}_{j} = \mathbf{W}$ and ${\kappa }_{j} - {\widehat{\kappa }}_{j} > \max \left( {{\alpha }_{j} - {\widehat{\alpha }}_{j},{\beta }_{j} - {\widehat{\beta }}_{j},{\zeta }_{j} - {\widehat{\zeta }}_{j}}\right)$ ,then we have $h\left( W\right)$ on both sides of identity (65). We increase ${\widehat{\kappa }}_{j}$ by one thus pairing the two terms $h\left( {G}_{j}\right)$ with one another. Condition (64) continues to hold. On the other hand,if ${G}_{j} = W$ and ${\kappa }_{j} - {\widehat{\kappa }}_{j} = \max \left( {{\alpha }_{j} - {\widehat{\alpha }}_{j},{\beta }_{j} - {\widehat{\beta }}_{j},{\zeta }_{j} - {\widehat{\zeta }}_{j}}\right)$ ,then $h\left( {\mathbf{G}}_{j}\right)$ already cancels out with one of the three terms $h\left( {{\mathbf{X}}_{j} \mid  {\mathbf{G}}_{j}}\right) ,h\left( {{\mathbf{Y}}_{j} \mid  {\mathbf{G}}_{j}}\right)$ ,and $h\left( {{\mathbf{Z}}_{j} \mid  {\mathbf{G}}_{j}}\right)$ on the LHS of (65),hence this case falls under the next case.

- 若存在$j \in  \left\lbrack  J\right\rbrack$使得${\mathbf{G}}_{j} = \mathbf{W}$与${\kappa }_{j} - {\widehat{\kappa }}_{j} > \max \left( {{\alpha }_{j} - {\widehat{\alpha }}_{j},{\beta }_{j} - {\widehat{\beta }}_{j},{\zeta }_{j} - {\widehat{\zeta }}_{j}}\right)$成立，则恒等式(65)两侧均有$h\left( W\right)$。我们将${\widehat{\kappa }}_{j}$增加1从而使两项$h\left( {G}_{j}\right)$相互配对，条件(64)仍保持成立。反之，若${G}_{j} = W$与${\kappa }_{j} - {\widehat{\kappa }}_{j} = \max \left( {{\alpha }_{j} - {\widehat{\alpha }}_{j},{\beta }_{j} - {\widehat{\beta }}_{j},{\zeta }_{j} - {\widehat{\zeta }}_{j}}\right)$成立，则$h\left( {\mathbf{G}}_{j}\right)$已与(65)式左侧的三个项$h\left( {{\mathbf{X}}_{j} \mid  {\mathbf{G}}_{j}}\right) ,h\left( {{\mathbf{Y}}_{j} \mid  {\mathbf{G}}_{j}}\right)$及$h\left( {{\mathbf{Z}}_{j} \mid  {\mathbf{G}}_{j}}\right)$之一相抵消，故此情形归入下一种情况。

- In all other cases,the term $h\left( W\right)$ must cancel out with some other term on the RHS of the identity (65). We recognize three cases similar to the original proof sequence construction from $\left\lbrack  {3,4}\right\rbrack$ :

- 其余所有情形中，项$h\left( W\right)$必与恒等式(65)右侧的某个其他项相抵消。我们识别出三种情形，类似于$\left\lbrack  {3,4}\right\rbrack$的原始证明序列构建：

- Case 1: $h\left( W\right)$ cancels out with some other term $h\left( {{Y}_{i} \mid  {X}_{i}}\right)$ for some $i \in  \left\lbrack  I\right\rbrack$ on the RHS where ${\mathbf{X}}_{i} = \mathbf{W}$ . In this case,we can compose the two terms using the rewrite from Eq. (59),which corresponds to a composition step:

- 情形1：$h\left( W\right)$与右侧满足${\mathbf{X}}_{i} = \mathbf{W}$的某个$i \in  \left\lbrack  I\right\rbrack$对应项$h\left( {{Y}_{i} \mid  {X}_{i}}\right)$相抵消。此时可通过式(59)的重写规则组合这两项，对应组合步骤：

$$
h\left( \mathbf{W}\right)  + h\left( {{Y}_{i} \mid  \mathbf{W}}\right)  \rightarrow  h\left( {{Y}_{i}\mathbf{W}}\right) 
$$

(Composition Step)

(组合步骤)

- Case 2: $h\left( W\right)$ cancels out with some term $- h\left( {{Y}_{p} \mid  {X}_{p}}\right)$ for some $p \in  \left\lbrack  P\right\rbrack$ where $W = {X}_{p}{Y}_{p}$ . In this case, we can apply the rewrite from Eq. (60), which corresponds to a monotonicity step:

- 情形2：$h\left( W\right)$与满足$W = {X}_{p}{Y}_{p}$的某个$p \in  \left\lbrack  P\right\rbrack$对应项$- h\left( {{Y}_{p} \mid  {X}_{p}}\right)$相抵消。此时可应用式(60)的重写规则，对应单调性步骤：

$$
h\left( W\right)  \rightarrow  h\left( {X}_{p}\right) 
$$

(Monotonicity Step)

(单调性步骤)

- Case 3: $h\left( W\right)$ cancels out with some term $- h\left( {{Y}_{q};{Z}_{q} \mid  {X}_{q}}\right)$ for some $q \in  \left\lbrack  Q\right\rbrack$ where $W = {X}_{q}{Y}_{q}$ . In this case, instead of applying the rewrite from Eq. (61), we apply the following rewrite:

- 情形3：$h\left( W\right)$与满足$W = {X}_{q}{Y}_{q}$的某个$q \in  \left\lbrack  Q\right\rbrack$对应项$- h\left( {{Y}_{q};{Z}_{q} \mid  {X}_{q}}\right)$相抵消。此时不采用式(61)的重写规则，而改用以下重写：

$$
h\left( \mathbf{W}\right)  - h\left( {{Y}_{q};{Z}_{q} \mid  {\mathbf{X}}_{q}}\right)  = h\left( \mathbf{W}\right)  - h\left( {{\mathbf{X}}_{q}{Y}_{q}}\right)  - h\left( {{\mathbf{X}}_{q}{Z}_{q}}\right)  + h\left( {\mathbf{X}}_{q}\right)  + h\left( {{\mathbf{X}}_{q}{Y}_{q}{Z}_{q}}\right) 
$$

$$
 = h\left( {\mathbf{X}}_{q}\right)  + h\left( {{\mathbf{Y}}_{q} \mid  {\mathbf{X}}_{q}{\mathbf{Z}}_{q}}\right)  \tag{66}
$$

The above rewrite corresponds to applying a decomposition step followed by a submodularity step:

上述重写对应执行分解步骤后接子模性步骤：

$$
h\left( W\right)  \rightarrow  h\left( {X}_{q}\right)  + h\left( {{Y}_{q} \mid  {X}_{q}}\right) 
$$

(Decomposition Step)

(分解步骤)

$$
h\left( {{Y}_{q} \mid  {X}_{q}}\right)  \rightarrow  h\left( {{Y}_{q} \mid  {X}_{q}{Z}_{q}}\right) 
$$

(Submodularity Step)

(子模性步骤)

In each one of the above three cases, the following integral quantity always decreases by at least one, thus proving that this process must terminate:

在上述三种情形中，下列整数量每次至少减少1，由此证明该过程必然终止：

$$
\parallel \widehat{\mathbf{w}}{\parallel }_{1} + \parallel \mathbf{m}{\parallel }_{1} + 2\parallel \mathbf{s}{\parallel }_{1} \tag{67}
$$

In particular,Case 1 decreases $\parallel \widehat{\mathbf{w}}{\parallel }_{1}$ by 1,Case 2 decreases $\parallel \mathbf{m}{\parallel }_{1}$ by 1,and Case 3 increases $\parallel \widehat{\mathbf{w}}{\parallel }_{1}$ by 1 and decreases $\parallel \mathbf{s}{\parallel }_{1}$ by 1 .

具体而言，情况1使$\parallel \widehat{\mathbf{w}}{\parallel }_{1}$减少1，情况2使$\parallel \mathbf{m}{\parallel }_{1}$减少1，情况3使$\parallel \widehat{\mathbf{w}}{\parallel }_{1}$增加1同时使$\parallel \mathbf{s}{\parallel }_{1}$减少1。

### E.5 Generalizing PANDA for Disjunctive Datalog Rules

### E.5 泛化PANDA算法用于析取Datalog规则

Consider a Boolean conjunctive query $Q$ of the form given by Eq. (1). Our algorithms for evaluating $Q$ rely heavily on the concept of degree in a relation,that is defined below.

考虑一个形如公式(1)的布尔合取查询$Q$。我们的查询评估算法高度依赖于关系中度的概念，其定义如下。

Definition E. 9 (Degree in a relation). Let $R\left( Z\right)$ be a relation over variable set $Z$ ,and let $X$ and $Y$ be two sets of variables such that $Y \smallsetminus  X \subseteq  Z$ . Given a tuple $x$ over the variable set $X$ ,we define the degree of $Y$ conditioned on $X = x$ in $R$ as follows:

定义E.9（关系中的度）。设$R\left( Z\right)$是变量集$Z$上的关系，$X$和$Y$为两个变量子集且满足$Y \smallsetminus  X \subseteq  Z$。给定变量集$X$上的元组$x$，我们定义$R$中$X = x$条件下$Y$的度如下：

$$
{\deg }_{R}\left( {Y \mid  X = x}\right) \overset{\text{ def }}{ = }\left| {{\pi }_{Y \smallsetminus  X}\left( {{\sigma }_{X \cap  Z = {\pi }_{X \cap  Z}\left( x\right) }\left( R\right) }\right) }\right|  \tag{68}
$$

Moreover,we define the degree of $Y$ conditioned on $X$ in $R$ as follows:

此外，我们定义$R$中$X$条件下$Y$的度如下：

$$
{\deg }_{R}\left( {\mathbf{Y} \mid  \mathbf{X}}\right) \overset{\text{ def }}{ = }\mathop{\max }\limits_{\mathbf{x}}{\deg }_{R}\left( {\mathbf{Y} \mid  \mathbf{X} = \mathbf{x}}\right)  \tag{69}
$$

As a building block for the next section which presents our algorithm for evaluating a query $Q$ in $\omega$ -submodular width time,we present in this section an algorithm for evaluating a specific type of disjunctive Datalog rules $\left\lbrack  {3,4}\right\rbrack$ . We can think of this algorithm as being concerned with evaluating a query ${Q}_{ \vee  }$ that has a disjunction in the head:

作为下一节的基础构件——该节将展示我们在$\omega$-次模宽度时间内评估查询$Q$的算法，本节提出一种评估特定类型析取Datalog规则$\left\lbrack  {3,4}\right\rbrack$的算法。可将该算法视为评估头部包含析取项的查询${Q}_{ \vee  }$：

$$
\mathop{\bigvee }\limits_{{\ell  \in  \left\lbrack  L\right\rbrack  }}{P}_{\ell }\left( {\mathbf{U}}_{\ell }\right)  \vee  \mathop{\bigvee }\limits_{{j \in  \left\lbrack  J\right\rbrack  }}\left( {{S}_{j}\left( {{\mathbf{X}}_{j}{\mathbf{G}}_{j}}\right)  \land  {T}_{j}\left( {{\mathbf{Y}}_{j}{\mathbf{G}}_{j}}\right)  \land  {W}_{j}\left( {{\mathbf{Z}}_{j}{\mathbf{G}}_{g}}\right) }\right) \; :  - \mathop{\bigwedge }\limits_{{R\left( Z\right)  \in  \operatorname{atoms}\left( Q\right) }}R\left( Z\right)  \tag{70}
$$

Evaluating the above query means computing output tables ${P}_{\ell }\left( {U}_{\ell }\right)$ for $\ell  \in  \left\lbrack  L\right\rbrack$ ,and ${S}_{j}\left( {{X}_{j}{G}_{j}}\right)$ , ${T}_{j}\left( {{Y}_{j}{G}_{j}}\right) ,{W}_{j}\left( {{Z}_{j}{G}_{j}}\right)$ for $j \in  \left\lbrack  J\right\rbrack$ so for that every tuple $t$ in the join of all atoms in the body of the query,the projection of $t$ must be present in either some output table ${P}_{\ell }$ or some triple of output tables $\left( {{S}_{j},{T}_{j},{W}_{j}}\right)$ .

评估上述查询意味着需要为$\ell  \in  \left\lbrack  L\right\rbrack$计算输出表${P}_{\ell }\left( {U}_{\ell }\right)$，为$j \in  \left\lbrack  J\right\rbrack$计算输出表${S}_{j}\left( {{X}_{j}{G}_{j}}\right)$和${T}_{j}\left( {{Y}_{j}{G}_{j}}\right) ,{W}_{j}\left( {{Z}_{j}{G}_{j}}\right)$，使得对于查询体中所有原子连接生成的每个元组$t$，其投影必须存在于某个输出表${P}_{\ell }$或某个输出表三元组$\left( {{S}_{j},{T}_{j},{W}_{j}}\right)$中。

THEOREM E.10 (EVALUATING A DISJUNCTIVE RULE (70)). Suppose we are given the following:

定理E.10（析取规则评估(70)）。假设给定以下条件：

- A Boolean query $Q$ of the form (1) along with a corresponding input database instance $D$ .

- 形如(1)的布尔查询$Q$及其对应输入数据库实例$D$

- An integral $\omega$ -Shannon inequality of the form (54) where for every $i \in  \left\lbrack  I\right\rbrack$ ,there exists an atom ${R}_{i}\left( {Z}_{i}\right)  \in  \operatorname{atoms}\left( Q\right)$ that satisfies ${Y}_{i} \smallsetminus  {X}_{i} \subseteq  {Z}_{i}$ .

- 形如(54)的整数型$\omega$-香农不等式，其中对于每个$i \in  \left\lbrack  I\right\rbrack$，存在满足${Y}_{i} \smallsetminus  {X}_{i} \subseteq  {Z}_{i}$的原子${R}_{i}\left( {Z}_{i}\right)  \in  \operatorname{atoms}\left( Q\right)$

Define the quantity:

定义量值：

$$
\text{obj}\overset{\text{ def }}{ = }\frac{\mathop{\sum }\limits_{{i \in  \left\lbrack  I\right\rbrack  }}{w}_{i}{\log }_{2}{\deg }_{{R}_{i}}\left( {{Y}_{i} \mid  {X}_{i}}\right) }{\parallel \lambda {\parallel }_{1} + \parallel \kappa {\parallel }_{1}} \tag{71}
$$

Then,there is an algorithm that runs in time $\widetilde{O}\left( {2}^{\text{obj }}\right)$ and computes tables ${P}_{\ell }\left( {U}_{\ell }\right)$ for $\ell  \in  \left\lbrack  L\right\rbrack$ and tables $\left( {{S}_{j}\left( {{X}_{j}{G}_{j}}\right) ,{T}_{j}\left( {{Y}_{j}{G}_{j}}\right) ,{W}_{j}\left( {{Z}_{j}{G}_{j}}\right) }\right)$ for $j \in  \left\lbrack  J\right\rbrack$ that satisfy the following:

则存在一个算法在$\widetilde{O}\left( {2}^{\text{obj }}\right)$时间内运行，计算出满足以下条件的表：为$\ell  \in  \left\lbrack  L\right\rbrack$生成表${P}_{\ell }\left( {U}_{\ell }\right)$，为$j \in  \left\lbrack  J\right\rbrack$生成表$\left( {{S}_{j}\left( {{X}_{j}{G}_{j}}\right) ,{T}_{j}\left( {{Y}_{j}{G}_{j}}\right) ,{W}_{j}\left( {{Z}_{j}{G}_{j}}\right) }\right)$：

- For each tuple $t \in  { \bowtie  }_{R\left( Z\right)  \in  \operatorname{atoms}\left( Q\right) }R\left( Z\right)$ :

- 对于每个元组$t \in  { \bowtie  }_{R\left( Z\right)  \in  \operatorname{atoms}\left( Q\right) }R\left( Z\right)$：

- either there exists $\ell  \in  \left\lbrack  L\right\rbrack$ where ${\pi }_{{U}_{\ell }}\left( t\right)  \in  {P}_{\ell }$ .

- 存在$\ell  \in  \left\lbrack  L\right\rbrack$使得${\pi }_{{U}_{\ell }}\left( t\right)  \in  {P}_{\ell }$。

- or there exists $j \in  \left\lbrack  J\right\rbrack$ where ${\pi }_{{X}_{j}{G}_{j}}\left( t\right)  \in  {S}_{j},{\pi }_{{Y}_{j}{G}_{j}}\left( t\right)  \in  {T}_{j}$ ,and ${\pi }_{{Z}_{j}{G}_{j}}\left( t\right)  \in  {W}_{j}$ .

- 或者存在$j \in  \left\lbrack  J\right\rbrack$使得${\pi }_{{X}_{j}{G}_{j}}\left( t\right)  \in  {S}_{j},{\pi }_{{Y}_{j}{G}_{j}}\left( t\right)  \in  {T}_{j}$，且${\pi }_{{Z}_{j}{G}_{j}}\left( t\right)  \in  {W}_{j}$。

- For each $\ell  \in  \left\lbrack  L\right\rbrack$ ,we have $\left| {P}_{\ell }\right|  = \widetilde{O}\left( {2}^{\text{obj }}\right)$ .

- 对于每个$\ell  \in  \left\lbrack  L\right\rbrack$，我们有$\left| {P}_{\ell }\right|  = \widetilde{O}\left( {2}^{\text{obj }}\right)$。

- For each $j \in  \left\lbrack  J\right\rbrack$ ,we have $\left| {S}_{j}\right| ,\left| {T}_{j}\right| ,\left| {W}_{j}\right|  = \widetilde{O}\left( {2}^{\text{obj }}\right)$ and there exists an $\omega$ -dominant triple $\left( {{\alpha }_{j}^{\prime },{\beta }_{j}^{\prime },{\zeta }_{j}^{\prime }}\right)$ such that

- 对于每个$j \in  \left\lbrack  J\right\rbrack$，我们有$\left| {S}_{j}\right| ,\left| {T}_{j}\right| ,\left| {W}_{j}\right|  = \widetilde{O}\left( {2}^{\text{obj }}\right)$且存在一个$\omega$主导的三元组$\left( {{\alpha }_{j}^{\prime },{\beta }_{j}^{\prime },{\zeta }_{j}^{\prime }}\right)$满足

$$
\left| {{\pi }_{{G}_{j}}\left( {S}_{j}\right) }\right|  \cdot  {\left( {\deg }_{{S}_{j}}\left( {\mathbf{X}}_{j} \mid  {\mathbf{G}}_{j}\right) \right) }^{{\alpha }_{j}^{\prime }} \cdot  {\left( {\deg }_{{T}_{j}}\left( {\mathbf{Y}}_{j} \mid  {\mathbf{G}}_{j}\right) \right) }^{{\beta }_{j}^{\prime }} \cdot  {\left( {\deg }_{{W}_{j}}\left( {\mathbf{Z}}_{j} \mid  {\mathbf{G}}_{j}\right) \right) }^{{\zeta }_{j}^{\prime }} = \widetilde{O}\left( {2}^{\text{obj }}\right) . \tag{72}
$$

Proof. The algorithm is a variant of the PANDA algorithm from [3, 4]. The quantity obj is a constant that is fixed at the beginning of the algorithm. The execution of the algorithm branches into a polylogarithmic number of branches, each of which is concerned with a different part of the data. Throughout the algorithm,and on each branch,we will modify the integral $\omega$ -Shannon inequality (54) and we will also update the tables ${\left( {R}_{i}\left( {Z}_{i}\right) \right) }_{i \in  \left\lbrack  I\right\rbrack  }$ that are associated with terms $h\left( {{Y}_{i} \mid  {X}_{i}}\right)$ on the RHS of inequality (54). However,on each branch,we will always maintain the following invariants:

证明。该算法是文献[3,4]中PANDA算法的变体。目标值obj是算法开始时设定的常数。算法执行会分支出对数多项式数量的分支，每个分支处理数据的不同部分。在整个算法过程中，每个分支都会修改积分$\omega$-香农不等式(54)，并更新与不等式(54)右侧项$h\left( {{Y}_{i} \mid  {X}_{i}}\right)$关联的表${\left( {R}_{i}\left( {Z}_{i}\right) \right) }_{i \in  \left\lbrack  I\right\rbrack  }$。但每个分支始终维持以下不变量：

(1) The current inequality is a valid integral $\omega$ -Shannon inequality (54).

(1) 当前不等式是有效的积分$\omega$-香农不等式(54)

(2) Every term $h\left( {{Y}_{i} \mid  {X}_{i}}\right)$ on the RHS of the inequality (54) has an associated table ${R}_{i}\left( {Z}_{i}\right)$ that satisfies ${Y}_{i} \smallsetminus  {X}_{i} \subseteq  {Z}_{i}$ and

(2) 不等式(54)右侧的每个项$h\left( {{Y}_{i} \mid  {X}_{i}}\right)$都有关联表${R}_{i}\left( {Z}_{i}\right)$满足${Y}_{i} \smallsetminus  {X}_{i} \subseteq  {Z}_{i}$且

$$
\left| {R}_{i}\right|  \leq  {2}^{\text{obj }} \tag{73}
$$

(3) The inequality satisfies:

(3) 该不等式满足：

$$
\parallel \lambda {\parallel }_{1} + \parallel \kappa {\parallel }_{1} > 0 \tag{74}
$$

(4) The inequality along with the associated tables satisfy:

(4) 不等式及其关联表满足：

$$
\frac{\mathop{\sum }\limits_{{i \in  \left\lbrack  I\right\rbrack  }}{w}_{i}{\log }_{2}{\deg }_{{R}_{i}}\left( {{Y}_{i} \mid  {X}_{i}}\right) }{\parallel \lambda {\parallel }_{1} + \parallel \kappa {\parallel }_{1}} \leq  \text{obj} \tag{75}
$$

On every branch, the algorithm follows the proof sequence construction from Theorem E.8. For that purpose,we represent the $\omega$ -Shannon inequality (54) using the representation from Eq. (63). Every proof step replaces one or two terms $h\left( {{Y}_{i} \mid  {X}_{i}}\right)$ on the RHS of the inequality with one or two new terms that are smaller. Simultaneously,the algorithm replaces the tables ${R}_{i}\left( {Z}_{i}\right)$ that are associated with the old terms with new tables that are associated with the new terms, where these new tables are produced through some algorithmic operation on the old ones. WLOG we can assume that on every branch,and for every $Z \subseteq  \mathcal{V}$ ,there is at most one table $R\left( Z\right)$ . This is because if we have multiple tables $R\left( Z\right)$ on the same branch,we can always take their intersection to obtain a single table $R\left( Z\right)$ that achieves the minimum degree ${\deg }_{R}\left( {Y \mid  X}\right)$ among all these tables for every $X$ and $Y$ . The algorithm terminates for a specific branch if either one of the following two conditions is met:

每个分支上，算法遵循定理E.8的证明序列构造。为此，我们使用式(63)的表示法来表达$\omega$-香农不等式(54)。每个证明步骤会用更小的一个或两个新项替换不等式右侧的一个或两个旧项$h\left( {{Y}_{i} \mid  {X}_{i}}\right)$，同时通过旧表的算法操作生成新表${R}_{i}\left( {Z}_{i}\right)$关联到新项。不失一般性，可假设每个分支上对于每个$Z \subseteq  \mathcal{V}$至多存在一个表$R\left( Z\right)$。因为若同分支存在多个表$R\left( Z\right)$，总可以取其交集得到单一表$R\left( Z\right)$，使得对于所有$X$和$Y$都能达到最小度${\deg }_{R}\left( {Y \mid  X}\right)$。当满足以下任一条件时，算法在特定分支终止：

(a) There exists some $\ell  \in  \left\lbrack  L\right\rbrack$ and $i \in  \left\lbrack  I\right\rbrack$ where ${\lambda }_{\ell },{\widehat{w}}_{i} > 0,{Y}_{i} = {U}_{\ell },{X}_{i} = \varnothing$ . In this case, invariant (73) implies that the associated table,denoted ${P}_{\ell }\left( {\mathbf{U}}_{\ell }\right) \overset{\text{ def }}{ = }{R}_{i}\left( {\mathbf{U}}_{\ell }\right)$ ,has size $\left| {P}_{\ell }\right|  \leq  {2}^{\text{obj }}$ .

(a) 存在某个$\ell  \in  \left\lbrack  L\right\rbrack$和$i \in  \left\lbrack  I\right\rbrack$使得${\lambda }_{\ell },{\widehat{w}}_{i} > 0,{Y}_{i} = {U}_{\ell },{X}_{i} = \varnothing$。此时，不变量(73)表明关联表（记作${P}_{\ell }\left( {\mathbf{U}}_{\ell }\right) \overset{\text{ def }}{ = }{R}_{i}\left( {\mathbf{U}}_{\ell }\right)$）的大小为$\left| {P}_{\ell }\right|  \leq  {2}^{\text{obj }}$。

(b) There exists some $j \in  \left\lbrack  J\right\rbrack$ where ${\widehat{\alpha }}_{j} = {\alpha }_{j},{\widehat{\beta }}_{j} = {\beta }_{j},{\widehat{\zeta }}_{j} = {\zeta }_{j},{\widehat{\kappa }}_{j} = {\kappa }_{j}$ and the associated tables,denoted ${S}_{j}\left( {{X}_{j}{G}_{j}}\right) ,{T}_{j}\left( {{Y}_{j}G}\right)$ ,and ${W}_{j}\left( {{Z}_{j}{G}_{j}}\right)$ ,satisfy the following: (WLOG we can assume that ${\pi }_{{G}_{j}}\left( {S}_{j}\right)  = {\pi }_{{G}_{j}}\left( {T}_{j}\right)  = {\pi }_{{G}_{j}}\left( {W}_{j}\right)$ because we can always semijoin reduce the tables together.)

(b) 存在某个$j \in  \left\lbrack  J\right\rbrack$使得${\widehat{\alpha }}_{j} = {\alpha }_{j},{\widehat{\beta }}_{j} = {\beta }_{j},{\widehat{\zeta }}_{j} = {\zeta }_{j},{\widehat{\kappa }}_{j} = {\kappa }_{j}$，且关联表（记作${S}_{j}\left( {{X}_{j}{G}_{j}}\right) ,{T}_{j}\left( {{Y}_{j}G}\right)$与${W}_{j}\left( {{Z}_{j}{G}_{j}}\right)$）满足以下条件：（根据无损失一般性原则，可假定${\pi }_{{G}_{j}}\left( {S}_{j}\right)  = {\pi }_{{G}_{j}}\left( {T}_{j}\right)  = {\pi }_{{G}_{j}}\left( {W}_{j}\right)$，因总可通过半连接操作对表进行约简）

$$
\left| {{\pi }_{{G}_{j}}\left( {S}_{j}\right) }\right|  \cdot  {\left( {\deg }_{{S}_{j}}\left( {X}_{j} \mid  {G}_{j}\right) \right) }^{\frac{{\alpha }_{j}}{{\kappa }_{j}}} \cdot  {\left( {\deg }_{{T}_{j}}\left( {Y}_{j} \mid  {G}_{j}\right) \right) }^{\frac{{\beta }_{j}}{{\kappa }_{j}}} \cdot  {\left( {\deg }_{{W}_{j}}\left( {Z}_{j} \mid  {G}_{j}\right) \right) }^{\frac{{\zeta }_{j}}{{\kappa }_{j}}} \leq  {2}^{\text{obj }} \tag{76}
$$

(Recall that because the inequality is an $\omega$ -Shannon inequality,the triple $\left( {\frac{{\alpha }_{j}}{{\kappa }_{j}},\frac{{\beta }_{j}}{{\kappa }_{j}},\frac{{\zeta }_{j}}{{\kappa }_{j}}}\right)$ must be $\omega$ -dominant.) The final output of the algorithm is the union of the tables ${P}_{\ell },{S}_{j},{T}_{j}$ and ${W}_{j}$ from all branches. In contrast, if condition (b) above is met except that inequality (76) is violated, then we drop the term $j$ from both sides of the inequality. In this case,both invariants from Eq. (74) and Eq. (75) continue to hold. In particular, Eq. (75) continues to hold because on the LHS, the denominator is reduced by ${\kappa }_{j}$ while the numerator is reduced by:

（注意由于该不等式是$\omega$-香农不等式，三元组$\left( {\frac{{\alpha }_{j}}{{\kappa }_{j}},\frac{{\beta }_{j}}{{\kappa }_{j}},\frac{{\zeta }_{j}}{{\kappa }_{j}}}\right)$必须具有$\omega$-支配性。）算法最终输出是所有分支中表${P}_{\ell },{S}_{j},{T}_{j}$与${W}_{j}$的并集。反之，若满足条件(b)但违反不等式(76)，则从不等式两端移除$j$项。此时，式(74)与式(75)的不变性依然成立。特别地，式(75)仍成立是因为左式分母减少了${\kappa }_{j}$，而分子减少了：

$$
{\kappa }_{j}{\log }_{2}\left| {{\pi }_{{G}_{j}}\left( {S}_{j}\right) }\right|  + {\alpha }_{j}{\log }_{2}{\deg }_{{S}_{j}}\left( {{X}_{j} \mid  {G}_{j}}\right)  + {\beta }_{j}{\log }_{2}{\deg }_{{T}_{j}}\left( {{Y}_{j} \mid  {G}_{j}}\right)  + {\zeta }_{j}{\log }_{2}{\deg }_{{W}_{j}}\left( {{Z}_{j} \mid  {G}_{j}}\right)  > {\kappa }_{j} \cdot  \text{obj}
$$

Eq. (74) continues to hold because if the new denominator was zero, then the LHS of Eq. (75) before we dropped the $j$ -term must have been greater than $\frac{{\kappa }_{j} \cdot  \mathrm{{obj}}}{{\kappa }_{j}}$ ,hence Eq. (75) could not have been satisfied to begin with.

式(74)保持成立的原因是：若新分母为零，则移除$j$项前的式(75)左式必然大于$\frac{{\kappa }_{j} \cdot  \mathrm{{obj}}}{{\kappa }_{j}}$，故式(75)初始条件本就不成立。

Initially, all invariants are satisfied except possibly for Eq. (73). However, if Eq. (73) is violated, we can use the Reset Lemma E. 7 to drop the large relation ${R}_{i}$ along with the corresponding term $h\left( {{Y}_{i} \mid  {X}_{i}}\right)$ . Just like above,dropping ${R}_{i}$ preserves all invariants including Eq. (75) and Eq. (74).

初始状态下，除式(73)外所有不变量均已满足。若违反式(73)，可运用重置引理E.7移除大关系${R}_{i}$及其对应项$h\left( {{Y}_{i} \mid  {X}_{i}}\right)$。如前所述，移除${R}_{i}$后包括式(75)和式(74)在内的所有不变量均得以保持。

On every branch, the algorithm translates each proof step from Theorem E. 8 into an algorithmic operation, as described in the original PANDA algorithm. We summarize these operations below for self-containment:

在每个分支上，算法将定理E.8的每个证明步骤转化为算法操作，如原始PANDA算法所述。为保持完整性，我们将这些操作概述如下：

- Composition Step $h\left( X\right)  + h\left( {Y \mid  X}\right)  \rightarrow  h\left( {XY}\right)$ : Let $R$ and $S$ be the two tables associated with $h\left( \mathbf{X}\right)$ and $h\left( {\mathbf{Y} \mid  \mathbf{X}}\right)$ respectively. We recognize two cases:

- 组合步骤$h\left( X\right)  + h\left( {Y \mid  X}\right)  \rightarrow  h\left( {XY}\right)$：设$R$和$S$分别是与$h\left( \mathbf{X}\right)$和$h\left( {\mathbf{Y} \mid  \mathbf{X}}\right)$关联的两张表。我们识别两种情况：

- If $\left| R\right|  \cdot  {\deg }_{S}\left( {Y \mid  X}\right)  \leq  {2}^{\text{obj }}$ ,then we compute the join $T\overset{\text{ def }}{ = }R \bowtie  S$ . The new table $T$ is associated with the new term $h\left( {XY}\right)$ ,and it satisfies Eq. (73).

- 若$\left| R\right|  \cdot  {\deg }_{S}\left( {Y \mid  X}\right)  \leq  {2}^{\text{obj }}$成立，则计算连接运算$T\overset{\text{ def }}{ = }R \bowtie  S$。新表$T$关联新项$h\left( {XY}\right)$，且满足式(73)。

- If $\left| R\right|  \cdot  {\deg }_{S}\left( {Y \mid  X}\right)  > {2}^{\text{obj }}$ ,then we replace the two terms $h\left( X\right)  + h\left( {Y \mid  X}\right)$ with $h\left( {XY}\right)$ and then drop the term $h\left( {XY}\right)$ using the Reset Lemma (Lemma E.7). Invariant (75) continues to hold because on the LHS of (75), the denominator is reduced by at most one (thanks to Eq. (57)) while the numerator is reduced by ${\log }_{2}\left| R\right|  + {\log }_{2}{\deg }_{S}\left( {Y \mid  X}\right)  >$ obj. Invariant (74) continues to hold because if the new denominator was zero, then the LHS of Eq. (75) before the reset must have been greater than obj, hence invariant (75) could not have been satisfied to begin with.

- 若$\left| R\right|  \cdot  {\deg }_{S}\left( {Y \mid  X}\right)  > {2}^{\text{obj }}$成立，则用$h\left( {XY}\right)$替换两项$h\left( X\right)  + h\left( {Y \mid  X}\right)$，再运用重置引理（引理E.7）消去项$h\left( {XY}\right)$。不变式(75)仍成立，因为其左端分母最多减少1（由式(57）保证），而分子减少${\log }_{2}\left| R\right|  + {\log }_{2}{\deg }_{S}\left( {Y \mid  X}\right)  >$个对象。不变式(74)亦成立，因为若新分母为零，则重置前式(75)左端必大于对象，故不变式(75)初始即无法满足。

- Monotonicity Step $h\left( {XY}\right)  \rightarrow  h\left( X\right)$ : Let $R$ be the table associated with $h\left( {XY}\right)$ . We associate $R$ with the new term $h\left( X\right)$ . Because ${\deg }_{R}\left( {X \mid  \varnothing }\right)  \leq  {\deg }_{R}\left( {{XY} \mid  \varnothing }\right)$ ,invariant (75) continues to hold.

- 单调性步骤$h\left( {XY}\right)  \rightarrow  h\left( X\right)$：令$R$为关联$h\left( {XY}\right)$的表。将$R$关联至新项$h\left( X\right)$。由于${\deg }_{R}\left( {X \mid  \varnothing }\right)  \leq  {\deg }_{R}\left( {{XY} \mid  \varnothing }\right)$，不变式(75)保持成立。

- Submodularity Step $h\left( {Y \mid  X}\right)  \rightarrow  h\left( {Y \mid  {XZ}}\right)$ : Let $R$ be the table associated with $h\left( {Y \mid  X}\right)$ . We associate $R$ with the new term $h\left( {Y \mid  {XZ}}\right)$ . Invariant (75) holds because ${\deg }_{R}\left( {Y \mid  {XZ}}\right)  \leq  {\deg }_{R}\left( {Y \mid  X}\right)$ .

- 次模性步骤$h\left( {Y \mid  X}\right)  \rightarrow  h\left( {Y \mid  {XZ}}\right)$：令$R$为关联$h\left( {Y \mid  X}\right)$的表。将$R$关联至新项$h\left( {Y \mid  {XZ}}\right)$。因${\deg }_{R}\left( {Y \mid  {XZ}}\right)  \leq  {\deg }_{R}\left( {Y \mid  X}\right)$，不变式(75)成立。

- Decomposition Step $h\left( {XY}\right)  \rightarrow  h\left( X\right)  + h\left( {Y \mid  X}\right)$ : Let $R$ be the table associated with $h\left( {XY}\right)$ . We partition $R$ into $k\overset{\text{ def }}{ = }O\left( {{\log }_{2}\left| R\right| }\right)$ buckets ${R}^{1},\ldots ,{R}^{k}$ based on the degree of $Y$ conditioned on $X$ . In particular,bucket ${R}^{i}$ contains all tuples $\mathbf{t} \in  R$ such that ${\deg }_{R}\left( {Y \mid  \mathbf{X} = {\pi }_{X}\left( \mathbf{t}\right) }\right)  \in  \left\lbrack  {{2}^{i},{2}^{i + 1}}\right)$ . Now, within each bucket ${R}^{i}$ ,we have

- 分解步骤$h\left( {XY}\right)  \rightarrow  h\left( X\right)  + h\left( {Y \mid  X}\right)$：令$R$为关联$h\left( {XY}\right)$的表。根据$Y$在$X$条件下的度数，将$R$划分为$k\overset{\text{ def }}{ = }O\left( {{\log }_{2}\left| R\right| }\right)$个桶${R}^{1},\ldots ,{R}^{k}$。具体而言，桶${R}^{i}$包含所有满足${\deg }_{R}\left( {Y \mid  \mathbf{X} = {\pi }_{X}\left( \mathbf{t}\right) }\right)  \in  \left\lbrack  {{2}^{i},{2}^{i + 1}}\right)$的元组$\mathbf{t} \in  R$。在每个桶${R}^{i}$内部，存在

$$
\left| {{\pi }_{\mathbf{X}}\left( {R}^{i}\right) }\right|  \cdot  {\deg }_{{R}^{i}}\left( {\mathbf{Y} \mid  \mathbf{X}}\right)  \leq  2 \cdot  \left| {{\pi }_{\mathbf{X}\mathbf{Y}}\left( R\right) }\right|  \tag{77}
$$

If we partition each bucket ${R}^{i}$ into two sub-buckets,we can drop the factor of two from the above inequality. We create a new branch of the algorithm for each bucket ${R}^{i}$ . Within the $i$ -th branch,we associate the two new terms $h\left( X\right)$ and $h\left( {Y \mid  X}\right)$ with ${R}^{i}$ . Invariant (75) continues to hold because inequality (77) implies (after dropping the factor of two):

若将每个桶${R}^{i}$划分为两个子桶，则可从上述不等式中消去系数2。我们为每个桶${R}^{i}$创建算法的新分支。在第$i$个分支内，将两个新项$h\left( X\right)$和$h\left( {Y \mid  X}\right)$与${R}^{i}$关联。不变式(75)仍然成立，因为不等式(77)隐含（消去系数2后）：

$$
{\log }_{2}{\deg }_{{R}_{i}}\left( {\mathbf{X} \mid  \varnothing }\right)  + {\log }_{2}{\deg }_{{R}_{i}}\left( {\mathbf{Y} \mid  \mathbf{X}}\right)  \leq  {\log }_{2}{\deg }_{R}\left( {\mathbf{{XY}} \mid  \varnothing }\right) 
$$

### E.6 Proof of Theorem 7.1

### E.6 定理7.1的证明

We are now ready to prove Theorem 7.1 about answering Boolean conjunctive queries in $\omega$ - submodular width time.

现在我们可以证明关于在$\omega$-次模宽度时间内回答布尔连接查询的定理7.1。

THEOREM 7.1. Assuming that $\omega$ is a rational number,given a Boolean conjunctive query $Q$ and a corresponding input database instance $D$ ,there is an algorithm that computes the answer to $Q$ in time $\widetilde{O}\left( {N}^{\omega  - \operatorname{subw}\left( Q\right) }\right)$ ,where $N$ is the size of $D$ .

定理7.1. 假设$\omega$为有理数，给定布尔连接查询$Q$及对应的输入数据库实例$D$，存在算法可在$\widetilde{O}\left( {N}^{\omega  - \operatorname{subw}\left( Q\right) }\right)$时间内计算出$Q$的查询结果，其中$N$表示$D$的规模。

In order to prove Theorem 7.1, we will need some further preliminaries. The following lemma is very similar to a lemma in $\left\lbrack  {3,4}\right\rbrack$ . Its proof relies on the observation that every feasible dual solution to the linear program (49) corresponds to an $\omega$ -Shannon inequality (54) of a certain structure. By strong duality, if we pick an optimal dual solution, we can match the optimal objective value of the (primal) LP (49).

为证明定理7.1，我们需要若干预备知识。下述引理与$\left\lbrack  {3,4}\right\rbrack$中的引理高度相似，其证明基于以下观察：线性规划(49)的每个可行对偶解都对应某种结构的$\omega$-香农不等式(54)。根据强对偶性，若选取最优对偶解，则可匹配(原始)LP(49)的最优目标值。

LEMMA E.11 (From LP (49) to AN $\omega$ -SHANNON INEQUALITY (54)). Given a hypergraph $\mathcal{H} = \left( {\mathcal{V},\mathcal{E}}\right)$ , let $\mathrm{{ED}}\overset{\text{ def }}{ = }{\mathrm{{ED}}}_{\mathcal{H}}$ ,and consider a linear program of the following form (which is the same as ${LP}\left( {49}\right)$ but where we drop the index $i \in  \left\lbrack  \mathcal{I}\right\rbrack$ to reduce clutter):

引理E.11(从LP(49)到$\omega$-香农不等式(54))。给定超图$\mathcal{H} = \left( {\mathcal{V},\mathcal{E}}\right)$，令$\mathrm{{ED}}\overset{\text{ def }}{ = }{\mathrm{{ED}}}_{\mathcal{H}}$，考虑如下形式的线性规划(与${LP}\left( {49}\right)$相同，但省略索引$i \in  \left\lbrack  \mathcal{I}\right\rbrack$以简化表达式)：

$$
\mathop{\max }\limits_{{t,h \in  \Gamma  \cap  \mathrm{{ED}}}}\left\{  {t\; \mid  \;\forall \ell  \in  \left\lbrack  L\right\rbrack  ,\;t \leq  h\left( {U}_{\ell }\right) ,}\right. 
$$

$$
\forall j \in  \left\lbrack  J\right\rbrack  ,\;t \leq  h\left( {{\mathbf{X}}_{j} \mid  {\mathbf{G}}_{j}}\right)  + h\left( {{\mathbf{Y}}_{j} \mid  {\mathbf{G}}_{j}}\right)  + {\gamma h}\left( {{\mathbf{Z}}_{j} \mid  {\mathbf{G}}_{j}}\right)  + h\left( {\mathbf{G}}_{j}\right) \}  \tag{78}
$$

Let opt be the optimal objective value of the above LP. Then,there must exist an $\omega$ -Shannon inequality of the form (54) that satisfies the following:

设opt为上述LP的最优目标值，则必然存在满足下列条件的(54)式$\omega$-香农不等式：

- For each $i \in  \left\lbrack  I\right\rbrack$ ,we have ${X}_{i} = \varnothing$ and ${Y}_{i} \in  \mathcal{E}$ .

- 对于每个$i \in  \left\lbrack  I\right\rbrack$，有${X}_{i} = \varnothing$且${Y}_{i} \in  \mathcal{E}$。

- For each $j \in  \left\lbrack  J\right\rbrack$ ,we have ${\alpha }_{j} = {\beta }_{j} = {\kappa }_{j}$ and ${\zeta }_{j} = {\kappa }_{j} \cdot  \gamma$ .

- 对于每个$j \in  \left\lbrack  J\right\rbrack$，有${\alpha }_{j} = {\beta }_{j} = {\kappa }_{j}$且${\zeta }_{j} = {\kappa }_{j} \cdot  \gamma$。

- The coefficients of the inequality satisfy:

- 不等式系数满足：

$$
\frac{\parallel \mathbf{w}{\parallel }_{1}}{\parallel \mathbf{\lambda }{\parallel }_{1} + \parallel \mathbf{\kappa }{\parallel }_{1}} = \text{ opt. } \tag{79}
$$

Moreover,if $\omega$ is rational,then the above $\omega$ -Shannon inequality can be chosen to be integral (Definition E.6).

此外，若$\omega$为有理数，则上述$\omega$-香农不等式可选取为整式(定义E.6)。

Given a hypergraph $\mathcal{H} = \left( {\mathcal{V},\mathcal{E}}\right)$ and a generalized variable elimination order $\bar{\sigma } = \left( {{X}_{1},{X}_{2},\ldots ,{X}_{\left| \bar{\sigma }\right| }}\right)$ $\in  \bar{\pi }\left( \mathcal{V}\right)$ ,each set of vertices ${X}_{i}$ can be eliminated by either a join algorithm or some matrix multiplication. The concept of an $\omega$ -query plan,that we define below,amends a generalized variable elimination order with a mapping that specifies how to eliminate each variable.

给定超图$\mathcal{H} = \left( {\mathcal{V},\mathcal{E}}\right)$和广义变量消序$\bar{\sigma } = \left( {{X}_{1},{X}_{2},\ldots ,{X}_{\left| \bar{\sigma }\right| }}\right)$$\in  \bar{\pi }\left( \mathcal{V}\right)$，每个顶点集${X}_{i}$可通过连接算法或矩阵乘法消除。下文定义的$\omega$-查询计划概念，通过指定变量消除方式的映射来修正广义变量消序。

Definition E. 12 ( $\omega$ -Query Plan). Given a hypergraph $\mathcal{H} = \left( {\mathcal{V},\mathcal{E}}\right)$ ,an $\omega$ -query plan is a pair $\left( {\overline{\mathbf{\sigma }},e}\right)$ where:

定义E.12($\omega$-查询计划)。给定超图$\mathcal{H} = \left( {\mathcal{V},\mathcal{E}}\right)$，$\omega$-查询计划是指二元组$\left( {\overline{\mathbf{\sigma }},e}\right)$，其中：

- $\bar{\sigma } \in  \bar{\pi }\left( \mathcal{V}\right)$ is a generalized variable elimination order.

- $\bar{\sigma } \in  \bar{\pi }\left( \mathcal{V}\right)$为广义变量消序。

- $e$ is a function that maps every index $i \in  \left\lbrack  \left| \bar{\sigma }\right| \right\rbrack$ (that satisfies ${U}_{i}^{\bar{\sigma }} \nsubseteq  {U}_{j}^{\bar{\sigma }},\forall j \in  \left\lbrack  {i - 1}\right\rbrack$ ) to a term $e\left( i\right)  \in  \left\{  {h\left( {U}_{i}^{\bar{\sigma }}\right) }\right\}   \cup  \operatorname{args}\left( {\mathrm{{EM}}{M}_{i}^{\bar{\sigma }}}\right) .$

- $e$ 是一个将每个满足${U}_{i}^{\bar{\sigma }} \nsubseteq  {U}_{j}^{\bar{\sigma }},\forall j \in  \left\lbrack  {i - 1}\right\rbrack$条件的索引$i \in  \left\lbrack  \left| \bar{\sigma }\right| \right\rbrack$映射到项$e\left( i\right)  \in  \left\{  {h\left( {U}_{i}^{\bar{\sigma }}\right) }\right\}   \cup  \operatorname{args}\left( {\mathrm{{EM}}{M}_{i}^{\bar{\sigma }}}\right) .$的函数

Note that,for a given hypergraph $\mathcal{H}$ ,the number of $\omega$ -query plans is finite.

注意，对于给定的超图$\mathcal{H}$，$\omega$查询计划的数量是有限的。

Proof of THEOREM 7.1. Let $\mathcal{H} = \left( {\mathcal{V},\mathcal{E}}\right)$ be the query hypergraph. Consider the $\omega$ -submodular width of $\mathcal{H}$ written in the form of Eq. (33). For each $i \in  \lbrack \mathcal{I}\rbrack$ ,the corresponding LP (49) has an optimal objective value that is upper bounded by $\omega$ -subw(H). Consider a fixed LP (49),written in the form of Eq. (78), and let opt be its optimal objective value. By Lemma E.11, there exists an $\omega$ -Shannon inequality of the form (54) that satisfies the following (in addition to other properties stated in the lemma):

定理7.1的证明。设$\mathcal{H} = \left( {\mathcal{V},\mathcal{E}}\right)$为查询超图。考虑以方程(33)形式表示的$\mathcal{H}$的$\omega$-子模宽度。对于每个$i \in  \lbrack \mathcal{I}\rbrack$，对应的线性规划(49)具有最优目标值，该值上界为$\omega$-subw(H)。考虑一个固定形式的线性规划(49)，以方程(78)表示，设opt为其最优目标值。根据引理E.11，存在一个形式为(54)的$\omega$-香农不等式，满足以下条件（除引理中所述的其他性质外）：

$$
\frac{\parallel \mathbf{w}{\parallel }_{1}}{\parallel \mathbf{\lambda }{\parallel }_{1} + \parallel \mathbf{\kappa }{\parallel }_{1}} = \text{ opt } \leq  \omega  - \operatorname{subw}\left( \mathcal{H}\right) .
$$

Note that for every atom $R\left( Z\right)$ ,we have ${\deg }_{R}\left( {Z \mid  \varnothing }\right)  \leq  N$ . Define obj $\overset{\text{ def }}{ = }$ opt $\cdot  {\log }_{2}N$ . Then by Theorem E.10,we can,in time $\widetilde{O}\left( {2}^{\text{obj }}\right)  = \widetilde{O}\left( {N}^{\omega \text{-subw }\left( \mathcal{H}\right) }\right)$ ,compute tables ${P}_{\ell }\left( {U}_{\ell }\right)$ for $\ell  \in  \left\lbrack  L\right\rbrack$ and tables $\left( {{S}_{j}\left( {{X}_{j}{G}_{j}}\right) ,{T}_{j}\left( {{Y}_{j}{G}_{j}}\right) ,{W}_{j}\left( {{Z}_{j}{G}_{j}}\right) }\right)$ for $j \in  \left\lbrack  J\right\rbrack$ that satisfy the conditions stated in the theorem. We apply this for every LP (49) that we obtain from Eq. (33).

注意对于每个原子$R\left( Z\right)$，我们有${\deg }_{R}\left( {Z \mid  \varnothing }\right)  \leq  N$。定义obj$\overset{\text{ def }}{ = }$opt$\cdot  {\log }_{2}N$。根据定理E.10，我们可以在时间$\widetilde{O}\left( {2}^{\text{obj }}\right)  = \widetilde{O}\left( {N}^{\omega \text{-subw }\left( \mathcal{H}\right) }\right)$内计算$\ell  \in  \left\lbrack  L\right\rbrack$的表${P}_{\ell }\left( {U}_{\ell }\right)$和$j \in  \left\lbrack  J\right\rbrack$的表$\left( {{S}_{j}\left( {{X}_{j}{G}_{j}}\right) ,{T}_{j}\left( {{Y}_{j}{G}_{j}}\right) ,{W}_{j}\left( {{Z}_{j}{G}_{j}}\right) }\right)$，这些表满足定理所述条件。我们对从方程(33)获得的每个线性规划(49)都应用此方法。

The following claim basically says that at the end of the above process, we would have computed all inputs to a number of $\omega$ -query plans such that every tuple $t$ in the join ${ \bowtie  }_{R\left( Z\right)  \in  \operatorname{atoms}\left( Q\right) }R\left( Z\right)$ is accounted for by at least one query plan. The number of $\omega$ -query plans only depends on $\mathcal{H}$ ,hence, it is a constant in data complexity.

以下主张基本说明：在上述过程结束时，我们将计算出多个$\omega$查询计划的所有输入，使得连接${ \bowtie  }_{R\left( Z\right)  \in  \operatorname{atoms}\left( Q\right) }R\left( Z\right)$中的每个元组$t$都至少被一个查询计划所覆盖。$\omega$查询计划的数量仅取决于$\mathcal{H}$，因此在数据复杂度中它是一个常数。

CLAIM E.1. At the end of the above process,for each tuple $t \in  { \bowtie  }_{R\left( Z\right)  \in  \operatorname{atoms}\left( Q\right) }R\left( Z\right)$ ,there must exist a generalized variable elimination order $\bar{\sigma } \in  \bar{\pi }\left( \mathcal{V}\right)$ ,such that for every $i \in  \left\lbrack  \left| \bar{\sigma }\right| \right\rbrack$ that satisfies ${U}_{i}^{\bar{\sigma }} \nsubseteq  {U}_{j}^{\bar{\sigma }},\forall j \in  \left\lbrack  {i - 1}\right\rbrack   :$

权利要求E.1 在上述过程结束时，对于每个元组$t \in  { \bowtie  }_{R\left( Z\right)  \in  \operatorname{atoms}\left( Q\right) }R\left( Z\right)$，必须存在一个广义变量消元序$\bar{\sigma } \in  \bar{\pi }\left( \mathcal{V}\right)$，使得对于每个满足${U}_{i}^{\bar{\sigma }} \nsubseteq  {U}_{j}^{\bar{\sigma }},\forall j \in  \left\lbrack  {i - 1}\right\rbrack   :$的$i \in  \left\lbrack  \left| \bar{\sigma }\right| \right\rbrack$

(a) either there exists a table $P\left( {U}_{i}^{\bar{\sigma }}\right)$ of size $\widetilde{O}\left( {N}^{\omega  - \operatorname{subw}\left( \mathcal{H}\right) }\right)$ where ${\pi }_{{U}_{i}^{\bar{\sigma }}}\left( t\right)  \in  P$ ,

(a) 要么存在一个大小为$\widetilde{O}\left( {N}^{\omega  - \operatorname{subw}\left( \mathcal{H}\right) }\right)$的表$P\left( {U}_{i}^{\bar{\sigma }}\right)$，其中${\pi }_{{U}_{i}^{\bar{\sigma }}}\left( t\right)  \in  P$

(b) or there exists a term $\operatorname{MM}\left( {X;Y;Z \mid  G}\right)  \in  \operatorname{args}\left( {\mathrm{{EMM}}}_{i}^{\bar{\sigma }}\right)$ along with all the following:

(b) 要么存在一个项$\operatorname{MM}\left( {X;Y;Z \mid  G}\right)  \in  \operatorname{args}\left( {\mathrm{{EMM}}}_{i}^{\bar{\sigma }}\right)$及以下所有条件：

(b1) Tables ${S}_{1}\left( {XG}\right) ,{T}_{1}\left( {YG}\right) ,{W}_{1}\left( {ZG}\right)$ of size $\widetilde{O}\left( {N}^{\omega \text{-subw }\left( \mathcal{H}\right) }\right)$ such that ${\pi }_{XG}\left( t\right)  \in  {S}_{1},{\pi }_{YG}\left( t\right)  \in$ ${T}_{1}$ and ${\pi }_{ZG}\left( t\right)  \in  {W}_{1}$ and an $\omega$ -dominant triple $\left( {{\alpha }_{1},{\beta }_{1},{\zeta }_{1}}\right)$ satisfying:

(b1) 大小为$\widetilde{O}\left( {N}^{\omega \text{-subw }\left( \mathcal{H}\right) }\right)$的表${S}_{1}\left( {XG}\right) ,{T}_{1}\left( {YG}\right) ,{W}_{1}\left( {ZG}\right)$满足${\pi }_{XG}\left( t\right)  \in  {S}_{1},{\pi }_{YG}\left( t\right)  \in$${T}_{1}$和${\pi }_{ZG}\left( t\right)  \in  {W}_{1}$，以及一个满足以下条件的$\omega$主导三元组$\left( {{\alpha }_{1},{\beta }_{1},{\zeta }_{1}}\right)$：

$$
\left| {{\pi }_{G}\left( {S}_{1}\right) }\right|  \cdot  {\left( {\deg }_{{S}_{1}}\left( \mathbf{X} \mid  \mathbf{G}\right) \right) }^{{\alpha }_{1}} \cdot  {\left( {\deg }_{{T}_{1}}\left( \mathbf{Y} \mid  \mathbf{G}\right) \right) }^{{\beta }_{1}} \cdot  {\left( {\deg }_{{W}_{1}}\left( \mathbf{Z} \mid  \mathbf{G}\right) \right) }^{{\zeta }_{1}} = \widetilde{O}\left( {N}^{\omega  - \operatorname{subw}\left( \mathcal{H}\right) }\right)  \tag{80}
$$

(b2) And tables ${S}_{2}\left( {XG}\right) ,{T}_{2}\left( {YG}\right) ,{W}_{2}\left( {ZG}\right)$ of size $\widetilde{O}\left( {N}^{\omega \text{-subw }\left( \mathcal{H}\right) }\right)$ such that ${\pi }_{XG}\left( t\right)  \in  {S}_{2}$ , ${\pi }_{YG}\left( \mathbf{t}\right)  \in  {T}_{2}$ and ${\pi }_{ZG}\left( \mathbf{t}\right)  \in  {W}_{2}$ and an $\omega$ -dominant triple $\left( {{\alpha }_{2},{\beta }_{2},{\zeta }_{2}}\right)$ satisfying:

(b2) 且大小为$\widetilde{O}\left( {N}^{\omega \text{-subw }\left( \mathcal{H}\right) }\right)$的表${S}_{2}\left( {XG}\right) ,{T}_{2}\left( {YG}\right) ,{W}_{2}\left( {ZG}\right)$满足${\pi }_{XG}\left( t\right)  \in  {S}_{2}$、${\pi }_{YG}\left( \mathbf{t}\right)  \in  {T}_{2}$和${\pi }_{ZG}\left( \mathbf{t}\right)  \in  {W}_{2}$，以及一个满足以下条件的$\omega$主导三元组$\left( {{\alpha }_{2},{\beta }_{2},{\zeta }_{2}}\right)$：

$$
\left| {{\pi }_{G}\left( {S}_{2}\right) }\right|  \cdot  {\left( {\deg }_{{S}_{2}}\left( X \mid  G\right) \right) }^{{\alpha }_{2}} \cdot  {\left( {\deg }_{{T}_{2}}\left( Y \mid  G\right) \right) }^{{\zeta }_{2}} \cdot  {\left( {\deg }_{{W}_{2}}\left( Z \mid  G\right) \right) }^{{\beta }_{2}} = \widetilde{O}\left( {N}^{\omega  - \operatorname{subw}\left( \mathcal{H}\right) }\right)  \tag{81}
$$

(b3) And tables ${S}_{3}\left( {XG}\right) ,{T}_{3}\left( {YG}\right) ,{W}_{3}\left( {ZG}\right)$ of size $\widetilde{O}\left( {N}^{\omega \text{-subw }\left( \mathcal{H}\right) }\right)$ such that ${\pi }_{XG}\left( t\right)  \in  {S}_{3}$ , ${\pi }_{YG}\left( \mathbf{t}\right)  \in  {T}_{3}$ and ${\pi }_{ZG}\left( \mathbf{t}\right)  \in  {W}_{3}$ and an $\omega$ -dominant triple $\left( {{\alpha }_{3},{\beta }_{3},{\zeta }_{3}}\right)$ satisfying:

(b3) 且大小为$\widetilde{O}\left( {N}^{\omega \text{-subw }\left( \mathcal{H}\right) }\right)$的表${S}_{3}\left( {XG}\right) ,{T}_{3}\left( {YG}\right) ,{W}_{3}\left( {ZG}\right)$满足${\pi }_{XG}\left( t\right)  \in  {S}_{3}$、${\pi }_{YG}\left( \mathbf{t}\right)  \in  {T}_{3}$和${\pi }_{ZG}\left( \mathbf{t}\right)  \in  {W}_{3}$，以及一个满足以下条件的$\omega$主导三元组$\left( {{\alpha }_{3},{\beta }_{3},{\zeta }_{3}}\right)$：

$$
\left| {{\pi }_{G}\left( {S}_{3}\right) }\right|  \cdot  {\left( {\deg }_{{S}_{3}}\left( \mathbf{X} \mid  \mathbf{G}\right) \right) }^{{\zeta }_{3}} \cdot  {\left( {\deg }_{{T}_{3}}\left( Y \mid  \mathbf{G}\right) \right) }^{{\alpha }_{3}} \cdot  {\left( {\deg }_{{W}_{3}}\left( Z \mid  \mathbf{G}\right) \right) }^{{\beta }_{3}} = \widetilde{O}\left( {N}^{\omega  - \operatorname{subw}\left( \mathcal{H}\right) }\right)  \tag{82}
$$

Note that Case (b) in the above claim implies that the matrix multiplication corresponding to the term $\operatorname{MM}\left( {X;Y;Z \mid  G}\right)  \in  \operatorname{args}\left( {\mathrm{{EM}}{\mathrm{M}}_{i}^{\bar{\sigma }}}\right)$ can be done in time $\widetilde{O}\left( {N}^{\omega  - \operatorname{subw}\left( \mathcal{H}\right) }\right)$ . In particular,if we take the tables ${S}_{i},{T}_{i},{W}_{i}$ for $i \in  \left\lbrack  3\right\rbrack$ from the claim,and define

注意上述命题中的情形(b)意味着，对应于项$\operatorname{MM}\left( {X;Y;Z \mid  G}\right)  \in  \operatorname{args}\left( {\mathrm{{EM}}{\mathrm{M}}_{i}^{\bar{\sigma }}}\right)$的矩阵乘法可在$\widetilde{O}\left( {N}^{\omega  - \operatorname{subw}\left( \mathcal{H}\right) }\right)$时间内完成。特别地，若我们采用该命题中针对$i \in  \left\lbrack  3\right\rbrack$的表格${S}_{i},{T}_{i},{W}_{i}$，并定义

$$
S\overset{\text{ def }}{ = }{S}_{1} \cap  {S}_{2} \cap  {S}_{3},\;T\overset{\text{ def }}{ = }{T}_{1} \cap  {T}_{2} \cap  {T}_{3},\;W\overset{\text{ def }}{ = }{W}_{1} \cap  {W}_{2} \cap  {W}_{3}, \tag{83}
$$

then, Eq. (80), (81), and (82) imply that

则由方程(80)、(81)和(82)可得

$$
\left| {{\pi }_{G}\left( S\right) }\right|  \cdot  \max \left( {{\left( {\deg }_{S}\left( \mathbf{X} \mid  G\right) \right) }^{{\alpha }_{1}} \cdot  {\left( {\deg }_{T}\left( Y \mid  G\right) \right) }^{{\beta }_{1}} \cdot  {\left( {\deg }_{W}\left( Z \mid  G\right) \right) }^{{\zeta }_{1}},}\right. 
$$

$$
{\left( {\deg }_{S}\left( \mathbf{X} \mid  G\right) \right) }^{{\alpha }_{2}} \cdot  {\left( {\deg }_{T}\left( Y \mid  G\right) \right) }^{{\zeta }_{2}} \cdot  {\left( {\deg }_{W}\left( Z \mid  G\right) \right) }^{{\beta }_{2}},
$$

$$
{\left( {\deg }_{S}\left( \mathbf{X} \mid  \mathbf{G}\right) \right) }^{{\zeta }_{3}} \cdot  {\left( {\deg }_{T}\left( \mathbf{Y} \mid  \mathbf{G}\right) \right) }^{{\alpha }_{3}} \cdot  {\left( {\deg }_{W}\left( \mathbf{Z} \mid  \mathbf{G}\right) \right) }^{{\beta }_{3}})\; = \widetilde{O}\left( {N}^{\omega  - \operatorname{subw}\left( \mathcal{H}\right) }\right) 
$$

However, using the same reasoning from the proof of Proposition E.2, the LHS above is lower bounded by the LHS below:

然而，运用命题E.2证明中的相同推理方法，上式左侧可由下式左侧给出下界：

$$
\left| {{\pi }_{G}\left( S\right) }\right|  \cdot  \max \left( {{\deg }_{S}\left( {X \mid  G}\right)  \cdot  {\deg }_{T}\left( {Y \mid  G}\right)  \cdot  {\left( {\deg }_{W}\left( Z \mid  G\right) \right) }^{\gamma },}\right. 
$$

$$
{\deg }_{S}\left( {X \mid  G}\right)  \cdot  {\left( {\deg }_{T}\left( Y \mid  G\right) \right) }^{\gamma } \cdot  {\deg }_{W}\left( {Z \mid  G}\right) ,
$$

$$
{\left( {\deg }_{S}\left( X \mid  G\right) \right) }^{\gamma } \cdot  {\deg }_{T}\left( {Y \mid  G}\right)  \cdot  {\deg }_{W}\left( {Z \mid  G}\right) \; = \widetilde{O}\left( {N}^{\omega  - \operatorname{subw}\left( \mathcal{H}\right) }\right)  \tag{84}
$$

In particular,the claim implies that we can,in time $\widetilde{O}\left( {N}^{\omega \text{-subw }\left( \mathcal{H}\right) }\right)$ ,go over all tuples $\mathbf{g} \in  {\pi }_{G}\left( S\right)$ and for each $\mathbf{g}$ ,perform a matrix multiplication over two matrices whose three dimensions are ${\deg }_{S}\left( {\mathbf{X} \mid  \mathbf{G} = \mathbf{g}}\right) ,{\deg }_{T}\left( {\mathbf{Y} \mid  \mathbf{G} = \mathbf{g}}\right)$ ,and ${\deg }_{W}\left( {\mathbf{Z} \mid  \mathbf{G} = \mathbf{g}}\right)$ .

特别地，该命题表明我们能在$\widetilde{O}\left( {N}^{\omega \text{-subw }\left( \mathcal{H}\right) }\right)$时间内遍历所有元组$\mathbf{g} \in  {\pi }_{G}\left( S\right)$，并对每个$\mathbf{g}$执行两个三维分别为${\deg }_{S}\left( {\mathbf{X} \mid  \mathbf{G} = \mathbf{g}}\right) ,{\deg }_{T}\left( {\mathbf{Y} \mid  \mathbf{G} = \mathbf{g}}\right)$和${\deg }_{W}\left( {\mathbf{Z} \mid  \mathbf{G} = \mathbf{g}}\right)$的矩阵乘法。

Before proving the above claim, we show how to use it to prove Theorem 7.1. The remaining steps of the algorithm are as follows: We go over all $\omega$ -query plans. Consider a fixed $\omega$ -query plan $\left( {\overline{\mathbf{\sigma }},e}\right)$ ,where $\overline{\mathbf{\sigma }} = \left( {{X}_{1},\ldots ,{X}_{\left| \overline{\mathbf{\sigma }}\right| }}\right)$ . Initially,each hyperedge $Z \in  \mathcal{E}$ has a corresponding atom $R\left( Z\right)  \in  \operatorname{atoms}\left( Q\right)$ . We use the generalized variable elimination order $\bar{\sigma }$ to eliminate the variable sets ${X}_{1},\ldots ,{X}_{\left| \bar{\sigma }\right| }$ and generate the corresponding hypergraph sequence ${\mathcal{H}}_{1}^{\bar{\sigma }},\ldots ,{\mathcal{H}}_{\left| \bar{\sigma }\right| }^{\bar{\sigma }}$ ,as described in Definition 4.1. Each time we eliminate a variable set ${X}_{i}$ ,we remove adjacent hyperedges ${\partial }_{i}^{\bar{\sigma }}$ and add a new hyperedge ${U}_{i}^{\bar{\sigma }} \smallsetminus  {X}_{i}$ . Our target below is to create a new corresponding atom $R\left( {{U}_{i}^{\bar{\sigma }} \smallsetminus  {X}_{i}}\right)$ in time $\widetilde{O}\left( {N}^{\omega \text{-subw }\left( \mathcal{H}\right) }\right)$ . To that end,for each $i \in  \left\lbrack  \left| \overline{\mathbf{\sigma }}\right| \right\rbrack$ in order,we do the following:

在证明上述命题前，我们先说明如何运用它来证明定理7.1。算法的后续步骤如下：遍历所有$\omega$查询计划。考虑一个固定的$\omega$查询计划$\left( {\overline{\mathbf{\sigma }},e}\right)$，其中$\overline{\mathbf{\sigma }} = \left( {{X}_{1},\ldots ,{X}_{\left| \overline{\mathbf{\sigma }}\right| }}\right)$。初始时，每个超边$Z \in  \mathcal{E}$对应一个原子$R\left( Z\right)  \in  \operatorname{atoms}\left( Q\right)$。按照定义4.1所述，我们使用广义变量消元序$\bar{\sigma }$来消去变量集${X}_{1},\ldots ,{X}_{\left| \bar{\sigma }\right| }$，并生成对应的超图序列${\mathcal{H}}_{1}^{\bar{\sigma }},\ldots ,{\mathcal{H}}_{\left| \bar{\sigma }\right| }^{\bar{\sigma }}$。每次消去变量集${X}_{i}$时，我们会移除相邻超边${\partial }_{i}^{\bar{\sigma }}$并添加新超边${U}_{i}^{\bar{\sigma }} \smallsetminus  {X}_{i}$。我们的目标是在$\widetilde{O}\left( {N}^{\omega \text{-subw }\left( \mathcal{H}\right) }\right)$时间内创建新的对应原子$R\left( {{U}_{i}^{\bar{\sigma }} \smallsetminus  {X}_{i}}\right)$。为此，按顺序对每个$i \in  \left\lbrack  \left| \overline{\mathbf{\sigma }}\right| \right\rbrack$执行以下操作：

- If ${U}_{i}^{\bar{\sigma }} \nsubseteq  {U}_{j}^{\bar{\sigma }},\forall j \in  \left\lbrack  {i - 1}\right\rbrack$ ,we recognize two cases:

- 若${U}_{i}^{\bar{\sigma }} \nsubseteq  {U}_{j}^{\bar{\sigma }},\forall j \in  \left\lbrack  {i - 1}\right\rbrack$，则需区分两种情况：

- If $e\left( i\right)  = h\left( {U}_{i}^{\bar{\sigma }}\right)$ ,we compute the new atom $R\left( {{U}_{i}^{\bar{\sigma }} \smallsetminus  {X}_{i}}\right)$ by semi-join reducing the table $P\left( {U}_{i}^{\bar{\sigma }}\right)$ from Case (a) of Claim E. 1 with atoms $R\left( Z\right)$ corresponding to the hyperedges $Z \in  {\partial }_{i}^{\bar{\sigma }}$ (Definition 4.1),and then projecting ${\mathbf{X}}_{i}$ away:

- 若$e\left( i\right)  = h\left( {U}_{i}^{\bar{\sigma }}\right)$成立，我们通过半连接运算缩减表$P\left( {U}_{i}^{\bar{\sigma }}\right)$（来自权利要求E.1案例(a)）与超边$Z \in  {\partial }_{i}^{\bar{\sigma }}$对应的原子$R\left( Z\right)$（定义4.1），然后投影移除${\mathbf{X}}_{i}$，计算出新原子$R\left( {{U}_{i}^{\bar{\sigma }} \smallsetminus  {X}_{i}}\right)$：

$$
R\left( {{U}_{i}^{\bar{\sigma }} \smallsetminus  {X}_{i}}\right)  :  - P\left( {U}_{i}^{\bar{\sigma }}\right)  \land  \mathop{\bigwedge }\limits_{{Z \in  {\partial }_{i}^{\bar{\sigma }}}}R\left( Z\right) 
$$

Computing $R\left( {{U}_{i}^{\bar{\sigma }} \smallsetminus  {X}_{i}}\right)$ above takes time proportional to the size of $P\left( {U}_{i}^{\bar{\sigma }}\right)$ ,which is $\widetilde{O}\left( {N}^{\omega \text{-subw }\left( \mathcal{H}\right) }\right)$ . By induction,the query $Q$ before eliminating ${\mathbf{X}}_{i}$ has the same answer as the following query resulting from eliminating ${\mathbf{X}}_{i}$ :

上述计算$R\left( {{U}_{i}^{\bar{\sigma }} \smallsetminus  {X}_{i}}\right)$的时间与$P\left( {U}_{i}^{\bar{\sigma }}\right)$的大小成正比，即$\widetilde{O}\left( {N}^{\omega \text{-subw }\left( \mathcal{H}\right) }\right)$。根据归纳法，在消除${\mathbf{X}}_{i}$前的查询$Q$与消除${\mathbf{X}}_{i}$后得到的下列查询结果相同：

$$
{Q}^{\prime }\left( \right)  \mathrel{\text{:-}} R\left( {{U}_{i}^{\bar{\sigma }} \smallsetminus  {\mathbf{X}}_{i}}\right)  \land  \mathop{\bigwedge }\limits_{\substack{{R\left( Z\right)  \in  \operatorname{atoms}\left( Q\right) } \\  {Z \notin  {\partial }_{i}^{\bar{\sigma }}} }}R\left( Z\right)  \tag{85}
$$

- If $e\left( i\right)  = \operatorname{MM}\left( {Y;Z;{X}_{i} \mid  G}\right)$ where $\operatorname{MM}\left( {Y;Z;{X}_{i} \mid  G}\right)  \in  \operatorname{args}\left( {\mathrm{{EMM}}}_{i}^{\bar{\sigma }}\right)$ ,then by Eq. (22),there must exist $\mathcal{A},\mathcal{B} \subseteq  {\partial }_{i}^{\bar{\sigma }}$ such that $\mathcal{A} \cup  \mathcal{B} = {\partial }_{i}^{\bar{\sigma }}$ where $\mathbf{A}\overset{\text{ def }}{ = } \cup  \mathcal{A}$ and $\mathbf{B}\overset{\text{ def }}{ = } \cup  \mathcal{B}$ satisfy the following:

- 若$e\left( i\right)  = \operatorname{MM}\left( {Y;Z;{X}_{i} \mid  G}\right)$且$\operatorname{MM}\left( {Y;Z;{X}_{i} \mid  G}\right)  \in  \operatorname{args}\left( {\mathrm{{EMM}}}_{i}^{\bar{\sigma }}\right)$成立，则根据式(22)，必存在$\mathcal{A},\mathcal{B} \subseteq  {\partial }_{i}^{\bar{\sigma }}$使得$\mathcal{A} \cup  \mathcal{B} = {\partial }_{i}^{\bar{\sigma }}$成立，其中$\mathbf{A}\overset{\text{ def }}{ = } \cup  \mathcal{A}$与$\mathbf{B}\overset{\text{ def }}{ = } \cup  \mathcal{B}$满足以下条件：

$$
{X}_{i} \subseteq  A \cap  B,\;\left( {A \cap  B}\right)  \smallsetminus  {X}_{i} \subseteq  G \subseteq  \left( {A \cup  B}\right)  \smallsetminus  {X}_{i}
$$

$Y = \left( {A \smallsetminus  B}\right)  \smallsetminus  G,$

Consider the tables $S\left( {{X}_{i}G}\right) ,T\left( {YG}\right)$ and $W\left( {ZG}\right)$ defined by Eq. (83) from Claim E.1. We compute two relations:

考虑由权利要求E.1式(83)定义的表$S\left( {{X}_{i}G}\right) ,T\left( {YG}\right)$和$W\left( {ZG}\right)$。我们计算两个关系：

$$
{M}_{1}\left( {{GY}{X}_{i}}\right)  \mathrel{\text{:-}} S\left( {{X}_{i}G}\right)  \land  T\left( {YG}\right)  \land  \mathop{\bigwedge }\limits_{{{Z}^{\prime } \in  \mathcal{A}}}R\left( {Z}^{\prime }\right) ,
$$

$$
{M}_{2}\left( {G{X}_{i}Z}\right)  :  - S\left( {{X}_{i}G}\right)  \land  W\left( {ZG}\right)  \land  \mathop{\bigwedge }\limits_{{{Z}^{\prime } \in  \mathcal{B}}}R\left( {Z}^{\prime }\right) .
$$

Now for each $\mathbf{g} \in  {\pi }_{G}\left( S\right)$ ,we view ${\sigma }_{G = \mathbf{g}}\left( {M}_{1}\right)$ and ${\sigma }_{G = \mathbf{g}}\left( {M}_{2}\right)$ as two matrices of dimensions $Y \times  {X}_{i}$ and ${X}_{i} \times  Z$ respectively and perform a fast matrix multiplication. By combining the resulting matrices for all $\mathbf{g}$ ,we obtain the new atom $R\left( {{U}_{i}^{\overline{\mathbf{\sigma }}} \smallsetminus  {\mathbf{X}}_{i}}\right)  = R\left( {GYZ}\right)$ . By Eq. (84),this takes time $\widetilde{O}\left( {N}^{\omega \text{-subw }\left( \mathcal{H}\right) }\right)$ . Moreover,by induction,the query $Q$ before eliminating ${X}_{i}$ has the same answer as the query ${Q}^{\prime }$ from Eq. (85) that results from eliminating ${X}_{i}$ .

现对每个$\mathbf{g} \in  {\pi }_{G}\left( S\right)$，将${\sigma }_{G = \mathbf{g}}\left( {M}_{1}\right)$和${\sigma }_{G = \mathbf{g}}\left( {M}_{2}\right)$视为维度分别为$Y \times  {X}_{i}$和${X}_{i} \times  Z$的矩阵进行快速矩阵乘法。通过整合所有$\mathbf{g}$的运算结果矩阵，得到新原子$R\left( {{U}_{i}^{\overline{\mathbf{\sigma }}} \smallsetminus  {\mathbf{X}}_{i}}\right)  = R\left( {GYZ}\right)$。根据式(84)，该过程耗时$\widetilde{O}\left( {N}^{\omega \text{-subw }\left( \mathcal{H}\right) }\right)$。此外由归纳法，消除${X}_{i}$前的查询$Q$与式(85)中消除${X}_{i}$后得到的查询${Q}^{\prime }$结果相同。

- If ${U}_{i}^{\bar{\sigma }} \subseteq  {U}_{j}^{\bar{\sigma }}$ for some $j \in  \left\lbrack  {i - 1}\right\rbrack$ ,then ${U}_{i}^{\bar{\sigma }} \subseteq  {U}_{j}^{\bar{\sigma }} \smallsetminus  {X}_{j}$ . Hence,we can obtain $R\left( {{U}_{i}^{\bar{\sigma }} \smallsetminus  {X}_{i}}\right)$ by projecting $R\left( {{U}_{j}^{\bar{\sigma }} \smallsetminus  {X}_{j}}\right)$ .

- 若${U}_{i}^{\bar{\sigma }} \subseteq  {U}_{j}^{\bar{\sigma }}$对某个$j \in  \left\lbrack  {i - 1}\right\rbrack$成立，则${U}_{i}^{\bar{\sigma }} \subseteq  {U}_{j}^{\bar{\sigma }} \smallsetminus  {X}_{j}$。因此，我们可通过投影$R\left( {{U}_{j}^{\bar{\sigma }} \smallsetminus  {X}_{j}}\right)$得到$R\left( {{U}_{i}^{\bar{\sigma }} \smallsetminus  {X}_{i}}\right)$。

Finally, we prove Claim E.1.

最后，我们证明主张E.1。

Proof of CLAIM E.1. For sake of contradiction,suppose that there exists a tuple $t \in  { \bowtie  }_{R\left( Z\right)  \in  \operatorname{atoms}\left( Q\right) }$ $R\left( Z\right)$ where for every generalized elimination order $\bar{\sigma }$ ,there exists an index in $\left\lbrack  \left| \bar{\sigma }\right| \right\rbrack$ ,let’s call this index ${f}^{ * }\left( \overline{\mathbf{\sigma }}\right)$ ,such that:

主张E.1的证明。反设存在元组$t \in  { \bowtie  }_{R\left( Z\right)  \in  \operatorname{atoms}\left( Q\right) }$$R\left( Z\right)$，其中对于每个广义消序$\bar{\sigma }$，在$\left\lbrack  \left| \bar{\sigma }\right| \right\rbrack$中存在某个索引（记作${f}^{ * }\left( \overline{\mathbf{\sigma }}\right)$）使得：

- We don’t have a table $P\left( {U}_{{f}^{ * }\left( \bar{\sigma }\right) }^{\bar{\sigma }}\right)$ that contains the projection of $\mathbf{t}$ .

- 我们不存在包含$\mathbf{t}$投影的表$P\left( {U}_{{f}^{ * }\left( \bar{\sigma }\right) }^{\bar{\sigma }}\right)$。

- And,for every term $\operatorname{MM}\left( {X;Y;Z \mid  G}\right)  \in  \operatorname{args}\left( {\mathrm{{EMM}}\frac{\bar{\sigma }}{{f}^{ * }\left( \bar{\sigma }\right) }}\right)$ ,either one of conditions (b1),(b2),and (b3) of the claim is violated. We map each one of the three conditions (b1), (b2), and (b3) to one of the three terms in args $\left( {\mathrm{{MM}}\left( {X;Y;Z \mid  G}\right) }\right)$ from Eq. (21) in order. Let ${g}^{ * }\left( {\bar{\sigma },\mathrm{{MM}}\left( {X;Y;Z \mid  G}\right) }\right)$ be one term in args $\left( {\operatorname{MM}\left( {X;Y;Z \mid  G}\right) }\right)$ corresponding to one condition from (b1),(b2),or (b3) that is violated. (If more than one condition is violated, we pick any one arbitrarily.)

- 并且对于每个项$\operatorname{MM}\left( {X;Y;Z \mid  G}\right)  \in  \operatorname{args}\left( {\mathrm{{EMM}}\frac{\bar{\sigma }}{{f}^{ * }\left( \bar{\sigma }\right) }}\right)$，主张中的条件(b1)、(b2)或(b3)至少有一项不成立。我们将这三个条件依次映射到式(21)中args$\left( {\mathrm{{MM}}\left( {X;Y;Z \mid  G}\right) }\right)$的三个项。设${g}^{ * }\left( {\bar{\sigma },\mathrm{{MM}}\left( {X;Y;Z \mid  G}\right) }\right)$为args$\left( {\operatorname{MM}\left( {X;Y;Z \mid  G}\right) }\right)$中对应被违反条件（(b1)、(b2)或(b3)中任意一个）的项（若多个条件被违反，则任选其一）。

But now consider Eq. (32). Specifically,fix $f\overset{\text{ def }}{ = }{f}^{ * }$ and ${g}_{f}\overset{\text{ def }}{ = }{g}^{ * }$ and consider the corresponding inner LP. By Lemma E. 11 and Theorem E.10,there must exist some $\overline{\mathbf{\sigma }}$ such that:

现在考虑式(32)。具体地，固定$f\overset{\text{ def }}{ = }{f}^{ * }$和${g}_{f}\overset{\text{ def }}{ = }{g}^{ * }$并考察对应的内部线性规划。根据引理E.11和定理E.10，必存在某个$\overline{\mathbf{\sigma }}$满足：

- Either there exists a table $P\left( {U}_{{f}^{ * }\left( \bar{\sigma }\right) }^{\bar{\sigma }}\right)$ containing the projection of $t$ .

- 要么存在包含$t$投影的表$P\left( {U}_{{f}^{ * }\left( \bar{\sigma }\right) }^{\bar{\sigma }}\right)$

- Or there exists a term $\operatorname{MM}\left( {X;Y;Z \mid  G}\right)  \in  \operatorname{args}\left( {\operatorname{EMM}}_{{f}^{ * }\left( \bar{\sigma }\right) }^{\bar{\sigma }}\right)$ and three tables ${S}_{i}\left( {XG}\right) ,{T}_{i}\left( {YG}\right)$ , ${W}_{i}\left( {ZG}\right)$ containing the projection of $t$ and satisfying one of the three conditions (b1),(b2), or (b3),specifically the one corresponding to the term ${g}^{ * }\left( {\overline{\mathbf{\sigma }},\operatorname{MM}\left( {X;Y;Z \mid  G}\right) }\right)$ ,which we had assumed to be violated.

- 要么存在项$\operatorname{MM}\left( {X;Y;Z \mid  G}\right)  \in  \operatorname{args}\left( {\operatorname{EMM}}_{{f}^{ * }\left( \bar{\sigma }\right) }^{\bar{\sigma }}\right)$及三个表${S}_{i}\left( {XG}\right) ,{T}_{i}\left( {YG}\right)$、${W}_{i}\left( {ZG}\right)$包含$t$的投影，且满足三个条件(b1)、(b2)或(b3)中对应项${g}^{ * }\left( {\overline{\mathbf{\sigma }},\operatorname{MM}\left( {X;Y;Z \mid  G}\right) }\right)$的那个条件（而我们先前假设该条件被违反）

This is a contradiction.

由此导出矛盾。
